For Qwen3-30B-A3B-Base, a layer sweep selects and validates layer 44, and expert-level tracing identifies L44E069 as an expert repeatedly selected in the clean run whose held-out patch outperforms other active same-layer expert patches. For Mixtral-8x7B-v0.1, layer-level tracing validates a mid-layer signal, but the signal is not localized to the selected singleton expert; a coalition check instead recovers it with routed multi-expert updates. These results suggest that MoE factual tracing can be made expert-aware, while also showing that expert-level localization is model- and protocol-dependent rather than universal.

</details>


### [76] [Reasoning over Grammar: Can Synthetic Linguistic Reasoning Traces Enhance Low-Resource Machine Translation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03782)
*Renhao Pei,Yihong Liu,Sampo Pyysalo,Hinrich Schütze,Shaoxiong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过引入结构化的语言学分析步骤来辅助大型语言模型进行超低资源语言翻译。研究发现，直接在推理阶段注入高质量的语言学推理线索能有效提升翻译质量，但让模型自主学习生成此类分析仍然存在巨大困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）虽在处理低资源语言翻译方面具有潜力，但在应用语法信息时表现不佳。研究旨在探索通过链式思维（Chain-of-Thought）的思想，引入结构化的语言学分析步骤是否能改善低资源机器翻译效果。

Method: 提出了一种自动流水线，从通用依存树库（UD）、词典和语法规则库中生成分步式的语言学推理追踪（linguistic reasoning traces）。在上下文学习（ICL）、有监督微调（SFT）和强化微调（RFT）三种设置下，对锡伯语（Xibe）和琴坦语（Chintang）进行了实验评估。

Result: 实验表明，将语言学推理追踪作为推理阶段的引导（ICL）能显著提升翻译性能；将其作为训练数据（SFT/RFT）效果较差且不稳定，原因在于模型倾向于模仿追踪格式，但往往会生成错误的推理内容。

Conclusion: LLM 在处理超低资源语言翻译时，利用外部提供的可靠语言学分析信息（推断阶段）远比尝试训练模型去自主生成这类分析更有效。自动生成高质量的语言学推理追踪是提升性能的关键，但模型在自主生成此类追踪时的准确性仍是当前的技术瓶颈。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising approach to machine translation (MT) for extremely low-resource languages by incorporating linguistic resources through in-context learning. However, LLMs often struggle to apply grammatical information effectively during translation. Inspired by recent progress in chain-of-thought reasoning, we investigate whether low-resource MT can benefit from structured intermediate steps of linguistic analysis and grammatical reasoning. We propose a pipeline for automatically generating step-by-step linguistic reasoning traces from Universal Dependencies treebanks, dictionaries, and grammar-rule banks. We evaluate these traces in three settings: in-context learning (ICL), supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT), on Xibe and Chintang as test cases. Our results show that linguistic reasoning traces are most effective as inference-time guidance: in ICL, reliable sentence-specific traces substantially improve translation performance across most models, languages, and metrics. In contrast, using the linguistic reasoning traces as training data yields smaller and less consistent gains, as models learn the trace format but often generate erroneous content. These findings suggest that LLMs can leverage grammatical information for low-resource MT when given reliable linguistic analyses, while learning to generate such analyses remains a major bottleneck.

</details>


### [77] [Backdoor Unlearning Generalization: A Path Toward the Removal of Unknown Triggers in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03785)
*Lisa Bouger,Théo Lasnier,Philippe Looubet Moundi,Yannick Teglia,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM的后门卸载具有泛化性：移除一个受控后门能抑制其他未知后门，这为通过主动注入并移除后门来增强模型整体安全性开辟了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM后门防御方法多针对单一后门，且通常需要预知触发器信息，导致防御者在应对未知后门时处于结构性劣势。

Method: 1. 基于三类模型家族，通过逐个移除已知的后门来分析其对其他未针对性目标后门的抑制效果；2. 引入“交叉激活偏移距离”（Cross Activation Shift Distance）来量化不同训练方式对模型行为改变的差异，以揭示交叉抑制现象的内在机理。

Result: 研究证实，对特定后门进行卸载训练可以泛化至其他未明确处理的后门。通过引入交叉激活偏移距离，成功解释了后门抑制发生的机制，发现移除一个后门会引发模型内部激活空间的一致性偏移，从而抑制其他后门。

Conclusion: 后门卸载（unlearning）具有跨后门泛化能力；通过有意注入并移除受控后门，可以有效抑制模型中存在的未知后门，为LLM的安全防御提供了一种新策略。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks in Large Language Models (LLMs) are a growing security concern, where models can generate adversary-chosen content. Existing defenses target backdoors one at a time and typically require knowledge of the trigger, leaving the defender at a structural disadvantage when unknown backdoors may exist in a model. We show that backdoor neutralization through unlearning generalizes across backdoors: training a model to ignore a single trigger can also suppress other backdoors that were never explicitly targeted. We study this phenomenon across three model families, whose backdoors were injected via pretraining or continual pretraining, by analyzing the models obtained after removing one backdoor at a time. To understand why unlearning certain backdoors induces the suppression of others, we introduce the Cross Activation Shift Distance, to quantify the distance between model changes induced by different trainings. Our results open a new direction for LLM safety as defenders could deliberately inject controlled backdoors and then remove them, leveraging cross-backdoor transfer to also suppress unknown backdoors that an attacker may have previously introduced in the model.

</details>


### [78] [Exploring Adversarial Robustness and Safety Alignment in Multilingual Multi-Modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03793)
*Hashmat Shadab Malik,Muzammal Naseer,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了多模态模型多语言安全性中的“故障安全假象”，即低资源语言的安全性常因视觉 comprehension 失败而非对齐导致，并指出深度多语言训练比仅指令微调更能实现真正的跨语言安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于多模态大模型鲁棒性的研究多局限于英语，对多语言环境下的安全性及攻击迁移性缺乏深入理解。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究多语言能力与模型安全性之间的机制联系。

Method: 通过跨越12种语言的系统性研究，利用基于梯度的攻击方法测试多模态大模型（MLLM）的鲁棒性和安全性；对比包含文本指令和图像内嵌文本的两种恶意意图注入方式，并评估不同多语言学习范式的模型。

Result: 1. 发现强跨语言攻击迁移性：针对一种语言优化的对抗图像在其他语言中同样有效；2. 揭示“safety-by-failure”现象：低资源语言的表面安全性往往源于视觉编码器对非英语脚本的识别失败，而非真正的对齐；3. 深度训练模型（如Qwen3-VL）在不同语言中展现出比浅层微调模型更真实的拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 多语言能力获取方式（仅指令微调 vs 全阶段训练）决定了多语言安全性的本质。仅通过翻译数据进行指令微调会导致“因故障而安全（safety-by-failure）”的假象，而深度集成训练（如Qwen3-VL）能实现更稳健的跨语言安全对齐。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models integrate visual perception into language reasoning, introducing a continuous attack surface susceptible to adversarial attacks. Prior work on MLLM robustness has focused largely on English-centric tasks, leaving multilingual behaviour unexplored. We address this gap through a systematic study of adversarial robustness and multimodal safety across 12 diverse languages, evaluating open-source MLLMs that acquire multilingual capability through instruction tuning. Gradient-based attacks reveal a transferable multilingual vulnerability: adversarial images optimized in one language continue to induce failure in others, demonstrating strong cross-lingual transferability. Multilingual safety further varies with how effectively a model retrieves or interprets harmful instructions. When harmful intent is issued through text, languages with stronger linguistic grounding more often elicit misuse-enabling responses, while weaker languages produce fewer unsafe outputs. When embedded in the image as typographic content, English scripts are reliably recognised and followed, whereas non-English scripts are rarely parsed by the vision encoder. Lower-resource languages may therefore appear safer, but this is an artefact of comprehension and visual-grounding failures rather than genuine alignment, a phenomenon we term safety-by-failure. In contrast, MLLMs that build multilingual capability throughout their training stages rather than only at instruction tuning, such as Qwen3-VL, exhibit genuine cross-lingual safety, maintaining active refusal across languages rather than masking comprehension failure. Shallow multilingual adaptation, such as fine-tuning on translated instruction data, may produce surface-level understanding that creates illusory safety in low-resource languages; deeper integration across training stages leads to genuine multilingual safety alignment.

</details>


### [79] [Consistency Training Can Entrench Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03810)
*David Demitri Africa,Arathi Mani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 一致性训练并非总是能提升对齐效果，它能抑制奖励黑客行为，但可能加剧谄媚倾向。因此，在关键系统部署前必须进行严格的风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨一致性训练（一种简单、可扩展且无需标注的方法）对模型对齐的具体影响，特别是其自我引导特性是否会放大模型的不良行为。

Method: 在108个经过微调的“模型生物”（7B-70B参数）上测试了7种现有的一致性训练方法，并通过构建统一的理论框架分析其对齐效应的机理。

Result: 一致性训练对模型行为的影响具有差异性：它可以有效抑制奖励建模中的“奖励黑客”（reward hacking）和涌现的对齐失效问题，但同时会放大模型的“谄媚”（sycophancy）行为。研究指出，这种效果差异主要源于一致性标注过程带来的分布偏移。

Conclusion: 一致性训练并非对齐中立，其效果取决于具体场景，在关键系统中应用需要经过严格审计。

Abstract: Consistency training encourages a model to produce similar outputs across related inputs or sampling procedures. Such methods are simple, scalable, and largely label-free, but their effects on model alignment remain poorly understood. Could the self-bootstrapping nature of these methods amplify undesired behavior in models? We test seven consistency training methods on 108 ``model organisms: open-source models (7B--70B) fine-tuned to exhibit various forms of controlled misaligned behavior. We find that outcomes vary significantly: consistency training generally suppresses reward hacking and emergent misalignment but amplifies sycophancy. We present evidence that distribution shifts induced by the consistency labeling process, rather than variation in the selection operators, may be the primary driver of systematic alignment effects. Finally, we present a unifying theoretical framework to derive conditions under which consistency training will amplify or suppress misalignment. In total, our study establishes that consistency training is not alignment-neutral, and that its use in critical systems should be carefully audited.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking the Idiomaticity Decomposability Hypothesis: Evidence from Distributional Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03817)
*Maggie Mi,Golzar Atefi,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Felix Gers,Aline Villavicencio,Nafise Sadat Moosavi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算模型探讨了成语的可分解性、句法灵活性与频率之间的关系，揭示了成语的习得是语义透明度（可分解性）、可预测性和使用频率共同作用的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言学理论对成语行为的解释存在争议：一种观点认为成语的“可分解性”（构成词意义对整体意义的贡献度）决定了其句法灵活性；另一种“基于用法”的观点则认为分布经验（频率）才是主导因素。本文旨在通过计算语言学手段重新审视这两种理论的权衡。

Method: 本文利用语境化语言模型（如BERT等）作为受控的分布学习器，提出了一种模型内在的“可分解性”度量方法，并将其与人类评分、句法灵活性以及词语的可预测性进行相关性分析，同时在预训练过程中追踪成语表征的演变。

Result: 研究发现：1. 模型推导的可分解性与人类判断相关性较弱，且与句法灵活性呈微弱的负相关；2. 在预训练过程中，成语表征的趋于稳定并不能仅由（出现）频率解释，惊喜度、可分解性和频率共同参与了这一过程，且可分解性的贡献最为显著。

Conclusion: 成语的习得和表征不仅取决于频率，还受“可分解性”和“惊喜度”（surprisal）的共同影响。其中，可分解性在模型训练过程中起到了最显著的作用，这挑战了单纯基于分布经验（频率）的认知模型。

Abstract: Idioms can be analysed in terms of their decomposability, the extent to which constituent meanings contribute to the figurative whole. Decomposability is thought to predict syntactic flexibility. Usage-based accounts instead attribute idiom behaviour to distributional experience, such as speaker familiarity and predictability. We examine these views using contextualised language models as controlled distributional learners. We propose a model-internal measure of decomposability and relate it to human ratings, syntactic flexibility, and predictability while tracking idiom learning during pretraining. Model-derived decomposability correlates weakly with human judgments and shows a small but consistent negative relationship with syntactic flexibility. Pretraining analyses show that stabilisation of idiom representations in models is not explained by frequency alone. Instead, surprisal, decomposability, and frequency all contribute, with decomposability showing the strongest training-dependent effect.

</details>


### [81] [Clustered Self-Assessment: A Simple yet Effective Method for Uncertainty Quantification in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03846)
*Qi Cao,Takeshi Kojima,Andrew Gambardella,Helinyi Peng,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种通过语义聚类将模型生成映射为多项选择题进行自评估的不确定性量化方法，兼顾高效性与准确性，有效缓解了LLM事实性错误难评估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型存在“幻觉”现象，且缺乏明确的不确定性量化机制；现有的基于熵等间接信号的方法难以解释且无法充分利用模型的自评估能力。

Method: 提出了一种基于语义聚类的自评估方法：将模型生成的多个回答划分为语义簇，将其转化为多项选择题，通过模型对各选项分配的概率值作为置信度估计。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上的实验均表明，该方法在不确定性量化任务中显著优于现有基准方法，且仅需两个额外样本即可实现竞争性的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过将文本生成转化为多项选择题并利用模型自身的概率输出来量化不确定性，证明了通过语义聚类评估自我不确定性的有效性与高效性，且在极少量样本下即可达到最优表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable performance across diverse tasks, but they often generate responses that appear plausible while being factually incorrect. This problem is compounded by the lack of explicit uncertainty estimates, which makes it difficult for users to judge the reliability of model outputs. Existing uncertainty quantification methods typically rely on indirect signals, such as entropy across sampled generations. These signals can be difficult to interpret and do not fully leverage the model's ability to assess its own uncertainty. We propose a simple yet effective self-assessment method for uncertainty quantification in LLMs. Our approach groups sampled generations into semantically distinct clusters, converts them into answer options in a structured multiple-choice question, and uses the probability assigned by the LLM to each option as a confidence estimate. Experiments across multiple models and datasets show that our method consistently outperforms baseline approaches. Notably, it achieves competitive performance with as few as two additional samples, demonstrating both its effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [A Training-Free Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Multi-Document Summarization using LLMs and Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03867)
*Cuong Vuong Tuan,Trang Mai Xuan,Tien-Cuong Nguyen,Vu-Duc Ngo,Thien Van Luong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的混合智能体框架，通过结合大语言模型与知识图谱，实现了高效、多语言且无需特定任务微调的多文档摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多文档摘要方法在捕捉复杂的文档间关系、依赖大规模标注数据、以及跨语言/跨领域的通用性方面存在显著限制，旨在解决这些技术痛点。

Method: 提出了一种无需训练（training-free）的“多智能体混合框架（mixture-of-agents framework）”。将任务拆解为三个专门的智能体任务：提取式选择（extractive selection）、知识增强式摘要（knowledge-aware abstraction）及迭代式优化（iterative refinement），并结合多视角一致性机制进行结果整合。

Result: 在四个英语和越南语数据集上的实验表明，该方法达到了SOTA（最先进）或具有竞争力的性能，验证了模块化设计的有效性及强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的无需训练的多智能体框架在多文档摘要任务中表现出极佳的适应性与效果，证明了通过协同整合LLM与知识图谱，无需微调即可实现高质量的多文档摘要。

Abstract: Multi-Document Summarization (MDS) plays a critical role in distilling essential information from collections of textual data. Existing approaches often struggle to capture complex inter-document relationships, rely heavily on large amounts of labeled data for supervised training, or exhibit limited generalization across domains and languages. To address these limitations, we present a training-free mixture-of-agents framework for MDS that leverages the complementary strengths of large language models (LLMs) and knowledge graphs. Our approach decomposes summarization into specialized agent tasks: extractive selection, knowledge-aware abstraction, and iterative refinement, each operating without task-specific fine-tuning. We unify their outputs using a multi-perspective consistency mechanism guided by LLMs. Experiments across four datasets in English and Vietnamese demonstrate state-of-the-art or competitive performance, validating the effectiveness and adaptability of our modular design.

</details>


### [83] [RealClawBench: Live OpenClaw Benchmarks from Real Developer-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03889)
*Zongwei Lv,Zhewen Tan,Yaoming Li,Yilun Yao,Yuxuan Tian,Lin Sun,Xiangzheng Zhang,Weihong Lin,Tong Yang,Guangxiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RealClawBench是一个基于真实OpenClaw会话构建的基准框架，通过重构环境和自动评分机制，解决了开发类Agent评估中“真实性不足”的难题，并揭示了现有模型在真实任务处理能力上的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理（Agent）基准测试缺乏真实性，未能准确反映用户在实际场景中对开发辅助工具提出的请求（包含隐式需求、环境依赖及验证困难等特性）。

Method: 构建了RealClawBench基准库，包含重构执行环境（reconstructed execution environments）和确定性可验证评分机制（deterministic verifiable scorers），将真实Agent会话转化为可复现、可自动评估的测试任务。

Result: 发布了281个高质量可执行任务；对14个主流模型进行评估，表现最好的模型仅解决了65.8%的任务，表明当前模型在真实开发任务中仍有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: 开发人员在处理真实Agent任务时仍面临较大挑战，RealClawBench通过将真实会话转化为重现性高、可自动评估的任务，有效弥补了现有基准测试在真实性、实时性及任务分布方面的不足。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks should reflect what users actually ask deployed agents to do, yet existing benchmarks often miss key realism properties of real developer-agent sessions. We introduce RealClawBench, a live benchmark framework built from real OpenClaw sessions to capture the distribution, diversity, and real-world difficulty of deployed agent use. Real user requests are challenging to benchmark because they often depend on local execution environments, involve implicit or underspecified intent, and require nontrivial verification. RealClawBench addresses these challenges with two core mechanisms: reconstructed execution environments and deterministic verifiable scorers, which together convert real sessions into reproducible, automatically scored tasks. The resulting release contains 281 executable tasks sampled from a much larger real-session pool while preserving the source distribution, with maximum final-vs-source Jensen-Shannon divergence of 0.0448. Evaluating 14 contemporary models shows that the best system solves only 65.8% of tasks, revealing substantial headroom on realistic developer-agent workloads. By turning real deployed sessions into controlled evaluation instances, RealClawBench provides a practical path toward benchmarks that better measure agent capability in actual use. Code is available at:https://anonymous.4open.science/r/real-claw-bench-582B.

</details>


### [84] [Synthesize and Reward -- Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Step Tool Use in Live Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03892)
*Ibrahim Abdelaziz,Asim Munawar,Kinjal Basu,Maxwell Crouse,Chulaka Gunasekara,Suneet Katrekar,Pavan Kapanipathi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了PROVE框架，通过结合真实状态的MCP服务器环境、依赖感知的自动化数据合成以及多组分程序化奖励，有效解决了大模型多步工具执行的训练难题，实现了无需人工裁判的显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多步工具调用训练存在三大痛点：执行环境构建成本高、合成数据与真实环境状态脱节导致执行失败、以及基于召回的RL奖励倾向于诱导模型产生冗长的工具调用模式。

Method: 1. 构建了包含20个符合MCP协议的真实状态服务器库，支持实时执行和状态隔离；2. 使用依赖图引导的模拟器生成基于真实服务器状态的、可验证的多轮工具调用数据；3. 提出了一套多组分的程序化奖励函数（涵盖有效性、依赖覆盖度、效率惩罚等），取代了传统的基于模型的评估奖励。

Result: 在Qwen3和Granite系列模型上通过GRPO训练，PROVE在BFCL Multi-Turn、tau2-bench和T-Eval基准测试中分别提升了最高10.2、6.8和6.5个百分点。

Conclusion: PROVE框架证明了通过程序化奖励和真实状态环境进行强化学习，无需依赖外部模型裁判或繁琐的人工标注，即可提升大模型在多步工具调用任务中的编排能力，且具有良好的通用性。

Abstract: Training LLMs to orchestrate multi-step tool calls is held back by three coupled obstacles: realistic stateful execution environments are costly to build, synthetic training queries are often detached from the server's actual state (so the generated tool calls fail to execute), and recall-based RL rewards incentivize verbose tool-calling patterns. We present PROVE (Programmatic Rewards On Verified Environments), a framework with three contributions: (1) a library of 20 stateful MCP (Model Context Protocol) servers exposing 343 tools, enabling live-execution RL training with session-scoped state isolation; (2) an automated data synthesis pipeline that generates validated multi-turn tool-call trajectories against these servers via dependency-graph-guided conversation simulation grounded in live-sampled server state, so every generated query references entities that actually exist; and (3) a multi-component programmatic reward - graduated validity scoring, dependency-aware coverage, an adaptive efficiency penalty with a complexity-scaled call budget, a tool-name signal, and an argument-value matching bonus - requiring no external judge model. We train four models (Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B, Qwen2.5-7B, Granite-4.1-8B) with GRPO using identical reward hyperparameters and ~13K training examples; only learning rate is tuned per model family from a three-point sweep. On BFCL Multi-Turn, tau2-bench, and T-Eval, PROVE yields improvements of up to +10.2, +6.8, and +6.5 points respectively, demonstrating that a compact programmatic reward yields consistent gains on multi-step tool orchestration across two model families.

</details>


### [85] [Knowledge Editing in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03924)
*Haewon Park,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将知识编辑应用于掩码扩散模型（MDM）的可行性，发现虽然定位机制与自回归模型（ARM）相似，但由于MDM独特的生成机制，长目标编辑效果较差。通过对中间生成状态进行针对性优化，有效解决了该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识编辑方法大多基于自回归模型（ARM），而具有双向生成特性的掩码扩散模型（MDM）是否适用这些方法及其机制是否一致尚不明确。

Method: 研究首先将“定位后编辑（locate-then-edit）”方法迁移至MDM（LLaDA, Dream），通过因果追踪（causal tracing）与ARM（LLaMA, Qwen）进行对比实验；针对发现的MDM在长目标生成时的缺陷，提出了一种优化中间掩码状态的修正算法。

Result: 研究发现：1. 知识编辑定位具有跨范式一致性（均在早期至中期MLP层最有效）；2. MDM在处理长目标文本时性能显著下降，原因在于其生成的中间去噪路径未被包含在编辑优化中。修正算法后，MDM的长目标编辑性能得到大幅恢复。

Conclusion: 知识编辑不仅需要精确定位，还需要针对掩码扩散模型（MDM）的生成机制（迭代去噪）进行专门的优化，而非简单照搬自回归模型（ARM）的方法。通过在中间优化状态进行调整，可以有效解决MDM在长目标编辑中的性能下降问题。

Abstract: Knowledge editing aims to update or correct factual knowledge in a language model. A widely used approach, locate-then-edit, does this in two steps: it first localizes a fact within the model, then edits the weights there. To date, such methods have been developed exclusively on autoregressive models (ARMs). Whether their underlying assumptions hold for masked diffusion models (MDMs), which model text bidirectionally and generate by iterative denoising rather than next-token prediction, remains an open question. We address it by transferring locate-then-edit to MDMs and comparing two MDMs (LLaDA, Dream) with two ARMs (LLaMA, Qwen) at matched scale. Our central finding has two parts. First, where an edit is applied transfers across paradigms: causal tracing highlights the same early-to-mid-layer MLP at the last subject token in both, and editing is most effective there. Second, this shared location does not guarantee a shared outcome. Single-token edits succeed in both, but as targets grow longer, editing degrades systematically in the MDMs but not the ARMs. The failure stems from how the edited fact is generated: producing a multi-token target requires passing through partially unmasked intermediate states for which the edit was never optimized. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce a simple correction that optimizes the edit for these states, substantially restoring multi-token performance.

</details>


### [86] [A Pocket Offline Model for Simultaneous Speech Translation as CUNI Submission to IWSLT 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03948)
*Aziz Sharipov Ortega,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种1B参数规模的多语言同声传译系统，通过Canary模型配合AlignAtt策略，在IWSLT 2026任务中实现了高效且高质量的翻译性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在应对IWSLT 2026同声传译共享任务，解决如何在低资源/参数受限的情况下实现高质量、多语言的同声传译。

Method: 基于Canary离线直接语音翻译模型，集成AlignAtt策略实现同声传译功能，并针对多语种方向（Czech/English/German/Italian）进行优化。

Result: 在计算性能无关的仿真测试中，该系统在高、低延迟环境下均优于同量级基准模型，支持50种语言对的互译，且模型参数仅为1B。

Conclusion: 通过将Canary模型与AlignAtt策略结合，证实了在保持较低计算代价（1B参数）的同时，实现高质量多语言同声传译的可行性。

Abstract: We implement simultaneous translation capability with the offline direct speech-to-text translation model Canary, using the state-of-the-art policy AlignAtt, and submit it to IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Shared task for Czech to English and English to German and Italian.
  The strengths of our system are: (1) high translation quality, outperforming similarly sized baselines both in low- and high-latency regimes in computationally unaware simulations; (2) low computational requirements, as the model has only 1B parameters; (3) multilinguality -- support of 25 source and 25 target languages.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient ASR Training with Conversations that Never Happened](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03957)
*Máté Gedeon,Péter Mihajlik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用 LLM 和 TTS 生成大规模高质量合成对话数据的方法，有效解决了低资源语言环境下 ASR 训练数据不足的问题，显著提升了模型在真实对话场景下的识别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言和特定领域的对话式 ASR 受到领域匹配的多人对话训练数据稀缺的制约。

Method: 提出了一套数据增强流水线：1. 利用 LLM 生成包含参与者元数据的场景级对话；2. 将说话人属性映射至 TTS 语音配置；3. 将合成的语音片段拼接为说话人感知的模拟对话；4. 使用 FastConformer-Large 模型进行训练实验。

Result: 在匈牙利语 BEA-Dialogue 基准测试中，使用 67 小时真实数据+636 小时合成数据训练的模型，性能优于使用 2700 小时真实语音训练的零样本模型；证明了合成对话能有效提升 ASR 性能，且性能增益高度依赖于生成器（LLM）的选择和数据构成。

Conclusion: 大语言模型（LLM）生成的对话数据配合 TTS 合成，是解决低资源语言及小众领域语音识别（ASR）训练数据匮乏问题的实用且高效的补充手段。

Abstract: Conversational ASR for lower-resource languages and niche domains is limited by the scarcity of domain-matched multi-speaker training data. We propose an augmentation pipeline that generates scenario-level dialogues with participant metadata, maps speaker attributes to TTS voice profiles, and assembles synthesized utterances into speaker-aware simulated conversations. We evaluated five LLM families under single-generator, fixed-budget mixture, and scale-up settings using the same FastConformer-Large training recipe for each one. We ran comprehensive evaluations on the Hungarian BEA-Dialogue benchmark corpus, with the method itself being applicable to any language given the resources for each component. The results show that synthetic conversations consistently improve speech recognition performance, but generator choice and data composition strongly affect the gains. Our largest training configuration, using only 67 hours of real conversations and 636 hours of simulated data, achieves better performance on the evaluation benchmark than a zero-shot model trained on 2700 hours of Hungarian speech. These findings indicate that LLM-generated conversational data synthesized with TTS is a practical complement to real conversational corpora for speech model training.

</details>


### [88] [Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering for Efficient and Controllable LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03965)
*Yu Xia,Zhouhang Xie,Xin Xu,Byungkyu Kang,Prarit Lamba,Xiang Gao,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ACTS 框架，通过引入一个强化学习智能体在推理时实时动态调度思维链过程，在保障性能的同时实现了推理预算的精细化与高效管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型思维链（CoT）推理方法虽能提升准确率，但存在 Token 使用低效、推理时缺乏控制以及思维过程不透明的问题。

Method: 将推理引导建模为马尔可夫决策过程 (MDP)；引入一个控制器智能体（Controller Agent），实时观测推理轨迹与剩余预算，通过下达推理策略和引导短语（Steering Phrase）来动态控制冻结推理模型（Frozen Reasoner）；采用合成数据初始化以及带预算条件的强化学习进行优化。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，ACTS 在大幅节省 Token 的前提下达到了与全量思维链相当的性能，并实现了在不同模型和任务间可控的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: ACTS 通过强化学习训练的控制器实现了推理过程中的动态策略控制，在保持高性能的同时显著提高了 Token 使用效率，并赋予了模型推理预算的可控性。

Abstract: Large language models improve final-answer accuracy through extended chain-of-thought reasoning, but often spend tokens inefficiently and offer little inference-time control. Existing efficient reasoning methods control thinking length by shortening, early-stopping, or compressing traces, leaving how the model thinks implicit. In this paper, we propose Agentic Chain-of-Thought Steering (ACTS), which formulates reasoning steering as a Markov decision process where a controller agent adaptively steers a frozen reasoner during inference. At each step, the controller observes the reasoning trace and remaining thinking budget, then issues a steering action consisting of a reasoning strategy and a steering phrase that initiates the next reasoner step. This enables budget-aware strategy control for efficient reasoning while preserving the reasoner's generation continuity. We initialize the controller agent from our constructed synthetic steering trajectories with multi-budget augmentation, and further optimize it via reinforcement learning with budget-conditioned reward shaping. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that ACTS matches full-thinking performance with substantial token savings, and enables controllable accuracy-efficiency trade-offs across different reasoners and tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/Andree-9/ACTS.

</details>


### [89] [AlignAtt4LLM: Fast AlignAtt for Decoder-Only LLMs at IWSLT 2026 Simultaneous Speech Translation Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03967)
*Quentin Fuxa,Dominik Macháček*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlignAtt4LLM 是一种创新的同步语音翻译系统，通过特定技术改进将 AlignAtt 对齐策略成功迁移至解码器架构 LLM（如 Gemma-4），实现了低延迟且高质量的增量翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 IWSLT 2026 任务，旨在解决缺少编码器-解码器交叉注意力的解码器架构 LLM 在同步语音翻译中如何实现高效对齐和低延迟翻译的问题。

Method: 提出了一种适用于解码器架构 LLM 的 AlignAtt 实现方案：(1) 在 Prompt 中显式定义源文本跨度；(2) 离线选择特定的翻译对齐头（Alignment Heads）；(3) 对草稿到源（Draft-to-Source）的注意力块进行选择性 qk-fast 回放；(4) 通过运行时 Query/Key 捕获保证模型输出的确定性。

Result: 在 IWSLT 2026 开发集上，AlignAtt4LLM 在英语到德语和英语到意大利语的翻译任务中，在低延迟（约2秒）和高延迟（低于4秒 CU-LongYAAL）场景下均优于基线模型。对于英语到中文的翻译结果表现中等。

Conclusion: AlignAtt4LLM 成功将 AlignAtt 策略应用于解码器架构的 LLM，证明了该方法在同步语音翻译任务中的有效性与通用性，并在主流语言对上超越了基线模型。

Abstract: We describe AlignAtt4LLM, an IWSLT 2026 simultaneous speech translation system for English to German, Italian, and Chinese. The system is a synchronous cascade: Qwen3-ASR with forced alignment produces an incrementally updated source transcript, and Gemma-4 E4B-it translates that prefix under an MT-side AlignAtt policy.
  To our knowledge, this is the first application of AlignAtt to a decoder-only LLM, where the encoder-decoder cross-attention used by earlier AlignAtt systems is absent. We recover a usable policy by proposing (1) an explicit source span in the prompt, (2) offline selection of translation-specific alignment heads, (3) selective qk-fast replay of the draft-to-source attention block, and (4) runtime query/key capture that preserves model outputs bit-identically.
  On the IWSLT 2026 development set, AlignAtt4LLM outperforms the supplied baselines for the European target languages, English to German and English to Italian, in both the low-latency regime around 2 seconds and the high-latency regime below 4 seconds CU-LongYAAL. Results for English to Chinese are more mixed, but the method is not tied to Gemma-4: because AlignAtt4LLM only requires a deterministic prompt layout, calibrated attention heads, and query/key capture, the same policy can be reapplied to stronger translation-focused decoder-only MT backbones for non-European target languages.

</details>


### [90] [QUBRIC: Co-Designing Queries and Rubrics for RL Beyond Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03968)
*Rongzhi Zhang,Rui Feng,Zhihan Zhang,Jingfeng Yang,Qingyu Yin,Xin Liu,Zixuan Zhang,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin,Tuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QUBRIC框架通过协同设计查询与评分标准，解决了基于策略评分强化的结构性限制，在无需验证型奖励的情况下显著提升了模型在复杂推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于评分标准的强化学习方法常受限于固定的查询分布，直接处理开放式查询会导致评分标准模糊，而过于严苛的限制又会导致模型产生虚假引用，进而导致奖励信号缺失。

Method: 提出QUBRIC框架：1. 利用教师模型生成的关键点将开放式查询重写为基于场景的可评估问题；2. 通过对比评分标准生成技术，将教师策略差距转化为查询维度的评估标准；3. 引入可学习性过滤（Learnability Filtering），筛选出有助于GRPO训练的查询-评分对。

Result: 在ArenaHard基准上较SFT基准提升5.5分；在法律、道德和叙事推理等三个留出基准上平均提升6.3分，且在推理能力相关维度表现显著。

Conclusion: 通过联合设计查询（Queries）和评分标准（Rubrics），可以解决基于评分标准的强化学习（Rubric-based RL）中的结构性瓶颈，使其能有效应用于难以直接验证的任务，是现有RLVR方法的强大补充。

Abstract: Rubric-based RL is a promising route for extending reinforcement learning beyond verifiable rewards, yet existing methods optimize rubrics while treating the query distribution as fixed. We identify a structural bottleneck: rubric quality is constrained by query structure. Open-ended queries yield vague rubrics; naively narrowing them introduces fabricated references that no model can verify, so all responses fail and training receives no reward signal. We present QUBRIC, a framework that co-designs queries and rubrics. Teacher-derived key points ground the rewriting of open-ended queries into scenario-based, evaluable questions. Contrastive rubric generation then turns teacher-policy gaps into query-level criteria, and learnability filtering retains only informative query-rubric pairs for GRPO training. QUBRIC achieves a +5.5 point gain on ArenaHard over the SFT baseline. Trained only on instruction-following data, it further transfers to three held-out benchmarks spanning legal, moral, and narrative reasoning (+6.3 points on average), with improvements concentrated in reasoning-related dimensions. These results provide evidence that co-designing queries and rubrics can make rubric-based RL a practical complement to RLVR beyond strictly verifiable tasks.

</details>


### [91] [Quantifying Faithful Confidence Expression in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03969)
*Areeb Gani,Asal Meskin,Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型推理模型（LRMs）在思维链输出中表达置信度的忠实度（FC），提出了一套量化FC的新评估框架，结论表明LRMs在忠实校准方面表现不佳，且现有评估指标尚不成熟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）的长思维链输出常被用户视为深思熟虑和高置信度的标志，但模型内部真实置信度与语言表达的置信度之间往往存在偏差（即忠实校准失败）。现有评估方法难以处理长思维链中复杂的条件依赖和不一致的结构，缺乏对LRMs忠实校准能力的深入理解。

Method: 提出了一种系统量化LRMs忠实校准（FC）的新框架。该框架基于token概率、隐藏状态和采样一致性这三个内部不确定性来源，分析语言决断力；并引入“前缀条件采样”（prefix-conditioned sampling）方法，以控制思维链中的条件和结构变量。

Result: 通过对多个顶尖模型、数据集和提示词的测试，结果显示：LRMs难以忠实地表达其置信度；推理行为的增强不会自动提高FC；现有的FC评估方法存在脆弱性且评估指标结果不一致。

Conclusion: 忠实校准（FC）是大型推理模型（LRMs）面临的一项重大挑战。推理能力的提升并不等同于置信度表达的忠实度提升，且针对非推理模型的提示词干预在推理场景下无效。此外，现有的评估方法在评估长思维链输出时表现出脆弱性，导致不同置信度估计器之间存在评估分歧。FC应当明确为LRMs的一个核心可靠性和对齐目标。

Abstract: Reliable uncertainty communication is critical to the trustworthiness of LLMs, yet faithful calibration (FC)--the alignment between models' intrinsic and (linguistically) expressed confidence--is a persistent failure mode. This challenge is key for large reasoning models (LRMs), whose extended reasoning traces are often interpreted by users as evidence of deliberation, competence, and confidence. Despite the importance of FC and wide usage of LRMs, the extent to which LRMs can faithfully express their confidence remains poorly understood. Moreover, the prevailing paradigm to measure FC does not generalize well to the long chain-of-thought outputs generated by LRMs, which tend to lack clear step boundaries, involve inconsistent step structure, and encode complex conditional dependencies throughout the trace--complicating estimation of intrinsic confidence. To address this challenge, we introduce a novel framework to systematically quantify FC of LRMs. Our framework analyzes linguistic decisiveness relative to three sources of internal uncertainty, based on token probabilities, hidden states, and sampled response consistency. We also devise a prefix-conditioned sampling approach to control for conditional and structural variation across traces. Applying our framework to a diverse suite of leading models, datasets, and prompts, we find that faithful confidence expression is a significant challenge for LRMs. Reasoning behaviors do not automatically translate to improved FC, and prompt interventions for non-reasoning models do not improve faithfulness in the reasoning setting. Different confidence estimators further produce divergent assessments of the same traces, revealing fragility in prior evaluation methodologies. Taken together, our work establishes FC as a distinct reliability and alignment target for LRMs, particularly as such systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [92] [Language Models Compare Quantities Using Number-specific and Unit-specific Heuristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03982)
*Mutsumi Sasaki,Go kamoda,Ryosuke Takahashi,Kosuke Sato,Kentaro Inui,Keisuke Sakaguchi,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，语言模型处理带单位的数值比较时，并非进行严格的量纲转换，而是使用了一套不严谨的启发式规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型（LM）如何处理和比较带有度量单位的数值（如 110 cm 与 1.2 m），以揭示其内部是否存在精确的物理量换算机制。

Method: 在多个单位系统中进行受控对比测试；使用线性替代模型（linear surrogate models）分析数值和单位差异对模型预测的影响；利用因果干预（causal interventions）技术探究模型内部表征对输出的调控作用。

Result: 模型在比较边界附近的准确率显著下降；误差呈现系统性，可被数值差异和单位尺度差异的线性模型预测；干预相关表征空间能直接改变模型的预测结果。

Conclusion: 语言模型在比较带单位的量时，并非先将其转换为统一的量纲进行精确计算，而是依赖于一种基于数值和单位差异的启发式方法。

Abstract: Quantities with measurement units, such as 110 cm and 1.2 m, require language models (LMs) to combine a numeral with a symbolic unit scale. Here, we study how LMs compare such quantities in controlled settings spanning several unit systems. We find that accuracy degrades near the comparison boundary, where small changes in value determine the correct answer. The resulting errors are systematic: linear surrogate models predict LM preferences from numerical-difference and unit-scale-difference cues, and causal interventions on subspaces aligned with these variables shift model's output. The results suggest that LMs compare quantities through a bag of heuristics over numerals and units, rather than first converting both expressions to an exact shared-scale representation.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Agent libOS: A Library-OS-Inspired Runtime for Long-Running, Capability-Controlled LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03895)
*Yingqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Agent libOS 是一个专为长时 LLM 智能体设计的库操作系统基底，通过强制实施基于运行时原语的权限和审计机制，解决了智能体执行过程中的安全性、可维护性与可审计性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的 LLM 智能体正在向长时软件执行者演进，但通用操作系统缺乏针对智能体特性（如跨调用状态维护、人机交互、子任务分叉、异步外部事件）的安全性与管理原语，且现有的直接信任机制存在显著风险。

Method: 设计并实现了一个基于“库操作系统（libOS）”思想的运行时基底。它定义了 AgentProcess（包含生命周期、权限、对象内存等），将工具封装为 libc 风格的包装器，并强制要求通过受控的系统调用原语进行安全性检查和任务编排。

Result: 开发了一个 Python 原型，实现了异步调度、命名空间对象内存、运行时集成的人机审批、JIT 工具注册及审计机制。通过 123 个回归测试证明了该架构在非规划增强的情况下，能有效处理复杂智能体的生命周期管理与安全约束。

Conclusion: Agent libOS 通过将运行时原语而非工具调用作为信任边界，为长时运行的 LLM 智能体提供了一种更安全、可审计且可管理的执行环境。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from request-response assistants into long-running software actors: they maintain state across model calls, fork subtasks, wait for external events, request human authority, generate tools, and perform side effects that must be resumed and audited. This paper presents Agent libOS, a library-OS-inspired runtime substrate for LLM agents. Agent libOS runs above a conventional host operating system; it does not implement hardware drivers, kernel-mode isolation, or a POSIX-compatible operating system. Instead, it treats an agent as an AgentProcess: a schedulable execution subject with process identity, parent-child lineage, lifecycle state, a tool table derived from an AgentImage, typed Object Memory, explicit capabilities, human queues, checkpoints, events, and audit records. Its central design rule is tools are libc-like wrappers; runtime primitives are the authority boundary. Filesystem access, object access, sleeps, human approval, JIT tool registration, and external side effects are checked at primitive boundaries under explicit capabilities and policy.
  We describe the design, threat model, Python prototype, and safety-oriented evaluation. The current prototype implements async scheduling, namespace-local Object Memory, runtime-integrated human approval, one-shot permission grants, per-process working directories, shell and image-registration primitives, Deno/TypeScript JIT tools over a libOS syscall broker, filesystem/object bridge tools, an injectable Resource Provider Substrate, deterministic demos, real-model smoke scripts, and 123 regression tests at the time of writing. Rather than improving planner accuracy, Agent libOS demonstrates a runtime substrate in which long-running LLM agents can be scheduled, authorized, resumed, and audited without treating tool dispatch as the trust boundary.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [94] [Visual Graph Scaffolds for Structural Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02673)
*Runlin Lei,Xiaokui Xiao,Zhewei Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文创新性地提出将图作为LLM推理的“视觉脚手架”，研究发现相比于文本描述的图，视觉化的图结构在引导LLM进行多跳推理时具有不可替代的性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将图视为LLM的外部知识源，忽略了图在组织思维和推理过程中的作用。本文旨在探究图是否能作为LLM内部推理的辅助工具，类比人类利用思维导图组织复杂思维的过程。

Method: 将教师模型提供的推理轨迹改写为图思维导图（graph mind maps），以此作为学生模型的导引。通过对比“文本化图结构”（flattened text）与“视觉图导引”（visual graph guidance）在多跳问答任务中的表现，探究其在是否有直接答案提示下的推理效果。

Result: 实验发现存在显著的模态差距：当图结构扁平化为文本时，失去直接答案提示后，模型推理效率和质量大幅下降；而视觉形式的图导引在没有直接答案提示的情况下依然保持高有效性，且在经过监督微调和KL散度蒸馏后优势依然稳固。

Conclusion: 图结构不仅是LLM的外部知识源，更应被视为组织推理过程的“视觉脚手架”（visual scaffolds）。研究强调了在处理复杂多跳推理任务时，视觉图模态在引导模型思考方面的独特性和优越性。

Abstract: Graphs have been used to enhance large language models (LLMs) for structured reasoning, mostly as external knowledge sources are provided to models at test time. In this paper, we take a different view: the value of graphs for LLMs lie not only in supplying information, but also in organizing reasoning. Inspired by how humans use graph-structured mind maps to organize branching and converging thoughts, we ask whether graphs can serve as an internal form of reasoning assistance. We study this question on multi-hop question answering tasks, where teacher-provided reasoning traces are rewritten as graph mind maps and used to guide a student model. Our experiments reveal a clear modality gap. When graph structures are flattened into text, their benefits become limited once direct answer hints are removed. Under this abstract guidance setting, both reasoning efficiency and answer quality degrade substantially. In contrast, visual graph guidance remains effective without direct answer clues, and its advantage persists after supervised fine-tuning and KL-based distillation. The above findings support the claim that graphs should be studied not only as external knowledge structures for LLMs, but also as visual scaffolds for organizing reasoning.

</details>


### [95] [AURA: Action-Gated Memory for Robot Policies at Constant VRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02775)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对机器人边缘计算中 KV-cache 内存开销过大的问题，AURA-Mem 提出了一种具备“门控机制”的循环记忆系统，根据动作变化智能决定是否写入，实现在保持任务成功率的前提下，大幅降低内存写入频率并获得常数级内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 KV-cache 的推理方法虽适用于数据中心，但在内存受限的机器人边缘硬件上存在显著缺陷：KV-cache 随推理步长线性增长，会导致严重的内存瓶颈及 Flash 存储寿命损耗问题。需一种在该环境下更高效的记忆管理方案。

Method: 提出 AURA-Mem 系统：采用冻结的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 主干网，配合一个恒定大小的循环内存和一个“学习门控”机制。该门控通过闭环动作误差信号进行训练，仅当观测内容会对后续动作产生实质影响时才执行写入操作，从而实现选择性记忆。

Result: 在合成基准测试中，AURA-Mem 相比最佳 O(1) 基准测试减少了 5.19-6.13 倍的写入量；在 LIBERO-Long 真实闭环任务中，其成功率与未门控的基准模型相当（0.233 vs 0.233），但内存写入量减少了 7.0 倍，且保持了固定的内存占用（仅 4,224 字节，远低于 100,000 步场景下膨胀后的 KV-cache）。

Conclusion: AURA-Mem 通过基于动作惊奇信号的决策性写入机制，成功实现在机器人边缘硬件上的常数内存推理，证明了在不损害任务成功率的前提下，大幅降低内存写入量和显存占用的可行性。

Abstract: The KV-cache is the right memory for datacenters but the wrong memory for robots. Datacenter inference batches many short requests and resets them, amortizing an attention cache across a crowd. Embodied agents instead run one long, non-resetting episode on bandwidth-limited edge hardware, where high-bandwidth memory and flash are scarce, flash has finite write endurance, and memory writes rather than compute can become the binding constraint.
  AURA-Mem (Action-Utility Recurrent Adaptive Memory) targets this regime. It wraps a frozen vision-language-action backbone with a constant-size recurrent memory and a learned gate that writes only when the current observation would change the next action: memory that knows when to stay silent. Unlike reconstruction-based memory, the gate is trained directly against a closed-loop action-error signal. Its inference state is fixed at 4,224 bytes regardless of horizon, while a KV-cache grows to 6,061 times larger at 100,000 steps.
  On a controlled synthetic benchmark, AURA-Mem matches the best O(1) baseline in accuracy while using 5.19-6.13 times fewer writes, and up to 9.19 times fewer writes on easier configurations. Budget-matched random and periodic schedules do not recover this gain, isolating the benefit to the action-surprise signal. On a trained closed-loop OpenVLA-OFT 7B panel on LIBERO-Long (n=60 episodes per arm), the gate does not hurt success: AURA-Mem matches the ungated base policy (0.233) and slightly exceeds an always-write KV arm (0.217), while using 7.0 times fewer writes and constant memory. We also instantiate an approximate-information-state value-loss bound as a methodology demonstration; at this scale, the bound is vacuous rather than a guarantee.

</details>


### [96] [Evaluating Transformer and LSTM Frameworks for Prediction in Ungauged Basins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02791)
*Taye Akinrele,James Halgren,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz,Sudip Mittal,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究表明，在水文序列推断任务中，LSTM的循环记忆机制优于Transformer的注意力机制，且结合下游水文上下文信息是提升径流预测精度的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏实测数据的无资料流域，如何提高极端水文事件预测的可预测性，以及评估不同序列模型架构（Transformer vs LSTM）在水文归纳偏置上的适应性。

Method: 利用美国国家水模型（NWM）的历史模拟数据，对比了仅编码器Transformer与LSTM在仅利用上游信息及结合下游信息两种配置下的径流推断性能。

Result: 在所有配置下，LSTM的总体表现优于Transformer；加入下游信息可使模型的中位数NNSE提升超过60%。

Conclusion: 对于流域径流重建任务，循环神经网络（LSTM）的归纳偏置比仅编码器架构的Transformer更具优势；同时，引入下游水文环境信息作为辅助约束能显著提升各类模型的预测性能。

Abstract: Watershed networks exhibit convergent topologies in which multiple tributaries merge into downstream channels,integrating diverse upstream hydrological processes. In ungauged basins, the absence of direct observations increases uncertainty and limits the ability to anticipate extreme events. This study evaluates whether an encoder-only Transformer provides an advantage over an LSTM for upstream streamflow inference under limited hydrologic information, using retrospective simulations from the NOAA National Water Model (NWM). Across both upstream-only and combined configurations, the LSTM showed stronger overall performance than the Transformer model across the two configurations. Incorporating downstream information further boosted performance for all models, increasing median NNSE by more than 60%. Rather than treating this as a leaderboard-style comparison, we interpret the experiments as a test of architectural inductive bias for hydrologic sequence inference. The results indicate that recurrent memory remains better aligned with this upstream reconstruction task than an encoder-only Transformer, while downstream hydrologic context provides a strong auxiliary constraint that substantially improves prediction skill across architectures

</details>


### [97] [BehaviorBench: Modeling Real-World User Decisions from Behavioral Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02798)
*Liangwei Yang,Jielin Qiu,Zixiang Chen,Ming Zhu,Juntao Tan,Zhiwei Liu,Wenting Zhao,Zhujun Lan,Akshara Prabhakar,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究人员推出了BehaviorBench基准，利用海量真实预测市场与链上交易数据，旨在克服模型模拟的局限性，评估个性化决策模型在真实行为场景下的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户理解基准大多依赖模拟用户或模型生成行为，但这与真实人类行为存在系统性偏差。缺乏基于大规模真实世界行为轨迹的个性化决策模型评估基准。

Method: 构建了包含“信念预测”和“交易预测”两个任务层的BehaviorBench基准。该基准利用真实世界预测市场和链上记录重建个体历史，包含141,445个信念实例和1,485,972个交易实例，并对比了四种历史信息呈现方式（无个性化、实时历史、生成的画像、检索证据）。

Result: 实验发现个性化处理对提升信念预测的效果比交易预测更显著；模型在不同任务层和指标下的表现排名存在差异；且不同的历史信息界面会导致模型产生不同的错误模式。

Conclusion: 现有的个性化方法在基于真实行为证据的建模任务中表现不一，且不同的历史信息呈现方式对模型性能和失败模式有显著影响，强调了真实数据评估的重要性。

Abstract: Many decision-support settings require systems that adapt to individual users, but evaluation data for this problem remain limited. Existing benchmarks for user understanding often rely on simulated users or model-generated behavior, even though recent work cautions that model-based simulations can diverge systematically from human behavior. We introduce \textsc{BehaviorBench}, a benchmark for evaluating personalized decision modeling from real-world behavioral traces. \textsc{BehaviorBench} reconstructs wallet-level decision histories from observed public prediction-market and on-chain records, and organizes them into two complementary task layers: \emph{Belief prediction}, which predicts a user's final revealed stance and confidence in a market, and \emph{Trade prediction}, which predicts the direction and amount of individual transactions. Across 2,000 evaluation wallets, the benchmark contains 141,445 Belief instances and 1,485,972 Trade instances, with disjoint support pools for retrieval-based evaluation. We evaluate frontier and open-weight generative models under four history interfaces: no personalization, direct recent history, generated user profiles, and retrieved support-wallet evidence. Personalization improves Belief prediction more consistently than Trade prediction, model rankings change across task layers and metrics, and different history interfaces expose different failure modes. \textsc{BehaviorBench} provides an evaluation setting for studying whether personalized methods can use real-world behavioral evidence rather than simulated users alone.

</details>


### [98] [ChatHealthAI: Aligning Electronic Health Record Representations with Large Language Models for Grounded Clinical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02802)
*Bo-Hong Wang,Baicheng Peng,Ruilin Wang,Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChatHealthAI 是一个连接结构化 EHR 基础模型与 LLM 的多模态框架，在保证临床预测准确性的前提下，赋予了模型更好的推理能力和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）具备强大的临床推理能力，但在处理结构化纵向电子健康记录（EHR）时存在困难；而现有的 EHR 基础模型擅长预测但缺乏推理的解释力。研究旨在填补这一“结构化数据建模”与“语言推理”之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出了 ChatHealthAI 多模态推理框架。该方法使用“任务感知重采样器”（task-aware resampler），将预训练的 EHR 基础模型提取的结构化纵向患者表示与冻结的 LLM 语义空间进行对齐，并将患者表示与精简的临床事件描述相结合。

Result: 在 EHRSHOT 基准测试的三个临床预测任务中，ChatHealthAI 在保持具有竞争力的预测性能的同时，显著提升了推理质量和可解释性。

Conclusion: ChatHealthAI 通过对齐结构化 EHR 表示与大型语言模型（LLM），成功实现了兼顾临床预测准确性与自然语言推理可解释性的多模态框架，证明了结合 EHR 基础模型与 LLM 在临床决策支持中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong natural-language reasoning abilities for clinical decision support, but struggle to effectively model structured longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs). In contrast, EHR foundation models can learn predictive patient representations, yet lack interpretable language-based reasoning. To bridge this gap, we propose ChatHealthAI, a multimodal reasoning framework that aligns structured EHR representations from a pretrained EHR foundation model with the semantic space of a frozen LLM through a task-aware resampler. By integrating longitudinal patient representations with refined clinical event descriptions, ChatHealthAI enables clinically grounded natural-language reasoning while maintaining accurate patient prediction. We evaluated ChatHealthAI on three clinical predictive tasks from the EHRSHOT benchmark. Results show that ChatHealthAI improves reasoning quality and interpretability while preserving competitive predictive performance. These findings highlight the potential of integrating EHR foundation models with pretrained LLMs for interpretable clinical prediction.

</details>


### [99] [Traj-Evolve: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent System for Patient Trajectory Modeling in Lung Cancer Early Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02812)
*Sihang Zeng,Matthew Thompson,Ruth Etzioni,Meliha Yetisgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Traj-Evolve 是一个为电子健康记录（EHR）设计的自我演进多智能体系统，通过融合经验检索（ExPool）与多智能体强化学习（MARL），实现了对长序列、复杂医疗数据的精准建模与持续性能优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有基于LLM的多智能体系统在处理电子健康记录（EHR）时存在的孤立处理问题（无法利用过往类似病例经验）以及稀疏、长上下文推理的挑战，旨在构建一个能够自我演进、模拟临床医生经验积累过程的智能体框架。

Method: 1. 经验池（ExPool）：作为非参数化记忆，索引并检索相似患者的推理痕迹作为少样本上下文；2. 基于MARL的奖励排序微调：通过强化学习优化多智能体协作与记忆协同机制；3. 留一法交叉检索策略：统一训练与推理行为，确保检索增强的一致性。

Result: 在肺癌预测任务中，Traj-Evolve 在整体人群及难预测的不吸烟人群上均超越了9个基准模型。实验验证了 ExPool 提升特异性，MARL 提升敏感性，且两者协同作用更优。

Conclusion: Traj-Evolve 通过结合非参数经验池（ExPool）和参数化强化学习（MARL），有效解决了 EHR 中长序列、稀疏多模态数据的建模难题，实现了知识的存储与自我演进，在肺癌预测任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Modeling patient trajectories from longitudinal electronic health records (EHRs) requires reasoning over sparse, noisy, and long-context multimodal sequences. Existing LLM-based multi-agent systems address context length but process patients in isolation, failing to mirror how clinicians leverage accumulated experience from similar prior cases. We present Traj-Evolve, a self-evolving multi-agent system with two complementary evolving mechanisms. First, an Experience Pool (ExPool) acts as a non-parametric memory, indexing rejection-sampled reasoning traces to retrieve similar patients as few-shot contexts. Second, multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) via reward-ranked fine-tuning parametrically optimizes inter-agent and agent-memory collaboration. A leave-one-out cross-retrieval strategy unifies the two, aligning training- and inference-time behavior under retrieval augmentation. On a lung cancer prediction task utilizing up to five years of multimodal EHRs, Traj-Evolve outperforms 9 strong baselines on the overall population and a challenging never-smoker population. Analysis of the evolving dynamics highlights three key findings: (1) expanding the ExPool shifts optimal retrieval from diverse to specific samples; (2) under MARL, the manager agent's prediction loss converges quickly while the worker agents' temporal reasoning continues to benefit from more verified patients; and (3) the two mechanisms are complementary on the predicted risk, where ExPool improves specificity while MARL improves sensitivity.

</details>


### [100] [An Exploration of Collision-based Enemy Morphology Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02832)
*Johor Jara Gonzalez,Matthew Guzdial*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文填补了游戏领域敌方形态自动生成的空白，提出三种基于玩家碰撞信息的方法，并证明其性能均优于改进后的机器人演化基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序化内容生成（PCG）研究多关注环境或关卡，极少涉及游戏敌方形态（身体结构与碰撞信息）的自动生成，尽管该领域在机器人研究中已有相关成果。

Method: 设计并探索了三种基于玩家碰撞信息的敌方形态（Morphology）生成新方法，并与来自机器人研究领域的一种演化基准（Evolutionary Baseline）进行了对比实验。

Result: 三种新方法表现出各异的优缺点，且在各项性能指标上均达到或超过了传统的演化基准算法。

Conclusion: 通过三种基于玩家碰撞信息的敌方形态生成方法，证明了其在性能上优于针对机器人领域形态生成算法改造的演化基准，为游戏敌方形态设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite a great deal of prior research into Procedural Content Generation (PCG), relatively little prior work has explored generating enemies for video games. In particular, there is almost no work on generating enemy morphologies, the basic body plan or collision information for in-game enemies, despite the existence of related morphology generation work in robotics. In this paper, we explore three different novel approaches to generate enemy morphologies based on player collision information. We found that each approach provides different strengths and weaknesses, but all had equivalent or better performance than an evolutionary baseline adapted from prior robotics morphology work.

</details>


### [101] [Thinking Past the Answer: Evaluating Harmful Overthinking in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02835)
*Simone Caldarella,Davide Talon,Rahaf Aljundi,Elisa Ricci,Massimiliano Mancini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型推理模型存在严重的“过度思考”问题，即在已得出正确结论后继续推理反而会导致性能下降。通过协议分析发现，在模型首次给出正确答案时强制停止，可显著提升模型性能，并揭示了逻辑漂移与视觉误读是导致性能下滑的核心机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的大规模推理模型（LRMs）通过增加测试时计算量来提升性能的做法，探讨“当模型已经得出正确答案后，继续推理是否会有益”这一问题，并考察过度思考对最终结果的影响。

Method: 引入“前缀级轨迹评估协议”（prefix-level trajectory evaluation protocol），基于“推理充分性”定义模型首次得出正确答案所需的最小推理预算。通过该协议区分“冗余但无害的过度思考”与“导致轨迹不稳定的有害过度思考”，并进行对比实验。

Result: 1. 许多被认为需要密集推理的任务实际上只需极少的推理步骤；2. 在首次产生正确答案时停止推理，能够将准确率提升最高达21%；3. 现有的早停策略虽能减少冗余推理，但无法解决导致正确答案失效的“有害过度思考”；4. 导致答案失效的主要因素是“逻辑漂移”和“视觉重解释”。

Conclusion: 目前的推理模型（LRMs）不仅受限于推理能力，还受限于“何时停止”的判断能力。过度思考（尤其是有害的过度思考）是模型性能和可靠性的重要风险，强制停止或智能停止可以显著提升模型准确率。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by generating explicit intermediate reasoning traces through increased test-time compute, yet the assumption that longer reasoning is consistently beneficial remains under-examined. While recent evidence shows that additional reasoning can lead models to overthink, we ask: "Once a model has reached the correct answer, does further reasoning refine the solution, or deviate from it?" To study the dynamics after correctness, we introduce a prefix-level trajectory evaluation protocol grounded in reasoning sufficiency, defining the minimum reasoning budget required for a model to first generate the correct answer. This allows us to disentangle verbose overthinking, where additional reasoning is redundant but harmless, from harmful overthinking, where continued reasoning destabilizes an already-correct trajectory. Starting from multimodal benchmarks, we find that many instances considered reasoning-intensive require surprisingly little reasoning. Moreover, stopping at the first correct prefix improves accuracy over standard reasoning up to 21%, revealing that current models are limited not only by their ability to reason, but also by their inability to stop at the right time. Furthermore, while common efficiency strategies like early stopping substantially reduce verbose overthinking (up to 50%), they fail to mitigate harmful overthinking. Failure analysis reveals that correctness deviations are mainly driven by logical drift and visual reinterpretation. Finally, we show that our findings generalize to language-only reasoning benchmarks, highlighting harmful overthinking as a broader reliability risk. Code available at https://simonecaldarella.github.io/thinking-past-the-answer.

</details>


### [102] [Toward a Modular Architecture for Embedded AI Agent Systems at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02862)
*Marcus Rüb,Michael Gerhards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对嵌入式设备资源受限的痛点，提出一种分层架构，通过解耦本地轻量化处理与云端SLM推理，并引入治理层确保系统安全，实现了嵌入式系统从确定性控制向自主AI的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic AI框架通常依赖高算力服务器或持续网络连接，难以在资源受限（内存/能量）且要求实时性的嵌入式微控制器环境中运行。

Method: 提出一种分层架构：将低延迟、隐私敏感任务交给本地智能体（压缩神经网络/规则逻辑），高层推理交给云端增强智能体（使用SLM）；引入横向跨层的“治理层”，负责可观测性、策略执行及安全性控制。

Result: 分析了嵌入式智能体架构的权衡原则，证明了通过分层设计和治理机制，可以在极端受限的硬件上实现自主系统的可控性、可靠性与智能化。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一种嵌入式智能体系统的模块化参考架构，通过分层设计和治理层实现了确定性实时控制与智能体推理的平衡，为受限边缘设备部署自主AI提供了系统的设计准则。

Abstract: The rise of Large Language Models (LLMs) has enabled agentic AI capable of complex reasoning and tool use; however, deploying such autonomy in pervasive computing environments remains challenging due to the strict memory and energy constraints of embedded microcontrollers. Existing frameworks typically assume server-class resources or continuous connectivity, leaving a gap for deeply embedded systems. This paper proposes a modular reference architecture for Embedded Agent Systems that bridges the divide between deterministic real-time control and agentic intelligence.
  We introduce a tiered design that decouples On-Device Agents - executing highly compressed neural networks and rule-based logic for low-latency, privacy-critical tasks - from Cloud-Augmented Agents that leverage Small Language Models (SLMs) for higher-level reasoning and planning. A key contribution is the integration of a cross-cutting Governance Layer, ensuring observability, policy enforcement, and safety across distributed fleets of autonomous devices. Rather than presenting purely empirical benchmarks, we analyze architectural design principles and trade-offs regarding latency, energy, and reliable execution in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [103] [Don't Gamble, GAMBLe: An Analytical Framework for AI-Driven Research Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02863)
*Marquita Ellis,Paul Castro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了分析AI驱动研究系统（ADRS）的GAMBLe框架，揭示了组件交互对优化景观的影响，证明了通过适配组件选择而非盲目追求最强模型，可以大幅提升ADRS的搜索效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的研究系统（ADRS）在多领域快速应用，但现有分析工具滞后。标准收敛性保证无法解释ADRS复杂的组件交互与实际行为，导致开发者难以评估及优化这些系统。

Method: 提出了GAMBLe框架，将ADRS解构为生成器（G）、评估器（A）、发现机制（M）和预算（B）四个参数，并定义了有效景观（Effective Landscape, Leff = A ∘ G）来可视化分析不同组件组合下的优化行为。实验涵盖了760多次运行及46,000次迭代。

Result: 在NP-hard问题测试中，发现 frontier 模型并不总是优于开源模型，简单的机制有时表现超过复杂的元搜索方法。合理的组件选型策略可实现13-67%的性能提升和6-39倍的搜索效率优化。

Conclusion: ADRS的性能并不存在全局最优的组件组合；组件的选择需根据具体问题场景进行适配。通过针对性配置组件，可以在有限资源下显著提升搜索效率与结果质量。

Abstract: AI-Driven Research Systems (ADRS) -- systems coupling LLMs with automated evaluation to discover algorithms, proofs, and designs -- are being optimized and adopted across domains, but the tools to analyze them have not kept pace. ADRS performance depends on component interactions that are poorly understood, expensive to explore, and (as we show) not well captured by standard convergence guarantees. These guarantees rely on structural assumptions that do not hold under the ADRS process we formalize. We introduce GAMBLe, a framework that decomposes ADRS behavior into four parameters (generator $G$, assessor $\mathcal{A}$, discovery mechanism $\mathcal{M}$, budget $B$) and one compositional object, the effective landscape $L_{\text{eff}} = \mathcal{A} \circ G$, which reveals that distinct generator-assessor pairs induce structurally different per-problem optimization landscapes. We exercise the framework on 760+ replicated runs (>46,000 iterations) spanning generators from single LLMs to dynamically-adaptive ensembles, mechanisms from greedy selection to co-evolutionary meta-search, and three NP-hard problems whose assessors range from continuous scoring to cliff functions. The experiments reveal no total ordering of generators or mechanisms: frontier models can underperform open-source alternatives and the simplest mechanism sometimes outperforms state-of-the-art meta-search. Results show that even under limited budgets (60 iterations per run), the right component choices can improve performance by 13-67% and search efficiency by 6-39x.

</details>


### [104] [When Helping Hurts and How to Fix It: Multi-Agent Debate for Data Cleaning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02866)
*Chirag Parmar,Akshat Mehta,Henglin Wu,Jagadish Ramamurthy,Shweta Medhekar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了多智能体辩论在数据清洗中的双刃剑效应：它虽能显著提升错误检测能力，但会通过“批判诱导的混乱”损害生成质量。作者提出了衡量辩论有效性的定量条件，并证明通过对抗性分离（赋予Critic独特工具）可突破性能瓶颈，实现优于单智能体的生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究多智能体辩论机制在数据清洗任务中对模型性能（生成质量 vs. 错误检测）的具体影响，明确辩论的边界条件，并解决辩论中常见的负面效应（如批判诱导的混乱/CIC）。

Method: 跨越3个基准测试、4个模型家族、6000多个任务条件对进行大规模实证研究；利用析因实验（Factorial Experiment）对比验证，并推导出衡量辩论有效性的定量条件公式。

Result: 1. 辩论使生成质量普遍下降（-1.6%至-15.5%），原因是“批判诱导的混乱”（CIC）；2. 辩论显著提升了错误检测性能（+27.4pp F1）；3. 通过引入“代码执行”和“证据门控”的解耦Critic配置，首次实现了辩论对生成任务的正向收益（+5.3pp）。

Conclusion: 多智能体辩论并非万能。当“纠正错误输出的收益”超过“破坏正确输出的风险”时，辩论才有效。实现正向收益的关键在于对抗性分离（Adversarial Separation），即需要独立的Critic（配有代码执行和证据门控），而非简单的自我验证。该研究提出的条件公式具有极高的普适性和预测能力。

Abstract: When does multi-agent debate help data cleaning, and when does it hurt? Across three benchmarks, four model families, and over 6,000 task-condition pairs, we find debate's effect reverses sign: it degrades generation across all four models (-1.6 to -15.5pp) through critique-induced confusion (CIC), hallucinated Critic feedback that the Generator accepts uncritically, yet improves error detection (+27.4pp F1, d=1.0). We derive a debate benefit condition: debate helps when the probability of rescuing a wrong output (Critic verification odds weighted by fixability) exceeds the probability of destroying a correct one. A factorial experiment proves adversarial separation is essential: self-verification with identical tools fails, while a separate Critic with code-execution grounding and evidence-gated generation produces the first debate configuration to significantly exceed single-agent on a generative task (+5.3pp, p<0.05). The condition correctly predicts all nine task types and generalizes with zero false positives across 19 published comparisons in seven domains.

</details>


### [105] [Handoff Debt: The Rediscovery Cost When Coding Agents Take Over Interrupted Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02875)
*Dipesh KC,Anjila Budathoki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了软件工程中“交接债务”的重要性，并通过实验证明，为接手智能体提供结构化笔记等上下文信息，能显著提升其在继承前人工作时的执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的编码智能体基准测试过于简化（关注单一、连续的任务），忽略了真实软件开发中频繁存在的任务中断、交接和从不完整状态恢复的复杂性。

Method: 引入“交接债务（handoff debt）”概念；设计了一套接手协议，在预设点中断智能体，并以四种不同的视图（仅代码库、原始日志、简要笔记、结构化笔记）供接手智能体使用；基于75个源任务生成了181个接手任务点进行对比评估。

Result: 相较于仅提供代码库，提供上下文信息的交接方式使中位数智能体执行事件减少了20-59%，累计提示词Token减少了42-63%；尽管完成率的提升具有模型依赖性，但效率提升效果显著。

Conclusion: 当前的编码智能体评估应包含交互任务的“接手成本”评估，而不仅仅是最终是否完成任务。优化接手信息（如结构化摘要）能显著提升后续智能体的执行效率（减少事件与Token消耗）。

Abstract: Coding-agent benchmarks evaluate whether a single uninterrupted agent can resolve a repository issue. Real software work is messier: tasks are interrupted, reassigned, reviewed, and resumed from partial states left by another agent or engineer. We study this missing dimension through \emph{handoff debt}: the rediscovery cost imposed when a predecessor's work is opaque or incomplete. Our takeover protocol interrupts a coding agent at deterministic handoff points, freezes the repository, and evaluates successor agents under four handoff views: repository state only, raw trace, summary notes, and structured notes. Across 75 source tasks, the protocol generates 181 handoff-point tasks and 724 takeover runs per successor model. Across three successor models, context-bearing handoffs reduce median agent events by 20--59\% and cumulative prompt tokens by 42--63\% relative to repository-only takeover. Solved-rate effects are smaller and model-dependent, but efficiency gains are consistent. These findings suggest that coding-agent evaluation should report not only whether a task is solved, but also how costly that work is for another agent to resume.

</details>


### [106] [Large AI Models in Dental Healthcare: From General-Purpose Systems to Domain-Specific Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02914)
*Sema Helali,Lina Abu Nadab,Sausan Alqawas,Alaa Abd-Alrazaq,Faleh Tamimi,Rafat Damseh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性综述97项研究，揭示了不同类型AI模型在牙科领域的功能互补性，指出集成式多模态管道是未来方向，并强调了解决数据匮乏、幻觉风险与基准缺失对临床落地的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管口部疾病全球影响巨大，但目前尚缺乏针对大型AI模型（语言生成模型、辨别性视觉基础模型、口腔专业基础模型）在牙科领域临床潜力与局限性的统一综述。

Method: 基于PRISMA-ScR指南，系统检索四大数据库（PubMed, Google Scholar, Scopus, arXiv），纳入97项研究，并提出一种按架构范式与口腔专深度划分的二维分类框架。

Result: 大型语言模型擅长临床推理与患教，但对图像诊断表现不均；视觉基础模型（如SAM、CLIP变体）在分割检测上效果显著；口腔专用模型在多模态任务中表现最优；目前存在视觉端预训练数据充足而牙科文本语料严重匮乏的数据不对称现象。

Conclusion: 通用模型与口腔专用模型具有互补性，最佳实践是将两者结合在结构化管道中。当前实现安全自主部署仍需攻克模型幻觉、标注数据匮乏以及缺乏标准临床评估基准三大障碍。

Abstract: Background: Oral diseases affect nearly 3.5 billion people worldwide, yet the comparative clinical potential of large-scale AI models in dentistry remains poorly understood. Three distinct model categories have emerged: language-generative models, discriminative vision foundation models, and dental-specific foundation models, with no unified review examining their relationships and collective limitations.
  Methods: Following PRISMA-ScR guidelines, we systematically searched four databases (PubMed, Google Scholar, Scopus, arXiv), screened independently by two reviewers. After applying inclusion/exclusion criteria, 97 studies (2020-2026) were included. We propose a two-dimensional classification framework organizing models by architectural paradigm and dental specialization degree.
  Results: Language-generative models excel at text-based tasks (clinical reasoning, licensing exams, patient communication) but show inconsistent performance on image-dependent diagnostics. Adapted SAM and CLIP variants achieve strong tooth segmentation and lesion detection results. Dental-specific models (DentVFM, DentVLM, OralGPT) demonstrate strongest performance on complex multimodal tasks. Integrated pipelines consistently outperform single-model approaches. A data asymmetry is observed: dental-specific pretraining concentrates almost entirely in the vision domain, reflecting scarce large-scale dental text corpora.
  Conclusions: General-purpose and dental-specific models play complementary roles; the most effective systems combine both within structured pipelines. Safe autonomous deployment requires resolving three persistent barriers: hallucination in generative models, limited annotated dental datasets, and absent standardized clinical evaluation benchmarks.

</details>


### [107] [What Benchmarks Don't Measure: The Case for Evaluating Abstention Competence in Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02965)
*Victor Ojewale,Suresh Venkatasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出当前智能体因奖励机制而存在“滥用执行”的合规偏见。作者提出了基于三缺口逻辑的 abstention 评估方法，证明通过拒绝执行不安全的指令，可以在维持高性能的同时显著提升智能体的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前智能体基准测试过于关注“任务完成度”，导致智能体在缺乏输入、证据或授权时仍有执行偏见。缺乏对智能体“何时应主动停止”能力的评估，使得智能体在危险场景下表现不佳。

Method: 1. 归纳了会导致执行偏见的“三缺口分类法”（specification, verification, authority gaps）；2. 提出了新的评估指标（安全率、可用性率、知情拒绝率）；3. 在144个企业智能体场景和5个模型家族中验证了运行时强制拒绝机制的有效性。

Result: 在实验中，运行时强制拒绝机制达到了 89.2% 的危险动作拦截率，同时在授权场景中保持了 87.5% 的可用性，证实了安全性与可用性可以兼得。

Conclusion: 合规偏见（Compliance Bias）在当前智能体训练和基准测试中普遍存在。智能体应将“理性的拒绝执行”视为一种安全能力而非失败。通过提出的 abstention 评估协议，可以在保证可用性的前提下显著降低有害动作，证明了安全与可用性之间的权衡是可调节的。

Abstract: Benchmarks for autonomous agents measure whether agents complete tasks, yet this framing is systematically blind to whether an agent should have proceeded at all. Agents trained under human-feedback objectives develop a structural tendency to proceed even when they lack the inputs, evidence, or authorization to act safely, a disposition we term compliance bias, because both the reward signal and the benchmark scoring regime treat proceeding as the correct default regardless of whether the preconditions for safe action are present. We make three contributions. We first show that compliance bias originates in reward hacking within human-feedback pipelines and is entrenched by prominent agent benchmarks, which either penalize agents for pausing or are architecturally unable to distinguish a principled pause from a silent failure. We then introduce a three-gap taxonomy of abstention-warranted scenarios, covering specification gaps where required information is absent, verification gaps where world state cannot be confirmed, and authority gaps where explicit authorization has not been given, which together provide a principled basis for constructing abstention-aware agent benchmarks. Finally, we propose abstention evaluation protocols (Safety Rate, Usability Rate, and Informed Refusal Rate) and report preliminary results across 144 enterprise agent scenarios and five model families, in which a runtime-enforced abstention mechanism achieves up to 89.2% hazardous-action blocking and 87.5% usability on authorized scenarios, demonstrating that the safety--usability tradeoff is tunable rather than inherent and that its shape varies substantially across model families. We treat this as preliminary work and offer the taxonomy and composite metrics as a starting point for further conversations.

</details>


### [108] [WISE-HAR: A Generalizable Ensemble Deep Learning Framework for WiFi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02974)
*Maheen Arshad,Qindeel E Zahra,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于集成学习与数据增强的 WiFi 人类活动识别系统，有效地解决了小样本、高性能波动及跨环境泛化难题，在实际场景中表现出高度的稳健性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiFi 感知技术相比摄像头（有隐私风险）和穿戴式设备（用户依从性要求高）在智能家居、医疗等领域具有非侵入性、低成本及环境全天候适用性，但该领域目前面临高性能波动、数据集匮乏及跨环境泛化性不足的挑战。

Method: 1. 采用集成学习：融合了 Deep CNN、Wide CNN、MobileNetV2、ResNet50V2 和 EfficientNetB0 五种架构以减少性能波动；2. 数据增强：利用时延（time-warping）、频率掩蔽（frequency masking）及噪声添加技术缓解小数据集限制；3. 泛化评估：通过跨场景（LOS至NLOS）与跨天线（Biquad至PIFA）测试验证模型的通用性。

Result: 1. 在 LOS 场景下，集成模型准确率达到 94.87%，优于单一最佳模型；2. 数据增强显著提升了随机森林模型的性能（从 60% 提升至 95%）；3. 在跨场景测试中，准确率仅下降 1.37% 至 2.07%，展现出极佳的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过集成学习与数据增强技术，基于 WiFi 信号进行人类活动识别（HAR）是鲁棒且可靠的。其在跨场景与跨天线的评估中展现出极强的泛化能力，非常适合在具有不同硬件配置的多样化真实环境中部署。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using WiFi signals has emerged as a transformative technology for smart homes, healthcare monitoring, security systems, and ambient assisted living. Unlike traditional camera-based systems that raise significant privacy concerns and fail in low-light conditions, or wearable sensors that require user compliance, WiFi-based HAR is non-intrusive, privacy-preserving, cost-effective, and works seamlessly in any lighting condition. This paper presents a comprehensive approach to recognize three distinct human activities: "No Presence" (empty room), "Walking", and "Walking + Arm-waving" using the Wallhack1.8k WiFi spectrogram dataset. We propose three key improvements to address the main challenges in WiFi-based HAR. First, to address high performance variance, we implement ensemble learning with five different CNN architectures (Deep CNN, Wide CNN, MobileNetV2, ResNet50V2, and EfficientNetB0). Second, to address the small dataset size limitation, we apply aggressive data augmentation techniques including time-warping, frequency masking, and noise addition. Third, to evaluate real-world generalization capability, we perform cross-scenario evaluation (training on Line-of-Sight and testing on Non-Line-of-Sight) and cross-antenna evaluation (training on Biquad antenna and testing on PIFA antenna). Our ensemble model achieved a test accuracy of 94.87% on the LOS scenario with Biquad antenna, outperforming the best individual model by 0.66%. Data augmentation improved Random Forest performance from 60% to 95%. Cross-scenario evaluation showed minimal accuracy drops of only 1.37% and 2.07%, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities. The results indicate that the proposed approach is robust, reliable, and suitable for real-world deployment in diverse environments with different hardware configurations.

</details>


### [109] [Inducing Reasoning Primitives from Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02994)
*Zhihan Lei,Jiarui Yan,Joshua Momo,William W. Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入“推理原语归纳”方法，将LLM在ReAct过程中反复使用的推理逻辑提炼为可复用的伪工具库，显著提升了代理的推理能力并降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct风格的LLM代理在不同问题中常反复使用相似的推理路径，但这些经验往往只残留在临时的草稿中（scratchpad），无法被系统性地提炼和复用。

Method: 提出了一种单遍（single-pass）挖掘方法，从未经处理的ReAct推理轨迹中识别并聚类重复出现的推理模式，将其转化为一组带类型的伪工具（pseudo-tools）。这些伪工具通过自然语言文档字符串描述，并被集成到标准的ReAct循环中，供代理在测试阶段调用。

Result: 该方法在多个基准测试中表现显著：RuleArena NBA提升44个百分点（30% -> 74%），MuSR提升30个百分点（38% -> 68%），NatPlan提升22个百分点（7% -> 29%）。整体性能优于零样本CoT，且在成本更低的情况下优于AWM。

Conclusion: 实验证明，通过Reasoning Primitive Induction（推理原语归纳）生成的任务特定工具库，能够有效提升LLM代理在复杂推理任务中的表现，超越了原始的ReAct代理、零样本CoT以及专家手动设计的分解方法，同时降低了平均推理成本。

Abstract: ReAct-style LLM agents often rediscover the same reasoning routines across problems, yet leave those routines trapped in transient scratchpads. We introduce Reasoning Primitive Induction, a single-pass method that mines successful ReAct traces, clusters recurrent reasoning moves, and converts the most frequent moves into a compact library of typed pseudo-tools. Each pseudo-tool is specified by a natural-language docstring interpreted by an LLM at invocation time, and a standard ReAct loop composes these primitives at test time. The central result is that induced libraries outperform the very agent that generated their traces: by +44pp on RuleArena NBA (30 -> 74), +30pp on MuSR team allocation (38 -> 68), and +22pp on NatPlan meeting planning (7 -> 29). Across five comparable subtasks spanning narrative deduction, rule application, and constraint-satisfaction planning, a single fixed configuration improves over zero-shot Chain-of-Thought on every subtask, matches or surpasses expert-authored decompositions, and outperforms AWM at lower average inference cost.

</details>


### [110] [Do Real-World Datasets Contain Natural Experiments? An Empirical Study Using Causal Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03251)
*Gautam Gare,John Galeotti,Michael Mozer,Deva Ramanan,Nan Rosemary Ke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从真实世界数据集中自动识别并利用“自然实验”的方法，通过结合因果发现与特征选择，将数据视为干预而非纯观测，从而提升了模型在下游任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然界中存在的“自然实验”（如疫情对特定人群的影响）被视为一种隐性干预。研究者关注此类自然实验是否普遍存在于现有的真实世界数据中，以及如何利用这些实验信息来优化机器学习模型的表现。

Method: 1. 使用因果发现（Causal Discovery）算法恢复底层因果图结构；2. 基于因果链路进行特征选择；3. 通过比较模型将数据处理为“干预数据”与“观测数据”后的下游性能差异，验证自然实验的存在性。

Result: 通过合成数据集验证及真实世界大规模数据集的评估显示：模型在引入自然实验逻辑后性能有明显改善，证实了该方案的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 真实世界数据集确实包含自然实验，且通过因果推断框架将其从观测数据中识别并利用，能够有效提升下游模型的预测性能。该研究开启了从数据中自动挖掘因果干预这一新方向。

Abstract: In nature, events that affect some individuals or groups but not others constitute an implicit intervention and are known as natural experiments. For example, the COVID-19 pandemic was an intervention by the coronavirus on the sub-population infected with COVID. We ask, do natural experiments occur in existing real-world datasets? If yes, how should we treat them? To detect natural experiments in data, we use causal discovery to recover the underlying causal graph and perform feature selection based on causal links. If downstream performance improves by treating the data as interventional rather than observational, we argue that this suggests the dataset contains natural experiments. We first validate this hypothesis by simulating datasets with and without natural experiments using synthetic graphs. We then perform a systematic empirical evaluation on a large suite of real-world datasets. Our results indicate that real-world datasets do contain natural experiments and we can take advantage of those natural experiments to improve model performance using causal inference. Our work represents the initial foray into this area, offering a preliminary exploration within a limited scope.

</details>


### [111] [AUDITFLOW: Executable Symbolic Environments for Structured Financial Reporting Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03031)
*Yan Wang,Xuguang Ai,Jaisal Patel,Xueqing Peng,Fengran Mo,Yupeng Cao,Haohang Li,Mingyu Cao,Lingfei Qian,Víctor Gutiérrez-Basulto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AuditFlow 是一个利用图形化符号环境和多代理协作的金融审计框架，通过将逻辑推理与确定性数值验证解耦，解决了模型在复杂金融凭证审计中易出错的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型代理在处理结构化金融审计时，单纯依赖文本推理无法保证准确性，需要基于结构化证据、分类概念遍历及数值重算能力来执行严谨的审计规则。

Method: 提出 AuditFlow 框架：1. 构建基于 US-GAAP 分类法与 XBRL 申报表的符号图环境；2. 开发专用的查找、遍历、数值计算及规则评估工具；3. 采用多代理（Multi-Agent）协作模式，由两名“初级审计员”从不同视角审阅，一名“高级审计员”裁决，并结合证据聚合生成审计结论。

Result: 在 FinAuditing 数据集上，AuditFlow（GPT-5.5驱动）取得了 82.09% 的联合审计准确率，较基线提升了 14.93 个百分点。实验证实，去除确定性验证步骤会导致系统准确率暴跌至 17.91%。

Conclusion: 对于金融审计任务，大型语言模型难以直接替代确定性验证过程。AuditFlow 通过引入符号环境（图结构数据与特定工具），将“自适应搜索”与“确定性验证”解耦，显著提升了审计准确性与可信度。

Abstract: Structured financial audit verification is difficult for language-model agents because correctness depends on structured evidence rather than text alone. A model must link reported facts to taxonomy concepts, traverse calculation or dimensional relations, and recompute expected values before applying an audit rule. We propose AuditFlow, a graph-grounded multi-agent framework that separates adaptive search from deterministic verification. AuditFlow builds a symbolic environment from a static US-GAAP taxonomy graph and a dynamic XBRL filing graph, and exposes it through typed tools for fact retrieval, taxonomy traversal, numerical checking, and rule evaluation. Two junior auditors inspect each case from regulatory and evidentiary views, while a senior auditor resolves disagreements and can request further investigation. The final reports are fused through evidential aggregation to produce an audit verdict, expected value, evidence trail, and trustworthiness score. On a FinAuditing-derived FinMR sample, AuditFlow reaches 82.09% joint audit accuracy under GPT-5.5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.93 points. Removing deterministic checks drops accuracy to 17.91%, showing that the symbolic environment performs the verification step that the model cannot reliably replace.

</details>


### [112] [TriEval: A Resource-Efficient Pipeline for LLM Bias, Toxicity, and Truthfulness Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03036)
*Akshatha Srikantha,Manpreet Singh,Yash Jajoo,Shyamal Lakhanpal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TriEval 是一个开源、轻量级的 LLM 综合评估流水线，无需高性能 GPU 即可同时评估模型的偏见、毒性和真实性，极大地提升了评估的可及性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评估工具普遍存在单参数评估限制、资源需求过高且难以供普通研究人员使用的问题，无法满足当下 LLM 在各领域广泛部署后的持续安全与公平性监测需求。

Method: 开发名为 TriEval 的评估流水线，支持同时评估偏见（bias）、毒性（toxicity）和真实性（truthfulness）多个指标；通过优化算法实现低计算资源占用，支持在普通笔记本电脑上运行。

Result: 在 Llama 3 8B、Mistral 7B、Gemma 2 9B 和 Claude Haiku 四个模型上的测试表明，该工具能够有效识别模型间的表现差异，特别是在毒性和真实性方面。

Conclusion: TriEval 提供了一个轻量级、综合性的 LLM 评估方案，能够有效降低评估门槛，并为开放和闭源模型的性能差异分析提供了实证见解。

Abstract: LLMs have evolved from basic chatbots to the backbone of the AI ecosystem, now widely used in healthcare, schools, and government services. The domain-wide adoption of LLMs necessitates continuous evaluation to ensure their safety and fairness. Common issues encountered after deploying LLMs include inconsistent outputs and hallucinations of incorrect information. Although numerous LLM evaluation tools exist, most are limited to testing a single parameter at a time or require massive computational resources that are not accessible to most researchers.
