This is first described in the general case and then considered in the specific cases of rational and lexicographic closures, providing a faithful translation of each inference into PDSL. We also show that entailment-checking in this fragment of PDSL can be done largely using algorithms from the propositional case, while preserving complexity bounds.

</details>


### [148] [Diagnosing Knowledge Gaps in LLM Tool Use: An Agentic Benchmark for Novel API Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03657)
*Jinnuo Liu,Yue Peng,Jinhan Niu,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 NovelAPIBench 评估大模型对未知API的调用能力，发现检索与参数微调在代码生成中具有互补作用，且过度增加代码上下文反而可能损害性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型代码生成基准多为静态、指标粗糙或基于合成API，无法有效评估模型处理预训练数据中缺失的、真实演进的外部API的能力。

Method: 提出 NovelAPIBench，一个自动化的动态基准测试框架，包含发现未知API、提取知识包、生成可执行任务及六类故障诊断功能。对比了检索增强与参数化适配（微调）在处理约1.9K个任务中的表现。

Result: 使用示例是效果最强的独立信号；不同背景模型在不同领域对“签名+机制/示例”的组合偏好不同。微调并非替代检索，而是通过教会模型使用提供的知识包，并将这种能力迁移到未见过的库中。

Conclusion: 检索（Retrieval）与微调（Fine-tuning）在处理未知API时互补：检索提供易变的API内容，而微调提升模型的程序整合能力。增加上下文（尤其是源代码）可能因增加导入路径错误而产生副作用。

Abstract: Large language models for code generation often need to use APIs that are absent from their pretraining data. This requires more than recalling a function name: models must coordinate signatures, module paths, input-output contracts, semantics, and executable usage patterns. Existing novel-API benchmarks are typically static, rely on coarse pass/fail metrics, or use synthetic APIs that may not reflect real library evolution. We introduce NovelAPIBench, a fully automated dynamic benchmark that, for any base model and target library, discovers novel APIs, extracts decomposed knowledge bundles, generates executable coding tasks, and assigns failed samples to six diagnostic categories. Across about 1.9K tasks, four base models, and five domains, we compare knowledge injected through retrieval with knowledge internalized through parametric adaptation. We find that knowledge components are not interchangeable: usage examples are the strongest standalone signal, while the best two-component setting pairs signatures with either mechanisms or examples depending on the domain and backbone. Adding more context, especially source code, can hurt by increasing import-path errors. Parametric adaptation also does not replace retrieval once external knowledge is removed; rather, fine-tuning mainly teaches models how to use provided bundles, and this ability transfers to held-out libraries. These results suggest that retrieval and tuning play complementary roles: retrieval supplies volatile API content, while tuning improves procedural integration.

</details>


### [149] [From Answers to States: Verifiable Process-Level Evaluation of Chemical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03660)
*Hongyu Guo,Hao Li,He Cao,Gongbo Zhang,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChemCoTBench-V2 是一款基于确定性规则的化学推理基准，通过强制模型展示中间逻辑步骤并利用规则校验，揭示了当前 LLM 在化学任务中“正确答案支撑错误逻辑”的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学基准测试仅关注最终答案，导致模型存在“虚假正确”问题（即答案正确但推理逻辑错误）。传统的基于 LLM 的过程评测成本高、一致性差且易产生幻觉，迫切需要一种可扩展、低成本、可审计且基于规则的诊断基准。

Method: 提出了 ChemCoTBench-V2 基准测试，包含 5,620 个样本，覆盖 18 个化学任务。通过专家设计的模板强制模型暴露中间推理步骤，并使用确定性化学规则（Verifier）和基准轨迹进行自动验证，而非依赖 LLM 判别器。对于开放式任务，采用预言机（Oracle）验证状态约束。

Result: 实验表明，模型在最终答案准确率与结构化推理逻辑一致性之间存在显著差距。许多模型能够满足输出格式要求，但在化学步骤检查中暴露了逻辑缺陷，且无法保证答案正确与推理合理性的统一。

Conclusion: 现有的前沿大语言模型在化学任务中存在“答案正确但推理逻辑违规”或“能够遵循格式规范但化学步骤错误”的问题。ChemCoTBench-V2 提供了一种可审计的诊断框架，揭示了模型在最终答案准确性与化学推理逻辑一致性之间的鸿沟，为优化模型化学推理能力提供了精细化评估工具。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as chemistry assistants, yet most chemistry benchmarks still score only final answers. This masks a critical failure mode: a model may output the correct molecule, product, or option while its reasoning violates chemical logic. Existing process-level evaluators are hard to scale because LLM judges and human step-level process annotation are costly, inconsistent, and vulnerable to hallucination. We introduce ChemCoTBench-V2, a rule-verifiable diagnostic benchmark for low-cost, auditable evaluation of structured, verifier-addressable chemical reasoning traces. It spans molecular understanding, molecule editing, molecular optimization, and reaction prediction, with 5,620 evaluation samples across 18 reporting tasks. Models must expose key intermediate steps in expert-designed templates, and those steps are checked with deterministic chemistry rules and, for closed-answer tasks, reference traces rather than another LLM judge. Open-ended molecular optimization is evaluated with oracle-verifiable state constraints rather than strict trace matching. The benchmark reports three separate signals: final-answer correctness, template adherence, and step-wise verifier correctness over expert-refined intermediate commitments. Experiments on frontier models reveal a persistent gap between final-answer success and structured-reasoning-state consistency: models often follow the requested format while failing chemical-step checks, or answer correctly with weak supporting reasoning. ChemCoTBench-V2 enables fine-grained model comparison and identifies the concrete step at which the trace first violates the verifier.

</details>


### [150] [EvoDrive: Pareto Evolution for Safety-Critical Autonomous Driving via Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03678)
*Tong Nie,Yuewen Mei,Yihong Tang,Junlin He,Jie Deng,Jian Sun,Wei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDrive 是一款基于 LLM 的智能体进化框架，通过 Actor-Critic 架构和 Pareto 归档机制，自动生成兼具对抗性和真实性的自动驾驶安全关键场景，解决了传统启发式方法覆盖面不足及现有智能体方法缺乏仿真接地的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶场景生成方法多依赖手工启发式规则，难以探索未知模式；而通用的智能体进化方法在大模型（LLM）背景下缺乏对仿真环境的深刻理解，且容易陷入单一指标优化的陷阱，无法平衡对抗性和真实性。

Method: 1. 采用基于仿真器的 Actor-Critic 架构：内存驱动的 Actor 负责提出改进方案，Critic 负责过滤不可信场景。
2. 引入自进化世界评估器（Self-evolving World Evaluator）来优化仿真资源分配。
3. 维护一个 Pareto 归档（Pareto Archive），保存并利用多样化的攻击-真实性权衡结果来指导后续进化。

Result: EvoDrive 在 MetaDrive 和 CARLA 基准测试中展现了卓越性能，显著扩展了场景生成的 Pareto 前沿（即在保持更高真实性的同时实现了更强的对抗性），并提升了自动驾驶策略的训练效果。

Conclusion: EvoDrive 成功突破了现有方法在生成安全关键驾驶场景时的局限性，通过 LLM 驱动的智能体进化框架有效平衡了对抗性与真实性。该方法在 MetaDrive 和 CARLA 上的表现证明了其在扩展 Pareto 前沿和辅助策略训练方面的有效性。

Abstract: Generating safety-critical scenarios is essential for validating and improving autonomous driving systems, yet it inherently requires maximizing adversariality to expose failures while preserving realism. Existing methods usually manage this trade-off with handcrafted heuristics, confining generation to known priors and overlooking underexplored patterns. While recent open-ended agentic evolution can push this limit, unconstrained general agents lack strict simulator grounding and tend to collapse the multi-objective tension into single-scalar maximization. Here we present EvoDrive, the first automated, LLM-based agentic evolution framework for multi-objective scenario generation. EvoDrive employs a simulator-grounded actor-critic architecture where a memory-driven actor iteratively proposes improvements to the generators and critics filter out implausible candidates, and a self-evolving world evaluator routes promising proposals to optimize simulation budgets. EvoDrive further maintains a Pareto archive of evaluated candidates to preserve diverse attack-realism trade-offs and guide future evolution via simulation feedback. Benchmark results on MetaDrive and CARLA show that EvoDrive not only significantly expands the Pareto frontier across various generators, but also produces valuable scenarios for policy training.

</details>


### [151] [The DeepSpeak-Agentic Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03686)
*Sarah Barrington,Maty Bohacek,Hany Farid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeepSpeak-Agentic 是一个包含 37 小时人机交互视频的大型数据集，旨在评估具身 AI 代理的辨识度、研究人机交互特性，并为具身智能前沿研究提供基准测试及一套可扩展的数据采集系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决当前缺乏用于评估具身智能体音视频真实性、辨识度以及研究人机交互质量的大规模高质量数据集的问题。

Method: 研发了一套可扩展的数据采集系统，实现 AI 智能体生成、与众包人员的自动匹配及各场景下音视频对话的录制与分离。

Result: 发布了包含 37 小时以上人机半结构化对话的 DeepSpeak-Agentic 数据集，并建立了用于自动取证识别（音、视、文）的基准测试。

Conclusion: DeepSpeak-Agentic 为具身智能领域提供了高质量的交互数据集与基准测试，有助于推动 AI 辨识技术、人机交互分析及大模型与多模态生成技术的发展。

Abstract: We present DeepSpeak-Agentic, a dataset of videos comprising over 37 hours of semi-structured conversations between a human and an embodied AI agent. We use this dataset to evaluate the automatic forensic identification (audio, video, or text) of AI agents, study the nature of human-agent interactions, and provide a benchmark for future advances in the large-language models and AI-generated voices and faces that power embodied AI agents. We also contribute a scalable data-capture system that creates agents, automatically pairs them with human crowd workers, records audiovisual conversations across specified scenarios, and identifies and separates the human and agent in the combined stream.

</details>


### [152] [Dynamic Objective Selection with Safeguards and LLM Oversight for Financial Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03704)
*Keigo Sakurai,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama,Anjyu Anan,Kei Nakagawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对动态金融市场目标函数选择滞后和不稳定的问题，DOSS 提出了一种基于分类学习的无隐变量目标选择机制，并结合置信度门控与 LLM 治理，实现了稳健、可控的实时金融策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融决策中固定的优化目标难以适应不断变化的市场环境；基于隐变量的制度切换（regime-switching）模型存在滞后、噪声大及操作不稳定的问题。

Method: 提出 DOSS (Dynamic Objective Selection with Safeguards) 框架，将目标函数选择定义为分类问题；利用可解释的近期收益统计特征进行实时决策；引入置信度感知门控机制，并集成 LLM 作为决策治理（监督与安全兜底）组件以约束切换频率和降低风险。

Result: 该方法在不需要中间隐状态变量的情况下，能够根据最新的市场统计特征，在预定义的多种目标函数（如追求回报、厌恶损失、风险调整）之间进行稳健切换，并通过安全机制有效抑制了过度切换和预测失误带来的风险。

Conclusion: DOSS 通过将客观选择过程从不确定性的隐变量建模转向直接的分类学习，并结合置信度门控和 LLM 治理，提供了一种在动态金融市场中实现稳健决策的有效框架。

Abstract: Financial decision-making tasks such as stock recommendation and portfolio allocation typically estimate future return and risk and then select trades or allocations for an investor, and the chosen optimization objective often determines realized performance. However, because market conditions evolve over time, a fixed objective can be suboptimal across regimes, while regime-switching pipelines that rely on latent regime estimates can be noisy or delayed and frequent switching can increase turnover and operational instability. In this paper, we propose DOSS (Dynamic Objective Selection with Safeguards), a learning-based selector that directly chooses the decision-relevant objective function at each time point from interpretable statistical summaries of recent returns, selecting among a small set of candidates (e.g., return-seeking, loss-averse, and risk-adjusted) without introducing intermediate regime variables. DOSS formulates objective selection as a classification problem over objectives and performs sequential updates with a rolling window to make forward-looking selections without temporal leakage, while also outputting a confidence score for each proposal. To mitigate misselection and excessive switching in deployment, DOSS applies confidence-aware gating with a fail-safe that overrides low-confidence proposals to a conservative default and enforces explicit controls tied to switching frequency. We further integrate governance by positioning a Large Language Model (LLM) as an oversight component rather than a generator of new objectives: the LLM is restricted to accept a proposed objective or override it to a predefined safe default, with deterministic rule-based constraints triggering overrides when needed.

</details>


### [153] [Code-on-Graph: Iterative Programmatic Reasoning via Large Language Models on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03705)
*Weiwei Ding,Zixuan Li,Long Bai,Zhuo Chen,Kun Su,Fei Wang,Xiaolong Jin,Jin Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CoG 框架，通过引入代码驱动的推理方式（将 KG 转化为代码接口），有效提升了 LLM 在处理复杂 KG 问答时的灵活性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-KG 集成方法存在两大瓶颈：一是预定义算子在处理复杂语义问题时灵活性不足（不可组合）；二是将大量事实直接注入 Prompt 限制了处理大规模知识的可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Code-on-Graph (CoG)”的编程推理框架。1）将检索到的 KG 模式表示为 Python 类；2）将事实实例作为这些类的对象；3）生成并执行这些类的 Python 代码进行推理，而非直接将事实注入 Prompt。

Result: 在 WebQSP、CWQ 和 GrailQA 数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法相比当前最先进模型（SOTA）性能提升高达 10.5%。

Conclusion: CoG 通过将 KG 模式抽象为 Python 类并生成可执行代码，成功解决了现有 LLM-KG 集成框架中预定义算子缺乏灵活性以及提示词中注入大规模知识导致的可扩展性差的问题。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs (KGs) are widely used to mitigate the limitations of Large Language Models (LLMs), such as outdated knowledge and hallucinations. Existing LLM-KG integration frameworks typically rely on predefined operators to retrieve factual knowledge from KGs and inject it into prompts for answer generation. This paradigm faces two critical bottlenecks: 1) Inflexibility: The predefined operators are limited in scope and thus lack sufficient compositional expressiveness to fully capture the complex semantics required by KG questions. 2) Unscalability: Direct injection of factual knowledge into prompts limits scalability in handling large-scale factual knowledge. To address these two bottlenecks, we propose Code-on-Graph (CoG), a programmatic reasoning framework for LLM-KG integration. Specifically, given the factual knowledge retrieved at each reasoning step, CoG first identifies the corresponding KG schemas and represents these schemas as Python classes, which serve as abstract interfaces to the retrieved facts. It then generates executable code grounded in these classes, with the retrieved facts instantiated as objects of the corresponding classes during execution. This design enables flexible code-based reasoning while avoiding the direct injection of large-scale factual knowledge into prompts. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and GrailQA demonstrate that CoG outperforms prior state-of-the-art models by up to 10.5%.

</details>


### [154] [Unveiling the Structure of Do-Calculus Reasoning via Derivation Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03719)
*Clément Yvernes,Emilie Devijver,Marianne Clausel,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入“推导图”框架，系统化了 do-calculus 的规则应用过程，证明了任何因果查询推导最多只需四步，并利用该方法提升了因果估计量的统计效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 do-calculus 推导过程复杂且缺乏系统性，导致研究者难以有效地组合和排序推导规则来寻找更好的因果估计量。

Method: 引入“推导图”（derivation graphs）概念，用于表示和组合 do-calculus 规则的应用过程；构建了一个完备空间，刻画了在 do-calculus 下所有等价的观测和干预概率。

Result: 提出了一个通用且简洁的程序来自动进行 do-calculus 推导；证明了利用该方法生成的多个等价估计量能够为因果推断提供统计效率上的优势。

Conclusion: 引入了偏差图（derivation graphs）这一建模工具，证实了通过该框架可以将任意因果查询的推导简化为最多四步操作，并表明通过利用等价因果表达式的生成，可以进一步筛选出统计效率更高的估计量。

Abstract: The do-calculus defines a general system of inference for interventional queries, allowing causal quantities to be transformed through successive applications of its rules. This process induces a rich space of equivalent interventional expressions, but combining and ordering these rules remains challenging. In this work, we introduce derivation graphs, which represent how do-calculus rules are applied and combined, and characterize the full space of observational and interventional probabilities which are equivalent under the do-calculus. The structure of these graphs yields a simple procedure that uses at most four applications of do-calculus rules. Finally, we show how applying identification algorithms to equivalent causal queries produces multiple valid estimands for the same causal quantity, eventually yielding more efficient estimators.

</details>


### [155] [When to Re-Plan: Subgoal Persistence in Hierarchical Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03741)
*Ayushi Chadha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了潜在推理系统中的长程规划问题，发现通过引入持续周期为中等长度的封建式子目标，能有效平衡稳定与适应，从而提升系统的组合式推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长程推理中的稳定性与适应性折中问题（Stability-adaptivity tradeoff）：若重规划过于频繁，计算缺乏多步结构；若规划周期过长，计划则会失效。作者试图通过潜在推理（Latent reasoning）手段改善这一问题。

Method: 扩展了分层推理模型（HRM），引入了封建式（Feudal-style）管理器-工作者架构。其中，高层模块周期性地生成持续P步的归一化方向性子目标，用于引导低层模块的隐藏状态更新，并辅以内在余弦对齐损失函数。

Result: 在ARC和ConceptARC数据集上，发现存在最佳子目标持续周期（P=3至6），且在P=3时取得最低语言模型（LM）损失（1.544）。此外，内在对齐权重（lambda）存在约0.05的窄幅最优区间。消融实验证明，这种性能提升源于习得的方向性结构。

Conclusion: 在潜在推理系统中，设计组合式规划的关键在于维持中等长度的意图相干性（Medium-horizon intent coherence），以促进组合结构的形成。合理的子目标持续周期（P）和适当的内在目标对齐权重（lambda）是实现该目标的关键因素。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires a system to commit to medium-horizon intent without becoming rigid: re-plan too often and computation never coheres into multi-step structure; commit too long and the plan goes stale. We study this stability-adaptivity tradeoff in the latent reasoning setting, where multi-step computation occurs inside hidden state rather than externalized token traces. We extend the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) with a feudal-style manager-worker interface: a slow high-level module periodically emits a normalized directional subgoal that persists for P low-level steps, biasing the worker's hidden-state updates and supplying an intrinsic cosine alignment loss. On ARC and ConceptARC, we find that subgoal persistence -- not subgoal injection alone -- is the central knob: moderate periods P in [3, 6] consistently outperform both very frequent (P=1) and very long horizons, with a clear minimum LM loss at P=3 (1.544 vs. 1.674 at P=1, 1.640 baseline; replicated over 5 seeds at mean 1.595, std 0.045). The intrinsic alignment weight lambda shows a complementary narrow optimum (lambda approximately 0.05). A controlled ablation at past-sweet-spot lambda isolates learned directional structure -- not architectural capacity or auxiliary loss alone -- as the source of interference when the alignment signal exceeds its optimum. Together these findings implicate a design principle for compositional planning in latent reasoning systems: medium-horizon intent must be coherent across enough computational steps for compositional structure to form.

</details>


### [156] [Proof-Refactor: Refactoring Generated Formal Proofs into Modular Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03743)
*Yiming Fu,Peixuan Liu,Zichen Wang,Kun yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为了解决 LLM 生成的 Lean 证明缺乏模块化和可读性的问题，Proof-Refactor 引入了一种模拟人类重构流程的四阶段代理架构，在不依赖长度指标的情况下，显著提升了证明代码的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 生成证明偏向“编译优先”，导致代码臃肿、缺乏模块化和可维护性；且现有的优化目标多为长度指标，无法涵盖人类对高质量代码的理解。

Method: 提出名为 Proof-Refactor 的代理架构，将证明重构分解为四个阶段：提取候选证明片段、设计辅助声明、对组件进行形式化验证、利用验证后的组件修复原证明。

Result: 在 PutnamBench 和 Putnam2025 数据集上，Proof-Refactor 在基于准则的重构评分上优于 Claude Code 基准，尤其在特征签名质量和人类可读性方面提升显著。

Conclusion: 流程引导的重构（Process-guided refactoring）能有效提升数学证明代码的质量（模块化、可读性），且无需将证明长度作为优化目标。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance in generating formal proofs, their outputs often remain less readable, modular, maintainable, and reusable than proofs in mature formal mathematics libraries. We argue that this gap stems in part from the compile-first objective implicit in most proof-generation pipelines, which encourages monolithic or ad hoc proof scripts rather than library-quality artifacts. Existing approaches to proof-quality improvement often rely on explicit, computable optimization objectives. In practice, however, the most tractable and experimentally validated objectives are largely length-based, while higher-level qualities such as readability, modularity, maintainability, and reusability are difficult to reduce to reliable automatic metrics. Instead of optimizing proof improvement against a single proxy metric, we take a process-guided approach inspired by human proof-refactoring workflows. We propose an agentic framework $\textbf{Proof-Refactor}$ that decomposes proof refactoring into four phases: extracting candidate proof fragments, designing helper declarations, formally proving the extracted and designed components, and repairing the original proof using the verified components. On generated Lean proofs from PutnamBench and Putnam2025, Proof-Refactor improves rubric-based refactoring scores over a strong Claude Code refactoring baseline, with the largest gains in signature quality and human readability. These results suggest that process-guided refactoring can improve proof structure without treating proof length as the primary objective.

</details>


### [157] [LAP: An Agent-to-Instrument Protocol for Autonomous Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03755)
*Linwu Zhu,Liqiang Gao,Yan Chen,Dan Zhu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自主科学实验中“代理-仪器”接口缺失的问题，本文提出了 Lab Agent Protocol (LAP)，提供了一套安全、标准化、具备科学计算语境的交互协议，填补了现有代理框架在物理实验领域的生态空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验中自主代理（Agent）与物理仪器（Instrument）之间的连接缺乏标准。现有的 SDK 和协议是为确定性软件设计的，无法有效处理具备状态性、安全性关键、物理独占性和测量精确性（如单位、标定、不确定性）的科学实验需求。

Method: 设计了一个包含六层架构的协议，引入了四种核心原语：1. InstrumentCard（能力与限制描述）；2. 独占性预约机制（资源锁）；3. 安全围栏握手（关键操作二次确认）；4. 结构化测量结果模式（支持单位、校准和不确定性表示）。LAP 与 A2A 和 MCP 兼容，并 encapsultes（封装）现有的仪器控制标准（如 SiLA 2 和 OPC-UA）。

Result: 提出了 LAP 协议，详细说明了其角色定义、架构层级、JSON-RPC 方法集、状态机模型及跨实验室联邦机制，并通过端到端的自主闭环实验验证了该协议的有效性。

Conclusion: Lab Agent Protocol (LAP) 填补了现有代理通信协议在科学仪表连接方面的空白，提供了一种安全、标准化且具备物理语境的框架，为构建可扩展、可互操作的自主实验生态系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous science is moving from demonstration to infrastructure. Large language model agents now plan experiments, and self-driving laboratories execute them. Yet every such system rebuilds the link between the reasoning agent and the physical instrument from scratch, against fragmented vendor SDKs and standards built for deterministic software clients rather than probabilistic, goal-directed agents. Recent agent-interoperability protocols clarify two of the three edges of an agentic ecosystem (Anthropic's Model Context Protocol (MCP) standardizes the agent-to-tool edge, and Google's Agent2Agent (A2A) the agent-to-agent edge), but neither models the agent-to-instrument edge, where operations are stateful, safety-critical, exclusively owned, physically embodied, and produce measurements with units, calibration, and uncertainty. We present the Lab Agent Protocol (LAP), a protocol design that fills this gap. LAP retains A2A's peer-to-peer, discovery-first, task-lifecycle structure and adds four physical-world primitives: (i) the InstrumentCard, a signed capability and physical-limit description; (ii) first-class reservation for exclusive instrument and sample locking; (iii) a safety-fence handshake with operator-confirmation tokens cryptographically bound to a specific task and its parameters, gating hazardous and irreversible operations; and (iv) a MeasurementResult schema that makes every result physically typed (QUDT/UCUM), calibration-anchored, uncertainty-bearing, and reproducible by construction. We specify roles, a six-layer architecture, the JSON-RPC method set, the task and safety state machines, the error model, and cross-laboratory federation, and walk a closed-loop autonomous campaign through the protocol end-to-end. LAP is transport-compatible with the A2A/MCP ecosystem and encapsulates rather than replaces existing device standards such as SiLA 2 and OPC-UA.

</details>


### [158] [From Control Boundary to Insurance Claim: Reconstructing AI-Mediated Losses Through the CER Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03777)
*Alex Leung,Rex Zhang,Kentaroh Toyoda,SiewMei Loh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对生成式与代理式AI系统动态演变的特性，论文提出了CER诊断框架，用于评估AI风险是否可进行残余风险转移及理赔，旨在解决保险行业在面对AI故障时的责任认定与证据还原难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的保险理赔机制难以应对生成式和代理式AI带来的复杂风险。AI系统在推理、检索、工具调用及行为过程中，其内部状态会动态演变，因此传统的事件回顾不足以理清因果链，急需一套针对AI系统独特运行机制的风险评估与证据还原体系。

Method: 提出了“CER诊断框架”：
1. Control boundary (C)：评估系统是否具备可执行的运行边界。
2. Evidence reconstruction (E)：分析能否通过保留的工件还原系统状态及因果链。
3. Insurance response (R)：确定损失是否属于承保范围及是否具备索赔所需的证据支撑。

Result: 论文通过解析PocketOS、Replit数据库删除事件及Moffatt v. Air Canada案例，验证了CER框架的有效性，定义了AI特有的重建问题，并界定了能够支持保险理赔的“索赔级”证据标准。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了CER框架，为AI系统的残余风险转移提供了诊断工具。通过控制边界界定、系统状态还原和保险可赔付性分析，该框架为处理AI导致的复杂损失提供了标准化的评估路径，并明确了保险索赔所需的特定证据要求。

Abstract: AI losses that arise through an insured organization's generative or agentic AI system require state reconstruction, not merely event reconstruction, because the relevant state changes as the system reasons, retrieves, calls tools, and acts. The relevant question is not only what loss occurred, but what the system was allowed to do, what it actually did, and whether that reconstructed loss can support insurance claim recovery. This paper addresses losses in which the insured's AI system is in the causal chain, including externally triggered failures such as prompt injection, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) poisoning, malicious tool output, credential misuse, and data poisoning.
  Specifically, this paper introduces CER, a use-case-level diagnostic for AI residual risk transfer. C (control boundary) asks whether the system had an enforceable operating envelope. E (evidence reconstruction) asks whether the system state and causal chain can be reconstructed from retained artifacts. R (insurance response) asks whether the reconstructed loss is insured: whether insurance coverage is available in the market and placed for the insured, together with the proof needed to support insurance claim recovery.
  The paper makes three contributions: it defines the AI-specific reconstruction problem, operationalizes that problem through CER, and specifies claim-grade evidence for AI reconstruction. Public examples include the reported PocketOS and Replit agentic database-deletion incidents and Moffatt v. Air Canada as an adjudicated output/reliance case.
  Keywords: AI systems; CER framework; residual risk transfer; agentic AI; generative AI; AI insurance; evidence reconstruction.

</details>


### [159] [Enhancing Operational Safety via Agentic Dialogue Hazard Identification Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03812)
*Sanjay Das,Ran Elgedawy,Ethan Seefried,Ryan Burchfield,Tirthankar Ghosal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型在安全关键领域危险识别能力不足的问题，本文通过HAZDIAL框架引入多智能体对话机制（对抗辩论或建设性讨论），证实了通过迭代交互可显著提升危险预测的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型在处理高风险领域的危险识别时，单次推理缺乏安全工程师迭代修正和审慎逻辑的特点，导致结果脆弱且不可靠。

Method: 提出了HAZDIAL框架，对比了两种对话模式（对抗性辩论与建设性讨论），并引入了基于算法的智能体交互优化机制。使用标准分类指标（准确率、精确率、召回率、F1）和定义的对话指标在精选数据集上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过多智能体、多轮次的结构化对话，能够有效改善NLP驱动的危险识别性能，且对话式交互框架在复杂场景下表现优于单次提示基线。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了基于多智能体对话（多轮交互）的方法能显著提升人工智能在工业及安全关键领域进行危险识别的质量，优于传统的单次推理（single-pass）基线方法，为对话驱动的安全分析提供了实证支持。

Abstract: Operational safety in high-stakes domains such as industrial process control, autonomous, and safety-critical systems, demand reliable hazard identification. While large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating safety analysis tasks, single-turn, monolithic inference is brittle: it lacks the self-correction, deliberation, and contextual refinement that safety engineers apply iteratively. In this paper, we introduce HAZDIAL, a framework that investigates whether structured agentic dialogue-multi-agent, multi-turn interactions improves the quality of NLP- based hazard identification over single-pass baselines. We systematically compare two dialogue modalities: adversarial debate and constructive discussion, and propose an algorithm-based agentic interaction optimization. We evaluate all configurations against a curated golden dataset using standard classification metrics (accuracy, precision, recall, F1) and novel dialogue metrics. This work advances the intersection of dialogue systems, multi-agent reasoning, and AI safety, providing an empirical evidence for dialogue-driven hazard analysis.

</details>


### [160] [Calibrating Urban Traffic Simulation from Sparse Road Observations via Genetic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03823)
*Hunter Sawyer,Jesse Roberts,Simon Matei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对交通模拟中数据缺失的问题，提出了一种基于遗传算法的校准框架，无需详尽的就业数据或全网交通监测，即可通过少量观测路段实现高精度的城市交通模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交通仿真对基础设施规划至关重要，但目前受到两大瓶颈限制：一是大多数城市缺乏细粒度的道路流量观测数据，二是缺乏模拟通勤流所需的必要高分辨率就业分布数据。

Method: 提出了一种基于遗传算法（Genetic Algorithm）的框架，通过优化就业分布（job distribution）和交通闸门参数（gate-traffic parameters），使得交通模拟结果与少数已知路段的实测流量数据对齐。该方法在 SUMO 交通仿真平台上进行了验证。

Result: 该方法在北卡罗来纳州格林斯伯勒市的仿真中表现良好，模拟数据与实测交通流高度相关，在未参与训练的路段上表现出良好的泛化能力，且生成的就业分布与普查数据在定性上基本吻合。

Conclusion: 本文证明了无需详细就业数据和全面交通观测，即可通过遗传算法校准实现逼真的城市交通模拟。这种方法降低了交通建模的门槛，为在不同城市部署可扩展的仿真模型提供了低数据依赖的有效途径。

Abstract: Urban traffic simulation is a critical tool for infrastructure planning, including the placement of electric vehicle charging stations. However, realistic traffic simulation across many cities is hindered by two fundamental data limitations: detailed real-world traffic measurements are available for only a small fraction of road segments in most cities, and employment distribution data critical for modeling commuter traffic is rarely available at the resolution needed for simulation. This paper presents a genetic algorithm-based framework that directly addresses both limitations, calibrating urban traffic simulations from sparse road observations without requiring detailed job location data. Using the SUMO traffic simulation platform for Greensboro, North Carolina, our approach optimizes job distributions and gate-traffic parameters to align simulated traffic with a small sample of roads with known traffic-flow rates. We demonstrate that this approach produces simulated traffic that correlates well with real-world measurements, generalizes to road segments withheld from training, and produces job distributions that show promising qualitative agreement with census employment data despite never directly training on that employment data. This work demonstrates that realistic urban traffic simulation can be achieved from minimal real-world observations, offering a scalable and data-light approach to simulation calibration that reduces the barrier to deploying traffic models across diverse cities.

</details>


### [161] [BigFinanceBench: A Workflow-Grounded Benchmark for Financial-Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03829)
*Alex Wang,Georg Meinhardt,Jacob Katz,Joseph H. Kim,Pratyush K. Chaudhary,Chase Blagden,Eric Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对金融研究缺乏“可审计性”评估的问题，论文提出了BigFinanceBench，通过拆解推导过程进行多点评分，发现现有模型在完整工作流上的表现仍有较大改善空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的金融基准测试主要评估孤立的子技能或最终答案，忽略了对决策至关重要的“可审计推导过程”的评估，导致研究结果的可靠性难以验证。

Method: 提出BigFinanceBench，这是一个包含928个由专家编写的开放式金融研究任务的基准测试，采用基于工作流的评分机制，将推导过程拆解为可独立核查的加权评分点，支持部分得分评估和故障定位。

Result: 对10个前沿模型进行测试，最佳系统仅获得58.8%的评分，且模型能力在不同的金融工作流环节中表现不均，最终答案准确度往往无法准确反映推导质量。

Conclusion: 现有的大模型在金融研究任务中的表现仍有显著提升空间（最高仅达58.8%），且仅关注最终答案准确性是不够的，必须通过评估完整的推导过程（如BigFinanceBench所提供的）来衡量金融研究的决策相关性。

Abstract: Financial-research answers are decision-relevant only when another analyst can audit how they were produced: which source was chosen, which period and accounting definition were used, which assumptions were made, and how the calculation was performed. Existing finance benchmarks largely evaluate isolated subskills or final answers, leaving the auditable derivation itself under-measured. We introduce BigFinanceBench, a 928-item expert-authored benchmark of open-ended financial-research tasks in which each item pairs a ground-truth reference answer with a point-weighted rubric that decomposes the derivation into independently checkable steps. BigFinanceBench is workflow-grounded in that it evaluates the full derivation rather than only the final output. Across 36,241 rubric points, the benchmark supports partial-credit evaluation and localization of failures across the analyst workflow. Evaluating ten current frontier and open-weight agents, we find substantial headroom: the best system reaches only 58.8% rubric score, final-answer accuracy is a useful but lossy proxy for derivation quality, and model capability varies non-uniformly across financial workflows.

</details>


### [162] [EvoDS: Self-Evolving Autonomous Data Science Agent with Skill Learning and Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03841)
*Zherui Yang,Fan Liu,Yansong Ning,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoDS是一款具备自我演化能力的数据科学智能体，通过引入自主技能获取和自适应上下文压缩技术，显著增强了LLM在处理长周期、多阶段数据分析任务中的能力与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM数据科学智能体存在静态动作集限制，且缺乏原则性的长期上下文管理能力，导致难以在多阶段迭代任务中积累经验及有效运作。

Method: 提出了EvoDS智能体框架，包含两个核心机制：1) 自主技能获取（ASA）：用于合成、验证及复用可执行技能；2) 自适应上下文压缩（ACC）：采用强化学习将上下文管理转化为学习控制问题。整体采用两阶段多智能体训练方案。

Result: 在四个数据科学基准测试中表现出色，性能平均超越现有最前沿开源智能体28.9%，并解决了长期运行中的token超限问题。

Conclusion: EvoDS通过自我演化的强化学习框架，成功解决了数据科学智能体在长期任务中的经验复用与上下文管理瓶颈，显著提升了任务完成效率与稳定性。

Abstract: Recent progress in Large Language Model (LLM) agents has enabled promising advances in automated data science. However, existing approaches remain fundamentally limited by their static action sets and lack of principled long-horizon context management, hindering their ability to accumulate reusable experience across tasks and operate reliably in multi-stage, iterative data science pipelines. To address these challenges, we introduce EvoDS, a self-evolving autonomous data science agent that learns to expand its skills and adaptively managing long-term context through agentic reinforcement learning. Specifically, EvoDS introduces two key strategies: (1) Autonomous Skill Acquisition (ASA) mechanism, which enables agents to synthesize, validate, and reuse executable skills; and (2) Adaptive Context Compression (ACC) strategy, which treats context management as a learned control problem rather than passive truncation. These strategies are orchestrated within a two-stage multi-agent training scheme, enabling EvoDS to autonomously improve over time. Theoretically, we prove that EvoDS's hierarchical design reduces tool-selection error, and its optimization objective aligns with an information bottleneck principle, ensuring efficient context use. Empirically, EvoDS outperforms state-of-the-art open-source data science agents by an average of 28.9% across four diverse benchmarks while eliminating out-of-token failures. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/EvoDS.

</details>


### [163] [PyraMathBench: Evaluating and Improving Mathematical Capability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03858)
*Zetian Ouyang,Linlin Wang,Gerard de Melo,Liang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了包含32,505个问题的PyraMathBench基准，揭示了LLM在数值与数学推理上的缺陷；并设计了SOLVE工具调用模块与IRPO优化策略，有效显著提升了模型的数学求解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对大规模语言模型（LLM）数值处理与数学推理集成能力的深入评估，导致数学任务中模型失效的原因难以解释，且LLM在处理复杂数值计算时表现欠佳。

Method: 1. 构建PyraMathBench基准：包含32,505个题目，涵盖4个认知维度、14个子类别和2种模态；2. 提出SOLVE模块：通过模糊匹配和低质量调用拒绝机制优化工具调用；3. 提出IRPO（交互式相对策略优化）：用于增强模型数值与数学推理的协同性；4. 使用Qwen-2.5模型进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明LLM在面对抽象数值问题和复杂计算时能力不足；应用SOLVE和IRPO后，Qwen-2.5模型在基准测试中的得分提升了5.0分。

Conclusion: 通过引入SOLVE模块和IRPO微调策略，可以有效提升大语言模型在数值计算与数学逻辑推理方面的协同能力，显著改善模型在复杂数学问题上的表现。

Abstract: Despite the pivotal role of numerical reasoning as the cornerstone of mathematical capabilities in large language models (LLMs) across applications, few benchmarks evaluate LLMs by integrating numerical processing and mathematical reasoning, hindering the interpretability of failures in math tasks. We introduce PyraMathBench, a comprehensive hierarchical benchmark with 32,505 questions derived from 7,404 math word problems, spanning 4 key cognitive aspects, 14 subcategories, and 2 modalities. Experiments reveal that LLMs' performance is severely compromised by inadequate numerical computation and weak handling of abstract numerical questions. To address this, we propose the Smart Optimization & Learning-based VErsatile module (SOLVE) and Interactive Relative Policy Optimization (IRPO), which enhance LLMs' numerical-mathematical synergy via efficient tool calls (fuzzy matching and low-quality call rejection). Comparative experiments show Qwen-2.5 achieves a 5.0 score improvement with SOLVE and IRPO training.

</details>


### [164] [Reasoning Structure of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03883)
*Frédéric Berdoz,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Fabian Farestam,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将LRM推理过程转化为可量化推理图的方法，通过引入推理效率等结构化指标，实现了对模型推理行为更深入、准确的性能评估与故障诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LRM评估指标（如最终答案准确率、Token计数）过于粗略，无法反映模型背后的推理逻辑差异，导致模型的好坏难以从根本上衡量。

Method: 1. 构建了一个逻辑谜题基准测试集；2. 开发了一个流水线，将非结构化的推理踪迹（traces）自动转换为包含声明（claims）和依赖关系（dependencies）的可验证推理图；3. 定义了推理效率指标（reasoning efficiency）以量化逻辑流的集中度。

Result: 分析表明，结构化度量指标能够区分那些在准确率和Token计数上表现相似但推理深度不同的模型行为，揭示了模型推理随难度增加的演变规律。

Conclusion: 通过将推理过程结构化为图，可以实现对大型推理模型（LRM）推理能力的量化分析和细粒度评估。结构化指标能够有效区分准确率和Token计数无法区分的推理质量差异，为诊断模型推理故障、量化推理效率提供了新方法。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) are often evaluated using metrics such as final-answer accuracy or token count. However, identical scores on these metrics can hide fundamentally different reasoning structures. To address this limitation, we introduce a scalable LRM benchmark of logic puzzles and a pipeline that converts unstructured traces into verifiable reasoning graphs of claims and dependencies. This turns reasoning into a structured, measurable object whose topology can be quantitatively analyzed. Building on this, we define a reasoning efficiency metric that quantifies how concentrated the model's logical flow is. Our analysis on open-source reasoning models shows that structural measurements separate behaviors that token count and accuracy conflate, providing a practical tool for diagnosing failure modes and comparing how reasoning scales with puzzle difficulty.

</details>


### [165] [scTranslation: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Single-Cell Multi-Omics Modality Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03906)
*Jiabei Cheng,Jingbo Zhou,Jun Xia,Changkai Li,Zhen Lei,Chang Yu,Stan Z. Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 scTranslation 基准工具，旨在系统地评估和优化单细胞多组学模态转换模型，填补了该领域缺乏统一评估标准的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管单细胞多组学模态转换计算方法层出不穷，但目前仍缺乏针对数据集、评估指标及关键影响因素的系统化基准评估，制约了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建了 scTranslation 基准平台，集成了多种当前最先进的单细胞多组学模态转换模型，定义了标准的评估指标，并设计了涵盖特征选择、特征质量和少样本学习等多种场景的系统性评估实验。

Result: 通过大规模实验，评估了现有主流方法在不同场景下的表现，发现并量化了多种因素对模态转换准确性的影响，为该领域提供了重要的性能洞察。

Conclusion: scTranslation 提供了一个系统性的评估基准，揭示了特征选择、数据质量和少样本设置对模态转换模型性能的关键影响，并为未来的算法开发指明了方向。

Abstract: Simultaneous measurement of multiple omics modalities in single cells enables researchers to gain a more comprehensive understanding of cellular states and regulatory mechanisms. However, due to high experimental costs, significant noise, and incomplete modality coverage, a variety of computational methods for modality translation have emerged in recent years. Despite the development of translation models, there is still a lack of systematic benchmark evaluation in terms of datasets, evaluation metrics, and influencing factors. To address this, we present scTranslation, a comprehensive benchmark for single-cell multi-omics modality translation tasks. It includes diverse translation datasets, integrates state-of-the-art models, and provides a comprehensive evaluation metrics. In addition, we assess model performance under different scenarios, such as feature selection, feature quality, and few-shot settings. These factors significantly affect model performance but have rarely been systematically studied before. Leveraging this benchmark, we conduct a large-scale study of current methods, report many insightful findings that open up new possibilities for future development. The benchmark is open-sourced to facilitate future research. The code is anonymously released at https://github.com/Bunnybeibei/scTranslation.

</details>


### [166] [Hedge-Bench: Benchmarking Agents on Hard, Realistic Tasks Pertaining to Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03918)
*Eric Cho,Shawn Huang,Alice Lu,Andy Lyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Hedge-Bench 1.0——一个基于真实对冲基金分析师任务的基准测试，用于评估AI在金融领域进行复杂推理的能力。研究发现，当前顶级AI模型在处理此类真实任务时表现欠佳，得分不足16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的AI主要擅长机械性任务，但在金融分析领域，高价值的开放性推理任务缺乏有效的基准测试。现有的评估方法常依赖模型互评，存在评估不准确和逻辑循环的问题。

Method: 构建了Hedge-Bench 1.0基准测试集，包含102个源自真实对冲基金分析师工作流程的实际任务。通过对比模型的推理路径与专家验证的步骤进行确定性评分，避免了以往依赖模型进行主观评估所带来的噪声与循环论证问题。

Result: Hedge-Bench 1.0评估显示，当前的前沿AI模型和智能体在处理真实对冲基金分析师的推理任务时表现极差，得分低于16%。

Conclusion: 现有的前沿AI模型在处理复杂的、开放性的金融分析推理任务上能力有限（得分低于16%），这一领域的自动化仍存在巨大挑战。未来的研究需要专注于提升模型在处理实际专业任务时的逻辑推理深度。

Abstract: AI agents can increasingly handle the mechanical tasks of financial analysis: retrieving documents, calculating formulas, updating spreadsheets. The harder, more valuable challenge is reasoning through the open-ended questions that define expert Analyst work. Existing benchmarks do not capture this class of problem, and those that attempt to evaluate open-ended reasoning rely on model-judged outputs that introduce noise and circularity. We present Hedge-Bench 1.0: a benchmark of 102 actual, on-the-job tasks grounded in the explicit reasoning traces of professional hedge fund analysts working with relevant information sources. This approach enables deterministic grading against verified expert steps. Frontier models and agents score below 16\% on the benchmark. We publish the dataset and evaluation harness at github.com/Trata-Inc/trata-hedge-bench.

</details>


### [167] [Imaginative Perception Tokens Enhance Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03988)
*Mahtab Bigverdi,Lindsey Li,Weikai Huang,Yiming Liu,Jaemin Cho,Jieyu Zhang,Tuhin Kundu,Chris Dangjoo Kim,Zelun Luo,Linda Shapiro,Ranjay Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对VLMs在空间推理中的局限，提出了IPT（想象感知标记）方法，通过引入中间感知表示来显式处理空间推理任务，显著优于仅依靠文本思维链的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在处理不可见空间推理任务（如从未知视角推断、路径追踪和空间整合）时表现不佳，亟需一种能够显式建模“想象感知”的方法。

Method: 提出了“想象感知标记”（Imaginative Perception Tokens, IPT），这是一种中间知觉表示，用于显式模拟模型在不同空间配置下的观察结果；同时构建了包含观点采择（PET）、路径追踪（PT）和多视角计数（MVC）的评估数据集。

Result: 在BAGEL模型上，使用IPT监督可显著提升空间推理准确率（MVC任务提升3.4%），在PT任务上达到甚至超过强力闭源模型水平，且证实了将空间计算强制转换为文本思维链会导致性能下降。

Conclusion: IPT作为一种监督信号，能有效提升视觉语言模型在不可见空间结构推理方面的能力，优于传统的文本思维链，且具有良好的可解释性和泛化性。

Abstract: Vision language models (VLMs) excel at many tasks but still struggle with spatial reasoning when critical information is not directly observable. Many such problems require imaginative perception: inferring what would be seen from an unseen viewpoint, tracing paths through occluded spaces, or integrating partial observations into a coherent spatial representation. We introduce Imaginative Perception Tokens (IPT), intermediate perceptual representations that externalize what a VLM would perceive under alternative spatial configurations while remaining consistent with the observed input.
  To study this capability, we formulate three tasks, Perspective Taking (PET), Path Tracing (PT), and Multiview Counting (MVC), and construct datasets of approximately 20K examples with ground truth imaginations, answers, and evaluation benchmarks. Using the unified VLM BAGEL as the backbone, IPT supervision consistently improves spatial reasoning and often outperforms textual chain of thought training, even without generating images at inference time. On MVC, IPT improves accuracy by 3.4% and achieves competitive performance with strong closed-source models on PT. We further find that combining IPT and label-only supervision yields additional gains, whereas textual chain of thought can substantially degrade performance, suggesting a modality mismatch when spatial computation is forced through language. Overall, IPT provides a principled supervision signal for reasoning about unobserved spatial structure, improving generalization while producing interpretable intermediate representations.

</details>


### [168] [VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00384)
*William Rudman,Abhishek Divekar,Kanishk Jain,Sebastian Joseph,Stella S. R. Offner,Matthew Lease,Kyle Mahowald,Greg Durrett,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VESTA 是一种引入动态工具探索机制的新型自动建模框架，通过让 VLM 主动创建并复用分析工具，显著提升了从复杂数据中进行定量科研建模的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于代理的自动建模系统在处理复杂科研任务时表现不佳，主要局限于简单的迭代批评，缺乏对数据的深度主动探索和工具的有效复用。

Method: 提出 VESTA 框架，通过引入动态生长的工具箱（包含数据转换、假设驱动可视化和统计检验工具）增强视觉语言模型（VLM）；引入 DAWN 基准测试数据集用于评估模型在分布拟合和时间序列建模上的能力。

Result: 在三个工具配置等级的测试中，VESTA 的动态工具创建表现最优；在 DAWN 基准测试（涵盖天文等领域）中表现超越现有基准；生成的工具比现有系统更复杂且更具诊断价值。

Conclusion: VESTA 框架通过动态工具创建和主动数据探索，在自动化定量建模任务中表现优于现有的迭代批评代理系统，特别是在复杂和领域特定的科学任务中优势显著。

Abstract: Fitting quantitative models to data is a central step in scientific workflows, yet it remains one of the least automated. Recent agent-based systems leverage language and vision-language models (VLMs) to iteratively propose and refine statistical models, but these systems struggle on more challenging modeling tasks. To address these limitations, we introduce VESTA: Visual Exploration with Statistical Tool Agents, a framework that equips VLMs with a dynamically growing exploration toolkit to guide model refinement through data transformations, hypothesis-driven visualizations, and robust statistical tests. Unlike prior systems that rely on iterative critique alone, VESTA actively explores data before and during refinement by selecting or creating diagnostic tools, which accumulate in the model's context and can be reused later. We evaluate VESTA against established baselines in three toolkit configurations: no tools, static expert-written tools, and dynamic model-written tools. To support this evaluation, we introduce DAWN (Dataset for Automated Workflows and Numerical Modeling), a benchmark targeting distribution fitting and time series modeling with varying difficulty tiers, and culminating in real-world astronomy tasks including modeling initial mass functions and gravitational-wave chirp signals. We find that VESTA's dynamic tool creation outperforms prior agentic pipelines, with the largest gains on complex and domain-specific tasks. We further show that dynamically generated tools are substantially more sophisticated than those produced by existing visual tool-creation systems, covering more diagnostic categories per function and strongly preferring visual outputs that the VLM critic can reason over directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [The Case for Text-to-SQL Friendly Logical Database Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03145)
*Shi Heng Zhang,Zhengjie Miao,Jiannan Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出“LLM友好型数据库设计”概念，通过模式抽象、分区和重命名三种语义保持转换技术，显著提升了LLM进行Text-to-SQL任务时的生成准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据库设计主要针对人类编写的查询进行优化，但LLM作为Text-to-SQL的新消费者，其理解能力对模式设计提出了新要求。因此需要通过优化模式结构，使其更易于LLM将自然语言映射为正确的SQL。

Method: 提出了三种保持语义不变的模式转换算子：模式抽象（+A：将重复连接路径物化为逻辑视图）、模式分区（+P：基于工作负载剪枝无关上下文）、模式重命名（+R：使用描述性标识符优化列链接与谓词构建）。这些算子可以组合使用，并能根据有无历史数据选择指导或自适应策略。

Result: 在BIRD-Union和Spider-Union数据集上，通过上述组合转换，执行准确率提升了最高4.2%，验证了模式设计优化在提升Text-to-SQL任务性能上的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM友好的逻辑数据库设计是一个实用且未被充分探索的数据库端优化目标，与现有的Text-to-SQL流水线互补，能够有效提升生成准确度。

Abstract: Logical database design has traditionally optimized database schemas, including tables, columns, keys, constraints, and views, for correctness, integrity, and human-written application queries. LLM-based Text-to-SQL changes the consumer: the schema is now often read as text by a language model, so design choices that preserve database semantics can still change SQL-generation accuracy. We argue that this creates a new design objective alongside the classical ones - LLM-friendly logical database design, the property that a schema is easy for a language model to map from natural language to correct SQL - and treat it as the optimization target of this paper. We instantiate this objective with three semantics-preserving schema transformations that re-purpose classical schema-design ideas: schema abstraction (+A: logical views that materialize recurring join paths), schema partitioning (+P: workload-aware logical partitions that prune irrelevant context), and schema renaming (+R: descriptive identifiers that improve downstream column linking and predicate construction). The three operators compose, and each preserves the underlying database semantics. When historical question-SQL pairs are available, they guide both partitioning and abstraction; in zero-shot settings, renaming applies directly, and abstraction falls back to an ad-hoc per-question variant. We evaluate the resulting schemas on BIRD-Union and Spider-Union across multiple Text-to-SQL pipelines and language model backbones, with gains of up to 4.2% in execution accuracy. The best transformation varies modestly across pipelines and models, with the full +A+P+R consistently improving; multiple operator combinations are competitive on each pipeline. These results show that LLM-friendly logical design is a practical and underexplored database-side optimization target, complementary to existing Text-to-SQL pipelines.

</details>


### [170] [Cost-Aware Optimization for Agentic Query Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03152)
*Lunyiu Nie,Yilin Xia,Yiren Liu,Christopher Jermaine,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对含有LLM算子的查询，本文提出了“代理查询执行”范式，并开发了EnumGRPO优化器，通过自改进强化学习自动优化算子布置与执行策略，在显著降低成本的同时提升了查询准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的查询优化假设等价计划仅在成本上有所不同，但在包含LLM算子的场景中，算子的放置、排序和粒度同时影响成本和质量，且最佳选择通常仅在运行时可知。这种环境下需要一种新的查询执行范式（代理查询执行）。

Method: 提出了EnumGRPO（一种自改进优化器），在学习阶段枚举执行范式、算子类型、放置、选择性范围和投影宽度等决策空间，利用质量-成本反馈，通过上下文强化学习将其转化为可重用的规划启发式规则。

Result: 在SWAN数据集的四个数据库上，EnumGRPO达到了35.4%的执行准确率，单次查询LLM算子成本仅为0.011美元，相比混合查询基线，成本降低了约317倍，准确率提高了18%。

Conclusion: 将代理工作流优化作为经典查询优化的对应物，并且通过EnumGRPO这种基于上下文的强化学习方法，可以有效解决LLM集成到查询执行中带来的复杂性、成本和准确性权衡问题。

Abstract: Classical query optimization searches over algebraically equivalent plans that differ only in cost. This assumption breaks once LLM-backed operators enter the picture: their placement, ordering, and granularity jointly determine both dollar cost and answer quality, and the right choice among the alternatives is often revealed only at runtime. We formalize this setting as agentic query execution, a query execution paradigm in which agent-based planning is interleaved with execution, and agent workflow optimization becomes the analogue of classical query optimization. We then present EnumGRPO, a self-improving optimizer for this setting. During a learning stage, EnumGRPO enumerates query plans over decisions such as execution paradigm, operator type, operator placement, selectivity scope, and projection width, then distills quality-cost feedback into reusable planning heuristics via in-context reinforcement learning. Across four databases in SWAN, EnumGRPO achieves 35.4% execution accuracy at $0.011 per query in LLM-operator cost, a ~317x cost reduction over the hybrid query baseline with an 18% relative improvement in answer accuracy.

</details>


### [171] [Formalizing all indexed mathematics as a benchmark for general reasoning, with the example of implementing dilatations of categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03835)
*A. Mayeux*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将数学通过交互式定理证明器实现全面机器形式化的可能性，重点关注大规模数学知识库的组织与可扩展性，并以范畴论中的“范畴扩张”为案例验证了这一方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用交互式定理证明器将已发表的数学知识转化为机器可验证、持续更新的语料库，以应对数学在大规模形式化过程中面临的可扩展性和组织挑战。

Method: 将数学形式化为由公理推导出的逻辑一致的机器可验证数据库；以“范畴的扩张（dilatations of categories）”为例，在交互式定理证明器中进行具体的形式化实现与验证。

Result: 通过在范畴论领域（范畴的扩张）的应用，验证了交互式定理证明器处理复杂数学概念的能力，并展示了数学形式化实践的流程。

Conclusion: 将数学视为结构化数据的研究方向是可行的，通过案例研究展示了交互式定理证明器在复杂代数结构（如范畴论）中形式化数学的可实践性，有助于推动大规模数学知识库的构建。

Abstract: Formal rigor distinguishes mathematics from other disciplines, in the sense that mathematical statements are derived from explicit axioms by logically verifiable steps. Interactive theorem provers support this by expressing definitions, theorems, and proofs in a fully formal language and verifying them mechanically. We consider the benchmark problem of formalizing all published mathematics as a machine verifiable and continuously updated corpus of mathematical knowledge. This viewpoint treats mathematics as a structured database of interdependent results and raises questions about scalability and organization of large formal libraries. As a case study, we present an ongoing formalization in categorical algebra, namely dilatations of categories, extending classical localizations and illustrating what such an implementation looks like in practice.

</details>


### [172] [MLSkip: Data Skipping for ML Filters via Lightweight Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03946)
*Mihail Stoian,Mark Gerarts,Pascal Ginter,Andreas Zimmerer,Jan Van den Bussche,Andreas Kipf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该研究针对AI函数过滤中的性能瓶颈，提出利用模型验证技术和改进的元数据结构（2D凸包）进行数据跳过，有效提升了数据库在处理复杂ML过滤任务时的查询处理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库应用AI函数（如ML模型过滤）时，由于模型计算昂贵且为黑盒，传统的针对整数/字符串的数据跳过（Data Skipping）技术失效，导致存储在blob存储中的数据无法有效剪枝。

Method: 1. 建立ML模型查询语言与神经网络验证技术之间的联系，利用Parquet已有的Min-Max元数据进行数据裁剪；2. 提出一种基于空间连接思想的“大小受限的2D凸包”元数据结构，用于增强数据裁剪精度。

Result: 在TPC-H和TPC-DS表上，针对选择度（selectivity）小于0.1%的过滤条件，Min-Max剪枝实现了27.4%的平均剪枝率；引入2D凸包后，剪枝率提升至38.31%（仅需不超过45字节额外开销），并在DuckDB中实现了1.07倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 通过利用现有Min-Max元数据并结合神经网络验证技术，以及引入空间约束的2D凸包元数据，能够有效地跳过AI模型过滤中的非必要数据，从而在保持低开销的同时提升查询性能。

Abstract: Database vendors recently released AI functions that can be used in filter predicates. As such functions often rely on costly, black-box ML models, they unveil new data management challenges. Concretely, traditional data skipping techniques for integer and string data fail to be applicable to the new filter type. Indeed, there is no known mechanism for pruning non-qualifying row groups, e.g., when reading files from blob storage.
  In this work, we initiate the study of data skipping techniques for ML filters. We make the case that Parquet's default min-max metadata is enough to enable pruning. To this end, we draw connections to two lines of research: (i) the recently proposed query language for ML models and (ii) neural network verification.
  Our preliminary results on ReLU architectures show that on tables from TPC-H and TPC-DS, the average pruning effectiveness for filters of selectivity below 0.1% amounts to 27.4%. Finally, inspired by research on spatial joins, we propose an enhanced metadata structure: a size-bounded 2D convex hull that verification tools can make better use of, increasing the pruning effectiveness to 38.31%, while occupying at most 45 bytes per row group and column pair. We observe an end-to-end speedup of 1.07$\times$ over PyTorch in DuckDB.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Heterogeneous Mapping for Analog In-Memory Computing Accelerators: A Unified Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02672)
*Corey Lammie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对模拟内存计算（AIMC）的精度局限，提出了异构架构下神经网络任务分区的四阶段工作流，并通过对GPT-2的深入分析，指出了Transformer部署中应优先保护的关键计算层，为AIMC硬件设计与模型映射提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟内存计算（AIMC）能显著提升神经网络推理效率，但受限于有效精度。为了平衡计算精度与功耗，需要开发异构架构，即结合模拟计算单元与数字处理单元，并解决如何高效分配计算任务（分区）的问题。

Method: 1. 对现有的混合精度/异构计算分区方法进行了分类（包含映射粒度、优化策略、模型支持）。2. 提出了一个统一的四阶段工作流。3. 将该工作流的前两个阶段应用于GPT-2模型，生成了首个针对解码器架构Transformer的AIMC特定精度敏感度图谱。

Result: 提出了一个通用的四阶段异构任务分区工作流，并成功量化了GPT-2对AIMC精度的敏感度，揭示了模型中存在突出的“关键投影”层。

Conclusion: 通过对GPT-2的分析发现，模型对精度的敏感度高度集中在少数投影（4/49），特别是首个解码器块的注意力输出。这表明针对Decoder-only Transformer在AIMC硬件上的部署，应采用投影级的映射策略，并对关键层执行数字计算，以兼顾效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Analog In-Memory Computing (AIMC) accelerators execute matrix-vector multiplications directly within memory arrays, reducing data movement and improving DNN inference efficiency. Their limited effective precision motivates heterogeneous architectures that combine analog compute tiles with digital processing units. This letter classifies existing methods for partitioning DNN workloads across these resources by mapping granularity, optimization strategy, and model support, and distills them into a unified four-stage workflow. To demonstrate the workflow on a model class not yet addressed by existing methods, we apply its first two stages to GPT-2, producing the first AIMC-specific precision sensitivity profile for a decoder-only transformer. Sensitivity is dominated by 4 of 49 projections, with the first decoder block's attention output dominating by an order of magnitude. This suggests that projection-level mapping and selective digital execution of early-block and output-facing projections are important for reliable decoder-transformer deployment on AIMC hardware.

</details>


### [174] [Fast Transformer Inference on ARM-Based HMPSoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02836)
*Hang Xu,Yixian Shen,Thanassis Giannetsos,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究通过在 ARM-CL 中增加 Transformer 原生内核并设计 CPU-GPU 协同推理机制，大幅度提升了边缘计算设备上的 Transformer 推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 模型虽表现优异，但其计算和内存资源需求巨大，难以直接在资源受限的边缘设备（ARM 架构）上进行高效的离线推理。现有 ARM-CL 框架缺乏对 Transformer 的原生支持。

Method: 1. 在 ARM Compute Library (ARM-CL) 中实现针对 Transformer 的专用算子；2. 提出一种异构 CPU-GPU 协同推理算法，根据算子特征（访存密集型 vs. 计算密集型）分配至不同处理器执行。

Result: 1. 扩展后的 ARM-CL 在嵌入式 ARM 板上实现了比当前最先进 CPU/GPU 实现快 3 倍的 Transformer 推理速度；2. 协同推理进一步降低了 15.72% 的推理延迟。

Conclusion: 通过扩展 ARM-CL 并引入 CPU-GPU 协同推理策略，可显著提升边缘设备上 Transformer 模型的推理性能，为边缘侧资源受限环境下的高效深度学习部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Transformer models have set new performance standards for machine learning (ML) tasks. However, their resource-intensive deployment on resource-constrained edge devices for cloud-free, on-chip transformer inference remains challenging. The ARM Compute Library (ARM-CL) framework provides low-latency CNN inference on ARM-based edge devices but lacks support for transformer inference. In this work, we implement several new transformer kernels in ARM-CL to support native transformer execution. Our extended ARM-CL achieves up to three times faster transformer inference compared to state-of-the-art CPU/GPU implementations on an ARM-based embedded board.
  Furthermore, heterogeneous multi-processor system-on-chips (HMPSoCs) powering edge devices provide both embedded CPUs and GPUs. We introduce cooperative CPU-GPU transformer inference, which executes memory-intensive operations on the CPU while utilizing the GPU for highly parallelizable, compute-intensive operations. This cooperative execution, implemented with minimal overhead, further reduces transformer inference latency by up to 15.72% compared to the best single-processor inference on ARM-CL.

</details>


### [175] [Multi-Segment Attention: Enabling Efficient KV-Cache Management for Faster Large Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02964)
*Chunan Shi,Yilei Chen,Yilin Chen,Xupeng Miao,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AsymCache 是一种计算延迟感知的无损 KV 缓存管理方案，通过将缓存驻留决策与底层 GPU 内核执行性能对齐，实现了 LLM 推理效率的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无损 KV 缓存管理主要依赖访问频率或位置启发式规则，忽略了 KV 缓存块对 GPU 注意力内核执行效率的具体影响，导致整体性能受限。

Method: 提出了 AsymCache 系统，包含三个核心组件：1) 多段注意力 (MSA)：支持高效的非连续 KV 上下文处理；2) 位置感知的缓存驱逐策略：联合优化缓存命中率与重计算成本；3) 自适应分块调度器：优化硬件利用率。

Result: 实验表明，与最新基准相比，AsymCache 将 TTFT 降低了 1.90-2.03 倍，TPOT 降低了 1.62-1.71 倍；在集成至 Agent 服务系统时，平均作业延迟进一步降低了 18.1%。

Conclusion: AsymCache 证明了通过结合计算延迟感知策略与底层执行优化（如多段注意力和自适应分块），可以在保持模型输出无损的前提下，显著提升大模型的推理性能（TTFT 和 TPOT），并能无缝集成到现有的智能体服务系统中。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference relies on key-value (KV) caches to avoid redundant attention computation. While approximate KV cache retention techniques reduce memory usage by sacrificing model accuracy, lossless approaches instead evict KV cache blocks from GPU memory and reconstruct them on demand to preserve exact outputs. Existing lossless KV cache management systems primarily base eviction decisions on access frequency or positional heuristics, without considering how different KV cache blocks affect the execution efficiency of GPU attention kernels.
  In this paper, we propose AsymCache, a computation-latency-aware KV cache management system for LLM inference that explicitly aligns cache residency decisions with GPU attention kernel performance, including three key components: Multi-Segment Attention (MSA) for efficient non-contiguous KV context processing, a cache eviction policy that jointly optimizes hit rate and position-aware recomputation cost, and an adaptive chunking scheduler for high hardware utilization. Experiments show that AsymCache reduces TTFT by up to 1.90-2.03x and time-per-output-token (TPOT) by 1.62-1.71x over latest baselines, confirming the effectiveness of the method in common workloads and validating its design goal of balancing computational efficiency with cache hit rate. Moreover, the low-level design of AsymCache allows seamless integration into agent serving systems such as Continuum, where it further reduces average job latency by up to 18.1%.

</details>


### [176] [ZK-Flex: A Flexible and Scalable Framework for Accelerating Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03046)
*Adiwena Putra,Cuong Manh Duong,Anh Quang Pham,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ZK-Flex 是一种针对零知识证明生成优化的软硬件协同加速框架，通过可重构的 TCore 架构和针对性的算法优化，显著提升了大规模模乘运算的并行处理能力与资源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零知识证明（ZKP）的证明生成过程计算密集，现有的硬件加速方案在处理不同精度的模乘运算时，存在灵活性差、资源利用率低及难以应对不同计算阶段动态切换的问题。

Method: 提出了软硬件协同设计的 ZK-Flex 框架：软件层包含 POLY（多项式）和 EC（椭圆曲线）优化器以协同算法选择；硬件层引入 TCore（基于 Toom-Cook 的多精度核心），结合灵活的片上网络（NoC）和链表内存机制，提升了并行度和资源利用率。

Result: 在代表性 ZKP 基准测试中，ZK-Flex 实现了 5 到 11 倍的速度提升，面积效率比现有最优方案提高了 3.8 倍。

Conclusion: ZK-Flex 通过软硬件协同设计，实现了对零知识证明计算瓶颈的有效突破，为高性能、可重构的 ZKP 加速提供了一种全新的架构基础。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge proofs (ZKP) allows a prover to convince a verifier of computational correctness without revealing private data, ensuring both privacy and verifiability. However, proof generation is highly compute-intensive, dominated by polynomial (POLY) and elliptic-curve (EC) operations. These workloads pose two key challenges for hardware acceleration: (1) efficiently supporting diverse large-precision modular multiplications, and (2) maintaining high utilization across workloads that dynamically shift between POLY and EC stages. Existing reconfigurable accelerators address these issues only partially, remaining limited in precision scalability, algorithmic flexibility, and resource efficiency.
  To overcome these limitations, we propose ZK-Flex, a flexible and scalable software-hardware co-designed framework for accelerating ZKP proof generation. The software layer incorporates POLY and EC optimizers that reduce computation through hardware- and workload-aware algorithmic choices, while the hardware integrates TCore, a Toom-Cook-based multi-precision core with a flexible NoC and a linked-list memory mechanism that improves parallelism under limited memory capacity. Across representative ZKP benchmarks, ZK-Flex achieves 5 to 11 times speedup and up to 3.8 times higher area efficiency over the state of the art, establishing a new foundation for high-performance, reconfigurable ZKP acceleration.

</details>


### [177] [ACRONYM: Accelerated Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in Memory for Dynamic Vector Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03151)
*Md Mizanur Rahaman Nayan,Tianqi Zhang,Flavio Ponzina,Tajana Rosing,Azad J Naeemi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ACRONYM 是一个软硬件协同设计的向量数据库搜索平台，通过基于 CAM 的内存内计算技术和分布无关的编码架构，实现了百万级动态向量数据的实时、高效、低能耗近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图索引（如HNSW）和分区索引（如FAISS-IVF）依赖数据分布，在频繁更新场景下需要频繁重构索引，导致高延迟和难以持续部署。

Method: 1. 算法层面：采用分布无关的编码和基于汉明距离的搜索；2. 架构层面：设计了基于CAM（内容可寻址存储器）的内存内并行距离计算及分时多路复用Top-k选择器；3. 流程层面：提出两阶段搜索（粗搜索+二进制精细化）；4. 编码层面：集成基于XOR-and-Accumulate (XAC) 的脉动阵列编码器以支持片上实时编码。

Result: 在百万级数据集上达到 >90% 的召回率，每秒查询吞吐量达 800 万次，内存占用仅 32MB，平均能耗 2.56μJ/query；相比 CPU 上的 HNSW 提速 ~400 倍，相比 GPU 上的 FAISS-IVF 提速 ~80 倍。

Conclusion: ACRONYM 通过软硬件协同设计，成功解决了动态向量数据库中频繁更新导致的高延迟和重构开销问题，在支持实时更新的同时，实现了极高的搜索吞吐量、极低的能耗和极小的内存占用，是高性能向量检索领域的一项重要突破。

Abstract: Vector database search with frequent updates is increasingly critical in applications such as retrieval augmented generation, recommendation systems, and large-scale embedding retrieval. Existing solutions, such as graph-based and partition-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS), suffer from frequent index rebuilding due to data distribution-dependent indexing that impacts continuous deployment and causes long rebuilding latency. This paper proposes an algorithm-hardware co-designed platform, ACRONYM, that addresses key problems with state of the art database search. Algorithmically, it leverages efficient encoding independent of data distribution and Hamming-distance based search for efficient hardware acceleration. Architecturally, we propose CAM-based in-memory parallel distance computation followed by time multiplexed approximated top-k selection to enable the exhaustive search. We propose two-stage search that includes coarse search followed by binary refinement to achieve high recall in CAM based search which is heavily limited to small vector dimension due to capacity and wordline parasitic. ACRONYM supports continuous update without stalling and integrates novel XOR-and-Accumulate (XAC) based systolic-array encoder for efficient on chip encoding during search. Across million-scale datasets, while serving dynamic database ACRONYM achieves >90% recall at a throughput of 8e6 queries per second, with a memory footprint of only 32MB and an average energy consumption of 2.56uJ per query, speedup over HNSW (CPU) of about 400x and FAISS-IVF (GPU) of about 80x.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Human-in-the-Loop Contextual Bandits for Short-Term Rental Dynamic Pricing: Structural Equivalence of Historical Warm-Up and Approval-Gated Live Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02595)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对高风险决策场景，论文提出了一种利用“人在回路”权限将历史数据转化为有效预热数据的方法，显著缩短了在线算法的冷启动时间，并通过实验证明了其在短租定价中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租（STR）市场面临定价风险大、模型需具备可解释性、以及反馈稀疏等挑战，导致传统的纯在线多臂老虎机（bandit）算法在实际应用中存在较长的冷启动期，难以直接部署。

Method: 提出“人在回路门控多臂老虎机（HITL-GB）”框架。该方法将历史确定性策略下的价格数据视为一种等效的同策略（on-policy）预热数据，通过正则化岭回归初始化老虎机的后验概率分布，并结合人工干预（批准/限制）机制进行动态定价。

Result: 基于1,461个真实短租定价数据进行的验证显示，该预热策略将分层因子汤普森采样（HF-TS）算法的有效冷启动时间从约150个周期压缩至约30个周期。

Conclusion: 在受监管或高风险领域，强制性的人工监督不仅是操作限制，更是一种可以利用的统计资产。该框架通过结合历史数据实现模型冷启动的加速，为在线决策提供了可行、可解释且风险可控的方案。

Abstract: Dynamic pricing in short-term rental (STR) markets presents a distinctive challenge for online learning algorithms: pricing decisions carry significant financial risk, operators require explainability, and market feedback is sparse (one booking outcome per listed night). We introduce the Human-in-the-Loop Gated Bandit (HITL-GB) framework, in which a contextual bandit algorithm generates price recommendations but a human agent retains authority to accept, modify, or reject each recommendation before it is applied. We show that under this approval constraint, historical pricing data -- collected under a prior deterministic policy -- is structurally equivalent to on-policy warm-up data for initialising the bandit's posterior, bypassing the weeks-to-months cold-start period that renders pure online bandit learning impractical in sparse-feedback markets. We formalise the approval-gated reward signal, derive a regularised ridge-regression warm-up procedure from historical episodes, and validate the approach on real STR production data (anonymised urban market, 2 rooms, April 2022 -- April 2026, 1,461 nightly pricing episodes). Our warm-up procedure compresses effective cold-start from ~150 episodes to ~30 episodes when initialising agents from the Hierarchical Factored Thompson Sampling (HF-TS) family. We further argue that the structural equivalence result is domain-agnostic: any high-stakes domain where human approval is legally or operationally required -- including clinical drug dosing, credit origination, content moderation, and radiological diagnosis -- satisfies the same conditions and benefits from the same warm-up strategy. In regulated industries, mandatory human oversight is thus a statistical asset rather than a deployment constraint.

</details>


### [179] [Spectral Asymptotics of Neural Network Loss Landscapes: An Exact Decomposition of the Curvature Exponent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02596)
*Anherutowa Calvo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度学习层中 Hessian 曲率指数 $α$ 变化的几何本质，提出了“谱对齐分解”公式，并利用其导出的属性开发出性能优于 AdamW 的架构自适应预处理优化器 Spectral Newton。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型中 Hessian 矩阵特征值的缩放行为（即曲率指数 $α$）在不同层类型（卷积、注意力机制、MLP）之间存在系统性差异，目前的理论尚无法解释这种变异背后的几何机制。

Method: 1. 提出了用于刻画 Hessian 特征值与梯度奇异值关系的“谱对齐分解”公式：$α = 2 + d\log Φ_k / d\log σ_k$；
2. 定义了用于度量 Kronecker 因子特征基与梯度奇异方向之间对齐程度的指标 $Φ_k$；
3. 导出了联系曲率指数、有效梯度秩衰减和 Hessian 衰减的谱转换恒等式 $s = αγ$；
4. 利用 Zeta 函数边界分析曲率分布；
5. 基于上述理论设计并评估了架构自适应预处理算法 Spectral Newton。

Result: 1. 该理论在 93 个层、5 种架构和 3 个数据集上通过了验证，预测误差中位数仅约为 2%；
2. 研究表明曲率在每一层通常集中在极少数（有效为 1 个）方向上；
3. 开发的 Spectral Newton 方法在 $α \approx 2$ 的视觉基准任务中表现优于标准优化器 AdamW。

Conclusion: 该研究提出并验证了“谱对齐分解（Spectral Alignment Decomposition）”理论，定量解释了不同深度学习层类型中曲率指数 $α$ 的变化原因。通过建立 $α$、梯度秩衰减 $γ$ 和 Hessian 衰减指数 $s$ 之间的代数恒等式，研究人员开发了架构自适应预处理器“Spectral Newton”，在特定任务上超过了 AdamW。

Abstract: The curvature exponent $α$ in $h_k \propto σ_k^α$ -- governing how Hessian eigenvalues scale with gradient singular values -- varies systematically across layer types ($α\approx 2$ for convolutions, $\approx 1$ for transformer attention, $< 1$ for MLP up-projections). Why? We prove the Spectral Alignment Decomposition: $α= 2 + d\logΦ_k / d\logσ_k$, where $Φ_k$ measures alignment between Kronecker factor eigenbases and gradient singular directions. This reduces "why does $α$ vary?" to a geometric question we answer for LayerNorm, residual connections, and softmax heads. The decomposition implies a spectral transfer identity $s = αγ$ linking curvature exponent, effective gradient rank-decay $γ$, and Hessian decay exponent $s$. The identity is algebraic; its empirical content is that $α$ and $γ$, fit on independent data (HVPs vs. SVD), recover $s$ to ~2% median error across 93 layers, five architectures, and three datasets -- with no free parameters. A zeta-function bound on participation ratio shows curvature concentrates onto effectively one direction per layer. As a proof of concept, we derive the architecture-adaptive preconditioner $T(σ;α)$ and show that Spectral Newton -- implementing $T$ in the gradient singular basis -- outperforms AdamW on vision benchmarks where $α\approx 2$.

</details>


### [180] [Making Brain-Computer Interfaces More Secure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02597)
*Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Gahangir Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对EEG-BCI系统的安全漏洞，本文提出了一种轻量级CNN架构，实验证明其在对抗性攻击下具有比现有主流模型更优的鲁棒性和分类表现，为构建可靠的BCI系统提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCI研究多关注分类准确率，忽视了安全性和鲁棒性。研究发现基于EEG的BCI容易受到微小对抗性扰动的攻击，导致误诊，因此亟需评估并提升模型在对抗环境下的稳健性。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的定制卷积神经网络（CNN）架构，并在两个EEG数据集上，通过对比EEGNet、DeepConvNet和SleepEEGNet，在梯度对抗攻击场景下评估了其分类准确率和鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果显示，该轻量化模型在对抗性扰动下始终保持比基准模型更好的分类性能，具有更高的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 轻量级卷积神经网络架构在面对基于梯度的对抗性攻击时，展现出比现有经典模型（EEGNet, DeepConvNet, SleepEEGNet）更强的鲁棒性，证明了轻量化设计在提升BCI系统可靠性方面的潜力。

Abstract: The development of brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) based on electroencephalograms (EEGs) has advanced significantly mainly to machine learning. Although the majority of earlier research has been on increasing classification accuracy, relatively little focus has been placed on security and robustness. According to recent research, EEG-based BCIs are susceptible to adversarial attacks, which can cause misdiagnosis due to minute, well-crafted disturbances. Evaluating model robustness against such perturbations is therefore critical for ensuring reliable deployment. In this study, we propose a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) architecture to investigate adversarial robustness in EEG-based BCIs. The suggested method is assessed using two EEG datasets and contrasted with three novel CNN models tailored to EEG, namely EEGNet, DeepConvNet, and SleepEEGNet, under gradient-based adversarial attack scenarios. According to experimental findings, the suggested model continuously performs better in classification under adversarial perturbations compared to baseline models, indicating improved robustness. These findings highlight the potential of lightweight architectures for enhancing the reliability of EEG-based BCI systems under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [181] [Assessing Region-Level EEG Contributions to Cognitive Workload Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02598)
*Jacob Wong,Sohan Singh,Prannaya Gupta,Jin Xing Ang,Kritika Johari,U-Xuan Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模跨数据集分析发现，EEG中认知负荷信息主要分布在前额和额中区域，仅使用这些关键区域的信号即可实现比全脑更高效、更通用的工作负荷预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于EEG的工作负荷评估缺乏跨任务、跨数据集和跨受试者的区域一致性研究；需要识别关键脑区以减少冗余，并提高实际应用中监控系统的准确性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出基于解剖学定义的区域级评估框架，利用模型无关的性能评估方法量化不同脑区的贡献；通过混合受试者和受试者独立两种协议，在四个公开数据集上进行大规模分析，并采用基于秩（rank-based）的策略聚合结果。

Result: 在所有数据集及受试者独立评估中，使用前额电极的方案比全脑电极基线在相对排名上提升了15-20%；前额至额中区域表现出最稳定的预测效能，而枕区及后部区域贡献较低且不稳定。

Conclusion: 前额和额中区域保留了最一致的认知负荷信息。研究证明，使用较少的电极（仅前额区）即可获得比全脑电极更优且更具泛化能力的预测性能，为开发高效、轻量化的EEG工作负荷监测系统提供了设计指导。

Abstract: Accurate and generalizable estimation of cognitive workload from electroencephalography (EEG) is critical for human-centered and safety-critical systems. Although EEG is widely used for workload assessment, the consistency of region-level EEG contributions across tasks, datasets, and subjects remains unclear. This paper presents a region-level evaluation framework for EEG-based workload prediction in which models are trained and evaluated using features extracted exclusively from electrodes belonging to anatomically defined scalp regions. We perform a large-scale analysis across four publicly available EEG workload datasets spanning diverse task demands, recording hardware, and electrode montages. Region importance is quantified using a model-agnostic, performance-based approach under both mixed-subject and subject-independent evaluation protocols, with results aggregated using a rank-based strategy to ensure robustness across experimental configurations. Across all datasets and subject-independent evaluations, frontal electrode groups outperform the full-scalp baseline by approximately 15-20% in relative rank position while using substantially fewer electrodes. Fronto-central regions exhibit the most stable predictive utility, whereas posterior and occipital regions contribute less consistently across experimental conditions. These findings indicate that workload-relevant EEG information is most consistently retained within frontal and fronto-central electrode groups, supporting the design of efficient and generalizable EEG-based workload monitoring systems.

</details>


### [182] [Testing the Test: Score-Direction Instability in Class-Split Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02601)
*Alejandro Ascarate,Leo Lebrat,Rodrigo Santa Cruz,Clinton Fookes,Olivier Salvado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了类划分异常检测评估协议的局限性，即当异常类与正常数据在表观空间重叠时，评价指标会失真。作者提出了“邻域类泄漏”诊断方法，建议谨慎对待此类基准测试 sonuç。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前广泛使用的基于类划分的异常检测评估方法，在异常类与正常分布在表观空间（representation space）存在重叠时会出现评估结果病态（ill-posed）的情况，导致异常得分失效或倒置。

Method: 提出了一种名为“邻域类泄漏”（neighborhood class leakage）的无需训练的评估诊断方法，用于量化和预测异常得分的方向不稳定性。

Result: 在Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10和Imagenette数据集（像素级和VAE潜空间）上的实验表明，“邻域类泄漏”指标能有效预测得分不稳定性，揭示了基准测试中得分方向对特定异常类的过度依赖。

Conclusion: 现有的基于类划分（class-split）的异常检测评估协议存在缺陷，仅应将其视为几何依赖的压力测试，而非评估模型无条件异常检测能力的通用指标。

Abstract: Within-dataset class-split evaluation is widely used as a proxy for fully unconditional out-of-distribution anomaly detection. We show that this protocol can become ill-posed when the held-out anomaly class overlaps the normal mixture in representation space. In this regime, anomaly scores may collapse toward chance or even invert, and the preferred score direction can depend on the unknown anomaly class. We introduce a simple training-free diagnostic, neighborhood class leakage, and show that it predicts score-direction instability across Fashion-MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette, in both pixel and VAE latent spaces. Our results suggest that class-split AD benchmarks should be treated as geometry-dependent stress tests rather than unconditional evidence of anomaly-detection ability.

</details>


### [183] [Graph Mamba Survival Analysis Based on Topology-Aware ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02602)
*Yuanfang Chen,Peiqiang Yan,Yuntao Shou,Qian Zhao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对WSI生存分析中Transformer计算瓶颈和Graph Mamba序列敏感性问题，本文提出了TopoMamSurv框架，通过拓扑感知排序、双向Mamba和GCN层级学习，提升了长距离依赖建模效率与准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在WSI生存分析中存在$O(N^2)$计算复杂性瓶颈；现有Graph Mamba方法因节点排序忽略图形拓扑连通性而导致建模效果受限，且缺乏对图像空间上下文的双向建模能力。

Method: 提出TopoMamSurv框架：1. 拓扑感知排序（TAO）：优化节点输入顺序以适应Mamba的序列敏感性；2. 双向Mamba模块：克服单向建模限制；3. 集成GCN：实现局部聚合；4. 层级特征学习：构建“局部聚合-全局捕获”的建模体系。

Result: 在五个TCGA数据集上验证了该框架的综合性能优势，可视化实验证明TAO策略能有效增强节点间的相似性以优化建模效果。

Conclusion: TopoMamSurv通过拓扑感知排序（TAO）、双向Mamba模块以及GCN的层级化架构，成功解决了WSI生存分析中长距离依赖建模、计算效率瓶颈与空间结构利用之间的矛盾，并在五个TCGA数据集上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: In computational pathology, Whole Slide Images (WSIs) survival analysis is crucial for patient prognosis assessment, but it faces multiple technical challenges. Although the Transformer captures long-range dependencies through its self-attention mechanism, its $O(N^2)$ time complexity causes a severe computational bottleneck in large-scale WSIs graph structures. The Mamba model breaks through the Transformer's computational bottleneck with linear complexity. But, owing to Mamba's high sensitivity to the order of input data, traditional node sorting methods in Graph Mamba, such as those based on node degree or subgraph size, fail to adequately account for the topological connectivity of graph data. This inadequacy consequently restricts the performance of Mamba's sequential modeling. Moreover, its unidirectional architecture cannot leverage the bidirectional spatial structure of images. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a novel Graph Mamba survival analysis framework based on topology-aware ordering (TopoMamSurv) to adapt to the sequential sensitivity of Mamba. Our visualization experiments further confirmed that the nodes extracted through the topology-aware ordering (TAO) strategy indeed exhibit higher similarity. Furthermore, we designed a bidirectional Mamba module and integrated a Graph Convolutional Network (GCN) to achieve bidirectional spatial context modeling of images, forming a hierarchical feature learning architecture for "local aggregation - global capture." This framework effectively reconciles the contradiction between long-range dependency modeling, computational efficiency, and spatial structure utilization in WSIs analysis through its systematic design of TAO, bidirectional semantic modeling, and hierarchical feature fusion. This framework has been validated for its comprehensive performance advantage on five TCGA datasets.

</details>


### [184] [Auditable Climate Risk Intelligence from Fragmented ESG Data: Deterministic Orchestration and Imbalance-Aware Learning for Scope 1-3 Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02604)
*Karan Sehgal,Khawar Naveed Bhatti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对当前ESG数据验证中缺乏审计性与可重复性的问题，提出了一种基于确定性可追溯性、异常检测及集成学习的气候风险智能治理框架，并提供了一个新的合成基准以支持行业透明化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前ESG与气候风险数据在不同报告标准下高度分散；且现有的验证流程缺乏溯源审计能力、隐含漂移检测及可重复性治理机制。

Method: 提出了一种集成“单一真理来源（SSOT）”编排、时间异常检测、对平衡敏感的集成学习以及面向可解释性治理的框架；构建了基于GHG Protocol、PCAF和ISSB标准的合成ESG基准；采用时间漂移分析、SMOTE过采样、TreeSHAP解释性分析及 provenance-aware（溯源感知）编排技术。

Result: 框架在召回率、F1分数、ROC AUC、校准指标（ECE, Brier score）以及审计跟踪完整性指标上表现优异，并通过了五折交叉验证与显著性检验。

Conclusion: 该框架将ESG报告重塑为确定性的气候风险治理基础设施，有效支持了ESG数据的可重复性、可解释性和操作可审计性。

Abstract: ESG and climate risk data remain fragmented across heterogeneous Scope 1, Scope 2, and Scope 3 reporting environments, while conventional validation pipelines lack provenance aware auditability, hidden drift detection, and reproducibility oriented governance. This paper proposes a deterministic climate risk intelligence framework integrating single source of truth orchestration, temporal anomaly detection, imbalance aware ensemble learning, and explainability oriented governance for auditable ESG validation.
