To support open reproducibility, we construct and release a synthetic ESG validation benchmark calibrated against publicly reported characteristics of the GHG Protocol, PCAF, and ISSB standards. The methodology incorporates temporal drift analysis, SMOTE based rare event optimization, ensemble learning, provenance aware orchestration, and TreeSHAP based interpretability for governance inspection and audit reconstruction. We evaluate the framework against statistical classifiers, anomaly detection methods, temporal forecasting baselines, and a threshold based system using classification metrics (recall, F1, ROC AUC), calibration metrics (ECE, Brier score), and a governance oriented audit trace completeness metric measuring the fraction of flagged anomalies for which a deterministic source to escalation provenance chain can be reconstructed. Results are reported as mean and standard deviation across stratified five fold cross validation with paired significance testing. The framework reframes ESG reporting toward deterministic climate risk governance infrastructure supporting reproducibility, explainability, and operational auditability.

</details>


### [185] [Cross-Modal Contrastive Learning of ECG and Angiography Representations for Severe Stenosis Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02605)
*Nikola Cenikj,Özgün Turgut,Alexander Müller,Alexander Steger,Jan Kehrer,Marcus Brugger,Daniel Rueckert,Philip Müller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 StenCE 预训练框架，通过深度学习分析心电图，有效实现了冠状动脉狭窄的早期诊断和严重性分层，克服了现有心电分析难以识别狭窄特征的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冠状动脉造影是诊断金标准但具有侵入性且资源昂贵，而心电图（ECG）虽普及且廉价，但目前缺乏公认的、可用于狭窄风险分层的特定信号，导致部分患者错过早期诊断。

Method: 提出名为 StenCE 的 ECG 预训练框架，通过学习 ECG 数据中的特征来对患者进行狭窄风险分层，并验证了该框架在不同 ECG 编码器上的通用性与有效性。

Result: 在不同狭窄严重程度阈值和其他心电疾病分类任务上，StenCE 表现均优于现有工作，并首次实现了在严重狭窄分类上的高性能检出。

Conclusion: StenCE 框架成功从心电图（ECG）中提取出与冠状动脉狭窄相关的特征，为无症状患者的早期诊断和风险分层提供了可靠的非侵入式工具，并在严重狭窄分类任务上达到了行业领先水平。

Abstract: Coronary artery stenosis is a common cardiovascular disease, with severe, untreated cases posing significant risks of heart attack. Although coronary (X-ray) angiograms remain the standard for stenosis diagnosis, they are invasive, time- and resource-intensive, and therefore only performed on patients with a high probability of disease based on symptoms and prior clinical tests. However, a subset of patients, especially those without symptoms, may remain undiagnosed. Detecting indications of stenosis from ECGs, which are fast, cheap, non-invasive, and thus routinely acquired even in asymptomatic patients, would support early diagnosis. However, as no reliable stenosis-specific signal has been identified in ECGs, they can not currently be used for stenosis risk stratification. To address this, we introduce StenCE, a pretraining framework, allowing stratification of patients based on features derived directly from ECGs. Evaluations across varying stenosis severity thresholds and additional ECG disease classification tasks demonstrate consistent performance improvements across different ECG encoders, outperforming previous work. The obtained models successfully detect signals for stenosis diagnosis in ECGs and are the first to achieve high performance in severe stenosis classification. The source code is available at https://github.com/NikolaCenic/ecg-stenosis-cls.

</details>


### [186] [MOSAIC: Efficient Mixture-of-Agent Scheduling via Adaptive Aggregation and Inference Concurrency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03014)
*Saptarshi Mitra,Yifan Zhang,Rachid Karami,Phyo Pyae Moe Aung,Nazmul Takbir,Sreetama Sarkar,Souvik Kundu,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MOSAIC 是一个专门针对多智能体（MoA）架构的调度与执行框架，通过ILP优化任务布局并引入自适应聚合机制，解决了MoA系统中资源不均与聚合开销大的问题，大幅提升了推理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体（MoA）系统在有限GPU资源下存在显著的性能瓶颈。由于技能导向的路由机制导致专家负载极度倾斜，且指令微调模型与长推理模型生成长度差异巨大，传统调度策略会导致严重的GPU闲置及吞吐量崩溃。

Method: 1. 基于整数线性规划（ILP）的调度器：利用离线配置文件信息，综合优化专家模型布局与Prompt任务分配，支持轻量级专家固定与重型推理专家的副本扩展；2. 置信度感知自适应聚合：通过评估专家间的一致性，在达成共识时跳过计算资源消耗极高的终结聚合模型。

Result: 在4-GPU系统中，与基准调度器相比，MOSAIC在专家阶段实现了最高2.5倍加速，聚合阶段实现了4.23倍加速，端到端速度提升1.7~2.3倍，同时精度偏差控制在0.1pp以内。

Conclusion: MOSAIC 通过优化任务调度与自适应聚合，在保证模型推理准确度几乎不变的前提下，显著提升了多智能体（MoA）系统的端到端吞吐量与执行效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Agents (MoA) systems improve reasoning accuracy by routing each query to multiple expert LLMs and aggregating their outputs. Efficiently executing this workload on limited GPU resources has bottlenecks. Skill-based routing creates skewed expert demand, and combining instruction-tuned LLMs with long-reasoning models results in extreme variability in generation lengths. Consequently, traditional scheduling strategies suffer from significant GPU idling and throughput collapse due to load imbalances. We present MOSAIC, a scheduling framework to accelerate MoA workloads. First, we formulate an Integer Linear Program (ILP) based scheduler that jointly optimizes expert placement and per-worker prompt assignment from offline-profiled costs, replicating reasoning experts across workers while pinning lightweight ones. Second, MOSAIC uses confidence-aware adaptive aggregation, leveraging inter-expert agreement to bypass the heavy final aggregator LLM for consensus queries. In our 4-GPU system, MOSAIC achieves up to 2.5x expert-stage, 4.23x aggregator-stage and 1.7~2.3x end-to-end speedups over the baseline scheduler, while matching accuracy within 0.1pp.

</details>


### [187] [ReLoRA: Knowledge-Reusing Adaptation for Fast Rollout of Evolving LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02606)
*Yang Xu,Zihuai Xu,Hongli Xu,Yunming Liao,Zhiwei Yao,Xitong Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型更新导致 LoRA 适配器失效的问题，ReLoRA 引入知识重用框架，通过自适应初始化与分阶段微调，实现了快速且高质量的适配器更新，有效降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务频繁更新导致旧的下游 LoRA 适配器失效。从零重训成本过高，而直接复用旧适配器又存在兼容性问题导致性能下降，需要一种低成本、高性能的适配器恢复机制。

Method: 提出 ReLoRA 框架，包含两个核心步骤：1) 基于贝叶斯优化的自适应 LoRA 初始化，融合旧适配器与基座模型的演进信息；2) 分阶段的正则化微调，先快速定位高质量区域，再进行任务特定精细化。

Result: 对比基准实验显示，ReLoRA 将重适配的就绪时间（time-to-readiness）缩短了 8.9 倍，任务准确率最高提升了 4.6%。

Conclusion: ReLoRA 是一种高效的知识重用重适配框架，通过自适应初始化和分阶段正则化微调，显著降低了 LLM 更新后的适配成本，同时提升了下游适配器的性能与部署效率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as continuously evolving services, where frequent base-model updates may invalidate previously deployed task-specific Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters. For service providers managing numerous downstream model services, retraining each LoRA adapter from scratch for every updated base model is computationally prohibitive and delays service rollout. Meanwhile, the simpler alternative, i.e., naively applying the original LoRA adapter to the updated base model, often leads to degraded service quality due to adapter-backbone incompatibility. To address this problem, we propose ReLoRA, a knowledge-reusing re-adaptation framework that efficiently restores service-ready LoRA adapters for evolving LLM services while preserving or improving task performance. Specifically, ReLoRA comprises two key optimization steps: 1) Adaptive LoRA initialization leverages Bayesian optimization to construct a compatibility-aware starting point by fusing information from both the previously deployed task adapter and the base model's evolution; 2) Fine-tuning with scheduled regularization first rapidly steers the adapter to a high-quality region via strong regularization, followed by relaxed regularization for task-specific refinement. This design enables rapid service-quality recovery with reduced re-adaptation overhead. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReLoRA reduces time-to-readiness by up to 8.9$\times$ and improves accuracy by up to 4.6\% compared to baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02607)
*David Turtora Zagardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GATD通过在扩散模型中显式引入列间的几何几何关系（角度与距离）作为辅助监督，在大幅降低参数量的前提下，显著提升了表格数据的生成质量和下游任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的表格扩散模型主要依赖隐式机制来捕捉列间关系，缺乏对表格数据内在几何结构（inter-column relationships）的直接建模。

Method: 提出了几何感知表格扩散模型（GATD）。在表格去噪器中引入了由列值差异计算出的成对角度（pairwise angles）和长度（lengths）作为输入及辅助学习目标，从而显式建模列间的几何关系。

Result: GATD在平均参数量减少3.5倍（分类任务最高达25倍）的情况下，在10个数据集上实现了SOTA性能。在Shape、Trend指标及下游任务（F1/RMSE）上均有显著提升（Shape/Trend误差分别降低27%和20%）。此外，该方法具有跨架构的迁移性（GNN/Transformer）。消融实验证实性能提升源于关系监督而非参数容量增加。

Conclusion: 显式的关系监督（Pairwise的角度和长度）是提升表格扩散模型性能的通用且有效的归纳偏置。通过引入此几何感知机制，模型可以用更少的参数量实现更优的生成质量和下游任务效用。

Abstract: Tabular synthesis is critical for privacy-preserving sharing and augmentation, yet diffusion models rely on implicit mechanisms to capture inter-column relationships. We introduce Geometry-Aware Tabular Diffusion (GATD), which augments tabular diffusion denoisers with pairwise angles and lengths computed from column value differences and used as inputs and auxiliary targets. Our MLP instantiation achieves state-of-the-art benchmark performance while using 3.5x fewer parameters on average (up to 25x for classification tasks): on ten datasets, it wins 8/10 Shape, 7/10 Trend, and 9/10 downstream utility (F1/RMSE), reducing Shape and Trend error by 27% and 20%. Default loss weights transfer to GNN and Transformer denoisers, improving Shape on 27/30 and Trend on 25/30 architecture-dataset cells. A matched ablation shows supervision (not extra inputs or capacity) drives the gain. This shows explicit relational supervision is a portable inductive bias for tabular diffusion.

</details>


### [189] [Pruning Deep Neural Networks via the Marchenko--Pastur Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02608)
*Leonid Berlyand,Theo Bourdais,Houman Owhad,Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机矩阵理论的剪枝框架，通过理论确定的剪枝证书，在极短的微调时间内高效压缩模型并保持高精度，显著提升了视觉Transformer与卷积神经网络的实际推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深层神经网络剪枝过程中需要长周期微调和重优化的问题，旨在实现低微调预算下的高性能模型压缩。

Method: 基于Marchenko-Pastur（MP）随机矩阵理论的剪枝机制；引入确定性数据路径证书（deterministic data-path certificates）以评估剪枝影响；结合剪枝-恢复（prune-restore）模型及L2正则化，通过拟合MP边缘信号确定剪枝预算。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k数据集上，ViT和CNN模型在保持高精度的同时实现了显著的MAC计算冗余降低（如ViT-B/16实现近60%的MAC减少），并在多种硬件平台上获得了可观的推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过Marchenko-Pastur定律提供的确定性数据路径证书，该方法能够在极低微调预算（甚至零预算）下实现深层神经网络的高效剪枝，同时在保持模型精度的前提下显著提升计算推理速度。

Abstract: We study a Marchenko--Pastur (MP) random-matrix approach to pruning deep neural networks with very small post-pruning fine-tuning budgets. The main practical contribution is accuracy retention under short calibration and fine-tuning schedules, rather than a long post-pruning reoptimization pipeline. The theory gives deterministic data-path certificates: if the removed component $R$ has small propagated logit effect $L_s \| R ψ_1(s) \|_\infty$, pruning decreases an elastic-net objective and preserves samples whose dense margin exceeds twice the perturbation. The zero-budget case gives perfect pruning; a prune--restore extension models weight restoration inside a fixed sparse-execution pattern; and an additive $L_2$-regularized model shows admissible random-like components vanish at the training limit, with persistent spikes stabilizing as the MP bulk collapses. Under iid-Gaussian sufficient conditions, the fitted MP edge $σ_+$ gives a high-probability layerwise budget signal.
  On ImageNet-1k, after only three distillation epochs, ViT-B/16 $2{:}4{+}$ToMe reaches $83.41\%$ top-1 ($-1.70$ pp from dense) at $59.81\%$ sparse-execution MAC reduction, with $1.388\times$ best-observed A40 native-$2{:}4$ backend speedup for the same checkpoint and ToMe graph; a separate no-ToMe A100 endpoint gives $2.705\times$. At structured sparsity, ViT-B/16 $6{:}12$ reaches $83.74\%$, ViT-L/16 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $85.33\%$ ($-0.51$ pp), and ConvNeXtV2-Base $12{:}16$ reaches $86.35\%$ ($-0.37$ pp). For CNNs, ResNet50 $8{:}16$ dense+permutation reaches $75.87\%$ ($-0.26$ pp), and ResNet152d CAST-conv+permutation reaches $81.33\%$ ($-1.53$ pp) at ${\sim}50\%$ MAC accounting with a $1.62\times$ A40 im2col$+2{:}4$ sparse-GEMM audit.

</details>


### [190] [Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03077)
*Kaiwen Chen,Xin Tan,Jingzong Li,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Libra 是一种针对智能体大模型强化学习的弹性资源管理系统，通过全局资源调度和因果驱动的反馈调度，解决了传统架构在处理复杂长尾轨迹和非平稳负载时的效率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体强化学习（Agentic RL）中，传统的资源管理策略难以应对长尾轨迹分布、训练与推理阶段的计算负载不对称，以及RL策略进化导致的轨迹长度动态分布漂移。

Method: 1. 基于全局资源规划器的弹性混合资源池，支持训练与推理（rollout）阶段的动态工作负载重定向。2. 引入基于因果反馈的多级队列调度算法（C-MLFQ），根据工具运行结果的因果信号动态调整任务调度，替代了基于长度预测的传统调度方式。

Result: 在48片A800 GPU集群上实验表明，Libra实现了高达3.0倍的吞吐量提升，奖励收敛速度加快了2.5倍。

Conclusion: Libra 通过引入弹性资源规划和基于因果反馈的调度机制，有效解决了智能体强化学习中计算资源分配不均和动态分布漂移的问题，显著提升了大模型强化学习的吞吐量与收敛速度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training paradigm for large language models (LLMs), extending beyond preference alignment to complex reasoning and multi-turn agentic behaviors. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that challenge conventional resource-management assumptions. Three fundamental challenges arise. First, due to the long-tail distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan. Second, rollout and training exhibit strong asymmetry in compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. Third, as the RL policy evolves, the trajectory-length distribution drifts over time, rendering any static resource split progressively suboptimal.
  We present Libra, which introduces two core mechanisms. The first is a periodic global resource planner that jointly optimizes GPU allocation across rollout and training clusters. It leverages an elastic hybrid pool to enable lightweight, non-blocking worker reallocation between stages. The second is a causality-driven multi-level feedback queue (C-MLFQ) scheduler, which routes requests to heterogeneous rollout buckets based on causal signals derived from tool-return outcomes, rather than relying on fragile length predictions. Evaluated on 48 A800 GPUs, Libra achieves up to 3.0$\times$ higher throughput and converges up to 2.5$\times$ faster in reward compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [191] [Building Better Activation Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02609)
*Jan Bauer,Celeste De Schamphelaere,Adam Karvonen,Niclas Luick,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有激活预言机（AO）的缺陷，本文提出了改进的训练框架及首个评估基准 AObench，旨在提升模型可解释性研究的质量与评估标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活预言机（AO）在解释残差流激活时存在幻觉、模糊性以及文本反转（text-inversion）混淆等严重问题，且缺乏有效的评估手段。

Method: 1. 改进训练机制：采用策略内（on-policy）采样、优化对话数据集、增加输入层数以及优化注入公式；2. 发布评估框架：推出专门用于评估 AO 质量的综合套件 AObench。

Result: 尽管模型在解释能力指标上的提升较小，但在模型质量和实用性方面有显著改进；同时成功建立了首个全面的 AO 质量评估基准。

Conclusion: 该研究通过改进“激活预言机”（AO）的训练机制，并发布了首个评估套件 AObench，为可扩展的端到端可解释性研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Activation Oracles (AOs) are promising methods for interpreting residual stream activations. However, current AOs face important issues, such as hallucinations and vagueness. Additionally, text-inversion confounds make them hard to evaluate. To this end, we improve the Activation Oracle (AO) training regime in four ways: training on on-policy rollouts, improving the conversational dataset, feeding more layers and an improvement to the injection formula. The capability improvements are marginal, but quality of life improvements are quite substantial. In addition, we open source the first comprehensive evaluation suite for AO quality, which we call AObench. Overall, we hope that our work sets a foundation that helps improve AOs and other models in the paradigm of scalable, end-to-end interpretability.

</details>


### [192] [Demystifying Pipeline Parallelism: First Theory for PipeDream](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03498)
*Ivan Ilin,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入随机化 PipeDream (RPD) 理论模型，首次为 PipeDream 风格的流水线并行提供了非凸收敛保证，并定量分析了其随阶段数扩展的延迟代价，与 LocalSGD 进行了对比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，分布式计算需求增加，虽然数据并行和张量并行是主流，但面对超大规模参数、激活值或优化器状态时，流水线（Pipeline）并行成为必然。该研究旨在从理论上分析 PipeDream 风格方法的收敛性及扩展性限制。

Method: 引入了“随机化 PipeDream (RPD)”作为陈旧块随机梯度下降（Stale Block-SGD）的抽象，提供了非凸收敛性保证；通过理论证明分析了 Pipeline 并行中的延迟尺度（Scaling Diagnosis）；通过模拟实验对比了 PipeDream 与 LocalSGD 的性能。

Result: 推导出了 PipeDream 在 $S$ 个阶段下的延迟增长量级为 $Θ(S^4/K)$，揭示了其在扩展性上的瓶颈，并对比了其与 LocalSGD 在不同任务下的表现差异。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不同并行策略的优劣取决于具体的目标函数：PipeDream 在二次目标函数和小型语言模型任务上表现更佳，而随着并行阶段数的增加，LocalSGD 在逻辑回归任务中更具优势。

Abstract: Training modern machine learning models increasingly requires computation to be distributed across many accelerators. Data parallelism remains the default choice and is often paired with tensor-parallel sharding, but model parallelism becomes unavoidable once parameters, activations, or optimizer states no longer fit on a single device. This paper studies pipeline model parallelism through the lens of PipeDream (PD) (Harlap et al., 2018). Our first contribution is theoretical: we introduce Randomized PipeDream (RPD), a stale block-SGD abstraction that yields, to our knowledge, the first clean nonconvex convergence guarantee for a PD-style method. Our second contribution is a scaling diagnosis: we prove that the delay induced by steady-state PD grows as $S^2 - S/2 + O(1)$ for $S$ stages, so the stale-read contribution in the convergence theorem scales as $Θ(γ^2 S^4)$, equivalently as $Θ(S^4/K)$ in the tuned-rate form. Our third contribution is a comparison with LocalSGD, whose periodic model averaging trades weight staleness for synchronization bubbles. In our reported simulated-time experiments, the better-performing method depends on the objective: PD performs better on the quadratic objective and on a small language-modeling training-loss task, while for logistic regression LocalSGD becomes superior as the number of stages increases.

</details>


### [193] [Hallucination Is Linearly Decodable from Mid-Layer Hidden States in Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02628)
*Aizierjiang Aiersilan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM的隐藏层中编码了可以直接被线性探测器提取的强大真相信号，其中层表现最佳；相比之下，传统的基于采样的检测手段在识别幻觉方面的效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究开源LLM的隐藏层中是否编码了线性可分的真相信号，以及该信号出现在网络的哪个深度。

Method: 使用7B-8B开源模型（Llama-3.1, Mistral, Qwen2.5），在四个幻觉基准测试上提取隐藏状态，对比了线性探测、MLP探测、INSIDE EigenScore、自洽性及注意力熵四种检测方法。

Result: 线性探测器在模型中层取得极高准确率（0.904–1.000 AUROC），而传统的基于采样的检测方法效果显著较差（≤0.541 AUROC）；真相信号表现出极强的线性特征。

Conclusion: LLM的隐藏层中确实存在高度可线性分离的真相信号，且该信号在网络中层最为显著，效果远优于基于采样的方法。此外，首层注意力熵在特定场景下可作为有效的补充信号。

Abstract: We investigate whether open-source LLMs encode a linearly separable truthfulness signal in their hidden states, and at which network depth this signal is strongest. Across three $7$B--$8$B instruction-tuned models (Llama-3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, Qwen2.5-7B) loaded in $4$-bit NF4 quantization, we extract per-layer hidden states on four hallucination benchmarks (TruthfulQA, HaluEval-QA, FEVER, and a controlled synthetic set) and compare four detection approaches: linear and MLP probes, INSIDE EigenScore, self-consistency, and attention entropy. A linear probe on a single mid-network layer achieves $0.904$--$1.000$ AUROC on held-out splits, while sampling-based detectors do not exceed $0.541$ AUROC under the same protocol. The truthfulness signal is approximately linear: MLP probes rarely surpass linear probes by more than $0.01$ AUROC. Peak probing layers fall in a consistent band across model families on natural-language benchmarks -- blocks~$13$--$18$ of~$32$ for Llama and Mistral, and blocks~$19$--$25$ of~$28$ for Qwen. First-block attention entropy provides a complementary signal in knowledge-grounded settings ($0.866$--$0.941$ AUROC on HaluEval-QA) at no additional inference cost. The low discriminability of sampling methods under this protocol reflects a structural mismatch between paired-label evaluation and the information these methods access, rather than an inherent limitation of those methods. Code and data are released for full reproducibility on a single $8$\,GB GPU.

</details>


### [194] [Regime-Arrival Uncertainty in Generalization Bounds under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02657)
*Prince Poudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种处理马尔可夫切换分布偏移下的泛化界框架，将因“体制组成”不匹配产生的额外风险与模型对体制的敏感性分离开来，并证明了该框架在金融市场分析中对危机特征识别的有效性，同时指出了其在预测未来体制切换时间点上的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准泛化界通常假设训练和部署的分布一致或静态，未能处理训练集与测试集在“冷静”和“危机”等不同经济体制状态下比例不一致的问题。因此，需要专门针对这种体制切换环境下的分布偏移提出分析框架。

Method: 1. 提出一个泛化模型框架，通过马尔可夫切换机制量化体制组成不匹配带来的额外风险；2. 实现了对泛化误差的精确分解（体制不匹配与体制敏感性分离）；3. 利用谱间隙（spectral gap）修正有效样本量，将泛化界扩展至贝塔混合（beta-mixing）数据；4. 通过合成数据及25年全球股票指数数据进行实证，并推导了极大极小下界。

Result: 提出了一个新的泛化误差惩罚项，该项与事后实现的泛化缺口（realized generalization gap）高度相关，而传统的仅基于训练集的估计器则表现不佳。实验显示，机器学习模型能够捕捉危机的特征几何结构，但无法预测危机的时间到达点。

Conclusion: 该框架成功量化了由体制（regime）分布不匹配带来的额外风险，证明了体制敏感性与由于体制组成差异造成的泛化损失是可以分离的。研究指出，该模型无法预测未来体制的到来，强调了预测体制组成在极端或稀有体制转换情况下的局限性。

Abstract: The standard generalization bounds assume that the training and deployment distributions are the same, or are static, and don't consider regime switching environments where the ratio of calm vs crisis states is different. This paper proposes a framework that generalizes regime-aware models by quantifying the extra risk due to regime composition mismatch, when distribution shifts are Markov-switching. We obtain an exact decomposition, separating regime mismatch from regime sensitivity; we extend the bound to beta-mixing data using the effective sample size corrected for the spectral gap; and we show a minimax lower bound for synthetic data and on 25 years of global equity indices. The proposed penalty is an ex post realized generalization gap, whereas the training-only estimator does not show significant correlation: the feature geometry of crises can be detected, but not the temporal arrival. Thus, the framework is not a forecast machine. Forecasting the composition of the future regime is an open question in the rare cases of regime change.

</details>


### [195] [CL-DMDF:Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion Model Based on Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02659)
*Dong Li,Lingling Zhang,Binghao Han,Linlin Ding,Yue Kou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CL-DMDF的动态多模态数据融合模型，专门解决模态缺失下的全局特征学习问题，通过引入双维度注意力、质心对比学习及自适应融合策略，显著提升了多模态数据的分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型大多针对特定任务且假设模态完整，难以处理现实中常见的模态缺失或不确定性问题；此外，现有模型往往过度关注局部交互，忽略了多模态表示中的全局互补线索。

Method: 1. 跨维度注意力机制：同时在特征和模态维度计算注意力权重；2. 实体质心对比学习（Entity-Centroid Contrastive Learning）：通过构建质心正样本增强特征的判别性；3. 自适应融合模块：实现动态融合策略以提升决策效率与准确性。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验结果表明，CL-DMDF在处理多模态融合任务时表现出更高的准确性和稳健性。

Conclusion: CL-DMDF通过创新的注意力机制、实体质心对比学习模块和自适应融合模块，有效解决了多模态数据缺失和不确定性问题，在多模态融合任务中展现了优越的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal data fusion involves integrating and analyzing information from multiple modalities to uncover latent correlations and complementary patterns, thereby enhancing data processing and decision-making. While existing methods for structured multimodal inputs are typically designed around specific tasks and assume fully observed modalities, real-world applications often suffer from uncertain or missing modality inputs due to various factors. Some traditional models overly emphasize local interactions within missing modalities, neglecting the global complementary cues embedded in multimodal representations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a Dynamic Multimodal Data Fusion model based on Contrastive Learning (CL-DMDF). CL-DMDF introduces a novel attention mechanism that operates across both feature and modality dimensions to compute reliable attention scores, effectively reflecting importance at each level. The CL-DMDF further incorporates an entity-centroid contrastive learning module that constructs centroid-based positive samples from entity features to enhance discriminative learning. Additionally, an adaptive fusion module is employed to improve the efficiency and accuracy of dynamic fusion strategies. Extensive experiments conducted on three datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the CL-DMDF across diverse multimodal fusion tasks.

</details>


### [196] [Neural Networks Provably Learn Spectral Representations for Group Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02993)
*Jianliang He,Leda Wang,Fengzhuo Zhang,Siyu Chen,Zhuoran Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过群论和傅里叶分析，揭示了神经网络在群合成任务中通过收敛于特定的不可约表示来实现特征学习与低秩压缩，并证明了其指数级的收敛特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络在训练过程中如何自发形成结构化内部表征，以及这些表征如何通过群论视角得到量化解释。

Method: 将投影梯度流提升至傅里叶域，分析其在表示论能量泛函上的黎曼梯度上升动力学；利用随机初始化下的表示竞争与相位对齐机理进行数学证明。

Result: 证明了神经元在训练中几乎必然收敛于单个不可约表示；发现了矩阵值群表示的低秩压缩现象；揭示了阿贝尔群下通过随机初始化促进多样化并诱导Haar均匀相位以近似指示函数的动态过程。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个群表示论框架，证明了神经网络在训练过程中通过向特定的不可约表示收敛，实现特征学习与低秩压缩。对于阿贝尔群，网络通过“多数投票”机制近似指示函数，并表现出指数级的收敛速度。

Abstract: Understanding how structured internal structure emerges during neural network training is central to the study of deep learning. We investigate this phenomenon through the group composition task, where a two-layer neural network is trained to predict $g_1 \star g_2$ for elements of a finite group $G$. By lifting the projected gradient flow to the Fourier domain, we demonstrate that the training dynamics are governed by a Riemannian gradient ascent on a representation-theoretic energy functional. We prove that, under random initialization, this flow drives each neuron to converge almost surely toward a single irreducible representation, while the cross-layer Fourier coefficients achieve a rotational rank-one alignment. This framework provides a representation-theoretic account of feature learning and characterizes a novel low-rank compression phenomenon for matrix-valued group representations. Moreover, for Abelian groups, we provide a complete population-level description: random initialization promotes uniform diversification across nontrivial representations and induces Haar-uniform phases, jointly approximating the indicator via a majority-vote mechanism. We further prove that both phase alignment and representation competition emerge with exponential convergence rates.

</details>


### [197] [Improvise, Adapt, Overcome: An On-The-Fly Multifidelity Algorithm for Efficient Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02662)
*Vivin Vinod,Peter Zaspel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对量子化学中机器学习训练数据昂贵且冗余的问题，提出了一种自适应多保真度学习框架，通过实时动态决策不同保真度数据的采样，显著降低了数据生成成本并提升了训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在量子化学领域应用广泛，但高精度训练数据的生成成本极高。现有的多保真度机器学习（MFML）方案依赖预定义的缩放因子，常导致数据冗余和效率低下。因此，需要一种能自动优化数据比例的机制。

Method: 开发了一种自适应“实时”（on-the-fly）多保真度机器学习框架。该方法通过动态查询不同保真度的训练样本，在提升模型精度时，先在低保真度下饱和模型性能，随后才逐步调用高昂的高保真度参考计算，从而自主决定训练数据集的组成。

Result: 数值实验表明，与单保真度方法相比，该自适应算法将数据生成成本降低了高达30倍；与标准MFML相比，效率提升了高达5倍。该方法在耦合簇能量（金标准）及激发能预测任务中均表现优异。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的自适应多保真度机器学习框架（Adaptive-MFML）能够通过消除数据冗余，为量子化学计算提供一种可持续且具有成本效益的高精度预测路径。

Abstract: Machine learning has accelerated quantum chemistry but is hindered by the prohibitive cost of generating high fidelity training data. Multifidelity machine learning (MFML) mitigates this overhead by systematically combining abundant low fidelity data with sparse high fidelity data. In spite of its success, standard MFML schemes rely on pre-defined scaling factors to determine sparse data ratio across fidelities, often generating redundant multifidelity data resulting in a loss of efficiency. Here, we introduce an adaptive on-the-fly multifidelity framework for machine learning that autonomously determines training dataset composition. By dynamically querying training samples at each fidelity, the algorithm saturates model accuracy at lower fidelities before moving up to more expensive reference calculations. We benchmark the novel adaptive-MFML across diverse chemical properties including the computational chemistry gold standard coupled cluster energies, and the more chemically challenging excitation energies. In our numerical experiments we show that our adaptive algorithm reduces data generation costs by up to a factor of 30 compared to single fidelity methods and improves upon standard MFML by up to a factor of 5. The mitigation of data redundancy establishes a high-accuracy low-cost pathway for sustainable cost-aware machine learning in quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [198] [AdaWeather: Adaptively Mixing Probabilistic Weather Forecasts with Logarithmic Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02663)
*Saptarishi Dhanuka,Sarvesh Iyer,Manmeet Singh,Mihir More,Rushil Gupta,Dhruman Gupta,Parthasarathi Mukhopadhyay,Sandeep Juneja*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有天气预报模型性能不均衡的问题，本文提出了 AdaWeather 框架，通过自适应加权机制整合多个预报输出，在提升预测精度的同时，在理论上实现了相对于最佳静态混合策略的对数遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚无单一的机器学习天气预报模型能在所有时空维度上保持领先，性能高度依赖上下文，因此需要一种能够整合多个预报源、具备自适应能力的鲁棒性方法。

Method: 提出 AdaWeather 框架，结合了机器学习技术与“专家启发式预测”（Prediction with Expert Advice）中的专家混合模型（Mixture of Experts），用于集成多个概率天气预报模型。

Result: 在温度预报实验中，AdaWeather 表现优于现有的单一预报方法和其他集成方法，且理论分析支持其具备优异的收敛性质。

Conclusion: AdaWeather 通过结合机器学习和专家混合算法，在天气预报任务中实现了更好的稳健性和预测性能，并在理论上证明了其相对于最佳静态专家组合具有对数遗憾界。

Abstract: Recent advances in machine learning have produced probabilistic weather forecasting models comparable to state-of-the-art numerical weather predictors. But no model consistently dominates spatio-temporally, and relative performance is highly context-dependent. This motivates adaptive methods for combining multiple forecasts to obtain improvements and robustness. While combined forecasts have been proposed in the literature, these are achieved either through supervised learning or through prediction with expert advice methods. We introduce AdaWeather, an adaptive framework that combines many probabilistic forecasts using both machine learning as well as mixture of experts to arrive at a unified improved probabilistic forecast. While traditional expert methods develop the regret bounds with respect to the best single expert in hindsight, we extend the algorithm and analysis to show our method has logarithmic regret compared to the best static mixture of experts in hindsight. Empirically, we focus on forecasting temperature, and observe improvements over existing methods.

</details>


### [199] [AugMask: Training Diffusion Models on Incomplete Tabular Data via Stochastic Augmentation and Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03347)
*Jungkyu Kim,Taeyoung Park,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AugMask 是一种为扩散模型设计的通用框架，通过将缺失值转化为条件上下文而非学习目标，实现了在存在缺失数据的表格集上的高效生成，显著优于现有特定处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于得分的扩散模型主干通常假设输入是完整的，难以直接应用于现实中普遍存在缺失值的表格数据。

Method: 1. 条件随机增强：利用轻量级辅助模型填补缺失值构建数值输入。 2. 分离条件与监督：将扩充后的缺失值作为“不确定的条件上下文”而非训练目标。 3. 掩码降噪监督：仅对观测到的坐标点应用降噪监督，并推导出 Rao-Blackwellized 目标函数，引入方差加权敏感度惩罚以降低模型对预测缺失值的不必要依赖。

Result: 在多种数据集和不同程度缺失的情况下，AugMask 能够有效适配现有的扩散模型，并在表格数据生成任务中超越了现有的专门处理缺失数据的基准模型。

Conclusion: AugMask 提供了一种通用的训练框架，能够使标准扩散模型在处理包含缺失值的表格数据时，表现优于专门针对缺失数据设计的基准模型，且具有良好的稳健性。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have emerged as prominent deep generative models; however, their application to tabular data remains challenging because their backbones assume fully specified inputs, whereas real-world tabular data often contain missing values. We propose AugMask, a plug-and-play training framework that adapts missing-unaware backbones to incomplete data by separating conditioning from supervision. AugMask 1) constructs numeric inputs via conditional stochastic augmentation using lightweight auxiliary models, and 2) applies denoising supervision only to observed coordinates. In effect, augmented missing entries serve as uncertain conditioning context rather than training targets. We connect this training rule to a Rao--Blackwellized objective and show that marginalizing missing entries yields a variance-weighted sensitivity penalty, discouraging over-reliance on uncertain completions. Across diverse datasets and missingness regimes, AugMask enables standard diffusion-based tabular generators to outperform specialized missing-aware baselines.

</details>


### [200] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，目前主流的MT-SAD基准数据集主要由单变量异常构成，缺乏有效的跨通道异常信号，导致现有的跨通道建模技术在这些数据集上无从体现其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MT-SAD模型大多基于“异常分布在多个通道”的隐含假设引入跨通道建模，但缺乏对该假设在公开基准数据集上的实证评估。

Method: 提出了一种分段诊断框架，将异常分类为“单变量偏差（univariate deviation）”和“跨通道相关性结构变化（cross-channel structure change）”。通过与合成数据（包含故意设计的仅跨通道异常）进行对比，验证方法的有效性，并对比了通道独立（CI）与通道相关（CD）模型在真实数据集上的表现。

Result: 实验表明，在8个主流基准数据集中，没有出现脱离单变量偏差的单纯跨通道异常；且在6个数据集中，大部分异常段在几乎所有时间点都表现出明显的单变量异常。CI模型与CD模型在这些现实数据集上的表现无本质差异，证明了目前基准集主要由单变量特征驱动。

Conclusion: 现有的多元时间序列异常检测（MT-SAD）基准数据集无法有效评估跨通道建模能力。研究建议开发更具结构多样性的评估集，并指出目前SOTA模型在跨通道建模上并没有带来实际性能增益。

Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MT-SAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no crosschannel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 79% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channelwise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

</details>


### [201] [Analytical Evaluation of DCA Convergence Properties for Minimizing Prediction Functions of Gaussian RBF Support Vector Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03559)
*Yohei Kakimoto,Yuto Omae,Hirotaka Takahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为RBF核SVR模型的非凸优化问题构建了DCA算法框架，通过定义关键标量 $C_αρ$，实现了在训练前或训练后对算法收敛性能的定量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对RBF核SVR模型预测函数的非凸优化问题，现有研究缺乏对DCA算法收敛行为及其对模型超参数依赖性的深入定量分析。

Method: 利用RBF核的解析结构构造显式的DC（凸函数之差）分解；推导出强凸参数下界 $\mu$ 和梯度Lipschitz常数上界 $L$ 的闭式解。

Result: 提出了基于 $C_αρ$ 的评估框架，并通过数值实验证明 $C_αρ$ 是决定DCA收敛特性和初始点敏感性的核心变量，且受SVR超参数 $(C, γ)$ 的直接调控。

Conclusion: 文章证明了对于RBF核SVR的非凸优化问题，其DCA算法的收敛性质和初始点依赖性可以通过 $C_αρ$ 这一标量准确刻画。这为预估算法性能提供了理论基础。

Abstract: For nonconvex optimization problems whose objective is the prediction function of a trained Support Vector Regression (SVR) model with the Gaussian radial basis function (RBF) kernel (RBF-SVR), we present a framework that applies the difference of convex functions (DC) algorithm (DCA) by exploiting the analytical structure of the RBF kernel to construct an explicit DC decomposition. Specifically, we derive in closed form both the lower bound $μ$ of the strong convexity parameter of the DC components and the upper bound $L$ of the gradient Lipschitz constant of the subproblem. Both $μ$ and $L$ are determined solely by the post-training dual-coefficient sum $C_α$ and the RBF kernel parameter $γ$, together with the DC decomposition parameter $ρ$, and they share a common leading term $C_αρ$. Through numerical experiments on six benchmark functions, we show that $C_αρ$ is the primary single quantity characterizing both the convergence properties and the initial-point dependence of DCA, and further demonstrate that it decomposes into two independent pathways, $C \to C_α$ and $γ\to ρ$, with its primary variation governed by the SVR hyperparameters $(C, γ)$. Together, these results allow the convergence properties of DCA on RBF-SVR to be assessed in advance through the single scalar quantity $C_αρ$: approximately from $(C, γ)$ before training, and exactly in closed form after training.

</details>


### [202] [Aligning Data-Driven Predictors with Allocation: A Decision-Focused Approach to Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02671)
*Itai Zilberstein,Ioannis Anagnostides,Tuomas Sandholm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对器官分配中预测指标与决策目标不一致的问题，提出通过优化NDCG而非传统生存指标，显著提升了移植分配策略的临床效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出机器学习模型在实际决策任务（如器官分配）中，单纯优化统计指标（如C-index）会导致严重的策略失调，甚至出现性能不如随机选择的情况。核心问题在于模型训练指标与最终决策目标的脱节。

Method: 提出了一种决策导向的学习方法，通过自举法（bootstrapping）优化生存模型的NDCG指标，并解决了生存分析中常见的右侧删失（right censorship）数据排序评估难题。

Result: 在心脏移植历史数据上，该方法将Baseline模型的NDCG提升了50-100%，在模拟部署中每年可增加数万个生命年。证明了该方法在生存分析中能够提供可量化的决策表现保证。

Conclusion: 通过优化NDCG（归一化折让累积收益）替代传统的生存分析指标（如C-index），可以有效弥合生存预测与器官分配策略之间的鸿沟，显著提升分配效率并带来巨大的临床获益。此范式有望扩展至其他决策辅助领域。

Abstract: Machine learning predictors have become essential tools for guiding automated decision making. However, a major misalignment persists: predictive models are typically optimized in terms of standard statistical metrics in isolation from the algorithmic tasks they inform. We highlight this incongruity in the high-stakes domain of organ allocation by demonstrating that any algorithm relying on (even highly accurate) survival predictors optimized for standard metrics -- such as the Concordance index (C-index) -- can yield arbitrarily poor outcomes when used for allocation, failing to guarantee utility better than a uniform random selection. To bridge the gap between survival analysis and policy optimization, we introduce a decision-focused learning approach based on optimizing normalized discounted cumulative gain (NDCG), a mainstay metric in information retrieval. We establish the utility of NDCG in survival analysis by proving that it translates to guarantees on the performance of allocation. Empirically, we propose a bootstrapping approach to optimize the NDCG of existing survival models. Unlike prior work, we also address the challenge of right censorship when evaluating ranking. On historical heart transplant data from the US, our method dramatically boosts the NDCG of baseline models by 50-100%, which translates to tens of thousands of additional life years gained annually when deployed for transplant allocation. We anticipate that our framework will find broader applications in decision making with predictions.

</details>


### [203] [Conformal Language Modeling via Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03731)
*Nicolas Emmenegger,Theo X. Olausson,Armando Solar-Lezama,Chara Podimata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型幻觉问题，本文提出了一种将校准约束直接嵌入生成过程的方法，解决了事后过滤导致的连贯性受损问题，实现了风险控制与生成质量的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于共形预测（Conformal Prediction）的事后过滤方法将采样视为原子操作，导致过滤后的文本逻辑不连贯、不一致或概率分布异常，且无法优化生成内容的有用性。

Method: 提出一种针对条件序列生成的校准方法，通过在模型后验概率的近似分布中进行采样，将“校准区域”作为生成过程的约束条件，而非在生成后再进行事后（post-hoc）过滤。

Result: 在开放式传记生成和数学问题求解任务中，该方法不仅实现了与既有研究相同的统计风险控制，并在生成质量及实用性上表现更好。

Conclusion: 通过将校准过程整合进序列生成阶段，该方法在保持风险控制统计保证的同时，显著提升了生成内容的质量和下游实用性，克服了事后过滤导致的逻辑不连贯问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models remain plagued by hallucinations. Recent work has sought to tame their prevalence using statistical techniques based on conformal prediction, with both theoretical and empirical success. However, these methods operate in a post-hoc fashion, treating the sampling procedure itself as atomic and then surgically altering samples to remove hallucinated claims. This disconnect between filtering and generation can result in samples that are incoherent, inconsistent, or simply unlikely under the model itself. Moreover, post-hoc surgery is unable to shift probability mass towards more useful and helpful responses. To address these issues, we propose to instead sample from approximations to an LLM posterior, where the conditioning event corresponds to a calibrated, high-scoring region. We develop a calibration procedure tailored to the setting of conditional sequential generation that effectively identifies this region and achieves target risk control. Empirically, we apply our method to case studies focused on open-ended biography generation and mathematical problem solving; compared to prior work, we obtain the same statistical guarantees, with higher downstream utility.

</details>


### [204] [Before Fusion, Ask What to Keep: Contextual Calibration of Multimodal Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02679)
*Jiyuan Liu,Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Xinpei Wang,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态融合中常见的干扰与冲突问题，本文提出了一种即插即用的校准模块，通过在融合前对原始特征进行动态调节，有效过滤噪声并增强有效信号，从而显著提升了多模态学习的鲁棒性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态系统中模态间存在干扰（Distraction）、冲突（Conflict）或信息不一致，导致融合后的预测性能难以保证，亟需在融合前对模态表示进行动态调整。

Method: 提出了一种紧凑的校准模块（Calibration Module），通过在总结层面比较各模态，提取跨来源支持与冲突的线索，并将其转换为实例级和维度级的调节信号，应用于融合前的原始模态特征。

Result: 在情感分析、动作识别等五个基准任务上均实现了性能提升，并在模态缺失、噪声数据干扰及特征可视化分析中表现出更强的稳定性和优化效果。

Conclusion: 在融合前对模态特征进行校准（抑制干扰、增强有效特征）能显著提升多模态模型的鲁棒性与预测性能，且该模块具备良好的通用性与即插即用特性。

Abstract: Multimodal systems often benefit from combining information across language, sound, and visual streams, but this benefit is not guaranteed. A modality that is useful for one input may become distracting for another, and local feature responses within the same modality can disagree with evidence from other sources. This work investigates how to adjust multimodal representations before they are merged by a downstream predictor. We develop a compact calibration module that compares each modality with the others at the summary level, extracts cues of cross-source support and conflict, and converts these cues into instance-wise and dimension-wise modulation signals. The calibration is applied to the original modality features rather than to already fused representations, enabling the model to suppress misleading components, preserve weak but useful evidence, and emphasize responses that are better supported by the current multimodal context. The module is designed as a plug-in component and can be attached to different fusion backbones without changing their prediction heads. Across five benchmarks covering sentiment understanding, action recognition, audio-visual event detection, and audio-visual emotion classification, the proposed pre-combination calibration strategy improves performance under both sequence-based and convolutional fusion settings. Additional analyses under modality removal, synthetic corruption, training dynamics, and feature-level visualization show that calibrating signals before fusion can reduce interference from unreliable modalities and produce more stable multimodal optimization.

</details>


### [205] [Online Learning with Gradient-Variation Interval Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03831)
*Yan-Feng Xie,Shuche Wang,Peng Zhao,Zhi-Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对非平稳在线学习，提出了一种高效的两层集成算法，不仅实现了首个基于梯度变分的区间遗憾界，还通过创新的元算法实现了超参数的自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线学习算法在处理非平稳环境时，缺乏在任意时间区间内同时具备良好性能（即区间遗憾，interval regret）且能自适应各种问题依赖量（problem-dependent quantities）的算法，且在超参数调优方面存在困难。

Method: 1. 提出了一种两层在线集成结构（two-layer online ensemble structure）。
2. 开发了一种 Lipschitz 和平滑度无关（Lipschitz- and smoothness-agnostic）的元算法变体，用于自动适应超参数。

Result: 1. 实现了首个与梯度变分（gradient variation）相匹配的区间遗憾界。
2. 算法具有极强的自适应性，能在保证最差情况下的极小极大最优率（minimax-optimal rate）的同时，利用问题依赖量提升性能。
3. 提供了区间动态遗憾（interval dynamic regret）的通用界，并给出了随机扩展对抗优化的分段表征。

Conclusion: 作者提出了一种基于两层在线集成结构的方法，该方法在处理非平稳在线学习时表现优异，不仅在理论上实现了区间遗憾的梯度变分边界，还通过 Lipschitz 自适应元算法解决了超参数调优的挑战，并将其应用范围扩展至区间动态遗憾和随机扩展对抗优化。

Abstract: This paper investigates non-stationary online learning using the metric of interval regret, which requires an online algorithm to perform well over every time interval. We propose the first online learning algorithm that achieves an interval regret bound scaling with gradient variation, a fundamental measure of the cumulative change in online function gradients, which relates to various problem-dependent quantities and is closely connected to stochastic optimization and other problems. Our method employs a simple and efficient two-layer online ensemble structure that achieves strong theoretical guarantees. Specifically, it enjoys a regret bound that simultaneously adapts to various problem-dependent quantities while also preserving the minimax-optimal rate in the worst case. Moreover, recognizing the challenge of hyperparameter tuning, we introduce a Lipschitz- and smoothness-agnostic variant that automatically adapts to these potentially unknown constants. This is primarily enabled by a novel Lipschitz-adaptive meta algorithm, which may be of independent interest. Beyond interval regret, our method also yields broader implications: it provides versatile bounds for interval dynamic regret, a stronger measure that competes with changing comparators over any interval, and yields the first piecewise characterization for stochastic extended adversarial optimization. Theoretical findings are validated by experiments.

</details>


### [206] [Locality Does Not Imply Reachability: Boundary Repair in Block-Sparse Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02680)
*Zhibo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了固定块稀疏注意力机制在处理序列边界时存在的“可达性断裂”问题，并提出了边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention）进行修复，证实了该方法能有效提升模型跨块信息交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的稀疏因果注意力机制，作者注意到在固定块因果注意力中，由于序列局部性与图可达性之间存在错配，会导致相邻标记在注意力图中出现连接断裂（boundary artifact），从而限制模型处理跨块信息的能力。

Method: 1. 形式化定义“结构依赖集”，揭示了固定块因果掩码可能导致目标表示仅能依赖于自身块前缀的局限性；2. 导出“相位条件覆盖函数”（phase-conditioned coverage functions），量化了可达性与目标在块内偏移量的关系；3. 提出“边界桥接注意力”（Boundary Bridge Attention），通过共享投影增加辅助因果边以修复连接；4. 通过控制实验和Qwen2.5-7B探针实验对理论进行验证。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了在固定块掩码下，K路边界复制分布的准确率上限仅为1/K；2. 实验表明边界桥接注意力能显著改善模型在覆盖对齐诊断任务中的表现；3. 在Qwen2.5-7B模型上的测试证实了该覆盖不相容模式在超长序列中的客观存在。

Conclusion: 本文识别了块稀疏因果注意力机制中“序列局部性”与“注意力图可达性”之间的不匹配问题。通过提出边界桥接注意力（Boundary Bridge Attention），在不增加参数的前提下解决了块边界处的连通性断裂，从而有效提升了模型的推理与长序列处理能力。

Abstract: Sparse causal attention is usually described by sequence locality: nearby tokens should remain easy to access, while distant tokens may be dropped to reduce cost. This paper studies a mismatch between sequence locality and attention-graph reachability. In fixed block causal attention, two adjacent tokens can be disconnected in the attention graph at every depth. We formalize this boundary artifact through structural dependency sets: if every attention layer uses the same fixed block causal mask and all remaining operations are positionwise, a target representation can depend only on tokens in its own block prefix. This yields an architecture-level boundary-copy separation for a constructed K-way boundary-copy distribution, with top-1 accuracy upper bound 1/K and expected cross-entropy lower bound log K. We then derive phase-conditioned coverage functions showing that reachability depends on both source-target distance and the target's offset within its block. These coverage laws predict when a sparse pattern should fail, when a repair can help, and why sliding-window attention and boundary repair are not interchangeable. Boundary Bridge Attention is treated as a constructive witness: it preserves the fixed block path and adds zero-additional-parameter auxiliary causal edges near block boundaries using shared projections. Controlled 1024-token experiments show that gains concentrate in coverage-aligned diagnostics. As secondary external-validity evidence, a fixed-checkpoint 8K-token Qwen2.5-7B probe shows the same coverage-incomparability pattern. The contribution is a theory-guided diagnostic framework for locality-reachability mismatch in block-sparse causal attention, together with phase-conditioned coverage analysis and a minimal constructive repair.

</details>


### [207] [Filter, Then Reweight: Rethinking Optimization Granularity in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02684)
*Yuying Li,Leqi Zheng,Yongzi Yu,Wenrui Zhou,Xuchang Zhong,Xing Hu,Jing Jin,Huangjie Yuan,Tao Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FiRe-OPD是一种协同轨迹过滤与Token软重加权的LLM在线蒸馏（OPD）方法，通过由粗到细的信号调节实现了更稳定且高效的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的On-Policy蒸馏（OPD）方法正趋向于更具选择性的训练范式，但缺乏对优化粒度的协同考量。通过重新思考轨迹与Token层级的优化关系，旨在解决现有方法中信息丢失和优化不稳定问题。

Method: 提出FiRe-OPD框架，包含两个阶段：1. 轨迹过滤（Filter）：剔除低质量的采样轨迹；2. Token重加权（Reweight）：在保留的轨迹中通过软加权机制强调高信息量Token，而非简单的硬筛选。

Result: 在多个基准测试中表现卓越，例如在Strong-to-Weak设定下AIME 2024基准分提升6.25，在多教师设定下Miner基准分提升18.81。

Conclusion: FiRe-OPD通过在轨迹和Token两个层级进行精细化优化，有效减少了信息丢失并提升了训练稳定性，在多种设定（强弱、单/多教师）下均优于现有的Token级OPD方法。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) in large language models is shifting from full-trace KL supervision toward more selective training paradigms. Recent OPD methods increasingly focus on selecting which trajectories to learn from, which tokens are most informative, and which supervision signals are most reliable. Motivated by this trend, we rethink optimization granularity of OPD and propose \fireicon\ FiRe-OPD (Filter, then Reweight), which jointly adjusts supervision signals at both trajectory and token levels. In details, FiRe-OPD first filters trajectories to remove low-quality rollout samples, and then applies soft reweighting within the retained trajectories to emphasize informative tokens. Compared with hard token selection, FiRe-OPD leverages a soft-weighting mechanism to effectively mitigate information loss and enhance optimization stability, thereby achieving finer-grained OPD optimization. We validate the effectiveness of FiRe-OPD across strong-to-weak, single-teacher, and multi-teacher settings, and demonstrate its superiority over recent token-level OPD methods ( (e.g., +6.25 on AIME 2024 in strong-to-weak, +18.81 on Miner in multi-teacher). Our code is available at https://github.com/YuYingLi0/FiRe-OPD.

</details>


### [208] [$Ψ$-Bench: Evaluating Persona-Sensitive Influencing in Persuasive Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02754)
*Peixuan Han,Hongyi Du,Jiayu Liu,Yihang Sun,Yutong Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为 Ψ-Bench 的评估基准，旨在测试大语言模型在对话中主动说服不同个性化用户的能力，并证实引入用户画像可显著提升模型的说服效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化智能体研究多局限于被动响应，缺乏主动交互、引导和提供建议的能力，亟需一种评估大模型在现实场景中主动个性化影响力的方法。

Method: 提出了 Ψ-Bench 基准，包含三个真实的交互场景；利用基于对话历史生成的显性用户画像为模拟客户端赋予个性特征；评估了 10 个前沿大模型在这些场景下的说服表现。

Result: 即使是最先进的模型在主动说服任务中表现仍有不足；将模型接入客户端个人画像后，平均性能提升了 18.24%，验证了用户特定信息对提升说服力的有效性。

Conclusion: 构建了 Ψ-Bench 基准测试，证实了用户个性化信息对于提升大模型主动说服能力至关重要，并指出现有前沿模型在主动个性化交互方面仍有较大进步空间。

Abstract: Personalization is a crucial capability of modern language agents. However, current research primarily positions personalized agents as passive responders to user preferences, limiting their ability to interact with users and provide suggestions or guidance proactively. To systematically evaluate such proactive personalization in realistic interactions, we propose $Ψ$-Bench, a benchmark for assessing LLMs' ability to influence realistic users through conversation. We design three real-world interaction scenarios that involve persuasion in $Ψ$-Bench, and endow simulated clients with personal characteristics through explicit user profiles derived from dialogue histories. We evaluate 10 frontier LLMs on $Ψ$-Bench and find that while most models can produce coherent and reasonable arguments, even state-of-the-art models still leave considerable room for improvement in persuasion. We also find that providing access to client profiles yields an average performance gain of 18.24\%, highlighting the importance of user-specific information for effective persuasion. Overall, our work highlights persona-sensitive influencing as a challenging yet practical direction for evaluating and developing more proactive personalized LLM agents. Codes are available at: https://github.com/Hanpx20/Psi-Bench.

</details>


### [209] [Binary Road Surface Classification Using Machine Learning on Production Vehicle Signals During Cruising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02762)
*Vishal Hariharan,Salar Basiri,Kanwar Bharat Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统动力学方法在车辆巡航时难以估计路面附着力的问题，本文提出了一种基于机器学习的数据驱动框架，通过分析车载传感器数据成功实现了巡航状态下的道路工况分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的动力学递归估计算法（基于滑移斜率）在车辆巡航（无明显滑移）时，由于传感器精度限制无法捕获“微滑移”，导致路面附着力估计失效。因此，需要研究机器学习来挖掘巡航状态下车辆信号与路面状况之间的关联。

Method: 采用了两种方法：基于特征的框架（feature-based）与端到端的数据驱动框架（end-to-end data-driven）。通过滑动窗口技术处理轮速、轮矩、纵向加速度、转向角及横摆率等车辆信号，并输入机器学习模型进行二分类任务（高附着力/干燥或潮湿路面 vs 低附着力/积雪或结冰路面）。

Result: 在公开道路数据集上的验证结果表明，该数据驱动方法即使在车辆巡航期间也能准确识别道路表面状态，证明了其在轮胎及整车动力学领域中作为路面摩擦状态估计器的技术潜力。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的方法能够有效克服传统动力学估计器在巡航状态下因微滑移难以测量而失效的局限，为实现道路摩擦力与路面工况的实时估计提供了可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Knowledge of real-time road slipperiness, or even better, a refined estimate of peak grip potential, is a critical input for vehicle warning and intervention control systems. Typically, friction is estimated through dynamics-based recursive estimators by calculating the slip slope; however, its efficacy is heavily constrained by the vehicle dynamic scenario. When the vehicle is cruising and there is little to no slip, these methods become ineffective due to the inability of present-day production-grade sensors, such as wheel speed sensors, and methods to either measure or accurately estimate micro slip, which is crucial for distinguishing different surfaces. To address this challenge, the correlation between vehicle signals and road surface condition during cruising needs to be uncovered using machine learning. In this paper, a feature-based framework and an end-to-end data-driven framework are used to correlate the statistics of vehicle dynamics behavior with the condition of the road surface and perform binary classification into grip, dry or damp, and slip, snow or ice, conditions. A sliding-window approach is adopted to batch a short buffered window of wheel speeds, wheel torques, longitudinal acceleration, steering angle, and yaw rate, which are fed into a machine learning module for predicting the road state. Validation results on public-road data show scenarios where the data-driven method identifies the road surface correctly even during cruising, showing promise for accurate data-driven friction-related state estimators in the field of tire and vehicle dynamics.

</details>


### [210] [Representational Capacity: Geometric Limits on Feature Representation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02765)
*Alexander Guha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将嵌入矩阵的正交性作为代理，修正了描述Transformer潜在空间表征能力的容量公式，证明了模型规模与正交约束严苛程度之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管$d_{model}$是Transformer的核心超参数，但其在特征表征的几何极限中的角色尚未得到充分探索，研究旨在探究模型潜在空间中能支持多少特征方向。

Method: 1. 将嵌入矩阵（embedding matrix）作为近似正交约束的度量指标；2. 通过pairwise cosine similarity分布的边界识别偏差$\varepsilon$；3. 对Johnson-Lindenstrauss引理进行改进，推导出依赖于向量与维度比率（$k/d$）的调整后容量公式。

Result: 1. 将模型分为高$\varepsilon$（缺乏近正交结构）和低$\varepsilon$（保持近正交结构）两类；2. 修正后的容量公式将预测误差降低了两个数量级；3. 发现表征容量对$\varepsilon$具有指数级敏感性。

Conclusion: 模型的表征容量受限于其对特征正交性保持的严格程度（$\varepsilon$），且较大规模的模型倾向于追求更严格的正交约束而非单纯的容量最大化。该研究定义的容量公式能更精准地预测模型在潜在空间中可支持的特征数量。

Abstract: Model dimension ($d_{model}$) is a fundamental hyperparameter in transformer language models, yet its role in setting the geometric limits of feature representation remains under-explored. Grounded in the Linear Representation and Superposition Hypotheses - which propose that models encode features as near-orthogonal directions in latent space - we develop a framework for estimating how many such directions a model can support. We first establish the embedding matrix as a measurable proxy for near-orthogonality constraints across the latent space: the boundary between meaningful token relationships and incidental similarity in the pairwise cosine similarity distribution gives a concrete estimate of the model's accepted deviation $\varepsilon$ from perfect orthogonality. Applying this metric across dozens of open-source models reveals two classes: models with high $\varepsilon$ whose embeddings lack near-orthogonal structure, and models with low $\varepsilon$ that maintain it. We then show that the standard Johnson-Lindenstrauss lemma greatly underestimates the packing efficiency of trained representations, and derive an adjusted capacity formula in which the number of near-orthogonal directions depends on the ratio of vectors to dimensions ($k/d$) rather than the raw count - a single modification that cuts prediction error by two orders of magnitude with no extra parameters. Combining these results, we define representational capacity as an upper bound on the number of distinguishable directions available for features and embeddings in a model's latent space. Capacity is exponentially sensitive to $\varepsilon$, and larger models favor tighter orthogonality constraints over maximizing raw capacity - a pattern compatible with several explanations (a stability-capacity trade-off, a ceiling on usable concepts, or confounds with model scale) that we leave to future work.

</details>


### [211] [QUIVER: Quantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations in Large ML Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02785)
*Aritra Bal,Michael Binder,Markus Klute,Benedikt Maier,Michael Spannowsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 QUIVER 框架，通过引入变分量子电路提取反映数据深层统计结构的“量子费雪信息”作为互补特征，在无需量子计算硬件的情况下，显著提升了大型模型在不同领域的处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型机器学习模型虽然受益于多模态输入，但往往难以捕捉某些深层的、高阶的统计相关性。研究旨在寻找一种能够以几何视角提供互补信息的特征提取方法，以强化模型对数据的表达能力。

Method: 引入 QUIVER 框架，该框架利用变分量子电路 (VQC) 捕捉数据的量子几何特征，具体为提取“量子费雪信息矩阵”作为额外特征，并将其与经典数据特征融合，通过对基础架构进行针对性改造，实现多模态表示学习。

Result: 在 QM9（分子性质预测）和 JetClass（LHC 喷注味道分类）两个跨领域数据集上，QUIVER 均改进了标准性能指标，证明了其在实际机器学习任务中的有效性和普适性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，利用变分量子电路提取的“量子费雪信息”特征能够作为一种真正互补的模态，显著增强经典机器学习模型在处理结构化复杂数据时的性能。这种量子几何特征在无需容错量子硬件的情况下，即可为广泛的机器学习任务提供可衡量的增益。

Abstract: Large machine learning models benefit substantially from multimodal inputs that provide a complementary view of the same example. We introduce QUIVER (QUantum-Informed Views for Enhanced Representations, a paradigm that enriches classical data-driven features with a quantum Fisher view: a geometrically motivated, basis-independent summary of higher-order correlations captured by a variational quantum circuit (VQC) trained to perform the same task. Unlike classical feature augmentation, the quantum Fisher information matrix encodes the intrinsic geometry of the learned quantum state manifold. While this feature map, motivated by quantum information theory, is ordinarily non-trivial to model classically, it can surface statistical structure that additional classical data or model capacity finds difficult to learn. This makes the quantum Fisher view a genuinely complementary modality rather than a redundant one. We demonstrate that QUIVER improves standard performance metrics on two benchmark datasets from very different fields: QM9 for predicting molecule properties, and JetClass for predicting jet flavor at the Large Hadron Collider (LHC). The core contribution, however, is domain-agnostic: the quantum Fisher view can be fused into a broad class of model architectures via targeted modifications to the base architecture, to incorporate information about the quantum geometry of the problem. These results demonstrate that quantum-geometric features, extracted from simulated variational circuits, can deliver measurable value for standard machine learning tasks, well before the advent of fault-tolerant quantum hardware.

</details>


### [212] [Qift: Shift-Friendly No-Zero W2 Post-Training Quantization for Rotated W2A4/KV4 LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02823)
*Chi-Wei Huang,Chia-Chi Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决两比特 LLM 量化性能下降的问题，Qift 通过分析 Hadamard 旋转后权重的类高斯分布特征，提出了一种无零点（no-zero）对称两比特量化层级，无需额外训练或码本，在保证推理高效性的同时显著提升了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的两比特（W2）量化层级在激进的 W2A4/KV4 量化设置下性能大幅下降，现有的量化方案往往过于复杂（如需要额外训练或重构码本）。作者希望探究权重的几何特征，寻找一种训练免费、无需额外码本且易于部署的高性能量化方案。

Method: 1. 分析 Hadamard 旋转后的权重的几何分布特征，指出其呈现近似零均值的类高斯分布；
2. 提出 Qift 方法，放弃传统的 {-2, -1, 0, 1} 层级，采用 {+/-0.5, +/-1.5} 或 {+/-1, +/-4} 等无零点对称层级设计；
3. 基于尺度不变比率分析，确定了最优的内外中心点比率范围（0.25-0.33），从而证明了该层级设计的理论有效性。

Result: Qift 在 LLaMA-2-7B 和 LLaMA-3.1-8B 模型上，显著改善了纯 W2A4 及混合精度的困惑度（perplexity），提升了下游任务的准确率，且在 L=16 混合精度下能够大幅缩小与 W3A4 的性能差距。

Conclusion: Qift 提出了一种基于无零点（no-zero）对称量化层级的两比特量化方法。该方法不需要训练或复杂的码本，仅通过简单的重参数化设计，就能在极大的降低存储成本（W2A4/KV4）的同时，有效缩小与高比特量化性能之间的差距，是目前复杂量化方案的有效且易于部署的替代方案。

Abstract: Two-bit weight quantization is attractive for memory-efficient LLM inference, but the standard W2 level set {-2,-1,0,+1} often collapses under aggressive W2A4/KV4 settings. We study the scalar level-set geometry of two-bit weights in a Hadamard-rotated quantization pipeline. Conventional asymmetric W2 substantially improves over the standard level set, indicating that W2A4 failure is not only a bit-width problem but also a reconstruction-level problem. Across all 224 linear modules in each of LLaMA-2-7B and LLaMA-3.1-8B, pretrained weights are already nearly zero-centered, while Hadamard rotation primarily Gaussianizes their standardized shape: excess kurtosis and Q-Q error drop by orders of magnitude. Based on this approximate zero-centered Gaussian-like source model, we propose Qift, a fixed no-zero W2 level set for rotated W2A4/KV4 inference. The main level set is {+/-0.5, +/-1.5}, equivalently {+/-1, +/-3} under a half-scale reparameterization; a power-of-two variant uses {+/-1, +/-4} for sign-and-shift decoded weight application. Qift redesigns the fixed two-bit code-to-level mapping and is training-free, learned-codebook-free, group-grid-free, and zero-point-free, retaining the standard per-channel scale. A scale-invariant ratio analysis identifies an effective inner/outer centroid ratio range of 0.25 to 0.33, explaining why mirror no-zero (MNZ), Lloyd, NF2, and PoT-MNZ perform well while {+/-1, +/-2} does not. On both models, the no-zero level sets consistently improve pure W2A4 perplexity, L-layer mixed W2/W4 perplexity, downstream accuracy, and GPTQ residual behavior over the standard W2 level set. At L=16 mixed precision, they substantially narrow the gap to W3A4 while keeping half of the transformer layers at two-bit precision, giving a simple, source-aware, and deployment-friendly alternative to more complex learned W2 codebooks.

</details>


### [213] [Mitigating Spurious Correlations with Memorization-Guided Dataset De-Biasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02830)
*Arda Fazla,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度学习模型对虚假相关性的依赖问题，提出了一种基于两阶段学习动态解耦的样本筛选算法，在显著降低数据需求量的同时提升了模型对核心因果特征的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据中普遍存在的虚假相关性（spurious correlations）会导致模型在处理缺乏这些相关性的少数样本时分类失效；且现有的样本选择方法严重依赖虚假特征，无法准确衡量因果特征的重要性。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段样本评分函数（two-stage sample scoring function），旨在解耦核心特征与虚假特征的学习动态，并分别评估其学习难度，据此实现对样本的筛选与优先级排序。

Result: 在标准 ERM 模型上使用所选样本进行训练，在大幅减少训练数据需求（仅需原始数据的 10%）的同时，性能优于当前最先进的去偏（debiasing）技术。

Conclusion: 提出了一种高效的样本选择策略，仅需 10% 的训练数据即可超越现有的去偏方法，有效解决了模型对虚假相关性的依赖。

Abstract: Real-world datasets often contain spurious correlations that are not causally related to the target label. When such correlations dominate the majority of training samples, models tend to rely on them, leading to misclassification of minority samples that do not exhibit the same spurious patterns. While a potential approach is to select subsets of data to better represent the minority samples, this may require access to group labels, which are typically unknown. Furthermore, as we demonstrate, widely used sample scoring functions in the invariant subset or coreset selection literature largely depend on spurious features and therefore fail to accurately capture the importance or difficulty of core, causally relevant features. Accordingly, we propose to mitigate spurious correlations by developing a two-stage sample scoring function that disentangles the learning dynamics of core and spurious features and evaluates their difficulty separately. Based on our proposed metric, we introduce a new algorithm to find and prioritize informative samples both with and without spurious correlations. Extensive experiments demonstrate that a standard ERM model trained on our selected samples achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art debiasing techniques, while requiring as little as 10\% of the original training data.

</details>


### [214] [Learning Coherent Representations: A Topological Approach to Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02841)
*Sigurd Gaukstad,Melvin Vaupel,Valdemar Kargård Olsen,Erik Hermansen,Benjamin Dunn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“相干性（coherence）”的几何约束，通过强制神经网络学习具有空间连通性的特征表示，解决了神经网络特征难以解释的问题，并引入了Coh目标函数实现此约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络中单个神经元的激活往往缺乏明确解释性，且现有的稀疏性（sparsity）约束仅关注激活频率而非空间几何分布。受到大脑神经网络（如网格细胞）几何响应的启发，旨在通过引入空间连通性约束来构建具有可解释性的特征空间。

Method: 1. 定义“相干性”：一种矩阵属性，要求行（样本）和列（特征）在几何上是稠密聚集的。2. 理论证明：证明相干矩阵能诱导样本与特征空间之间有限的拓扑交织（Vietoris-Rips filtrations）。3. 算法实现：引入Coh目标函数（基于Fréchet方差的可微损耗函数），在训练过程中强制执行相干性约束。

Result: 实验表明，加装Coh目标函数的编码器能在合成数据、旋转MNIST以及BERT词嵌入中产生具备拓扑连通性的特征，使得模型不仅能提供可解释特征，还能提供可解释的特征空间（如将圆形数据结构平铺为连续弧）。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了“相干性”（coherence）作为一种几何约束，通过拓扑结构上的关联，有效提升了深度学习模型特征的解释性，且证明了在大规模预训练模型（如BERT）中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Deep neural networks learn representations where individual features often lack interpretable meaning; a single neuron may activate for scattered, unrelated inputs. We introduce coherence, a geometric property inspired by neural coding in the brain, where neurons like grid cells and head direction cells respond to contiguous regions of state space. A non-negative matrix is coherent if each row (sample) attends to geometrically clustered columns (features) and vice versa, and in addition every sample is well described by some feature and every feature is needed by some sample. We prove that coherent matrices induce a bounded interleaving between the Vietoris-Rips filtrations of samples and features, guaranteeing that both spaces share compatible topological structure. This geometric constraint facilitates interpretability. For example, if data lies on a circle, coherent features must tile that circle into contiguous arcs. We introduce Coh, a differentiable objective function based on Fréchet variance that enforces coherence during training. Unlike sparsity, which bounds how many samples a feature activates on, coherence bounds which samples, requiring geometric connectivity rather than only rarity. This yields not just interpretable features but an interpretable feature space. We validate Coh in an auto-encoder using synthetic and rotated MNIST datasets and in a token embedding of BERT using language data.

</details>


### [215] [Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow for Lightweight Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02842)
*Yixian Shen,Zhiheng Yang,Qi Bi,Changshuo Wang,Shuai Wang,Jia-Hong Huang,George Floros,Prayag Tiwari,Anuj Pathania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpecFlow 是一种通过离散余弦变换压缩视觉思维状态的多模态推理框架，实现了推理延迟与内存占用不随推理深度增加，有效解决了多模态空间推理中的高计算开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态空间推理方法依赖长链条视觉/文本思维，导致大量的视觉 Token 积累和密集的跨模态注意力计算，带来了巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级框架 SpecFlow：1. 利用离散余弦变换（DCT）的能量压缩特性，将视觉思维表示为固定大小；2. 根据推理需求动态引入高频细节；3. 使用无分类器引导（Classifier-free guidance）通过文本意图驱动视觉状态的更新，无需扩展上下文。

Result: 相比基线方法，SpecFlow 计算量和 KV Cache 成本降低高达 2.1 倍，同时保持了同等甚至更优的空间推理任务性能。

Conclusion: SpecFlow 通过将视觉工作空间限制在固定大小的离散余弦空间，实现了推理成本与推理深度解耦，在降低计算资源开销的同时保持了前沿的多模态空间推理性能。

Abstract: Multimodal spatial reasoning often relies on long chains of intermediate textual and visual thoughts, where accumulating visual tokens and dense cross-modal attention incur substantial computation and memory overhead. To address this challenge, we propose Spectral-Progressive Thought Flow (SpecFlow), a novel lightweight multimodal spatial reasoning framework that represents intermediate visual thoughts in a fixed-size discrete cosine space. By exploiting strong energy compaction, SpecFlow preserves global layout and relational structure while introducing high-frequency details only when increased spatial precision is required. To align visual state evolution with linguistic intent, classifier-free guidance enables autoregressive textual thoughts to steer flow-based updates of the visual workspace/state without expanding the context. As a result, SpecFlow maintains a bounded visual workspace whose updates depend only on the current visual state and accumulated textual trace, enabling long-horizon inference with stable latency and memory usage independent of reasoning depth. Empirical results show that SpecFlow achieves competitive or superior reasoning performance while reducing computation and KV cache costs by up to 2.1 times.

</details>


### [216] [A Systematic Evaluation of Current Architectures in Wind Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02849)
*Vinicius Bortolini,Gilson Adamczuk Oliveira,Erick Oliveira Rodrigues,Matheus Henrique Dal Molin Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了风电区间预测中深度学习、模态分解与统计方法的混合应用，通过LDA分析发现模态分解技术能显著提升预测性能，并建议未来研究聚焦于标准评估体系、计算效率优化及实际应用验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风能资源具有内在的不确定性，区间预测对于风能高效并网和电力系统运行至关重要，因此有必要对现有的混合预测方法进行梳理与评价。

Method: 本研究通过系统文献综述方法，并应用隐含狄利克雷分布（LDA）主题建模技术对风电区间预测领域的研究趋势与模式进行分析。

Result: 研究发现结合VMD/EEMD等分解技术与深度学习模型（如LSTM）的混合模型能有效缩小预测区间宽度并提升覆盖率；双模型策略（分别独立预测上下界）是目前主流且有效的区间构建方法。

Conclusion: 混合模型结合模态分解技术对于提升风电区间预测的准确性和置信度至关重要，未来的研究应致力于标准化评估指标、降低计算复杂度并强化实际应用场景下的验证。

Abstract: Interval wind speed forecasting is essential for the efficient integration of wind energy into power systems, as it accounts for the inherent uncertainty of wind resources. This study presents a systematic literature review focused on hybrid approaches to interval forecasting of wind generation, exploring the combination of deep learning, modal decomposition, and statistical methods. To guide the paper selection, Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) was applied for topic modeling, enabling the identification of patterns and research trends. The findings emphasize that integrating hybrid models with decomposition techniques-such as Variational Mode Decomposition (VMD) and Ensemble Empirical Mode Decomposition (EEMD)-enhances forecast accuracy and reliability by narrowing prediction intervals without compromising coverage. Regarding interval construction, most studies adopt a dual-model strategy, independently forecasting the lower and upper bounds. Input data are commonly decomposed using techniques like EMD, EEMD, or VMD, which extract frequency-based components. These components serve as inputs to models such as LSTM or ELM, trained separately for each bound. This approach allows for targeted modeling of uncertainty, improving flexibility and precision, Interval quality is typically evaluated through metrics that balance coverage and interval width. The review also highlights challenges, including the lack of standardized evaluation metrics, computational complexity, and limited real-world validation. Overall, the study reinforces the value of interval forecasting for wind energy operations and offers insights for advancing model robustness and decision-making.

</details>


### [217] [RESCAST-100K: A Comprehensive Dataset for Cross-Domain Residential Load and Indoor Temperature Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02852)
*Jainam Dhruva,Yousaf Raza,A. B. Siddique,Simone Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了 RESCAST-100K 数据集，这是一个覆盖 10 万户住宅、包含模拟与真实数据的跨域预测基准，旨在推动住宅能源负荷与室内温度预报在多场景下的泛化能力研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住宅能源负荷与室内温度预测研究受限于数据集规模小、覆盖范围窄以及缺乏结构化的跨域评估手段，导致模型在处理真实场景下的数据异质性和稀缺性时泛化能力较差。

Method: 构建了基于 EnergyPlus 模拟的 RESCAST-100K 数据集（包含约 10 万户住宅），整合了五个真实世界数据集；提出了一个配置驱动的接口，通过地理、气候、建筑结构等维度定义跨域评估任务；并对循环神经网络、基于注意力机制的模型及 MLP-mixer 架构在零样本性能、缺失输入和跨域场景下进行了基准测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，Cross-attention 和 MLP-mixer 模型在应对域偏移（Domain Shift）时，其表现显著优于循环神经网络（RNN）和传统的 Transformer 基准模型。

Conclusion: RESCAST-100K 为住宅能源预测提供了一个大规模、可配置的基准测试，证明了跨注意力机制（Cross-attention）和 MLP-mixer 模型在处理域偏移时具有更强的泛化能力，为未来的能源管理研究提供了统一的评估框架。

Abstract: Accurate short-term forecasting of residential energy load and indoor temperature is essential for home energy management systems, grid-level demand response, and community energy efficiency efforts. Domain adaptation and transfer learning have shown promise for improving forecasting accuracy under data heterogeneity and scarcity commonly seen in residential settings. However, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive residential datasets: existing benchmarks are narrow in target coverage and rarely support structured cross-domain evaluation. We introduce RESCAST-100K, a large-scale residential forecasting benchmark for studying cross-domain generalization. It provides a configuration-driven interface that instantiates source and target domains along interpretable axes, including geography, climate zone, wall construction, and heating equipment, enabling systematic evaluation of transfer learning, domain adaptation, and zero-shot generalization under controlled domain shifts. The benchmark covers approximately 100,000 EnergyPlus-simulated U.S. homes derived from ResStock, with 15-minute time series for three coupled targets per home: total load, HVAC load, and indoor temperature. These are paired with weather channels, HVAC setpoints, and over 40 static building covariates. RESCAST-100K also integrates five real-world residential datasets under a unified schema, supporting sim-to-real evaluation on the same tasks. We benchmark recurrent, attention-based, and MLP-mixer architectures for zero-shot performance across domains, missing-input conditions, and forecasting tasks. Cross-attention and MLP-mixer models consistently outperform recurrent and classical transformer baselines under domain shift. RESCAST-100K is intended to aid the machine learning and building analytics communities advance cross-domain residential forecasting at home, community, and grid scale.

</details>


### [218] [GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857)
*Liyan Tan,Yequan Zhao,Yifan Yang,Ruijie Zhang,Xinling Yu,Zheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRZO通过创新的群组相对归一化技术，有效降低了零阶优化在大模型微调中的梯度估计方差，在实现比MeZO更优性能的同时显著降低了内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对零阶（ZO）优化在微调大模型时因梯度估计方差过高、收敛性差而导致部署受限的问题，旨在提供一种内存高效且性能更佳的替代策略。

Method: 提出了GRZO（Group-Relative Zeroth-Order）优化器，通过引入“群组相对归一化”策略，对小批量数据中每个样本的损失进行聚合，在不增加前向传播开销的前提下，将有效梯度方向计数从1提升至批大小。

Result: 在RoBERTa-large、Llama3-8B及OPT-13B等模型和多项任务上表现优异。相比MeZO，在Llama3-8B上平均准确率提升3.0%，峰值GPU内存降低23%；作为MeZO的核心插件，可使稀疏、低秩及量化等ZO变体性能平均提升6.0%。

Conclusion: GRZO显著提升了零阶优化在大型语言模型微调中的精度与内存效率，证明了通过群组相对归一化有效解决高方差问题的可行性，是MeZO的有力改进方案。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization is a memory-efficient alternative to backpropagation for fine-tuning large language models, but its deployment is limited by the high variance of gradient estimation. We propose GRZO, a Group-Relative Zeroth-Order optimizer that draws one pseudo-independent perturbation per mini-batch example and aggregates the per-example losses through group-relative normalization, raising the effective gradient-direction count from one to the batch size at no additional forward cost while preserving inference-level memory. We prove that GRZO is directionally unbiased with variance shrinking proportionally to the batch size, yielding a tighter nonconvex convergence bound than MeZO. Across RoBERTa-large, Llama3-8B, and OPT-13B over multiple tasks, GRZO improves average accuracy on Llama3-8B by $+3.0$ over MeZO at $23\%$ lower peak GPU memory; as a drop-in replacement for the MeZO core, it lifts sparse, low-rank, and quantized ZO variants by $+6.0$ on average.

</details>


### [219] [Forgetting is Not Erasure: Recovering Latent Knowledge via Transport Keys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02860)
*Archie Chaudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了持续学习中的“灾难性遗忘”很大程度上是由于模型内部层间接口漂移（interface drift）导致的，通过使用“传输密钥”对齐网络阶段，无需强制锁定权重也能恢复旧任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有观点中关于持续学习中权重的灾难性遗忘被归因为特征在训练中被彻底擦除的看法提出质疑，探究是否可以通过修复阶段间的交互接口来恢复遗忘任务的表现。

Method: 提出了一种“缝合评估协议”（stitched evaluation protocol）：将更新后网络的早期层计算与更新前网络的后期层计算结合，并利用“传输密钥”（transport keys，一种通过少量锚点激活估计的接口对齐算子）来克服两者之间的接口漂移。

Result: 在 Split CIFAR-100 数据集和 ResNet/ViT 模型上实验表明，通过传输密钥，模型在学习新任务后能够找回并恢复大部分旧任务的原始性能。

Conclusion: 持续学习中的灾难性遗忘并非完全源于权重的永久性改变导致特征丢失，很大程度上是由于内部计算阶段之间的“接口漂移”导致难以访问先前的表示。未来的研究应侧重于开发能够对潜在计算进行高效索引和重新访问的机制，而非仅仅限制权重更新。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting is often framed as a representational problem: after sequential training, a model appears to lose the features that supported performance on earlier tasks. We challenge the stronger form of this view. Across controlled continual-learning settings, we find that a significant portion of apparent forgetting can be attributed to interface drift between internal stages rather than permanent erasure of task-relevant computation. We study this phenomenon through a stitched evaluation protocol that combines early computation from a post-update network with late computation from its predecessor, optionally mediated by a compact, task-specific transport key. We describe transport keys at a systems level as compact interface-alignment operators estimated from a small set of paired anchor activations and evaluated through model stitching. On split CIFAR-100 with a ResNet-style network, transport keys recover most of the original Task A performance after sequential training on Task B. On a compact vision transformer, we observe a similar recovery pattern. These results suggest that continual learning may require better mechanisms for indexing and re-accessing latent computations, not only methods that prevent weight change.

</details>


### [220] [RRISE: Robust Radius Inference via a Surrogate Estimator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02876)
*Jong-Ik Park,Shreyas Chaudhari,Carlee Joe-Wong,José M. F. Moura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RRISE是一个高效的随机平滑框架，通过用单次神经网络前向传播替代大规模蒙特卡洛采样，实现了实时、可证明的鲁棒性认证，显著降低了计算开销并提升了在高难度数据集上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机平滑（RS）虽能提供通用鲁棒性认证，但因依赖单输入蒙特卡洛（MC）采样，计算成本过高，无法满足实时系统需求。

Method: 提出RRISE框架，通过学习一个代理模型（Surrogate）来预测认证半径；利用软标签交叉熵损失在预计算的MC标签上训练代理，并引入保形校准（Conformal Calibration）步骤，确保生成的半径是保守且可证明正确的。

Result: 在单一推理步骤内实现认证，将推理延迟降低了高达 $10^4$ 倍；在CIFAR-100和Tiny ImageNet等数据集上，认证准确率比现有离线代理方法高出 1.23 到 1.91 倍，且与固定预算的MC认证精度差异不到 0.84 个百分点。

Conclusion: RRISE通过替代昂贵的蒙特卡洛采样，为重复部署场景下的确定性鲁棒性认证提供了一种高效且实用的可行方案，并弥补了现有离线代理方法在复杂数据集上的性能缺陷。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing (RS) uses a smoothed classifier to provide architecture-agnostic certificates of $\ell_2$ classification robustness, but its dependence on per-input Monte Carlo (MC) sampling undermines its use in real-time systems. We argue that this cost is structural rather than fundamental, such that it can be significantly reduced by sharing information across the deployment stream. We introduce RRISE, an RS framework that compresses certification into a single forward pass through a learned surrogate. RRISE trains the surrogate against precomputed MC class-count targets via a soft-label cross-entropy loss and converts surrogate predictions into provably conservative certified radii through a one-time conformal calibration step. The resulting certificate is deployment-verifiable: whenever the calibrated radius is positive, the surrogate's prediction provably matches the smoothed classifier's and the smoothed classifier is constant on a ball of that radius around the input.
