Across image classification benchmarks, RRISE matches fixed-budget MC certified accuracy within $0.84$ percentage points while replacing up to $10^4$ noisy base-model evaluations per query with a single surrogate forward pass, recouping MC training cost after $\approx 10^5$ deployment queries. On CIFAR-100 and Tiny ImageNet, where the only prior offline-surrogate method collapses, RRISE achieves $1.23$ to $1.91\times$ higher certified accuracy, establishing efficient randomized smoothing as a practical path to certified robustness in repeated-deployment settings.

</details>


### [221] [Are we really tilting? The mechanics of reward guidance in flow and diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02884)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Nicholas M. Boffi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究揭示了扩散模型奖励引导中的“奖励欺骗”是由有限粒子逼近Doob h函数产生的固有数学误差所致，并提出了对应的阻尼修正策略以恢复模型保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励引导方法在生成任务中虽然有效，但普遍存在“奖励欺骗”（reward hacking）问题，即过度优化奖励目标导致生成质量或分布保真度下降。目前的解释多停留在经验层面或模型复杂性上，缺乏对该现象根本物理机制的理解。

Method: 1. 对比分析有限粒子插值（plug-in estimation）在奖励引导扩散中的行为；2. 在高斯混合目标和二次奖励的受控环境下，从数学上推导并隔离出两种失效模式；3. 提出一种低计算成本的闭式奖励阻尼（reward damping）调度策略；4. 明确best-of-n采样在模式选择中的补偿作用。

Result: 理论上证明了有限粒子估计导致的偏置和模式选择困难；通过阻尼调度校正了内部模式偏置；实验验证了该方法在合成数据集和大规模文本到图像模型（FLUX.1）上的有效性。

Conclusion: 奖励引导扩散模型中存在的“奖励欺骗”现象并非单纯源于奖励函数或模型训练偏差，而是由有限粒子插值估计Doob h函数引起的固有缺陷。通过引入闭式奖励阻尼调度，可有效缓解该类偏差并提升生成性能。

Abstract: Reward guidance algorithms steer a learned generative process toward the reward-tilted measure at inference time. While empirically powerful, these methods are prone to reward hacking: the guided model over-optimizes the reward at the cost of fidelity to the learned distribution. Prior work has attributed this to the complexity of neural reward functions or implicit biases in diffusion training, but its fundamental origins remain poorly understood. We show that reward hacking arises from an approximation made in most practical implementations of reward-guided diffusion -- finite-particle plug-in estimation of the Doob h-function -- even in the simplest non-trivial settings of Gaussian and Gaussian mixture targets with quadratic rewards. In closed form, we isolate two distinct failure modes of the plug-in estimator: it leads to reward hacking within each mode and it cannot select high-reward modes. We propose a closed-form reward damping schedule that corrects the within-mode bias with no additional compute, and clarify the role of best-of-n sampling in compensating for the mode selection failure. Experiments on Gaussian mixture targets, a 2D checkerboard, and FLUX.1 text-to-image generation confirm that our theoretical insights carry over to practical settings.

</details>


### [222] [Scalable Uncertainty Quantification for Extreme Weather Forecasting via Empirical Neural Tangent Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02886)
*Jose Marie Antonio Miñoza,Rex Gregor Laylo,Sebastian C. Ibañez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了 NTK-UQ 方法，通过理论分析网络架构对不确定性量化的影响，利用特征分解自动生成高性能、可自适应的极端天气预测区间，解决了深度天气预测模型缺乏不确定性估计的痛点，且推理开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习天气预报模型虽然在准确性上已媲美数值预报，但缺乏不确定性估计。在极端天气等高风险决策场景中，这种确定性预测存在严重局限，无法为决策提供有效的风险参考。

Method: 提出基于神经正切核（NTK）的不确定性量化（UQ）方法，利用最后一层的经验特征。结合独立成分分析（ICA）与奇异值分解（SVD）进行特征分解，并构建数据驱动的自动选择规则。研究了特征空间的本征谱集中度与架构关系（如注意力机制 vs 谱算子网络）对UQ质量的影响。

Result: NTK-UQ 在 90% 覆盖率下实现了比分层保形预测（split conformal prediction）高出 31-37% 的预测区间锐度；能够根据极端天气的严重程度自适应调节区间；整个过程无需重新训练模型，推理时仅需单次矩阵-向量乘法。

Conclusion: NTK-UQ 是一种无需重训练的轻量级不确定性量化方法，能够显著提升深度学习天气预测模型在极端天气下的风险评估能力，生成比传统保形预测更锐利且具有自适应性的预测区间。

Abstract: Deep learning weather models now match numerical weather prediction accuracy while running orders of magnitude faster, but produce deterministic forecasts without uncertainty estimates, a critical gap for high-stakes decisions during extreme weather events. This paper proposes Neural Tangent Kernel-based uncertainty quantification (NTK-UQ) using last-layer empirical features. Theoretical analysis predicts that UQ quality is architecture-dependent through two mechanisms. First, a variance collapse mechanism explains when UQ fails: when the eigenvalue truncation rank approaches the effective rank of the feature space, the GP correction term consumes nearly all prior variance, destroying discrimination between tropical cyclones and routine conditions; architectures with concentrated spectra (spectral operators) require aggressive truncation ($k \leq 10$), while attention-based models tolerate full-rank computation. Second, decomposition performance depends on the non-Gaussian, heavy-tailed structure of extreme weather: Independent Component Analysis exploits higher-order statistics (kurtosis, negentropy) to isolate heavy-tailed extreme-event features, achieving higher discrimination than singular value decomposition, which captures only second-order variance. A data-driven selection rule chooses ICA or SVD from the feature eigenspectrum concentration ratio, correctly prescribing the superior decomposition for all four evaluated architectures. Compared to split conformal prediction (the natural post-hoc baseline), NTK-UQ achieves 31--37\% sharper prediction intervals at 90\% coverage, and uniquely produces \emph{adaptive} intervals that scale with extreme event severity, which conformal prediction cannot achieve by construction. The framework requires no retraining; inference-time uncertainty requires only a single matrix-vector product per sample.

</details>


### [223] [Multi-Modal Machine Learning for Breast Cancer Recurrence Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02892)
*Jiahao Shao,Xudong Wang,Anam Nawaz Khan,Christopher Brett,Xueping Li,Bing Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过整合结构化治疗记录与从非结构化病理报告中提取的临床特征，构建了多模态预测框架，有效优化了乳腺癌复发的风险评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型多依赖单一来源数据，难以全面捕捉临床背景，导致乳腺癌复发风险评估的准确性受限。本研究旨在通过整合多种临床模态（结构化记录与非结构化文本）来提升预测精度。

Method: 采用基于规则的正则表达式提取机制，结合优先级冲突调节策略，从非结构化病理报告中提取关键肿瘤特征，将其与结构化治疗记录整合，并对比了多种机器学习模型在单模态与多模态输入下的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，融合了多模态数据输入（结构化数据+提取的文本特征）的模型，在预测准确性上显著优于仅使用单一模式数据的传统方法。

Conclusion: 多模态临床数据（结构化数据+从非结构化文本中提取的特征）的整合能够显著提高乳腺癌复发预测的准确性，证明了利用自然语言处理技术挖掘临床非结构化数据对于优化疾病预后模型的必要性。

Abstract: Breast cancer recurrence, a leading cause of long-term mortality among survivors, requires timely and accurate risk assessment to guide follow-up care and treatment planning. Traditional predictive models, often limited to either structured or unstructured data alone, struggle to capture the full clinical context. This study examines the impact of integrating multi-modal clinical data, including treatment records, pathology reports, and clinician notes, on recurrence prediction. By integrating a rule-based regular expression extraction mechanism with a rigorous precedence-based conflict reconciliation strategy, our approach effectively recovers definitive tumor characteristics from free-text pathology narratives to augment structured records. We also benchmark performance against commonly used feature sets from prior breast cancer studies to assess the added value of multi-modal integration. Single-source and multi-modal inputs are evaluated across a range of machine learning models. Results show that multi-modal integration consistently improves predictive accuracy compared to single-modal methods.

</details>


### [224] [Fast Unlearning at Scale via Margin Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02920)
*Federico Di Gennaro,Alexander Shevchenko,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MASC 是一种高效的语言模型遗忘方法，通过主动缩减对数几率差及在线自动停止规则，在大幅降低计算成本的同时实现有效遗忘，具有良好的扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型遗忘方法依赖固定的训练预算和离线检查点选择，存在计算冗余、存储开销大以及需要重复进行下游验证等限制，缺乏可扩展性。

Method: 提出“边际自我修正”（MArgin Self-Correction, MASC）方法。通过在遗忘过程中主动减少原始下一个标记与其他最可能替代标记之间的对数几率差（logit gap）来更新模型，并引入在线停止规则，在达到预设阈值时自动停止训练，无需下游验证。

Result: 在 TOFU、MUSE News 和 MUSE Books 数据集上，MASC 以远低于现有基线方法的计算成本达到了具有竞争力的“遗忘-保留”权衡。研究还发现，随着模型参数增加，遗忘表现保持稳定，同时保留效用有所提升。

Conclusion: MASC 是一种高效的机器遗忘方法，通过在线停止规则实现了计算效率的提升，并验证了随着模型规模扩大，遗忘与保留效用之间的权衡得到了优化。

Abstract: Language-model unlearning updates a trained model to behave as if it had not seen selected training examples, while preserving utility and avoiding costly retraining. Existing approaches typically fine-tune the pretrained model with a fixed training budget and select the final model afterwards by evaluating several saved checkpoints on downstream validation data. Two sources of unnecessary computation limit scalability: training beyond the desired forget-retain trade-off, and checkpoint selection that requires extra storage and repeated evaluations. To address these limitations, we introduce MArgin Self-Correction (MASC), an efficient unlearning method with an online stopping rule that does not require downstream evaluation. Given a text sequence to be forgotten, MASC actively reduces the logit gap between the original next token and the most likely alternatives. It outputs a final model once this gap is small on average over a sufficiently large proportion of token positions across all forget sequences. On TOFU, MUSE News, and MUSE Books, MASC achieves a competitive forget-retain trade-off at a fraction of the computational cost of existing baselines. We further observe that as we increase model size (a.k.a. number of parameters), the trade-offs improve for both MASC and SimNPO -- the forget metrics remain comparable while retain utility increases.

</details>


### [225] [ERP-XTTN: Interpretable Prototype-Guided Cross-Attention for Cross-Subject ERP Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02939)
*Charlotte Genevier Wyman,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对BCI缺乏通用解释性模型的问题，本文提出ERP-XTTN，通过基于原型的交叉注意力机制实现无需校准的鲁棒ERP分类，并以结构化可解释性取代后验分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决脑机接口（BCI）中跨受试者分类器在无需校准情况下泛化能力差及模型透明度不足的问题。

Method: 提出ERP-XTTN架构，利用只包含查询-键（query-key-only）的交叉注意力机制将EEG片段映射到自动提取的差分波原型上；取消了值（value）投影，使得分类决策完全依赖于注意力路由，从而实现固有的结构化可解释性。

Result: 在三个公共数据集的八种ERP组件上进行LOSO验证显示，ERP-XTTN在AUC性能上与EEGNet及xDAWN+RG基准模型表现接近（差距在0.018-0.034之间），但提供了更好的神经生理学可解释性，能够揭示分类错误背后的结构化信号模式。

Conclusion: ERP-XTTN证明了在无需校准的跨受试者测试中，基于原型的交叉注意力机制能够实现具有结构化可解释性和竞争力的ERP分类，是首个针对ERP CORE数据集的epoch级LOSO基准测试。

Abstract: Interpretable brain-computer interface classifiers that generalize across subjects without calibration remain an open challenge. We test whether prototype-based cross-attention can provide competitive, interpretable event-related potential (ERP) classification under deployment-compatible conditions. We propose ERP-XTTN, a cross-attention architecture that routes input EEG patches to fixed difference-wave prototypes via query-key-only cross-attention with no value projection, so classification depends entirely on attention routing and attention faithfulness is structural rather than post-hoc. Prototypes are derived automatically from extrema in the training-fold difference wave. We evaluate across three public sources (BNCI Horizon 2020, HRI Cursor, and ERP CORE) spanning eight ERP components (ERN, LRP, ErrP, N170, P300, N2pc, MMN, N400), using leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) evaluation with causal filtering at two channel counts (3-channel and full montage), against EEGNet and xDAWN with Riemannian geometry (xDAWN+RG). The mean gap between the best baseline and ERP-XTTN was .018 AUROC at 3 channels and .034 at full montage, arising from two largely distinct sources: a temporal-flexibility cost relative to EEGNet and a spatial-exploitation cost relative to xDAWN+RG, the latter driven by signal-to-noise ratio at full montage. Beyond accuracy, the transparent routing reveals cross-subject signal structure that black-box models cannot: false positives resembled true positives more than true negatives did, indicating that classification errors are neurophysiologically explicable. ERP-XTTN generalizes across diverse ERPs under causal, calibration-free conditions with a small interpretability cost at minimal montages. To our knowledge, this is the first epoch-level LOSO benchmark on ERP CORE.

</details>


### [226] [BYORn: Bootstrap Your Own Responses to Defend Large Vision-Language Models Against Backdoor Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02947)
*Ivan Sabolić,Marin Oršić,Josip Šarić,Sven Lončarić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言模型在微调中易受后门攻击的问题，提出了一种名为 BYORn 的防御框架，通过识别并动态纠正语义不一致的投毒响应，在实现稳健防御的同时保障了模型的通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在微调过程中极易受到后门攻击，且现有防御手段在面对开放式生成任务时效果不佳。

Method: 提出一种名为 BYORn 的后门防御微调框架。该方法观察到投毒目标响应与输入通常存在语义不一致，利用预训练模型动态识别并替换这些异常响应，从而修正模型梯度更新方向，使其近似于干净数据分布的经验风险上界。

Result: 实验表明，BYORn 在提升模型对后门攻击鲁棒性的同时，有效地保持了模型在正常任务上的性能，并优于现有的防御方法，能够有效抵御针对性的自适应攻击。

Conclusion: BYORn 通过在训练中实时纠正中毒样本的语义不一致性，成功打破了触发器与恶意输出之间的关联，在防御后门攻击的同时保持了模型在干净任务上的表现，并对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning is the predominant approach for adapting autoregressive vision-language models to downstream tasks. Recent work has shown that this paradigm is highly vulnerable to backdoor attacks, and that existing defenses are ineffective in open-ended generation settings. In response, we propose BYORn, a backdoor-robust fine-tuning framework motivated by the observation that poisoned target responses are often semantically implausible given the corresponding image-text inputs and a pretrained model. BYORn identifies such misaligned responses and dynamically replaces them with alternative responses generated by the model, thereby breaking the correlation between triggers and target outputs. The resulting objective gradient corresponds to the gradient of the empirical estimate of the population risk upper bound over the clean data distribution. Empirically, BYORn consistently improves robustness to backdoor attacks while preserving clean-task performance, establishing a new trade-off frontier between generalization and attack success rate. Finally, we demonstrate that BYORn remains effective against adaptive attacks specifically designed to circumvent the proposed defense.

</details>


### [227] [From Non-Convex to Strongly Convex: Curvature-Adaptive FTPL for Online Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02948)
*Moses Charikar,Chirag Pabbaraju,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应曲率的FTPL算法，使其在非凸优化中不仅能维持鲁棒的$O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，还能在曲率累积时利用曲率信息加速收敛至$O(\log T)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FTPL在非凸情形下能达到最优的$O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾界，但无法利用损失函数的曲率信息来提升收敛速度；本文旨在实现非凸优化下的曲率自适应。

Method: 提出了一种改进的Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) 算法，引入了基于历史信息动态调整扰动尺度的自适应机制（Follow-the-Leader tuning rule），摆脱了对预知曲率累积情况的依赖。

Result: 该算法在非凸Lipschitz损失下达到$O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾；若曲率呈线性累积，算法可达到$O(\log T)$遗憾；此外，通过下界证明，确认了非凸最差情况遗憾与曲率主导快速收敛之间的权衡是内在固有的。

Conclusion: 本文证明了FTPL算法在非凸优化场景下实现曲率自适应的可行性。研究表明，通过随时间动态调整扰动尺度，FTPL算法能够兼顾非凸损失函数的一般性保证（$O(\sqrt{T})$）与强凸损失函数下的最优收敛率（$O(\log T)$），且这种权衡是本质上的。

Abstract: Curvature adaptivity is a classical theme in online optimization: for convex Lipschitz losses, adaptive methods interpolate between the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for general convex losses and $O(\log T)$ regret under strong convexity. Recent work has shown that Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader (FTPL) achieves optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret even for online non-convex Lipschitz losses, assuming access to an approximate offline-optimization oracle, but these guarantees do not exploit curvature. We show that FTPL can be made curvature-adaptive in the non-convex setting, without knowing in advance how curvature will accumulate over time. Our algorithm replaces the fixed perturbation scale of standard FTPL with a time-varying scale chosen using only past information. We give a simple follow-the-leader tuning rule for this scale and show that it competes, up to constants, with the best choice in hindsight. The resulting method achieves $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret for arbitrary non-convex Lipschitz losses and improves as cumulative curvature grows; with sufficiently accurate oracle calls, it achieves $O(\log T)$ regret when cumulative curvature grows linearly, which includes the classical strongly convex regime. We complement these upper bounds with matching lower bounds for prescribed cumulative-curvature sequences, already for one-dimensional convex losses, showing that the tradeoff between worst-case non-convex regret and curvature-driven fast rates is intrinsic.

</details>


### [228] [KForge: LLM-Driven Cross-Platform Kernel Generation for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02963)
*Taras Sereda,Burak Bartan,Ankita Nayak,Tom St. John,Natalie Serrino,Zain Asgar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KForge是一个利用生成与分析双代理协作的自动化算子开发框架，通过闭环迭代机制不仅能自动生成高性能算子，还能有效解决多硬件后端的兼容与性能优化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对当前AI生产推理中异构硬件（多后端）混合部署带来的挑战，手写高性能计算算子工作量巨大且难以扩展。现有的LLM自动代码生成方案在底层算子生成和跨硬件后端泛化能力方面均存在不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为KForge的跨平台框架，采用双代理驱动的迭代细化循环：1. 生成代理（Generation Agent）：负责算子生成与功能正确性修复；2. 性能分析代理（Performance-Analysis Agent）：利用性能剖析数据提供优化建议。该过程分为功能性修正（确保正确）和优化（提升性能）两个阶段。

Result: 在NVIDIA B200后端上，KForge在gpt-oss-20b推理基准测试中实现了2.12%的吞吐量提升；在Intel Arc B580后端上，针对37个GEMM+尾部算子组合工作负载，平均加速比达到5.13倍。

Conclusion: KForge展示了基于LLM代理的协同迭代机制在解决异构硬件后端自动算子优化问题上的有效性，能够通过自动化生成和性能调优实现甚至超越手写基准的表现。

Abstract: Production inference increasingly targets a heterogeneous mix of accelerators. Agentic pipelines interleave reasoning, tool calls, and multi-agent coordination, each with distinct compute and memory profiles. For optimal efficiency, each stage should run on the accelerator best suited to it. This creates a systems challenge: each pipeline now requires high-performance kernels across a growing set of hardware backends and programming models. Writing these kernels by hand is time-consuming, demands deep low-level expertise, and does not scale as kernel complexity grows. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have been leveraged for automatic kernel generation, but challenges in low-level code generation and cross-backend generalization persist. We present KForge, a cross-platform framework built around an iterative refinement loop driven by two collaborating LLM-based agents: a generation agent that produces and progressively refines kernels using compilation and correctness feedback, and a performance-analysis agent that interprets profiling data, from programmatic APIs to GUI-based tools, and emits recommendations that steer the next round of synthesis. The loop alternates between functional passes, which drive a candidate to correctness, and optimization passes, which close the performance gap to hand-tuned baselines. We evaluate KForge on two backends with very different baseline reference availability. On NVIDIA B200, KForge achieves a 2.12$\%$ improvement in end-to-end throughput compared to TensorRT-LLM on the gpt-oss-20b inference speed benchmark. On Intel Arc B580, KForge generates Triton kernels achieving a 5.13$\times$ geometric mean speedup over the faster of PyTorch eager and torch.compile on 37 GEMM + tail-ops workloads from KernelBench Level 2, primarily via operator fusion and mixed-precision execution.

</details>


### [229] [CoughSense: Five-Class Respiratory Disease Classification via Whisper Encoder Fine-Tuning and Dual-Encoder Cross-Attention Fusion with Balanced Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02998)
*Nikhil Vincent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoughSense 是一款利用 Whisper 架构实现五类呼吸系统疾病咳嗽分类的系统，通过创新的“Active-frame QKV attention pooling”技术解决了短音频在长上下文模型中的有效性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化咳嗽分析大多仅局限于二分类（如 COVID-19 检测），缺乏区分多种呼吸系统疾病的能力。同时，将短音频输入长上下文模型（如 Whisper）时存在“静音稀释”问题，导致模型性能下降。

Method: 1. 基于 OpenAI Whisper 编码器作为骨干网络。
2. 提出 Active-frame QKV attention pooling 技术，仅聚焦前 200 个 Token 以解决静音填充问题。
3. 采用多种训练策略应对数据不平衡与域偏移（WeightedRandomSampler, SpecAugment, Balanced Mixup, 监督对比损失, FiLM 条件化, 域对抗训练）。
4. 实现双编码器模型，通过交叉注意力（Cross-attention）融合 Whisper 与 OPERA-CT 基础模型。

Result: 1. CoughSense (Whisper-tiny) 达到 82.3% 的平衡准确率（F1: 0.817, AUC: 0.941）。
2. 在五类疾病中，所有类别的召回率均超过 74%，其中四类超过 80%。
3. 双编码器模型性能进一步提升至 85.4% 的平衡准确率。
4. Active-frame pooling 对性能提升最为显著（5.1个百分点）。

Conclusion: CoughSense 通过引入 Active-frame QKV attention pooling 有效解决了短音频在大型长上下文编码器中的“静音稀释”问题，证明了利用预训练语音模型进行多类别呼吸系统疾病分类的有效性，为低成本呼吸筛查提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated cough analysis offers a path to low-cost respiratory screening, but most existing work stops at binary COVID-19 detection. A practical tool needs to tell apart several respiratory conditions from one cough recording on a consumer smartphone. We present CoughSense, a system that sorts cough recordings into five classes. These are healthy, COVID-19, asthma or respiratory condition, bronchitis, and pneumonia. We aggregated 18,301 recordings from four public datasets (Coswara, CoughVID, Virufy, and the West China Hospital Pediatric Cough Dataset) and used the OpenAI Whisper encoder as a pretrained backbone for cough disease classification. The main contribution is active-frame QKV attention pooling, which restricts attention to the first 200 of 1500 encoder tokens. This avoids the silence-dilution problem that arises because a 3-second cough fills only 150 tokens of Whisper's 30-second input window. Other training parts handle the 19 to 1 class imbalance and the four-dataset domain shift. These include WeightedRandomSampler, SpecAugment, Balanced Mixup with forced minority pairing, a supervised contrastive auxiliary loss, FiLM symptom conditioning, and gradient-reversal domain adaptation. A dual-encoder model fuses Whisper with the OPERA-CT respiratory foundation model through cross-attention. CoughSense (Whisper-tiny, 8.6M parameters) reached 82.3 percent balanced accuracy on five-fold cross-validation (macro-F1 of 0.817, AUC of 0.941). It beat an ImageNet-pretrained EfficientNet-B2 by 11.1 points and a ViT trained from scratch by 29.6 points. All five classes passed 74 percent recall and four of five passed 80 percent. The dual-encoder model reached 85.4 percent balanced accuracy. Active-frame pooling is the largest single contributor across all ablation components at 5.1 points, which should help any short-audio task using Whisper as a backbone.

</details>


### [230] [How Quantization Changes Interpretable Features: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03002)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现大模型量化虽然能保持任务性能，但会导致大量可解释特征失效。因此，仅凭下游任务指标难以保证可解释性研究在量化模型上的有效性，需建立特征级审计标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化模型通常通过困惑度和下游任务准确率来评估，但缺乏对其内部可解释特征（Interpretability Features）在量化后是否依然有效的验证。这对于依赖这些特征进行安全审计或转向干预的部署场景构成风险。

Method: 以冻结的稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为固定基准，对比全精度模型与舍入到最近（RTN）量化模型的激活分布；通过皮尔逊相关系数衡量特征的存活率；针对Pythia-70M和Gemma-2-2B模型进行从INT8到INT4的位宽扫描；分析特征存活率与全精度统计量之间的预测关系。

Result: 1. 量化导致特征发生系统性降解而非彻底失效（大部分非存活特征呈现模糊化）；2. 特征存活率可基于全精度统计量预测（AUC 0.92-0.97），峰值激活度是关键指标；3. 任务指标会掩盖特征损伤（如Gemma-2-2B中INT7虽提升了困惑度，却破坏了18.7%的特征）；4. 量化与稀疏剪枝在特征破坏模式上高度重叠，揭示了压缩引起的共同脆弱性。

Conclusion: 量化过程中任务指标（如困惑度）的性能保持并不代表模型内部特征的可解释性得以保留。因此，在模型压缩部署中，必须引入特征级别的审计机制，以确保在大规模部署中利用可解释性发现（如安全干预、控制）的有效性。

Abstract: Quantization is a standard path to deploying large language models, and a quantized model is typically judged acceptable when its perplexity or downstream accuracy stays close to the full-precision original. Whether the model still computes in the same way, or whether the interpretable features identified in the full-precision model survive weight rounding, is rarely tested, even as safety audits and steering interventions increasingly rely on those features. We ask whether sparse autoencoder (SAE) features extracted from a dense full-precision model remain faithful once that model is quantized. Using a frozen SAE as a fixed measurement basis, we encode full-precision and round-to-nearest (RTN) quantized activations on identical tokens and quantify per-feature survival by Pearson correlation, sweeping bit-widths from INT8 to INT4 on Pythia-70M and Gemma-2-2B. We find that feature survival is graded: features degrade systematically rather than failing all at once, with 62.4 percent of active features surviving at INT6 on Pythia-70M and 51.3 percent surviving at INT6 on Gemma-2-2B, and with most non-survivors blurred rather than destroyed. Survival is predictable from full-precision statistics alone, with cross-validated AUCs of 0.92 to 0.97 and peak activation as the strongest marginal predictor. Critically, task metrics can miss this damage: on Gemma-2-2B, INT7 improves perplexity while degrading 18.7 percent of features. Finally, quantization and matched-perplexity magnitude pruning damage strongly overlapping feature sets, with Jaccard overlap of 0.79 to 0.86 and damage-score Spearman correlation of 0.98, suggesting a shared mode of compression-induced vulnerability. These results show that behavioral parity is insufficient evidence that interpretability findings transfer to quantized deployments, motivating feature-level audits of compression.

</details>


### [231] [Exact equivariance, kept through training, buys zero-shot generalisation across the symmetry group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03003)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用群等变性构建的潜空间世界模型，通过数学上的严格不变性，使其仅需在部分数据上训练即可精确外推到整个对称空间，显著提升了模型在动力学预测和闭环控制中的泛化能力与参数效率，有效解决了长程预测中的误差积累问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界模型在面对空间对称性转换（如旋转、平移）时，往往需要通过大规模数据增强或增加模型容量来拟合，导致泛化能力弱、误差随步数增长且效率低下。本文旨在研究如何利用对称性（ equivariance）减少搜索空间，实现仅凭部分状态推导全轨道动力学。

Method: 提出了一种由等变编码器（E）和等变预测器（f）组成的潜空间世界模型。利用群表示论，通过约束模型学习过程中的正交表示ρ(g)，确保在群操作下的动力学损失函数具有数学上的严格不变性（即“举一反三”）。通过端到端训练（Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg）验证了该框架在2D/3D环境及SE(3)群下的鲁棒性和精确度。

Result: 1. 相比非等变基准，等变模型在2D/3D及SE(3)空间的分布外误差显著降低（最高可提升至157倍）。2. 等变模型参数量减少了4.5-7.4倍。3. 在闭环控制中，轨迹预测实现了近乎“浮点精度”的不变性。4. 等变性在长步长滚动（Rollout）下能够抑制误差累积，保持平稳。

Conclusion: 通过构建等变隐空间世界模型（Equivariant World Model），实现了对动力学群演化的数学不变性，从而解决了非等变模型在分布外（OOD）预测误差随时间积累及空间跨度显著增大的问题，证明了等变性在样本效率和泛化稳定性上的显著优势。

Abstract: A latent world model built from an equivariant encoder $E$ and an equivariant predictor $f$ inherits a provable symmetry of its training loss: when the world's dynamics genuinely carries a group $G$ acting on latents by an orthogonal representation $ρ(g)$, the one-step prediction relMSE is exactly invariant across the whole group, so fitting the dynamics on a restricted slice of orientations mathematically determines it on the entire orbit (jǔ yī fǎn sān). We verify this end-to-end at laptop scale (CPU/MPS, fully seeded). [A] The symmetry survives a real Muon/AdamW + EMA + VICReg run -- composed encode-then-predict residual $\sim 10^{-6}$ after optimisation, not just at initialisation, and under any optimiser. [B] One-step error is flat to five digits across the group, while a same-hypothesis-class non-equivariant baseline fits the slice but breaks out-of-distribution (VN $\times 1.00$ vs baseline $\times 13.8$ in 2D, $\times 17.2$ in 3D, $\times 157$ over the full $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ ladder), with the equivariant model $4.5$-$7.4\times$ smaller. [C] The same isometry argument lifts to closed loop: under a matching equivariant planner the control trajectory at orientation $g$ is exactly $ρ(g)$ applied to the seen one, so closed-loop error is invariant across the group -- float-floor-exact in 2D/$\mathrm{SO}(2)$ on real PushT and statistically flat in 3D/$\mathrm{SE}(3)$ (disjoint 95% CIs). We stress-test the prior against Sutton's Bitter Lesson: augmentation, brute-force scale, and soft-equivariance each close at most the across-group task metric, never the float-floor exactness. Because equivariance is closed under composition, the $H$-fold rollout stays flat ($\times 1.00$, $\le 2\times 10^{-7}$) at every horizon, while the baseline's residual compounds with $H$. Out of scope: task-success sweeps, planner-free invariance, and scaling.

</details>


### [232] [ConTraIRL: Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03017)
*Yikang Gui,Bikramjit Banerjee,Prashant Doshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ConTraIRL 框架，通过引入双编码器和双重对比学习目标，将环境动力学和任务目标解耦，实现了更具可靠性和组合性的逆强化学习奖励迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆强化学习（IRL）在环境动力学和任务目标发生组合变动时，奖励迁移能力不可靠，难以在未见过的组合场景中实现有效泛化。

Method: 提出 ConTraIRL 框架，采用双编码器架构，利用双重对比学习目标（Dual Contrastive Objective）：通过时间对齐（Temporal Alignment）约束动力学编码器捕获目标不变的结构特征，通过对比机制使目标编码器捕获动力学不变的特征。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试中，ConTraIRL 在面对未见的动力学与目标匹配时，表现出显著的少样本迁移能力，其样本效率和奖励恢复准确度均优于基准 IRL 方法。

Conclusion: ConTraIRL 通过解耦环境动力学和任务目标，成功实现了具有组合性的跨环境/任务的奖励函数迁移，在未见过的配置下展现了优越的鲁棒性和样本效率，证明了表示学习中结构化解耦对于增强强化学习迁移能力的重要性。

Abstract: Reward transfer in Inverse Reinforcement Learning (IRL) is unreliable when policies must generalize to unseen combinations of environment dynamics and task goals. We propose Factorized Contrastive Abstractions for Transferable IRL (ConTraIRL), a framework that enables compositional reward transfer by learning decoupled latent representations of these two factors. ConTraIRL uses a dual-encoder architecture that maps observations into separate dynamics and goal latent spaces, trained with a dual contrastive objective. Temporal alignment encourages the dynamics encoder to learn goal-invariant structure, while the goal encoder captures dynamics-invariant features. This factorization supports reward inference under recombined dynamics-goal settings. Experiments on continuous control benchmarks demonstrate effective few-shot transfer to unseen dynamics-goal pairings, improving sample efficiency and reward recovery over transfer IRL baselines.

</details>


### [233] [Will Accurate Fields Mislead Photonic Design? FromGlobal Accuracy to Port Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03038)
*Yitian Zhang,Yonghong chen,Youming Chen,Yiyang Li,Xing Zhe,Renhe Lu,Shaolin Liao,Yuzhe Ma,Zhong Guan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对光子设计中“全局场准确但器件性能指标偏差”的问题，本文提出了PaNO模型，通过将神经算子的潜状态与光传播物理特性（模态、传播路径、端口输出）对齐，大幅提升了设计指标的预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经场替代模型虽然在全局场误差（cMAE）上表现良好，但在光子器件设计（如MMI分束器）中，最终决策依赖于局部输出端口的读数。全局场精度的高低并不直接代表设计指标（如端口功率、相位、耦合）的准确性，导致模型在器件排序时可能出现决策偏差。

Method: 提出“场/中介/读出”（Field/Mediator/Readout）分析框架，以此设计了传播对齐神经算子（PaNO）。该模型摒弃了单一的全局场预测，转而将潜状态组织为局部边界结构、横向模态内容、轴向传播和跨模式交互。同时引入PaNO-R2变体，在端口区域增加输出感知反馈以处理残余场组件。

Result: 在3x3 MMI基准测试中，PaNO显著降低了端口功率误差（从0.2018降至0.0739）。PaNO-R2则实现了最优表现，将端口功率和输出轮廓误差分别降低了72.7%和72.5%。

Conclusion: PaNO和PaNO-R2通过针对光子器件设计链路（特别是多模干涉仪MMI）进行架构优化，成功解决了神经场替代模型在全局场精度与关键设计指标（如端口功率）之间不匹配的问题，证明了为特定下游任务定制架构和反馈机制的重要性。

Abstract: Neural field surrogates can accelerate photonic design loops, but a surrogate that looks accurate in global field error can still mis-rank candidate devices when the final decision depends on localized output-port readouts. This risk is acute in propagation-dominated MMI splitters and couplers, where port power, splitting, phase, and coupling are determined by accumulated modal interference and output-window aggregation rather than by average field similarity alone. We study this field-to-design mismatch through a Field/Mediator/Readout view that separates dense complex-field error from propagation-profile and output-window errors before port aggregation. To align the surrogate with this chain, we propose PaNO, a propagation-aligned neural operator that keeps the full-field prediction interface while organizing latent states around local boundary structure, transverse modal content, axial propagation, and cross-mode interaction. We also evaluate PaNO-R2, an output-aware feedback variant for residual field components near the port region. On a 15-wavelength tunable $3{\times}3$ MMI benchmark with 4608 held-out fields, PaNO lowers NeurOLight's port-power error from 0.2018 to 0.0739 despite slightly higher cMAE, showing that global field accuracy alone is not sufficient for design-relevant readout fidelity. PaNO-R2 attains the best cMAE, propagation-profile error, output-profile error, and port-power error, reducing NeurOLight's port-power and output-profile errors by 72.7\% and 72.5\%.

</details>


### [234] [What Do Students Learn? A Feature-Level Analysis of Dark Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03052)
*Seungu Kang,Songkuk Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过分析发现KD的作用是修剪样本特定的低频特征，据此提出了一种名为“混淆蒸馏(CD)”的无需教师模型的高效自我蒸馏方法，在性能上超越了现有同类技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前知识蒸馏(KD)的具体机制尚不明确，论文旨在剖析学生模型如何获得特征表示，并寻找更高效的蒸馏替代方案。

Method: 利用“交互张量(Interaction Tensor)”框架分析学生模型特征学习过程；提出“混淆蒸馏(Confusion Distillation, CD)”，通过模型自身的混淆矩阵指导训练。

Result: 在CIFAR-100数据集上的ResNet-34和ResNet-50模型上，CD方法优于CS-KD和PS-KD等现有的自我蒸馏方法1.2%。

Conclusion: 通过利用模型自身演化的混淆矩阵作为动态软目标，提出的“混淆蒸馏(CD)”方法不仅验证了KD背后的理论机制，还提供了一种无需教师模型的、高效且高性能的模型自我蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) is a powerful tool for model compression, yet the precise mechanisms by which student models acquire feature representations remain underexplored. In this work, we analyze student feature learning using the Interaction Tensor framework. Our analysis reveals that effective KD acts as a regularizer that prunes low-frequency, sample-specific features, encouraging the student to rely on a compact set of highly reusable features. Crucially, we observe that the dataset-level confusion matrix contains structural information analogous to the teacher's "Dark Knowledge." Leveraging this insight, we propose Confusion Distillation (CD), a teacher-free self-distillation method that utilizes the model's own evolving confusion patterns as dynamic soft targets. CD achieves competitive performance on ResNet-34 and ResNet-50 for CIFAR-100, outperforming existing self-distillation methods like CS-KD and PS-KD by 1.2% while offering a computationally efficient alternative to standard KD.

</details>


### [235] [Rethinking Molecular Text Representations for LLMs: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03057)
*Arun Raja,Garrett M. Morris,Kian Ming A. Chai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对16个大语言模型在9种分子表示下的化学任务表现进行了系统性基准测试，发现没有单一表示法适用于所有任务，并揭示了模型对不同分子表示的编码机制差异，从而提出了开发任务感知的分子表示路由技术的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前LLM在分子任务中被广泛应用，但尚不清楚哪种分子表示法（Representation）最适合，且现有的评估多基于单一表示，缺乏系统性比较。

Method: 通过基准测试，在9种分子表示和8项化学任务下，评估了5个模型系列共16个LLM（包括通用推理模型、化学专用模型及闭源前沿模型），并辅以token审计、线性探测和注意力机制等机理分析。

Result: 结构化文本（CML、MolJSON）在结构任务中表现最优；IUPAC在语义任务中表现最佳；SMILES尽管在预训练中常用，但在多项任务中表现并非最优。此外，化学专用模型过度依赖SMILES，导致在处理结构化表示时性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 分子表示方式对LLM的化学任务性能影响巨大，不存在通用的最优表示。研究建议应摒弃表示无关的评估方法，并推动面向特定任务的分子表示路由策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for molecular tasks, but it remains unclear which molecular representation to use. We present a systematic benchmark evaluating LLM molecular competence across nine representations and eight chemical tasks. We benchmark 16 LLMs across five model families, including reasoning and non-reasoning variants, chemistry-specialized LLMs, and closed frontier models. Performance is strongly representation-dependent and no single representation wins across tasks, though CML is the best, followed by MolJSON, InChI, and then canonical SMILES. Explicit structured text representations (CML and MolJSON) dominate structural tasks; IUPAC dominates semantic tasks, winning molecule retrieval for all 16 LLMs; and SMILES variants are rarely optimal despite their prevalence in pretraining. Chemistry-specialized models perform well with SMILES at the cost of large degradations with structured text representations, suggesting SMILES-only evaluation rewards specialization that does not generalize. Using LLM-as-a-judge, we find that IUPAC produces the highest fraction of correct molecule generations. A mechanistic study via tokenization audits, linear probes and attention shows that representations are encoded differently inside the model; for example, structured representations require higher attention across the molecular span. Our results argue against representation-invariant evaluation and motivate task-aware representation routing for LLM-based chemistry.

</details>


### [236] [Learn When and Where to Connect: Adaptive Virtual Nodes for Dynamic Message Passing on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03068)
*Jaejun Lee,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAVN 是一种动态、可微分的 MPNN 增强框架，通过按需自适应引入虚拟节点并利用双视角评分机制优化连接，克服了传统虚拟节点方法的硬性约束，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于虚拟节点（VN）的 MPNN 模型存在连接受限、连接模式静态化以及节点与 VN 间交互独立性不足的问题，限制了消息传递的灵活性与深度。

Method: 提出了 MAVN (Multi-Adaptive Virtual Nodes) 框架。该方法引入了可微分的连接机制，利用双视角评分策略（节点对虚拟节点的偏好与虚拟节点对节点的偏好）动态决定虚拟节点的引入层级、数量及其覆盖的节点子集。

Result: 在九个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，MAVN 能持续提升主流 MPNN 主干模型的性能，最高提升幅度达 46.5%，并优于现有的基准方法。此外，文中从理论上证明了 MAVN 能够模拟任意节点-VN 连接模式。

Conclusion: MAVN 通过提出端到端的动态虚拟节点构建机制，解决了传统固定虚拟节点架构的局限性，实现了更灵活、更高效的跨层消息传递，显著提升了图神经网络的表达能力和性能。

Abstract: While Virtual Nodes (VNs) are often utilized in Message Passing Neural Networks (MPNNs) to facilitate effective message passing, existing VN-based methods have limitations, such as constraining all nodes to connect to the same number of VNs, fixing the connections before applying MPNNs, and connecting a node to a VN independently of the other nodes that connect to the same VN. We propose MAVN, an end-to-end differentiable MPNN framework that allows non-constrained connections between nodes and VNs and dynamically introduces VNs on demand in response to evolving node representations across layers. Specifically, MAVN learns to adaptively determine when (at which layer) and where (to which nodes) to introduce and connect VNs based on the relative importance of connections. From a pool of candidate VNs, MAVN selects the necessary VNs in each layer, where each selected VN is connected to a nonempty subset of nodes, guided by a dual-perspective scoring mechanism that jointly captures the nodes' preferences for VNs and the VNs' preferences for nodes. We theoretically prove that for any node-VN connectivity pattern, there exists a set of MAVN's parameters that can simulate the pattern. Experiments on nine real-world datasets demonstrate that MAVN consistently improves the performance of backbone MPNNs, achieving up to 46.5% improvement over the backbones and outperforms the baselines.

</details>


### [237] [ASymPO: Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization for Asynchronous LLM Post-Training Without Behavior Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03070)
*Zehua Liu,Yuxuan Yao,Xiaojin Fu,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对异步强化学习中的分布偏移及规模失衡问题，提出 ASymPO 算法，通过当前策略的平均负对数概率归一化损失，在无需行为策略（Behavior Policy）的情况下实现了高效稳定的模型后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习虽然能提升语言模型后训练吞吐量，但因滞后响应（stale responses）导致的分布偏移问题，传统方法依赖行为策略概率（behavior-policy probabilities）进行校正，这在工程实现上存在版本对齐和数值一致性约束。作者致力于探索仅使用当前策略概率来实现异步 RL 的稳定性。

Method: 1. 识别了异步 RL 中由于策略滞后导致的“规模失衡（scale-imbalance）”失效模式；
2. 提出“非对称规模策略优化（ASymPO）”，通过当前策略的平均 Token 负对数概率对每个响应的 Token 损失进行归一化；
3. 引入“规模化策略优化（SPO）”作为一种基于固定负缩放的 Baseline。

Result: ASymPO 有效恢复了响应级别的零和平衡，并保留了有效的学习信号，在异步数学推理任务的后训练中表现优异。

Conclusion: ASymPO 通过归一化处理解决了异步 RL 中的规模失衡问题，证明了仅依赖当前策略概率即可稳定异步训练，且无需行为策略概率，能够有效提升数学推理任务的强化学习效率。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning can improve language-model post-training throughput by decoupling response generation from policy optimization, but stale responses introduce distribution drift. Standard behavior-corrected methods control this drift with behavior-policy probabilities, importance ratios, or clipping, which requires token-aligned, versioned, and numerically consistent behavior log-probabilities across rollout and learner systems. We ask whether asynchronous group-relative RL can instead be stabilized using only current-policy probabilities. We identify a scale-imbalance failure mode: when stale responses are evaluated under the current policy, positive and negative loss terms can appear at different negative log-probability scales, so zero-sum advantages no longer imply balanced loss contributions. We propose Asymmetric-Scale Policy Optimization (ASymPO), which normalizes each response's token loss by its current average token negative log-probability. ASymPO requires no behavior-policy probabilities, restores response-level zero-sum balance, and preserves a nonzero learning signal. We also introduce Scaled Policy Optimization (SPO), a fixed negative-scaling baseline, and evaluate both current-policy-only objectives in asynchronous mathematical reasoning post-training.

</details>


### [238] [Efficient Hyperparameter Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03073)
*Minping Chen,Bowen Xiao,Du Liang,Chuxuan Zeng,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 强化学习中超参数优化昂贵的问题，本文提出了 JF-HPO。该方法通过联合调整模型规模和训练时长（多保真度），结合代理模型、改进的早停策略及高效检查点机制，大幅降低了计算成本并显著提升了模型最终性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习（RL）微调 LLM 对超参数高度敏感，而传统的超参数优化（HPO）在 LLM 规模下计算代价极其昂贵，亟需一种更高效的搜索方案。

Method: 提出联合高保真超参数优化（JF-HPO）框架，核心机制包括：(i) 利用目标 LLM 的小型代理模型进行快速训练评估；(ii) 基于训练动态设计的早期停止策略；(iii) 高效的检查点（checkpointing）保存机制以规避冗余计算。

Result: 与现有 HPO 方法相比，JF-HPO 将单次试验效率提升了 14.9 倍；在相同时间预算下，性能超越基线，且相比 VeRL Recipe 配置，性能提升幅度达 5.8% 至 111.6%。

Conclusion: JF-HPO 是一种高效的 LLM RL 超参数优化方法，通过联合调整模型规模和训练预算，在显著降低算力成本的同时，实现了优于基线的模型性能，证明了多维度协同优化在超资源密集型任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is highly sensitive to hyperparameter configurations, making hyperparameter optimization (HPO) essential yet computationally expensive. Existing multi-fidelity HPO methods remain inefficient for LLM RL due to the massive model scale and resource-intensive training cycles. In this paper, we propose Joint Fidelity Hyperparameter Optimization (JF-HPO), which simultaneously adapts both model size and training budget as fidelity. JF-HPO is empowered by: (i) it leverages a small proxy model of the target LLM for efficient training and evaluation in each HPO trial; (ii) it integrates carefully designed early-stopping strategies based on training dynamics; (iii) it introduces an efficient checkpointing mechanism to eliminate redundant computations. Compared with existing HPO methods, JF-HPO significantly improves the computational efficiency of each trial (up to 14.9 times), while achieving better or competitive predictive accuracy under the same time budget. Notably, compared with utilizing hyperparameter configurations from the VeRL Recipe, JF-HPO delivers performance improvements ranging from 5.8% to 111.6%.

</details>


### [239] [RMPrior: Bridging Propagation Priors and Diffusion Refinement for Efficient Radio Map Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03074)
*Zixuan Guo,Xiucheng Wang,Nan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散模型构建无线电地图计算成本高的问题，本文提出从传播模型先验出发进行中点启动采样，实现了显著的加速与精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然能实现高保真度的无线电地图构建，但其迭代去噪的采样成本过高，无法满足动态无线系统中频繁刷新的需求。现有的扩散推理往往从纯高斯噪声开始，完全丢弃了经典传播模型中蕴含的场景级先验知识。

Method: 提出了一种“中点启动采样策略”（mid-start sampling strategy）。具体而言，将匹配的传播模型先验扰动至扩散过程的某个中间时间步，然后仅运行剩余的扩散反向步骤，利用扩散模型进行多径感知细化，替代从纯噪声开始的全程重建。此外，文中还通过理论分析给出了初始化误差上界及截断条件的充分性分析。

Result: 在 IRT4HighRes 数据集上，当 $P_{\text{start}}=0.5$ 时，该方法实现了 $2.01\times$ 的加速，并在 NMSE、RMSE、SSIM 和 PSNR 指标上均优于全步骤基线模型。消融实验证实重建精度与先验质量相关，且符合理论预测。

Conclusion: 本文提出的中点启动（mid-start）采样策略有效地结合了传播模型先验与扩散模型生成能力，在大幅降低无线电地图构建推理成本的同时，证明了减少推理步骤不仅能提升速度，还能通过利用先验信息改善重建精度，且该方法可作为评估传播模型忠实度的工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high-fidelity radio map construction through iterative denoising, yet their sampling cost limits practicality in dynamic wireless systems where radio maps must be refreshed repeatedly. Meanwhile, classical propagation models encode valuable scene-level knowledge that standard diffusion inference discards entirely by initializing from pure Gaussian noise. This paper bridges propagation priors and diffusion refinement through a mid-start sampling strategy. A matched propagation prior is perturbed to an intermediate diffusion timestep, and the pretrained diffusion backbone executes only the remaining reverse steps, focusing computation on multipath-aware refinement rather than full reconstruction from noise. We provide theoretical analysis establishing an upper bound on the initialization gap, a sufficient condition under which truncation improves reconstruction fidelity, and a formal characterization of prior-quality sensitivity under aggressive truncation. Experiments on IRT4HighRes show that, at $P_{\text{start}}=0.5$, the proposed method achieves a $2.01\times$ speedup while simultaneously improving NMSE, RMSE, SSIM, and PSNR over the full-step baseline. A prior-quality ablation across three propagation models of different fidelity confirms that reconstruction quality tracks prior quality, with the sensitivity amplified under shorter reverse trajectories, consistent with the theoretical predictions. These results also suggest that mid-start reconstruction quality can serve as a proxy for ranking the scene-level fidelity of different propagation models.

</details>


### [240] [Multi-component Causal Tracing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03085)
*Zirui Yan,Dennis Wei,Dmitriy A. Katz,Prasanna Sattigeri,Ali Tajer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的多组件因果追踪框架，通过将复杂的组合搜索问题转化为连续优化问题，高效识别大模型中影响特定性能指标的关键组件子集（如注意力头和神经元）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果追踪研究主要集中在单一组件或单层，无法分析大语言模型中多个组件之间的复杂交互作用，且难以系统地量化多个组件对特定目标指标的联合影响。

Method: 提出了一种统一的多组件因果追踪框架。利用“软干预”（soft interventions）结合特定的指标变换，将复杂的组合搜索问题转化为连续优化问题，从而高效识别关键组件子集并输出二元决策。

Result: 实验证明该方法能够在大规模模型中高效识别出对目标指标具有高影响力的关键组件子集，且性能优于现有的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的多组件因果追踪框架通过高效的算法有效地识别了对大语言模型特定指标（如准确性和公平性）影响最大的组件子集，优于现有的单组件分析方法。

Abstract: Causal tracing systematically intervenes on a large language model's (LLM's) internal representations to uncover and quantify the causal pathways linking specific inputs or computations to specific metrics of interest, quantifying the LLM's behavior. Building on previous single-component or single-layer studies, this paper presents a unified framework for causally tracing multiple components simultaneously. This framework systematically identifies the subsets of components (e.g., attention heads and multi-layer perceptron neurons) most critical to a desired target performance metric (e.g., accuracy and fairness). This is achieved by incorporating flexible interventions applied to a wide range of desired metrics. To address the combinatorial complexity of the multi-component problem, an efficient algorithm is designed that leverages soft interventions and a carefully designed metric transformation, converting the combinatorial search problem into a continuous one that can be solved efficiently under proper constraints, thereby generating proper binary decisions for selecting components. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method efficiently identifies subsets of the model's components that have a high impact on the target metric, outperforming existing baseline approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZiruiYan/multi-component-causal-tracing.

</details>


### [241] [Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03089)
*Ming Wen,Yuxuan Liu,Kun Yang,Yunhao Feng,Zhuoer Xu,Yuhao Sun,Shiwen Cui,Xiang Zheng,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对安全对齐中在线策略蒸馏导致模型表达能力丧失的问题，本文提出了 COPSD 算法，通过两阶段训练成功缓解了安全压力导致的“几何泄漏”，显著提升了模型的安全与有用性比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）在强化模型时很有效，但在安全对齐任务中存在“模型塌陷”问题，即受到宪法约束的教师模型倾向于生成简短、保守的回复，并在反向 KL 散度作用下导致模型表达能力下降。

Method: 1. 理论分析：形式化定义了安全边界下的“几何泄漏”（Geometric Leakage）现象；2. 改进算法：提出 Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation (COPSD)，包括交叉 SFT (Cross-SFT) 冷启动阶段和随后的宪法条件在线策略蒸馏。

Result: 实验表明，COPSD 在 12 个基准测试中表现优于基准方法，在保持高安全性的同时，显著降低了对模型通用推理能力的“安全税”（safety tax）。

Conclusion: COPSD 通过交叉 SFT 冷启动和宪法条件在策略蒸馏的结合，有效解决了安全领域在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中的模型塌陷问题，实现了更好的安全性与有用性平衡。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as an efficient post-training paradigm by using a teacher conditioned on privileged information to provide dense token-level supervision. Prior work has shown that OPSD can collapse in verifiable reasoning tasks, but safety alignment differs in that it is guided by high-level constitutions rather than explicit target answers, making it a natural setting to revisit dense distillation. However, our pilot study show that safety OPSD still suffers from severe collapse: constitutional conditioning contracts the teacher distribution toward short and overly conservative responses, and Reverse KL further amplifies this contraction into reduced expressiveness. We formalize this effect as geometric leakage under safety boundaries in a non-orthogonal semantic space, where safety pressure transfers into the expressiveness dimension. Based on this analysis, we propose Constitutional On-Policy Safe Distillation (COPSD), which first calibrates the teacher through a Cross-SFT cold-start and then performs constitution-conditioned on-policy distillation. Experiments on 12 benchmarks show that COPSD achieves a consistently stronger safety--helpfulness trade-off than baselines while substantially reducing the safety tax on general reasoning ability.

</details>


### [242] [FGRPO: Federated GRPO with Adaptive Aggregation on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03094)
*Pengyu Chen,Shaowei Li,Kai Wang,Yunsheng Yuan,Kai Han,Jun Luo,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对GRPO训练过程中的隐私泄露与异构任务稳定性问题，提出了一种联邦GRPO（FGRPO）框架，通过引入相对性能增益的自适应聚合策略实现了隐私保护下的稳健分布式推理模型优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然GRPO在推理模型训练中表现优异，但其数据收集过程面临严重的隐私风险；同时，在联邦学习场景下，异构数据所有者之间任务难度和回报尺度的差异会导致训练不稳定。

Method: 提出了联邦组相对策略优化（Federated GRPO, FGRPO）框架，并设计了一种基于相对性能增益（relative performance gain）的自适应聚合机制，以处理异构任务中的非独立同分布（non-IID）数据及其导致的回报尺度偏差。

Result: FGRPO能够在保证数据隐私的情况下，有效处理异构任务间的奖励差异，并确保模型在非独立同分布数据上的稳健收敛。

Conclusion: FGRPO框架通过引入基于相对性能增益的自适应聚合机制，成功地在保护数据隐私的前提下，实现了异构数据下推理模型的稳定联邦强化学习训练。

Abstract: Recent advances in language models have established reinforcement learning as the primary paradigm for eliciting self-correction and long-chain reasoning. While group relative policy optimization (GRPO) offers superior scalability by eliminating the critic network, deploying it on a central infrastructure entails collecting a large volume of data from distributed owners, which poses significant privacy risks. To address these concerns, we introduce federated GRPO (FGRPO), a framework designed to decentralize the fine-tuning of reasoning models across heterogeneous data owners. To effectively mitigate the instability caused by divergent reward scales across heterogeneous tasks, FGRPO incorporates an adaptive aggregation mechanism based on relative performance gain. By characterizing each client's improvement relative to its personalized historical baseline, the framework dynamically prioritizes effective learning trajectories regardless of local task difficulty. FGRPO ensures robust convergence on non-IID data while preserving data privacy.

</details>


### [243] [Learning to See via Epiretinal Implant Stimulation in silico with Model-Based Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03118)
*Jacob Lavoie,Marwan Besrour,William Lemaire,Jean Rouat,Réjean Fontaine,Eric Plourde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过深度强化学习优化视网膜假体的电刺激渲染，巧妙地将各向异性“笔触”与各向同性“像素”结合，显著提升了人工视觉的成像辨识度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜植入物刺激会导致轴突束被激活，产生非预期的各向异性（笔触状）视觉伪影，限制了图像的可理解性。现有研究旨在通过避免轴突刺激来消除伪影，但本研究试图反其道而行之，利用形状建模优化成像质量。

Method: 利用强化学习（RL）环境（rlretina）进行基于笔触的渲染任务；采用经心理物理学验证的轴突映射模型生成模拟图像；通过对比不同误差及感知度量指标来评估强化学习Agent，实现各向异性光幻视（Phosphenes）的优化组合。

Result: 通过训练深度强化学习Agent，成功实现了对各向同性与各向异性形状的自动组合。实验结果表明，与传统方法相比，该Agent能为不同虚拟受试者生成更清晰、可理解性更高的视觉图像。

Conclusion: 本研究提出了一种结合各向同性及各向异性形状的强化学习视觉渲染策略，有效提升了视网膜假体成像的可理解性，为人工视觉补偿提供了新思路。

Abstract: Objective: Diseases such as age-related macular degeneration and retinitis pigmentosa cause the degradation of the photoreceptor layer. One approach to restore vision is to electrically stimulate the surviving retinal ganglion cells with a microelectrode array such as epiretinal implants. Epiretinal implants are known to generate visible anisotropic shapes elongated along the axon fascicles of neighboring retinal ganglion cells. Recent work has demonstrated that to obtain isotropic pixel-like shapes, it is possible to map axon fascicles and avoid stimulating them by inactivating electrodes or lowering stimulation current levels. Avoiding axon fascicle stimulation aims to remove brushstroke-like shapes in favor of a more reduced set of pixel-like shapes. Approach: In this study, we propose the use of isotropic and anisotropic shapes to render intelligible images on the retina of a virtual patient in a reinforcement learning environment named rlretina. The environment formalizes the task as using brushstrokes in a stroke-based rendering task. Main Results: We train a deep reinforcement learning agent that learns to assemble isotropic and anisotropic shapes to form an image. We investigate which error-based or perception-based metrics is adequate to reward the agent. The agent is trained in a model-based data generation fashion using the psychophysically validated axon map model to render images as perceived by different virtual patients. We show that the agent can generate more intelligible images compared to the naive method in different virtual patients. Significance: This work shares a new way to address epiretinal stimulation that constitutes a first step towards improving visual acuity in artificially-restored vision using anisotropic phosphenes.

</details>


### [244] [Rethinking Neural Width for Alternating Current Optimal Power Flow Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03125)
*Dhruvi Khandelwal,Anurag Basistha,Ayushi Jolotia,Parikshit Pareek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为LG-ND的算法，通过渐进式架构扩容，为ACOPF代理模型找到了“最小化”的神经网络尺寸，在实现高精度近似的同时大幅提升了模型简洁性，适配安全关键电网的验证需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前用于交流最优潮流（ACOPF）的深度学习代理模型缺乏确定架构规模的系统性方法，过度参数化不仅浪费计算资源，还阻碍了安全性要求极高的电网运维中的形式化验证（formal verification）。

Method: 提出了损失引导神经致密化（Loss-Guided Neural Densification, LG-ND）算法。该方法通过在当前模型无法进一步优化损失函数时，增量式地扩展网络拓扑，从而自动确定实现精确近似所需的最小化架构容量。

Result: 在多个IEEE电力系统算例中，LG-ND相较于现有文献基准，在达到同等性能水平的同时，可以将单层神经元数量减少多达十倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过损失引导的架构搜索（LG-ND），可以在保持ACOPF近似精度的同时，显著降低神经网络的复杂度，这对于电力系统安全关键场景下的形式化验证至关重要。

Abstract: Deep learning proxies for Alternating Current Optimal Power Flow (ACOPF) lack systematic methods for determining architectural size. This paper conducts a constructive thought experiment to answer a fundamental inquiry: how wide must a neural network be to almost accurately approximate the ACOPF manifold? We introduce a Loss-Guided Neural Densification (LG-ND) algorithm that incrementally discovers necessary capacity by expanding only when the current deep neural network topology fails to improve further. Empirical results across various IEEE systems show that LG-ND achieves performance parity with literature baselines using up to ten times fewer neurons per layer. Such architectural minimalism is critical for the formal verification required in safety-critical grid operations.

</details>


### [245] [Synthetic Hallucinations, Real Gains: Hard Negatives from Frontier Models for FIM Hallucination Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03130)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种通过合成虚假代码作为负样本进行监督微调的方法，有效解决了小型开源代码模型在IDE自动补全任务中常见的“幻觉”问题，大幅提升了代码生成的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源代码模型在IDE补全任务中常产生语法合理但语义错误（幻觉）的代码，而现有解决方案（沙盒执行、复杂的大规模人类标注）存在部署难度大或反馈循环慢的问题。

Method: 利用前沿大模型针对GitHub语料生成“似是而非”的幻觉代码（hard negatives），结合原始代码作为正样本构建对比数据集，对Qwen2.5-Coder等模型进行监督微调（SFT）。提出了基于Delulu分类法的评价体系，并进行了多维度消融实验。

Result: Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct在Delulu基准上的精确匹配度提升了18.8个百分点，编辑相似度提升了0.22；3B版本模型也获得了显著提升；同时改善了HumanEval-Infilling及多个SAFIM子集的性能。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需执行、基于对比学习的微调方案，通过生成并利用“似是而非”的幻觉代码作为困难负样本，显著提升了小型开源代码模型在FIM（中间填充）任务中的准确性和可靠性，且具有良好的跨语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Small open-source code models that power IDE autocomplete still emit hallucinated Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) completions: syntactically natural calls to methods, parameters, variables, and imports that do not exist in the surrounding project. Existing mitigations either require per-language execution sandboxes that do not apply at mid-keystroke or preference-optimisation pipelines that need large human-labelled corpora. We propose an execution-free alternative: use frontier code models to synthesise plausible-but-wrong completions as hard negatives, then leverage the contrast between these synthetic hallucinations and the ground-truth developer edit as a supervised fine-tuning signal. Our pipeline scrapes multilingual FIM contexts from public GitHub across eight languages and asks a panel of three frontier generators to produce one hard negative per context for each of four hallucination types drawn from the Delulu taxonomy, a Docker-verified multilingual FIM hallucination benchmark, yielding a paired chosen/rejected dataset. Fine-tuning Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct on a 100K-row curated subset lifts Delulu exact match by +18.8 points and edit similarity by +0.22 on every language and every type, while also improving every HumanEval-Infilling split and every SAFIM subset. The same recipe at 3B lifts Delulu by +12.8 EM with a small, characterised general-FIM trade-off. Five-axis ablations (size, type mix, language coverage, base-model family, and a difficulty-aware fool rate) plus a head-to-head SFT vs. DPO/ORPO comparison map which design choices drive the gain. We release the full pipeline source code -- generation, fool-rate LLM judging, curation, and the FIM fine-tuning recipe -- so that the experiments in this paper can be reproduced end-to end on any permissively licensed corpus.

</details>


### [246] [HARVE: Hacking-Aware Reward-Head Vector Editing for Robust Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03131)
*Shuang Liu,Yuxuan Bo,Qiuyang Zhao,Caiyue Huang,Xiaorong Chen,Yanguang Liu,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决大模型奖励模型的脆弱性，本文提出了RewardHackBench基准测试，并设计了一种无需微调的“奖励头编辑”方法（HARVE），通过消除残差流中的欺骗子空间来实现高效鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励模型在语言模型对齐中至关重要，但容易受到“奖励欺骗”（Reward Hacking）的影响，现有模型在这一方面表现出严重脆弱，亟需一种高效且稳健的鲁棒性提升策略。

Method: 1. 构建了包含13种奖励欺骗模式的基准测试集RewardHackBench；2. 提出了HARVE方法：利用对比样本定位残差流中的“欺骗子空间”，并通过修改奖励头的权重向量来移除该子空间，从而抑制模型对欺骗特征的敏感度；3. 该方法无需导数更新或微调。

Result: 在8个奖励模型上的实验表明，HARVE显著提升了模型抵御奖励欺骗的鲁棒性，表现优于传统的fine-tuning基线，同时有效保留了模型原有的通用能力。

Conclusion: 奖励模型中存在多维度的残差空间结构，而非单一表层特征；HARVE通过无需训练的方式有效提升了奖励模型对各类奖励欺骗（Reward Hacking）的鲁棒性，且优于微调方法。

Abstract: Reward models are central to large language model (LLM) alignment, but they remain vulnerable to reward hacking. To evaluate reward-model robustness, we introduce RewardHackBench containing 13 reward-hacking patterns covering real life high-stakes domains and general settings, and we find severe failures on specific subcategories across eight reward models. To mitigate these failures, we propose HARVE, a training-free reward-head editing method for scalar reward models. Instead of fine-tuning the reward model, HARVE identifies a multi-directional hacking subspace from residual stream directions associated with selected hacking subcategories, and removes the component of the reward-head vector aligned with that subspace. This directly reduces the reward head's sensitivity to hacking-related features using only a small set of contrastive gold-hacked examples, without gradient updates or fine-tuning. Comprehensive experiments across eight reward models indicates that \model improves hacking robustness, outperforms fine-tuning baselines, and preserves reward-models' general capability. Further analyses suggest that reward hacking is better captured as a multidimensional residual-space structure than by isolated surface cues.

</details>


### [247] [FederatedSkill: Federated Learning for Agentic Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03143)
*Jingbo Yang,Guanyu Yao,Yang Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Shiyu Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FederatedSkill 是一种保护隐私的联邦智能体进化框架，通过交换“语义技能补丁”而非原始轨迹，在保障用户私密性的同时，实现了跨用户的个性化技能增强与高效进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）智能体依赖技能库提升能力，但孤立的学习缺乏多样性；传统的轨迹共享协作方案存在隐私泄露问题，且强制统一的全局库无法适应客户端的个性化需求。

Method: 引入 FederatedSkill 框架，采用“语义技能差异（Semantic Skill Diffs）”作为通信单位；在服务器端通过聚合这些结构化补丁，动态建模客户端特定的能力边界，实现差异化的个性化技能进化，而非强制统一的全局模型。

Result: 在 20 个任务簇上的评估显示，FederatedSkill 相比自进化基准，任务执行成功率提升最高可达 44.4%，计算成本降低 37.5%。

Conclusion: FederatedSkill 通过联邦学习范式有效地解决了智能体跨用户协作中的隐私保护与个性化需求冲突，证明了基于语义差异补丁的协作进化在提升智能体能力与效率方面的优越性。

Abstract: Modern LLM agents increasingly rely on skill libraries to handle complex tasks, making skill evolution a primary driver of self-improvement. However, isolated single-user task streams lack the diversity required to build comprehensive skills. While cross-user collaboration can overcome this data bottleneck, current trajectory-sharing approaches compromise user privacy and impose a uniform global library that fails to accommodate client heterogeneity. We introduce FederatedSkill, a privacy-preserving framework for collaborative agent evolution. Moving beyond raw trajectory sharing, FederatedSkill utilizes semantic skill diffs, structured patches over local libraries, as the fundamental unit of communication. On the server side, an evolution agent aggregates these patches to dynamically model client-specific capability boundaries, facilitating strictly personalized skill evolution rather than a suboptimal global average. Evaluated across 20 distinct agent task families, FederatedSkill demonstrates substantial gains over self-evolving baselines, achieving up to a 44.4% increase in success rate and a 37.5% reduction in computational cost.

</details>


### [248] [Fast Organic Crystal Structure Prediction with Unit Cell Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03199)
*Alston Lo,Luka Mucko,Austin H. Cheng,Andy Cai,Alastair J. A. Price,Wojciech Matusik,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Clari 是一种秒级有机晶体结构生成模型，通过流匹配与 pair-bias 注意力机制实现高效率与高精度的 CSP，支持复杂化学结构且无需繁琐的预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 CSP 计算成本极高（数年/分子），虽然现有生成模型如 OXtal 改善了这一问题，但其对大尺度块体材料的建模方法仍较慢（分钟/分子），且对输入格式有严格限制。

Method: 提出了一种基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的生成模型 Clari，将任务简化为生成无冗余晶胞，并采用纯 pair-bias 注意力机制替代昂贵的三角层（triangle layers）；模型仅需输入原子类型和键信息，无需 RDKit 可处理的分子输入，并支持直接能量排序。

Result: 在 OXtal 测试集上，求解率超越 OXtal，推理速度提升 15-30 倍；在进行能量排序优化后，求解率进一步提升，且相比基准仍保持 5-8 倍的速度优势；同时引入了包含复杂分子的 CSD Teaching Subset 基准数据集。

Conclusion: Clari 实现了秒级的有机晶体结构预测（CSP），显著降低了计算成本，不仅提升了求解率，还通过明确的氢原子建模支持直接能量排序，使大规模虚拟筛选成为可能。

Abstract: Organic crystal structure prediction (CSP) is a requirement for computational modelling of organic solids, but traditionally costs several CPU-years per molecule. Generative models such as OXtal dramatically reduce this cost by sampling stable organic crystal structures directly. However, OXtal forgoes explicit lattice parametrization in favour of modelling large crops of the bulk material with expensive triangle layers, which can incur a computational cost of minutes per molecule. In this paper, we reduce this to seconds with Clari, a large-scale flow matching model that generates redundancy-free unit cells and replaces triangle layers with pure pair-bias attention. Clari requires only atom types and bonds as input and does not need an RDKit-sanitizable input molecule, which expands its applicability to challenging chemistries such as fullerenes, metal complexes, and atom clusters. We further ablate key design choices such as auxiliary losses, timestep distributions, noise priors, and self-conditioning. On OXtal's test sets, we surpass OXtal's solve rate while obtaining a speedup of $15$-$30\times$. Because Clari also models explicit hydrogens, it supports inference-time scaling via direct energy ranking, without any decoration or relaxation step. When generating 150 crystals and selecting the top-30 by energy, we further improve solve rate while maintaining a speedup of $5$-$8\times$. We also introduce the CSD Teaching Subset as a new test split of diverse and complex molecules for future benchmarking. Our contributions enable CSP within seconds, making large-scale virtual screening of organic solids practical. Code is available at https://github.com/aspuru-guzik-group/clari.

</details>


### [249] [DECA: Decentralizing Block-Wise Adam for Efficient LLM Full-Parameter Fine-Tuning on Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03209)
*Yunsheng Yuan,Shaowei Li,Kai Wang,Zhongyuan Sun,Zheng Zhang,Kai Han,Jun Luo,Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECA 是一种用于大模型分散式全参数微调的高效框架，通过参数块级优化与改进的动量同步机制，解决了算力限制与非独立同分布数据下的训练稳定性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模语言模型（LLM）微调中，现有的分散式微调方案要么因采用全参数微调（FPFT）而面临沉重的算力负担，要么因采用参数高效微调（PEFT）而限制了性能，且无法很好地应对非独立同分布（non-IID）数据带来的客户端偏移和收敛不稳定问题。

Method: 提出 DECA 框架，将模型参数划分为不相交的块（blocks），采用顺序块级 Adam 优化（sequential block-wise Adam optimization），并引入一阶和二阶块级动量估计（结合局部梯度统计和共识差异信号）来稳定训练。

Result: 实验证明 DECA 实现了快速收敛，在保持显著资源效率的同时，达到了媲美或优于现有方法的下游任务性能。

Conclusion: DECA 通过块级优化策略和创新的动量估计，在分散式环境下实现了资源高效且高性能的全参数微调，有效解决了数据非独立同分布（non-IID）带来的收敛不稳定问题。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) in privacy-sensitive and resource-constrained environments remains challenging. Since training data are often distributed across multiple clients, decentralized fine-tuning offers a natural paradigm for collaborative adaptation without a central server. However, enabling full-parameter fine-tuning (FPFT) in this decentralized setting is difficult: FPFT provides strong adaptation capacity but incurs prohibitive resource consumption for billion-scale models. Existing decentralized LLM fine-tuning methods therefore mainly rely on parameter-efficient updates, which improve efficiency but may restrict downstream performance. Moreover, client data are typically non-IID, making decentralized optimization more vulnerable to client drift and unstable convergence. To address these challenges, we propose DECA, a resource-efficient decentralized FPFT framework for LLMs on non-IID data. DECA partitions model parameters into disjoint blocks and performs sequential block-wise Adam optimization, reducing resource consumption while preserving decentralized full-parameter adaptation. To stabilize training, DECA further introduces first- and second-order block-wise moment estimates with fresh local gradient statistics and consensus-derived discrepancy signals. We provide rigorous theoretical analysis and extensive experiments, showing that DECA achieves fast convergence, strong downstream performance, and significant resource efficiency.

</details>


### [250] [Bayesian Tensor Decomposition with Diffusion Model Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03212)
*Zerui Tao,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对传统低秩张量分解在严重损耗下表现受限的问题，本文提出 DiffBCP，通过结合自动秩选择的贝叶斯 CP 分解与预训练扩散模型，利用分治吉布斯采样实现了稳健且高精度的张量重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低秩张量分解在严重缺失或噪声下表现不佳，且手工设计的先验（如稀疏性、平滑度）难以捕捉真实数据的复杂统计特征。现有的扩散模型难以直接与张量分解及其后验推断结合。

Method: 提出 DiffBCP 框架：1. 使用累计收缩过程先验（Cumulative Shrinkage Process Prior）在 CP 分解中自动选择秩；2. 引入预训练扩散模型作为数据驱动的隐式先验；3. 开发分治吉布斯采样器（Split Gibbs Sampler），实现共轭更新与低秩引导的去噪采样；4. 设计噪声自适应耦合调度机制（Noise-adaptive coupling schedule）。

Result: 在图像修复（Inpainting）和去噪任务中，包括高分辨率及分布外（OOD）图像，DiffBCP 均优于现有的贝叶斯模型、非线性方法和“即插即用”（Plug-and-Play）张量分解基准。

Conclusion: DiffBCP 有效结合了低秩先验和扩散模型生成的先验，通过提出的分层吉布斯采样器解决了贝叶斯张量分解在复杂先验下的后验推断难题，并在多种图像修复任务中表现出优于现有基准的性能。

Abstract: Low-rank tensor decomposition (TD) is usually effective on clean, fully observed data, but it often degrades under severe missingness or noise. Low-rankness is itself a useful but limited structural prior, and additional handcrafted priors (e.g., sparsity or smoothness) still fall short of capturing the rich statistics of real-world data. To compensate for this weak inductive bias under heavy corruption, one would like to inject a learned, data-driven prior; however, the state-of-the-art diffusion models are not readily compatible with current TD and tractable posterior inference. To address these challenges, we introduce DiffBCP, a hybrid-prior Bayesian CP decomposition framework that couples a cumulative shrinkage process prior over the CP factors for automatic rank selection with an off-the-shelf pre-trained diffusion model as an implicit data prior on the reconstructed tensor. To make posterior inference tractable despite the coupling among the likelihood, low-rank constraint, and diffusion prior, we develop a split Gibbs sampler: CP factors admit conjugate updates, while the diffusion block is sampled via low-rank-guided denoising. A noise-adaptive coupling schedule further reduces sensitivity to hand-tuned annealing. Experiments on image inpainting and denoising, including high-resolution out-of-distribution images, show consistent gains over Bayesian, nonlinear, and plug-and-play TD baselines.

</details>


### [251] [Learning Temporal Causal Structure via Smooth Differentiable Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03227)
*Tong Zhao,Ce Guo,Wayne Luk,Emil Lupu,Ray Dipojjwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于 Gumbel-Sinkhorn 算子的可微三角化方法，高效解决了多变量时间序列中的瞬时因果发现问题，在保持高性能的同时显著提升了计算效率和扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多变量时间序列瞬时因果发现方法存在计算开销高的问题，主要原因在于其通常采用多阶段流程或复杂的增广拉格朗日优化来强制执行瞬时结构的非循环性约束。

Method: 提出了一种新方法，利用 Gumbel-Sinkhorn 算子学习变量的可微排列（differentiable permutation），并据此对结构向量自回归（SVAR）模型的瞬时系数矩阵进行三角化，从而将硬性非循环约束转化为模型参数化的一部分。

Result: 在三个真实世界基准测试中，该方法在因果发现的准确性和效率上均优于 12 个基线模型。在大规模基准测试中，该方法展现了卓越的扩展性，比同类方法提速超过 6 倍。

Conclusion: 通过引入可微排序策略，将瞬时因果结构的非循环性约束转化为参数化过程，实现了既高效又准确的多变量时间序列因果发现。

Abstract: Causal discovery with instantaneous effects in multivariate time series is challenging, as the instantaneous structure must be acyclic. Prior methods enforce this by either separating instantaneous and lagged estimation into multi-stage pipelines or imposing algebraic acyclicity constraints via complex augmented Lagrangian optimization, both of which incur high computational cost. In this work, we propose a different approach: we learn a differentiable permutation of variables using the Gumbel--Sinkhorn operator and triangularize the instantaneous coefficient matrix of a Structural Vector Autoregressive (SVAR) model in the learned order. This converts acyclicity from a hard constraint into a parameterization and keeps it valid throughout optimization. In doing so, our method enables unified, continuous optimization with gradient-based learning, leading to improved efficiency in time--series causal discovery. Across three real-world benchmarks, our method achieves the best overall performance compared with 12 baselines in both discovery accuracy and efficiency. On the large-scale benchmark, it further demonstrates strong scalability, achieving more than a 6x speedup over competing methods.

</details>


### [252] [GFFMERGE: Efficient Merging of Graph Neural Force Fields and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03232)
*Parth Verma,Parv P. Singh,Vipul Garg,Ishita Thakre,N. M. Anoop Krishnan,Sayan Ranu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GFFMERGE 是首个用于图神经网络的闭式模型融合框架，通过创新利用消息传递层的线性结构，以极低成本解决了神经力场模型难以高效适应新系统的问题，在多个基准测试中实现了性能与效率的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经力场 GNN 模型在适应新化学系统时需要昂贵的重新训练，而源自视觉和语言领域的模型融合方法在原子模拟任务中效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种基于凸嵌入对齐（convex embedding-alignment）问题的闭式求解方案，利用 GNN 中消息传递层的线性结构进行模权重融合，并针对 GNN 的特性提出了 GFFMERGE 及其通用版本 GNNMERGE。

Result: 在 MD17、MD22 和 LiPS20 等数据集上，GFFMERGE 实现了 5-27 倍的加速，且在不进行微调的情况下优于所有基线方法，并为后续微调提供了更优的初始化点。

Conclusion: GFFMERGE 是首个针对图神经网络（GNN）的闭式模型融合框架。它通过有效利用消息传递层的线性结构，在不需要昂贵重新训练的情况下，实现了接近联合训练的性能，显著提升了模型在各种分子和材料模拟任务中的计算效率及模块化能力。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have revolutionized Neural Force Fields for atomistic simulations, achieving near-quantum accuracy at reduced cost, yet adapting these models to new chemical systems requires expensive retraining of foundation models. Inspired by model merging in vision and language processing, we introduce GFFMERGE, the first principled framework for closed-form model merging in GNNs. We exploit the linear structure of message-passing layers and formulate merging as a convex embedding-alignment problem with an analytical solution. Through the first systematic benchmarking of model merging for GNNs, we show that existing methods designed for vision and language catastrophically fail on force field regression, while GFFMERGE recovers performance approaching gold standard joint training. Across molecular (MD17, MD22), solid-state (LiPS20), and large-scale graph benchmarks, GFFMERGE and GNNMERGE (its generic GNN counterpart) achieve 5-27$\times$ speedups while enabling modular composition of specialized models. Remarkably, our closed-form solution alone outperforms all baseline methods before fine-tuning and provides superior initialization for faster, data-efficient convergence.

</details>


### [253] [Right Makes Might: Aligning Verified Hidden States Empowers RL Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03234)
*Ziyue Wang,Aomufei Yuan,Yongfu Zhu,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Yiran Yao,Weichu Xie,Yuqi Xu,Caoyuan Ma,Wenqi Shao,Xiaoying Zhang,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Hidden-Align 通过在强化学习训练中显式对齐正确推理路径在锚点处的隐状态，有效提升了大模型的数学推理能力，无需额外训练或推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化学习方法（RLVR）仅将正确答案视为单个奖励位，忽略了正确推理路径在隐空间中的共同几何结构。作者观察到正确回答的路径在锚点处表现出明显的收敛性（高余弦相似度），认为应显式地利用这种结构来强化一致的“正确决策”表示。

Method: 提出一种名为 Hidden-Align 的辅助损失函数，在强化学习训练过程中，对齐所有正确回答路径在“锚点（anchor token）”处的末层隐状态。

Result: 在八个数学推理基准测试上，Hidden-Align 相比 DAPO 基线显著提升了 Qwen3 不同规模模型（1.7B, 4B, 14B）的 pass@1 和 pass@k 指标，且不增加训练或推理阶段的开销。

Conclusion: 通过在辅助损失中对齐正确推理路径的锚点隐状态，Hidden-Align 有效地增强了模型的数学推理能力，且无额外计算开销。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the dominant approach for improving mathematical reasoning in large language models, yet current methods reduce each correct rollout to a single reward bit, ignoring the geometric structure shared among their hidden states. Investigating this structure, we find that at the anchor token (the position immediately before the answer marker), correct rollouts converge naturally because they must produce the same answer (cosine similarity ~0.84), yet each retains residual variance from its unique reasoning path. Encouraging full alignment at this point pushes the model to extract a unified "correct decision" representation, reducing sensitivity to which reasoning path was taken. Based on this observation, we propose Hidden-Align, an auxiliary loss function that aligns the last-layer hidden states of correct rollouts at the anchor token during RL training, with zero overhead in both training and inference. On eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, Hidden-Align improves average pass@1 over the DAPO baseline by 3.8, 6.2, and 5.4 percentage points on Qwen3-1.7B, 4B, and 14B respectively, with consistent pass@k gains across all three scales, supported by ablations on loss type, anchor position, layer depth, and loss weight.

</details>


### [254] [When RLHF Fails: A Mechanistic Taxonomy of Reward Hacking, Collapse, and Evaluator Gaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03238)
*Zelalem Abahana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对RLHF训练过程中的“奖励劫持”进行了系统性实证研究，证明了这一现象是可以被分类、局部化监测及预测的动态训练过程，而非不可避免的终极病理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF利用可学习的代理目标取代模糊的人类目标，这导致了系统性的失效风险（即奖励劫持），例如奖励提高但模型质量下降。本文旨在深入研究这些失效模式的性质，而非将其视为不可控的偶然事件。

Method: 通过对包含PPO、DPO、UP-PPO等多种算法的紧凑型RLHF流水线进行实证分析，利用奖励模型不确定性、策略漂移监测及外部LLM评测工具，分析了61个检查点及其1920次状态转换。提出了一种基于奖励、评测分数及平均分数的分类方法来识别奖励劫持行为，并利用逻辑回归模型预测奖励劫持风险。

Result: 1. 激进的PPO策略表现出最高的局部奖励劫持率（14.45%），而UP-PPO在同等条件下可降低此比例；2. 局部逐行分析比全局检查点平均值更能发现隐蔽的奖励劫持；3. 构建的逻辑回归模型能够以0.821的ROC-AUC准确预测未来的奖励劫持倾向。

Conclusion: RLHF的失效并非仅发生在最终模型中，而是一种贯穿整个训练过程的动态现象。这些失效模式是可以被分类、定位并部分预测的，研究人员不应仅关注最终结果，而应深入分析训练动力学。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) makes large-scale post-training possible by replacing an underspecified human objective with learned and scalable proxies. The same substitution creates a structured failure surface: optimization can raise the learned reward while external quality falls, degrade both proxy and judge scores, reveal proxy under-alignment, or produce evaluator-specific disagreement. We present an empirical failure-mode study of a compact RLHF pipeline with proximal policy optimization (PPO), direct preference optimization (DPO), uncertainty-penalized PPO (UP-PPO), reward-model uncertainty, approximate policy drift, diversity and repetition diagnostics, and two external LLM judges. Rather than treating reward hacking as a single terminal event, we classify matched transitions between checkpoints using the directions of the learned reward, judge scores, and average judge score. Across 61 checkpoint rows and 1920 row-level transitions, aggressive PPO has the highest localized reward-hacking rate (14.45%; bootstrap 95% CI: 10.16-18.75), while UP-PPO yields lower rates in the same aggressive regime (11.33-10.94%). A pre-transition logistic model predicts future row-level reward hacking with ROC-AUC 0.821, and row-level analysis finds localized reward hacking that checkpoint averages miss in 3 of 12 settings. The central conclusion is methodological: RLHF failures are not only final-model pathologies, but training dynamics that can be classified, localized, and partially anticipated.

</details>


### [255] [EqGINO: Equivariant Geometry-Informed Fourier Neural Operators for 3D PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03260)
*Sungwon Kim,Juho Song,Seungmin Shin,Guimok Cho,Sangkook Kim,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EqGINO通过在谱域强制执行等方性，解决了傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）在3D PDE建模中难以实现几何等变性的难题，实现了高效且具备几何不变性的物理模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习PDE代理模型在处理几何变换时泛化能力差；已有的等变网络计算开销大，而傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）虽然能捕捉全局交互，却难以高效实现3D等变。需要一个既能保持全局感受野又具备几何鲁棒性的框架。

Method: 引入EqGINO框架，通过在谱域利用结构先验（structural prior）强制执行各向同性，规避了传统傅里叶神经算子（FNOs）中代价高昂的谱群卷积，同时保证了对离散对称性的等变性。

Result: EqGINO在几何变换下表现出更强的鲁棒性，即使在SE(3)变换的训练样本有限的情况下，也能有效泛化至任意连续方向。

Conclusion: EqGINO提出了一种在谱域强制执行各向同性的新框架，成功实现了对离散对称性的精确等变性，并有效泛化至连续旋转，从而在复杂几何结构上实现了坐标无关的物理规律建模。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates for 3D Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) often fail to generalize across geometric transformations because they depend heavily on specific coordinate systems. While equivariant networks offer a solution, they typically rely on local operations in the spatial domain, making the global receptive field, which is essential for PDE dynamics, computationally expensive. Conversely, Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) efficiently capture global interactions, yet establishing 3D equivariance within them remains impractical due to the prohibitive cost of spectral group convolutions. To bridge this gap, we introduce EqGINO, a geometrically robust framework that enforces isotropy in the spectral domain. By design, EqGINO guarantees exact equivariance to the discrete symmetries inherent to the discretized computational domain. Beyond this discrete guarantee, our structural prior enables effective generalization to arbitrary continuous orientations even with a limited number of SE(3)-transformed training samples. Consequently, our method robustly models coordinate-invariant physical laws on complex irregular 3D geometries. Our code is available at https://github.com/sung-won-kim/EqGINO

</details>


### [256] [Let There Be Light: Reflection, Refraction and Scattering for Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03262)
*Keke Wu,Yixuan Zhang,Jingrun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiNO 是一种新型神经算子，它通过类比光学的反射、折射和散射机制来解耦局部调制与全局通信，在降低计算复杂度至线性的同时增强了模型的可解释性与扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经算子方法在物理可解释性、全局空间通信、网格扩展性和计算成本之间存在权衡，缺乏一种既能保持高效计算又能兼顾架构模块化的通用网络。

Method: 提出了一种受光传输启发的神经算子(LiNO)，将潜空间演化分解为反射（特征重定向）、折射（各向异性调制）和散射（非局部空间传播）三个机制；通过正特征全局传播和局部扩散分支替代显式的两两交互，将空间复杂度从二次方降为线性。

Result: LiNO 在保持物理架构可解释性的同时，将原有的二次方空间复杂度优化为线性复杂度，提升了在大规模网格下的计算效率和扩展能力。

Conclusion: LiNO 提供了一种高效且可解释的神经算子架构，通过显式分离局部特征转换与全局空间传播，实现了复杂物理系统的快速求解，同时降低了计算复杂度。

Abstract: Neural operators learn mappings between infinite-dimensional function spaces and provide a data-driven surrogate modeling paradigm for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs). Existing architectures typically obtain expressivity by parameterizing integral kernels in prescribed transform domains or by applying attention-like interactions over discretized spatial points.
