While these approaches have achieved substantial progress, they often face a persistent trade-off among physical interpretability, nonlocal spatial communication, mesh scalability, and computational cost. We propose a Light-inspired neural operator(LiNO), an operator-learning architecture whose latent evolution is decomposed into three mechanisms motivated by elementary light transport: reflection, refraction, and scattering. Reflection and refraction act as adaptive pointwise transformations in latent feature space, enabling local feature reorientation and anisotropic modulation, whereas scattering performs input-dependent nonlocal propagation over the physical domain. We first formulate scattering as a normalized pairwise kernel with relative positional bias, and then develop an efficient scattering variant that replaces explicit pairwise interactions with positive-feature global propagation and a local diffusion branch, reducing the dominant spatial complexity from quadratic to linear. This yields a structured neural operator that separates local feature modulation from global spatial communication while retaining a modular and interpretable latent evolution.

</details>


### [257] [Are Common Substructures Transferable? Riemannian Graph Foundation Model with Neural Vector Bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03270)
*Li Sun,Zhenhao Huang,Yiding Wang,Qin Chen,Pietro Lio,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼几何和神经向量丛的图基础模型框架 GAUGE，通过学习图的内在几何属性，解决了图结构在不同任务间难以迁移的理论与实践难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对图结构迁移能力的理论基础理解不足，且局限于离散子结构的统计，缺乏对图的函数行为（functional behavior）和表示空间内在几何的深入探索。

Method: 1. 基于黎曼几何提出了“神经向量丛（Neural Vector Bundle）”框架，用于解析带有局部坐标的图内在几何； 2. 设计了预训练架构 GAUGE，通过平坦化几何兼容的局部坐标来构建向量丛； 3. 引入了一种新的 Dirichlet 损失函数，用于衡量和优化结构的迁移工作量。

Result: 在最具挑战性的零样本（zero-shot）链路预测和图同构任务中，验证了所提方法相比现有图基础模型的卓越性能和表达能力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入黎曼几何视角和神经向量丛（Neural Vector Bundle）框架，成功定义并学习了图数据的内在几何结构，从而实现了图结构表征的有效迁移，在零样本链路预测和图同构等任务中表现出卓越的表达能力。

Abstract: Foundation models have sparked a revolution via a pretraining-adaptation paradigm, with recent efforts extending this success to graphs. Unlike other modalities, graphs contain rich structural patterns, yet their structural transferability remains poorly understood. Prior studies consider common substructures in the discrete realm, and we are motivated by a fundamental question: Are common substructures transferable? The underlying theory is largely underexplored. In this work, we shift toward learning transferable structures through the lens of functional behavior. Theoretically, we connect transferable substructures to intrinsic geometry of the representation space. However, characterizing such intrinsic geometry has rarely been touched. Grounded in Riemannian geometry, we develop a graph intrinsic geometry learning framework called Neural Vector Bundle, which enables parsing intrinsic geometry with local coordinates. Building on this, we design GAUGE, a pretrainable neural architecture that constructs the vector bundle, flattening geometrically compatible local coordinates, and a new Dirichlet loss, which also measures the transfer effort. We empirically validate its superior expressiveness in challenging tasks including zero-shot link prediction and graph isomorphism.

</details>


### [258] [A Geometric Lens on Physics-Aligned Data Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03279)
*Aleix Segui,Wesley Armour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了科学数据压缩中“物理感知损失”与“重建保真度”之间的矛盾，提出了一个基于局部几何的理论框架来解释这种速率-失真权衡，并提供了一种诊断工具来评估两者的对齐程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学数据压缩中，物理感知损失虽能提升特定物理指标，但会导致重建保真度下降且机制不明，缺乏对这种速率-失真权衡的系统理解。

Method: 建立了一个局部几何理论，分析由熵模型、物理观测算子和失真度量诱导的潜在空间敏感度之间的交互，并提出了一个实用的对齐诊断指标。

Result: 理论证明了这种权衡源于潜在空间敏感度方向的不对齐（各向异性误差分配），实证研究验证了诊断指标与物理空间及数据空间权衡的相关性。

Conclusion: 研究量化了物理感知损失与重建保真度之间的根本权衡，建立了局部切空间速率-失真定律，并提出了基于特征空间重叠的对齐诊断工具。

Abstract: In AI for Science, physics-informed losses are increasingly used to train learned compressors for scientific data, but their rate-distortion implications remain poorly understood. At fixed bitrate, these objectives often improve preservation of a target physical observable while degrading standard reconstruction fidelity. We develop a local geometric theory showing that this tradeoff is governed by the interaction of latent-space sensitivities induced by the entropy model, the physical observable, and the distortion metric. At each operating point, these induce preferred directions along which compression noise should be suppressed, yielding an anisotropic error-allocation mechanism. When these directions are misaligned, improving the observable at fixed rate necessarily worsens standard distortion, establishing a fundamental limit on simultaneous preservation. We formalise this through a local tangent-space rate-distortion law and introduce a practical alignment diagnostic based on dominant eigenspace overlap. Experiments across scientific domains test the theory and validate that the alignment diagnostic correlates with observed data- and physics-space trade-offs.

</details>


### [259] [Message Tuning Outshines Graph Prompt Tuning: A Prismatic Space Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03290)
*Yancheng Chen,Dun Ma,Shuai Zhang,Yang Liu,Xixun Lin,Xiangyu Zhao,Wenguo Yang,Wei Chen,Chuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文定义了PS-Theory来量化图学习中提示微调的适应能力，并据此提出了一种更强大且轻量化的微调范式——消息微调（MTG），从理论和实验上证明了其超越传统图提示微调的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前图基础模型（GFMs）的适配主流方法是图提示微调，但缺乏严谨的理论来衡量其“适应能力”（Adaptation Capacity），这限制了对图提示微调极限的理解及更优方法的开发。

Method: 1. 提出Prismatic Space Theory (PS-Theory)：一个用于量化适应方法能力的数学框架，用于确立图提示微调的边界。
2. 提出Message Tuning for GFMs (MTG)：一种轻量级方法，通过在GNN骨干网络的每一层注入少量可学习的消息原型，在不更新预训练权重的情况下实现对消息融合的自适应引导。

Result: 1. 通过PS-Theory证明了MTG的适应能力超过了图提示微调的理论上限。
2. 大规模实验表明，MTG在多项基准数据集上持续优于现有的图提示微调基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出了PS-Theory作为评估图提示微调（Graph Prompt Tuning）适应能力的理论框架，并证明了新方法MTG在适应能力上能够突破传统图提示微调的理论上限，在实证层面验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Graph Foundation Models (GFMs), built upon the Pre-training and Adaptation paradigm, have emerged as a research hotspot in graph learning. For GNN-based GFMs, graph prompt tuning has become the prevailing adaptation method for downstream tasks. Although recent methods explain why graph prompt tuning works, how to rigorously measure its adaptation capacity remains an open problem. Addressing this problem is critical for understanding the capability limits of graph prompt tuning and for developing more powerful adaptation methods. In this paper, we propose Prismatic Space Theory (PS-Theory), a novel mathematical framework to quantify the capacity of adaptation methods, while focusing on establishing the upper bound for the adaptation capacity of graph prompt tuning. Building upon the proposed PS-Theory, we further introduce Message Tuning for GFMs (MTG), a lightweight approach that injects a small set of learnable message prototypes into each layer of the GNN backbone to adaptively guide message fusion without updating pre-trained weights. Through our PS-Theory, we prove that the adaptation capacity of MTG can exceed the theoretical upper bound of graph prompt tuning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MTG consistently outperforms graph prompt baselines across diverse benchmark datasets, providing strong empirical support for our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [260] [Learning Multi-Scale Hypergraph for High-Order Brain Connectivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03310)
*Jaeyoon Sim,Soojin Hwang,Seunghun Baek,Guorong Wu,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有脑网络模型难以捕捉高阶交互和多尺度模式的问题，本文提出了 MuHL 框架，通过自适应多尺度超边学习，显著提升了阿尔茨海默病和帕金森病的分类精度，并增强了对疾病相关脑区交互分析的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的脑网络模型多局限于两两交互，无法建模复杂的群体依赖；而现有的超图方法往往依赖预定义的超边，灵活性不足，难以捕捉多分辨率的结构模式，限制了对神经退行性疾病的分析能力。

Method: 提出了一种自适应多尺度超边学习框架（MuHL）。该框架通过构建分层节点特征，利用多分辨率图信号进行连续的超边构建，从而动态学习高阶交互信息，克服了传统方法对预定义超边或固定权重的依赖。

Result: 在多个脑网络基准数据集上的实验表明，MuHL 在各类疾病阶段的分类任务中均表现优异，并能从中学到具有临床意义的感兴趣区域（ROIs）及其高阶交互模式。

Conclusion: MuHL 框架通过捕捉多尺度高阶大脑网络依赖关系，有效提升了神经退行性疾病（AD/PD）的分类性能，并能识别出与疾病进展相关的关键脑区及其群体交互模式。

Abstract: Understanding complex interactions between brain regions is critical for early neurodegenerative disease classification such as Alzheimer's Disease (AD) and Parkinson's Disease (PD). While graph-based models are widely used to analyze brain networks, most existing approaches primarily focus on pairwise interactions between directly connected nodes, limiting their ability to capture higher-order dependencies across multiple regions. Although hypergraph-based methods have been proposed to model higher-order relations, many rely on predefined hyperedges or restrict learning to hyperedge weights, reducing flexibility and limiting their capacity to capture multi-resolution structural patterns. In this regard, we introduce an adaptive multi-scale hyperedge learning framework, i.e., MuHL, which constructs hierarchical node features and dynamically learns high-order interactions through continuous hyperedge construction over multi-resolution graph signals. Extensive experiments on multiple brain network benchmarks demonstrate that MuHL consistently improves disease classification performance across different stages, and further identifies key regions of interest (ROIs) and their group-wise interactions from the learned hyperedges that are associated with disease progression, highlighting its potential as a powerful tool for brain network analysis in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [261] [A Graph Foundation Model with Spectral Parsing and Prototype-Guided Spatial Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03315)
*Ankang Yang,Jitao Zhao,Dongxiao He,Liang Yang,Di Jin,Weixiong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了改善图基础模型的跨域泛化能力，论文提出了 SPG 模型，通过频谱分解处理不同频率的信号，并采用 Gromov-Wasserstein 原型几何来捕捉通用的拓扑结构模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图基础模型（GFMs）在跨图迁移时面临特征空间不一致和图结构多样性的挑战；传统的结构令牌或预定义子图方法覆盖范围有限，且现有的模型缺乏对图信号中高/低频信息的有效区分与利用。

Method: 提出了一种名为 SPG 的图基础模型，包含两个核心模块：1. 频谱解析（Spectral Parsing）：利用可学习的切比雪夫滤波器将节点特征分解为不同频率的响应；2. 原型引导的空间传播：构造 Gromov-Wasserstein 原型几何空间，提炼可迁移的成对关系，并将其转化为原型引导的传播算子。

Result: 在多项实验中，SPG 模型在跨领域图任务的泛化性能上实现了持续的提升。

Conclusion: SPG 通过频谱解析（Spectral Parsing）和原型引导的空间传播，有效解决了跨图数据在拓扑结构和信号特性上的不一致性，在跨领域泛化任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Graph foundation models aim to learn transferable knowledge from diverse graphs for generalization to unseen graphs and tasks. Unlike text and images, graphs lack a shared vocabulary or regular spatial grid, making cross-graph transfer challenging. This challenge comes from both feature discrepancies and, more critically, diverse graph structures. Existing GFMs mainly improve transferability by unifying feature spaces or incorporating structural tokens and vocabularies. However, existing topology-aware designs still have limitations. Structural tokens are usually discrete, while structural vocabularies often rely on predefined substructures such as trees and cycles, whose limited coverage may miss richer relational patterns across graphs. Moreover, graph signals contain both high-frequency local patterns and smoother low-frequency patterns, which require different propagation behaviors. These components are often entangled in raw graph signals, while this spectral perspective is rarely explored in existing GFMs. To address these challenges, we propose SPG, a graph foundation model with spectral parsing and prototype-guided spatial propagation. SPG applies learnable Chebyshev filters to decompose node features into multiple spectral responses, reducing the mismatch between frequency-specific graph signals and propagation behaviors. It then constructs a Gromov-Wasserstein prototype geometry to distill transferable pairwise relations beyond predefined substructures into a shared structural space. The learned prototype geometry is further projected back as a prototype-guided propagation operator. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [262] [Validation-Gated Multi-Agent Governance for Online Adaptation of Thermal-Hydraulic Surrogate Models under Operating-Regime Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03321)
*Doyeong Lim,Seungyoon Lee,In Cheol Bang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对热工水力预测中AI代理难以应对工况漂移的问题，该论文提出了一种由多智能体协作、具备安全门控功能的持续自适应框架，通过动态替换模型实现了预测精度的大幅提升与可审计的部署演进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI热工水力代理模型通常在线下训练并固定，在部署到复杂的动态环境时，一旦偏离预训练的工况包络（condition-locked），性能会迅速退化，缺乏有效的适应与自主更新机制。

Method: 提出了一种受限的（guarded）持续适应框架，包含五个角色分离的智能体（监控、诊断、自适应、安全审计、编排），辅以确定性“冠军-挑战者”门控机制和后台影子学习策略。实验选取时域傅里叶神经算子作为初始冠军模型，并对比了全生命周期内的自适应预测表现。

Result: 在热工水力循环实验数据上，对比静态部署模型（MAE 7.06），采用多智能体全自适应模式（MA-Full）的MAE降低至5.72，警告超标率从56.8%降至35.8%，实现了19.0%的性能提升。证明了受控的自动演进机制优于静态模型或单一的影子学习更新。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的多智能体持续适应框架通过监控、诊断、自适应、安全审计和编排等分工，结合确定性门控机制，有效解决了AI代理在热工水力预测中因超分布导致模型性能下降的问题。实验证明，该框架在保持模型审计性和部署安全性的同时，能够显著提升预测精度（MAE降低19%）。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence surrogates can support second-by-second thermal-hydraulic forecasting, but models selected and frozen offline may become condition-locked once deployed outside their pretraining envelope. This study develops a guarded continual-adaptation framework for experimental thermal-hydraulic loop data in which role-separated agents - Monitor, Diagnosis, Adaptation, Safety-Auditor, and Orchestrator - diagnose error signatures, prioritize candidate model families, and review promotions, while deterministic champion-challenger gates and background shadow learning retain final authority over model replacement. Seven surrogate families were screened by blocked three-fold cross-validation, and a temporal Fourier neural operator was selected as the initial champion for 60-s-history-to-10-s-trajectory forecasting on two held-out transients, with three seeds per adaptive mode. Static deployment gave a channel-averaged MAE of 7.06 and a 56.8% warning-exceedance ratio; rule-based adaptation reduced MAE to 6.54, whereas shadow refresh alone remained close to Static. The MA-Full mode, in which the role-separated multi-agent council reviews every evaluated stream step, achieved the lowest mean error, 5.72, and 35.8% exceedance, corresponding to a 19.0% improvement over Static. Paired bootstrap intervals against Static excluded zero, although intervals among adaptive modes overlapped and the six paired units limit broad statistical claims. Validated promotions from the neural operator to Transformer and graph neural network indicate that logged, gate-controlled adaptation can support auditable surrogate evolution while deterministic gates retain deployment authority.

</details>


### [263] [Multi-Modal Graph Neural Network with Transformer-Guided Adaptive Diffusion for Preclinical Alzheimer Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03322)
*Jaeyoon Sim,Minjae Lee,Guorong Wu,Won Hwa Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对现有GNN在脑网络分析中长短程信息提取的不足，本文提出一种结合扩散核与多头注意力的融合框架，实现了对阿尔茨海默病（AD）的精准分类与关键生物标志物识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经网络（GNN）在解析脑网络时存在局限：卷积方法难以有效聚合远距离邻域信息，而注意力机制在保留关键节点特征方面存在不足，导致难以从多模态特征中识别针对疾病的特定变化。

Method: 提出了一种整合框架，在每个节点使用下游Transformer引导扩散过程。具体而言，通过扩散核（diffusion-kernel）聚合图的短程属性，并通过多头注意力机制（multi-head attention）聚合长程属性。

Result: 该模型在临床前阿尔茨海默病（AD）的分类任务中表现优越，超过了现有的基准模型，并成功定位了与AD临床前期高度相关的关键脑区。

Conclusion: 该模型通过有效整合局部和全局图信息，提升了临床前阿尔茨海默病（AD）的分类性能，并能精准识别与疾病早期阶段密切相关的关键脑区（ROI），为AD的早期诊断和预测提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The graphical representation of the brain offers critical insights into diagnosing and prognosing neurodegenerative disease via relationships between regions of interest (ROIs). Despite recent emergence of various Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to effectively capture the relational information, there remain inherent limitations in interpreting the brain networks. Specifically, convolutional approaches ineffectively aggregate information from distant neighborhoods, while attention-based methods exhibit deficiencies in capturing node-centric information, particularly in retaining critical characteristics from pivotal nodes. These shortcomings reveal challenges for identifying disease-specific variation from diverse features from different modalities. In this regard, we propose an integrated framework guiding diffusion process at each node by a downstream transformer where both short- and long-range properties of graphs are aggregated via diffusion-kernel and multi-head attention respectively. We demonstrate the superiority of our model by improving performance of pre-clinical Alzheimer's disease (AD) classification with various modalities. Also, our model adeptly identifies key ROIs that are closely associated with the preclinical stages of AD, marking a significant potential for early diagnosis and prevision of the disease.

</details>


### [264] [Calibration Data Trade-offs Across Capability Dimensions: Why Multi-Source Mixing Matters for High-Sparsity LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03328)
*Hu Xu,Zhaolong Xing,Congcong Liu,Jiaxing Wang,Zhida Jiang,Junshi Huang,Zhen Chen,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型剪枝，现有工作忽视了校准数据对不同任务维度的差异性影响。本文发现校准困惑度与模型通用/专业能力保留存在相反的权衡，并提出自动构建多源校准集的IGSP协议，有效解决了高压缩比下的能力损伤问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有观点认为训练后剪枝中校准数据集的选择对平均准确率影响有限，但该研究质疑忽略了对不同维度能力保留的差异性，试图通过细粒度分析揭示校准源与特定能力下降之间的本质联系。

Method: 1. 提出了“能力维度分解”分析法：将模型能力拆解为通用、常识、代码、数学四个维度；2. 分析了15种校准源与各维度保留率的相关性（OIT指标）；3. 提出了IGSP（Information-Guided Self-Calibration Protocol）：一种通过最小化4-gram重叠和平衡多维困惑度（Perplexity）来自动构建多源校准集的协议。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B模型及60%稀疏度设定下：1. 校准困惑度与通用能力呈正相关，但与代码和数学能力呈负相关；2. 多源校准混合显著优于单一来源，总保留率提升8.8%-18.8%；3. IGSP方法在性能上优于现有的Self-Cal（+2.4）和SGS（+4.8）方法。

Conclusion: 针对大语言模型训练后剪枝，单一同质数据集无法同时兼顾模型的通用能力与专业能力（如代码、数学）。本文提出的IGSP协议通过多源校准集混合与信息导向优化，在不依赖特定能力标注数据集的前提下，显著提升了高稀疏度下的模型能力保留率，证明了校准数据构成对不同维度能力影响的关键性。

Abstract: Post-training pruning compresses large language models to high sparsity using a small unlabelled calibration set, and recent work has concluded that the choice of calibration source has only modest impact on averaged post-pruning accuracy. We ask whether this conclusion survives once calibration impact is evaluated separately across distinct capability dimensions rather than aggregated. Decomposing post-pruning capability into General, Commonsense, Code, and Math, and analysing $n{=}15$ calibration sources via Spearman correlations between OIT information metrics and per-dimension retention, we uncover an opposite-sign trade-off: calibration perplexity correlates positively with General retention ($ρ{=}{+}0.71$) but negatively with Math and Code retention ($ρ{=}{-}0.53,\,{-}0.59$; $p{<}0.05$), so no single source can preserve all capabilities. We respond with multi-source calibration mixing, and propose IGSP, an information-guided self-calibration protocol that automates multi-source construction without capability-aligned corpora by minimising 4-gram aggregation and balancing perplexity across dimensions. On LLaMA-3.1-8B at SparseGPT 60% sparsity, a uniform multi-source mix reaches 58.8% total retention, outperforming the best single source (MetaMath, 50.0%) by $+8.8$ and the C4 default (40.0%) by $+18.8$; IGSP improves over Self-Cal by $+2.4$ and SGS by $+4.8$.

</details>


### [265] [FLIPS: Instance-Fingerprinting for LLMs via Pseudo-random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03330)
*Gurvan Richardeau,Gohar Dashyan,Erwan Le Merrer,Gilles Tredan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了FLIPS，一种能够区分同一LLM不同运行时配置（实例）的指纹识别技术，通过利用生成结果中的统计偏差，有效解决了当前合规性监管中无法确认具体模型运行配置的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM指纹识别技术追求模型鲁棒性以保护版权，但无法识别同一模型在不同实例级参数下的差异。然而，LLM的输出行为（尤其是安全性）受这些参数影响显著，这导致目前的识别技术无法满足监管部门对特定部署行为进行合规评估的需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为FLIPS的实例级指纹识别方法。该方法利用LLM生成二进制随机序列中的统计偏差（biases）作为特征，在不同配置（提示词、采样配置、量化等）下对同一LLM的实例进行区分和识别。

Result: 在237个模型实例的测试中，FLIPS在闭集识别中达到了96%的准确率，在开集识别中达到了90%的准确率，显著优于基准方法（LLMmap，准确率仅为35%）。

Conclusion: 现有的LLM指纹识别技术侧重于版权保护，忽略了实例级参数对模型行为（如安全性）的影响。FLIPS的成功证明了识别特定运行时配置的可行性与必要性，为面向监管的AI合规评估提供了一种实用的技术范式。

Abstract: Literature reveals that a Large Language Model's (LLM) behavior is not only conditioned by its original weights but also its instance-level parameters, such as instructional prompt, sampling configuration or quantization. A model that generates safe outputs under one configuration may produce toxic content under another. However, current LLM identification techniques (such as fingerprinting) focus on intellectual property protection, and their design favors robustness to changes in these instance-level parameters. This poses a critical challenge for AI regulation in which compliance assessments target actual deployed behaviors, not model provenance. In this paper, we introduce instance-level fingerprinting, a regulator-oriented paradigm that distinguishes configurations of the same LLM. Our method FLIPS, exploits biases in generated binary random sequences to reach 96% (closed-set) and 90% (open-set, where some targets are unknown) identification accuracy across 237 model instances, versus 35% for the adapted LLMmap baseline. This shows that instance-level fingerprinting is both necessary for regulation and practically feasible. Code available at https://github.com/GurvanR/FLIPS-LLM-Instance-Fingerprinting.

</details>


### [266] [Tailoring Strictly Proper Scoring Rules for Downstream Tasks: An Application to Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03332)
*Roman Plaud,Alexandre Perez-Lebel,Antoine Saillenfest,Thomas Bonald,Marine Le Morvan,Gaël Varoquaux,Matthieu Labeau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对因果推断中倾向评分估计偏差带来的下游问题，本文提出了一种基于下游任务误差曲率的定制化损失函数优化框架，有效提升了ATE估计的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统概率模型通常使用与任务无关的损失函数（如对数损失），导致在因果推断（如IPW）中产生显著的下游估计偏差，尤其是在倾向评分接近0或1时。因此需要能够最小化特定下游任务误差的训练目标。

Method: 提出了一种推导特定任务严格恰当评分规则（strictly proper scoring rules）的通用框架。通过匹配ATE估计中IPW误差函数的局部曲率，推导出一个闭式损失函数及其规范的概率映射，并可嵌入任意模型（如神经网络或梯度提升树）。

Result: 在多个因果推断基准测试中，该方法在估计平均处理效应（ATE）时，性能均优于标准的似然基方法和协变量平衡方法。

Conclusion: 提出了一种能够通过匹配下游评估指标的局部曲率来导出特定任务评分规则的框架。通过针对ATE估计量身定制损失函数，该方法在因果推断任务中显著优于传统的似然估计和协变量平衡方法。

Abstract: Probabilistic models are typically trained using task-agnostic objectives like log-loss, which can lead to significant errors in downstream estimation. This disconnect is especially critical in Inverse Probability Weighting (IPW) for causal inference, where propensity score errors near $0$ and $1$ often lead to high bias and variance. We propose a principled framework for deriving task-specific strictly proper scoring rules by matching the local curvature of the downstream error metric. We apply this to the Average Treatment Effect (ATE) estimation, deriving a closed-form loss and its corresponding canonical probability mapping that can be readily integrated with any model like a neural network or a gradient boosting algorithm. Extensive evaluations on causal inference benchmarks demonstrate that our tailored objective consistently outperforms standard likelihood-based and covariate-balancing approaches.

</details>


### [267] [IdEst: Assessing Self-Supervised Learning Representations via Intrinsic Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03338)
*Julie Mordacq,Vicky Kalogeiton,Steve Oudot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 IdEst 方法，通过测量自监督学习表征的固有维度（ID）作为质量评估指标，能够在降低计算成本的同时准确预估下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性探测评估法存在计算成本高、对超参数敏感，且难以直观揭示表征空间几何结构的问题。作者旨在寻找一种更高效、基于几何特性的评估方式来衡量 SSL 表征。

Method: 提出了一种名为 IdEst 的方法，利用最小生成树维度估计器（$\mathrm{dim}_\mathrm{MST}$）来估算 SSL 表征的固有维度。

Result: IdEst 与下游线性探测性能呈现强相关性；该指标能有效辅助超参数选择，并显著降低评估时的计算成本。

Conclusion: 固有维度（Intrinsic Dimension, ID）是评估自监督学习（SSL）表征质量的有效几何代理指标，可以作为传统线性探测（Linear Probing）的重要补充或替代方案。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for learning meaningful representations from unlabeled data. However, the standard protocol for evaluating these representations, linear probing, is computationally expensive, sensitive to hyperparameters, and provides limited insight into the geometric structure of the representation space. In this work, motivated by connections between neural network generalization and intrinsic dimension (ID) we propose IdEst, a method for estimating the ID of SSL representations via the Minimum Spanning Tree dimension estimator ($\mathrm{dim}_\mathrm{MST}$). Across diverse datasets, architectures, and SSL pretraining objectives, we show that IdEst strongly correlates with downstream linear probe performances. Furthermore, we demonstrate that IdEst enables efficient hyperparameter selection, significantly reducing the computational cost compared to supervised alternatives. Our results highlight intrinsic dimensionality as a principled geometric proxy for assessing SSL representations, complementing standard supervised probing protocols.

</details>


### [268] [APIC: Amortized Physics-Informed Calibration using Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03355)
*Aishwarya Venkataramanan,Sai Karthikeya Vemuri,Joachim Denzler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: APIC 是一种将神经过程引入物理校准的新架构，实现了对物理模型偏差的摊销贝叶斯推断，解决了个体模型校准的可扩展性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理模型因未知的机制缺失存在系统性偏差；经典的 Kennedy-O'Hagan (KOH) 校准框架由于非摊销设计，导致在处理大规模或多系统实例时存在扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 提出“摊销物理信息校准”（APIC）框架，利用神经过程进行跨实例的贝叶斯推理；采用双分支潜变量架构，解耦物理参数与系统性结构偏差；整合可微物理引擎实现摊销推理后端。

Result: 在阻尼弹簧振子、Lotka-Volterra 系统及对流扩散偏微分方程实验中，APIC 能够利用少量观测数据快速校准未见过的实例，并准确量化预测不确定性，表现优于现有的校准方法。

Conclusion: APIC 框架通过结合神经过程与可微物理模型，有效解决了传统 KOH 框架在处理复杂系统时的可扩展性问题，在参数恢复和系统偏差识别方面表现出优越性。

Abstract: Physics models are inherently imperfect due to misspecified or missing mechanisms, resulting in systematic discrepancies between model predictions and real-world observations. The Kennedy-O'Hagan (KOH) framework addresses this issue through explicit discrepancy modeling. However, its non-amortized, per-instance formulation limits scalability across families of related systems. We introduce Amortized Physics-Informed Calibration (APIC), a population-level extension of KOH that leverages Neural Processes to perform scalable Bayesian inference across realizations. Our framework employs a two-branch latent architecture to disentangle instance-specific physical parameters from shared, state-dependent structural discrepancies. By integrating differentiable physics into an amortized inference backbone, APIC enables rapid calibration of unseen realizations from sparse observations while quantifying uncertainty. Experiments on the damped spring oscillator, the Lotka-Volterra system, and the advection-diffusion PDE with misspecified physics demonstrate improved parameter recovery and consistent identification of the systemic discrepancy structure compared to other calibration approaches.

</details>


### [269] [The Impact of Temporal Granularity on Socio-Demographic Inference from Household Load Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03358)
*Dejan Radovanovic,Maximilian Schirl,Andreas Unterweger,Günther Eibl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了智能电表数据采样率与个人隐私泄露之间的关系，发现降低数据分辨率存在两个“效用平台”，证实了通过减小分辨率进行数据最小化的可行性，并指出XGBoost配合手工特征是实现属性推断的高效方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电表数据可能泄露敏感的社会人口信息，尽管已知存有隐私风险，但关于数据采样时间分辨率如何具体影响推断性能的研究尚不充分，缺乏量化的隐私-效用权衡分析。

Method: 构建了一个评估框架，使用涵盖1,589个家庭的一年期智能电表数据集，测试了从15分钟到7天不等的时间分辨率对8种社会人口属性预测的影响；对比了手工特征/tsfresh特征与CNN自编码器嵌入，并评估了包括XGBoost在内的多种分类器。

Result: 1. 预测精度随分辨率降低而下降，但存在两个稳定性平稳期；2. 基于手工特征与tsfresh提取的显式特征集在性能上与深度学习嵌入相当，且XGBoost表现最佳；3. 静态和动态属性对时间分辨率的依赖性不同（如住宅面积可在粗粒度下推断，而游泳池使用则需要细粒度数据）。

Conclusion: 数据的时间分辨率与社会人口统计学属性的推断能力之间存在非线性关系，存在两个明显的性能平稳期（15分钟-1小时，1天-7天），为在不显著降低效用的前提下进行隐私保护的数据最小化提供了依据。XGBoost结合可解释特征在推断任务中表现优异，且不同属性对时间分辨率的敏感度存在显著差异。

Abstract: Smart meter data can reveal sensitive socio-demographic characteristics of households, raising privacy concerns. While this risk has been demonstrated at fixed granularities, the role of temporal resolution in shaping inference performance remains insufficiently explored. This paper addresses this gap by analyzing how load profiles with granularities from 15 minutes to 7 days affect the predictability of eight socio-demographic attributes in a dataset of 1,589 households over one year. We introduce an evaluation framework where classifiers are trained on year-round data but tested on arbitrary weeks, forcing generalization across seasonal and weekly variations. Our results show three main findings. First, while coarsening granularity reduces predictive accuracy, two plateaus emerge: performance is stable between 15 minutes and 1 hour, and again between 1 and 7 days. This reveals opportunities for data minimization without sacrificing utility. Second, interpretable handcrafted and tsfresh features remain competitive with CNN-based autoencoder embeddings, while XGBoost consistently outperforms alternative classifiers. Third, feature importance analysis highlights differences between static and dynamic attributes: dwelling size can be inferred even from coarse data, whereas swimming pool usage requires fine-grained temporal signals. Overall, our study provides new insights into the privacy-utility trade-off in smart metering, showing how temporal resolution, feature extraction, and classifier choice jointly influence socio-demographic inference.

</details>


### [270] [WUSH: Near-Optimal Adaptive Transforms for LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00956)
*Jiale Chen,Vage Egiazarian,Roberto L. Castro,Torsten Hoefler,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WUSH是一种针对大模型量化的新型数据依赖型线性变换方法，通过优化离群值分布显著提升了W4A4量化精度并保持了高效的GPU执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型量化中，显著的极端离群值会导致量化动态范围拉大，从而加剧量化误差；现有的基于Hadamard的变换方法（如QuIP#等）通常是固定的且与数据无关，其量化最优性尚不明确。

Method: 推导了针对RTN AbsMax缩放块量化的闭式最优线性分块变换；构建了WUSH方法，即一种结合Hadamard变换与数据依赖成分的非正交变换；实现了融合化的GPU内核以优化计算效率。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型上进行W4A4量化时，相比最强的Hadamard基线，RTN下平均准确率提升了2.8个点，GPTQ下提升了0.7个点；在FP4矩阵乘法中，单层吞吐量相比BF16提升高达5.8倍。

Conclusion: WUSH通过结合Hadamard变换与数据依赖的二阶矩成分，提供了一种在理论上近乎最优且在实践中高效的量化预处理方法，显著提升了低比特（如W4A4）量化下的LLM性能。

Abstract: Quantizing LLM weights and activations is a standard approach for efficient deployment, but a few extreme outliers can stretch the dynamic range and amplify low-bit quantization errors. Prior transform-based mitigations (e.g., Hadamard rotations) are fixed and data-agnostic, and their optimality for quantization has remained unclear. We derive closed-form optimal linear blockwise transforms for joint weight-activation quantization under standard RTN AbsMax-scaled block quantizers, covering both integer and floating-point formats. The resulting construction, WUSH, combines a Hadamard backbone with a data-dependent second-moment component to form a non-orthogonal transform that is provably near-optimal for FP and INT quantizers under mild assumptions while admitting an efficient fused GPU implementation. Empirically, WUSH improves W4A4 accuracy over the strongest Hadamard-based baselines (e.g., on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct in MXFP4, it gains +2.8 average points with RTN and +0.7 with GPTQ) while delivering up to 5.8$\times$ per-layer throughput over BF16 via FP4 MatMul. Source code is available at https://github.com/IST-DASLab/WUSH.

</details>


### [271] [Mitigating False Credit Propagation: Probabilistic Graphical Reward Aggregation for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03361)
*Can Lv,Mingju Chen,Heng Chang,Shiji Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决大语言模型评分表奖励机制中存在的“虚假信用传播”问题（即缺乏前置条件仍获得后置奖励），GEAR 引入概率图模型来显式约束标准间的依赖关系，显著提升了奖励分配的准确性与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的评分表（Rubric）强化学习方法通常将各项奖励简单线性相加，忽略了标准间的先后置依赖关系，导致模型即便未满足前置条件（如未陈述事实）仍能因后续条件（如语言流畅度）获得奖励，导致“虚假信用传播”（FCP）。

Method: 提出了一种名为 GEAR (Graphical Event Aggregation for Rubric rewards) 的概率图框架。该方法将评分标准视为图中的潜在伯努利事件，通过建模父子节点的依赖关系，实现从“未满足的先决条件”向“派生标准”的软抑制传播，从而计算归一化的期望效用。

Result: 在 HealthBench、WritingBench 和 PLawBench 等基准测试中，GEAR 相比传统的平铺聚合方式，相对增益达 15.5%；诊断显示 FCP 导致的奖励泄露减少了 96.5%，且比确定性门控方法保留了更多的有效奖励。

Conclusion: GEAR 通过引入显式的依赖关系建模，解决了基于评分表的强化学习中的“虚假信用传播”（FCP）问题。实验证明，该方法能显著提升奖励信号的质量，并在保持计算效率的同时优于传统的平铺聚合方式。

Abstract: Rubric-based rewards are increasingly used for open-ended language model post-training, but criterion-level scores are often aggregated as independent utilities. This flat scalarization ignores rubric-specified prerequisite and activation relations among criteria, allowing reward or penalty to be counted even when the condition that licenses it is absent. We call this structural reward-aggregation failure \textbf{False Credit Propagation} (FCP). To address this limitation, we propose \ourname (\textbf{G}raphical \textbf{E}vent \textbf{A}ggregation for \textbf{R}ubric rewards), a probabilistic graphical framework for dependency-aware rubric aggregation. \ourname models each criterion outcome as a latent Bernoulli event in a typed rubric graph, propagates soft suppression from unsupported parent events to their children, and aggregates the resulting event probabilities into a normalized expected signed utility. This yields a linear-time reward computation that can be plugged into standard rubric-based RL pipelines without changing the outer optimization algorithm. Experiments on HealthBench, WritingBench, and PLawBench with two policy backbones show that \ourname consistently improves over flat aggregation and deterministic gating, achieving relative gains of up to 15.5\% over flat aggregation. FCP diagnostics further show that \ourname reduces leakage by 96.5\% relative to flat aggregation while preserving more licensed downstream utility than deterministic gating. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/LvCan926/GEAR.

</details>


### [272] [Link Prediction or Perdition: the Seeds of Instability in Knowledge Graph Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03365)
*Guillaume Méroué,Fabien Gandon,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 知识图谱嵌入模型的评估存在普遍的随机不稳定性，现有的高排名指标掩盖了模型在预测一致性和嵌入空间构建上的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识图谱嵌入模型（KGEMs）评估过于依赖基于排名的指标（如MRR或Hits@K），忽略了随机种子对结果稳定性的影响，且上述指标无法揭示单个预测或嵌入空间的潜在不稳定性。

Method: 系统性分析了多种知识图谱嵌入模型（KGEMs）在多个数据集上的表现。研究通过隔离随机因素（包括模型初始化、三元组排序、负采样、Dropout和硬件差异）进行控制变量分析，并评估了基于多数投票的补救机制。

Result: 研究发现，高性能模型在三元组层面产生显著的预测差异，且嵌入空间极不稳定。上述随机因素对不稳定性贡献相当。此外，高MRR的超参数配置并不意味着更高的稳定性，且投票机制仅能提供有限的稳定性提升。

Conclusion: 当前知识图谱嵌入模型的基准评估协议存在严重缺陷，未考虑随机因素引起的预测不稳定性。高性能模型在三元组级别预测和嵌入空间构成上表现出的不一致性，质疑了现有模型在知识图谱补全任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Embedding models (KGEMs) constitute the main link prediction approach to complete knowledge graphs. Standard evaluation protocols emphasize rank-based metrics such as MRR or Hits@$K$, but usually overlook the influence of random seeds on result stability. Moreover, these metrics conceal potential instabilities in individual predictions and in the organization of embedding spaces. In this work, we conduct a systematic stability analysis of multiple KGEMs across several datasets. We find that high-performance models actually produce divergent predictions at the triple level and highly variable embedding spaces. By isolating stochastic factors (i.e., initialization, triple ordering, negative sampling, dropout, hardware), we show that each independently induces instability of comparable magnitude. Furthermore, for a given model, hyperparameter configurations with better MRR are not guaranteed to be more stable. Moreover, voting, albeit a known remediation mechanism, only provides a limited enhancement of stability. These findings highlight critical limitations of current benchmarking protocols, and raise concerns about the reliability of KGEMs for knowledge graph completion.

</details>


### [273] [Local Guidance, Global Impact: Gaussian-Reshaped Trust Region Unlocks Behavior Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03382)
*Bingxu Liu,Jiashun Liu,Johan Obando-Ceron,Hao Wang,Runze Liu,Pablo Samuel Castro,Aaron Courville,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 PPO 在非平稳环境下的优化低效问题，本文提出了 GTR 方法，通过基于高斯核的非单调信任域和自适应锚点机制，显著提升了强化学习在复杂非平稳环境中的策略适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 PPO 在连续/非平稳环境中表现不佳，原因在于其优化范式缺乏几何感知能力，导致更新方向低效，难以实现有效的行为模式迁移；且传统正则化方法的单调惩罚在需要大幅度策略偏移时会阻碍适应。

Method: 提出高斯信任域策略优化（Gaussian Trust Region Policy Optimization, GTR）。核心包括：1. 利用高斯核重塑信任域，引入有界且非单调的约束；2. 引入“混合高斯锚点”（Mixture Gaussian Anchor）以适应近期策略轨迹，降低过时引用带来的方差影响。

Result: GTR 在游戏、仿真机器人控制、开放世界探索以及语言模型后训练等多种任务中展现了强大的性能和鲁棒性，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 GTR 方法通过引入高斯核重塑信任域以及混合高斯锚点机制，能够有效应对非平稳环境下的强化学习挑战，证明了引入几何感知信任域设计的必要性与优越性。

Abstract: While Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) demonstrates strong performance in stationary settings, we show that its standard optimization paradigm struggles in continual and non-stationary environments. The failure does not stem from insufficient model capacity or overly restrictive clipping. Instead, PPO performs persistent, directionally inefficient local updates, which indicates a lack of geometry-aware guidance for accumulating meaningful behavioral change and ultimately hindering transitions toward new behavior patterns. Although divergence-based regularization introduces partial geometric awareness, its monotonically increasing penalties implicitly discourage large policy deviations, even when such shifts are necessary for effective adaptation. To address this limitation, we propose Gaussian Trust Region Policy Optimization (GTR), which reshapes the trust region using a Gaussian kernel. The resulting constraint is bounded and non-monotonic, providing strong local stability while progressively relaxing under sustained high-advantage updates. To further improve robustness, we introduce a Mixture Gaussian Anchor that adapts to recent policy trajectories, reducing variance induced by stale references. GTR is architecture-agnostic and achieves strong performance across games, simulated robotic control, open-world exploration, and language model post-training. These results demonstrate that geometry-aware trust-region design can be a promising direction for robust reinforcement learning in complex non-stationary environments. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GTR_demo/README.md.

</details>


### [274] [When Model Merging Breaks Routing: Training-Free Calibration for MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03391)
*Canbin Huang,Tianyuan Shi,Xiaojun Quan,Jingang Wang,Jianfei Zhang,Qifan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MoE 模型在合并过程中出现的“路由崩溃”问题，本文提出了 Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC) 框架，通过二阶曲率信息重对齐合并后的路由器，无需训练即可显著提升 MoE 模型合并的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型合并技术在强化的 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 架构上表现不佳，研究发现这是由于“路由崩溃”（routing breakdown）现象导致的，即合并后的路由器因参数扰动无法将 Token 正确分发至合适的专家。

Method: 提出了 Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC) 框架。该方法利用二阶曲率信息（Hessian 信息）对合并后的路由器进行校准，并采用无矩阵共轭梯度法（matrix-free conjugate gradient method）以获得高效的闭式解。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上的实验表明，HARC 能够有效缓解各种 MoE 合并基线中的路由崩溃问题，并显著改善模型性能。

Conclusion: HARC 提供了一种高效、无需训练的解决方案，通过利用二阶曲率信息校准合并模型的路由器，有效解决了 MoE 模型合并中的“路由崩溃”问题，提升了合并模型的性能。

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a cost-effective approach for consolidating the capabilities of multiple LLMs without retraining. However, existing merging techniques, largely based on linear parameter arithmetic or optimization, struggle when applied to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures. We identify a critical failure mode in MoE merging, termed routing breakdown, in which the merged router fails to dispatch tokens to suitable experts. Routing breakdown stems from the sensitivity of the non-linear softmax and discrete Top-k routing mechanisms to parameter perturbations from merging, a sensitivity further amplified by load-balancing constraints imposed during MoE pretraining. Because fine-tuned experts exhibit distinct specializations, even modest misrouting can cause severe performance degradation. To address this issue, we propose Hessian-Aware Router Calibration (HARC), a training-free framework that leverages second-order curvature information to realign the merged router. This approach admits a closed-form solution that can be efficiently solved using a matrix-free conjugate gradient method. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks show that HARC effectively mitigates routing breakdown across diverse MoE merging baselines and leads to substantial performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/huangcb01/HARC.

</details>


### [275] [Flicker-DDPM: Accelerating Denoising Diffusion via 1/f Colored Noise Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03393)
*Kexiang Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Flicker-DDPM，通过使用符合自然图像频谱特性的 1/fα 彩色噪声代替传统白噪声，利用频率域线性理论实现了采样路径的线性化，在维持高性能的同时显著加速了生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 DDPM 使用各向同性的白噪声，这与自然图像通常遵循的幂律谱（1/k^α）分布不符。研究旨在通过引入匹配自然图像统计特性的“闪烁（1/f）噪声”，优化扩散过程的逆向采样路径。

Method: 提出了一种基于空间相关性核 σ(d) = (d + 1)^{-η} 的彩色噪声模块，通过调节参数 η 控制噪声的功率谱指数 α，使其与目标数据集的频率成分相匹配。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10 数据集上，Flicker-DDPM 在采样步数减少至原基础模型 1/3.33 的情况下，达到了与之持平或更优的生成质量，且单步计算开销几乎无增加。

Conclusion: Flicker-DDPM 通过引入符合自然图像统计特性的 1/fα 彩色噪声，成功实现了扩散模型采样过程的线性化，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了采样效率，证明了利用自然界自组织临界现象进行生成模型设计的有效性。

Abstract: We propose a novel diffusion model, Flicker-DDPM, which incorporates flicker (1/f) noise inspired by self-organized criticality (SOC), a widely observed phenomenon in natural systems. Unlike denoising diffusion probabilistic models (DDPMs), which employ isotropic white noise in the forward process, Flicker-DDPM adopts colored noise with power-law spectra to better match the spectral statistics of natural images, whose power spectra typically follow P(k) proportional to 1/k^α. To this end, we develop a colored-noise module based on a spatial correlation kernel, σ(d) = (d + 1)^{-η}, and theoretically establish that adjusting η controls the spectral exponent α of the generated 1/fα noise, enabling adaptation to datasets with diverse spectral characteristics. On CIFAR-10, Flicker DDPM matches or surpasses the generation quality of a standard DDPM baseline using 3.33 times fewer sampling steps, with negligible additional computational cost per step. We further develop a frequency-domain linear theory demonstrating that spectrally matched colored noise linearizes the reverse trajectory, theoretically explaining the observed sampling acceleration.

</details>


### [276] [KVarN: Variance-Normalized KV-Cache Quantization Mitigates Error Accumulation in Reasoning Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03458)
*Lorenz K. Muller,Philippe Bich,Chiara Boretti,Hyun-Min Chang,Jiawei Zhuang,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长序列推理中 KV-cache 内存瓶颈问题，KVarN 提出了一种无需校准的量化策略，通过 Hadamard 变换与双向方差归一化解决了 autoregressive 生成中的误差累积难题，有效实现 2-bit 高效量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在进行长序列测试时延推理（test-time scaling）时，KV-cache 占用大量内存，而现有的量化方法主要针对预填充阶段，在自回归生成阶段存在因 token 尺度异常导致的误差累积问题。

Method: 提出 KVarN 方法：在 KV-cache 上应用 Hadamard 旋转以平滑异常值，并实施跨 K 和 V 矩阵轴的双向方差归一化（dual-scaling variance normalization）。

Result: KVarN 显著减少了自回归过程中的误差累积，在 MATH500、AIME24 和 HumanEval 等主流生成式基准测试中，以 2-bit 精度刷新了 KV-cache 量化的性能指标。

Conclusion: KVarN 是一种无需校准的 KV-cache 量化方法，通过 Hadamard 变换和双向方差归一化有效抑制了长序列自回归生成的误差累积，在 2-bit 量化下达到该领域 SOTA 水平。

Abstract: Test-time scaling is a powerful approach to obtain better reasoning in large language models, but it becomes memory-bottlenecked during long-horizon decoding, as the KV-cache grows. KV-cache quantization can help improve this, but current methods are evaluated under prefill-like settings and errors behave differently under autoregressive decoding. We show that in the latter regime, quantization errors accumulate across timesteps, driven primarily by incorrect token scales. We introduce KVarN, a calibration-free KV-cache quantizer that applies a Hadamard rotation followed by a dual-scaling variance normalization across both axes of the K and V matrices. We find that this combination fixes outlying token-scale errors and substantially reduces error accumulation over existing baselines. KVarN establishes a new state-of-theart for KV-cache quantization on generative benchmarks, including MATH500, AIME24 and HumanEval, at 2-bit precision. A vLLM implementation of the KVarN method is available at https://github.com/huawei-csl/KVarN

</details>


### [277] [Topology-Aware Gaussian Graph Repair for Robust Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03462)
*Anubha Goel,Juho Kanniainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为了解决GNN处理不规范图拓扑的难题，本文提出了TAGR框架。它通过高斯特征邻域修复和拓扑感知残差校正，在不改变GNN模型架构的前提下，通过轻量级的图修复显著提升了模型在含噪或缺失边场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法在处理存在噪声边（冗余）或缺失边（不完整）的原始拓扑时性能受限。传统的边修剪方法无法恢复缺失边，而现有的图学习方法又存在优化复杂度高的问题。因此，需要一种简单、轻量且能同时修复噪声和缺失边的方案。

Method: 提出了一种“拓扑感知高斯修复”（TAGR）框架。该方法分为两部分：1. 使用自适应高斯核构建稀疏特征邻域图（在特征相似节点间引入辅助边）；2. 采用拓扑感知残差校正（根据局部特征和结构一致性对原始图结构进行保留与重加权）。修复后的图可直接用于标准GNN，无需更改架构。

Result: 在基准引文网络上的广泛实验表明，TAGR在存在噪声边和缺失边的场景下均提升了GNN的鲁棒性。消融实验证实，高斯特征邻域修复是鲁棒性提升的主要贡献者，而拓扑感知残差校正增强了处理不完整图时的稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的TAGR框架证明了通过轻量级的稀疏图修复（而非复杂的图结构学习）即可有效提升图神经网络的鲁棒性。实验表明，高斯特征邻域修复在处理各类噪声方面效果显著，而拓扑感知残差校正则提升了面对不完整图结构时的稳定性。

Abstract: Graph neural networks have achieved strong performance on graph-structured data, but their effectiveness depends heavily on the quality of the observed graph. In real applications, graph topology is often imperfect: noisy edges may connect unrelated nodes, while missing edges may prevent useful information from being propagated. Existing robust graph learning methods mainly address this problem by removing suspicious edges or by learning a new graph structure during training. However, edge removal alone cannot recover missing connections, and graph structure learning may introduce additional optimization complexity. In this paper, we propose Topology-Aware Gaussian Repair (TAGR), a simple graph repair framework for robust message passing in graph neural networks. Instead of learning a dense adjacency matrix, TAGR constructs a sparse feature-neighborhood graph using an adaptive Gaussian kernel and combines it with a topology-aware residual correction of the observed graph. The Gaussian repair component introduces auxiliary edges between feature-similar nodes, while the residual correction preserves and reweights the original topology according to local feature and structural consistency. The repaired graph can be used directly with standard graph neural networks without changing their architectures. Extensive experiments on benchmark citation networks show that TAGR improves the robustness of GNNs under both noisy-edge and missing-edge settings. The analysis further show that Gaussian feature-neighborhood repair provides the main robustness gain, while topology-aware residual correction improves stability when the observed graph is incomplete. These results suggest that effective graph robustness can be achieved through lightweight sparse graph repair rather than dense graph structure learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [278] [Position: Prioritize Identifying Structure, Not Complex Models, for Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02632)
*Tyler H. McCormick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 在大模型科学发现中，预测能力强不等于发现了真实机制；由于存在机制不可判定性，作者提倡为“机制性机器学习”制定严格的行业准则，防止科学发现沦为“模拟叙事”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现代机器学习（尤其是LLM）在从观测数据推导科学机制时存在的“机制不可判定性”（Mechanistic Underdetermination）问题，指出仅靠预测成功或叙述连贯性不足以证明发现了真实机制。

Method: 本文是一篇立场论文（Position Paper），旨在通过理论论证揭示机器学习在高维代理机制下的不确定性问题，并基于此提出规范性的科学性准则。

Result: 论证了在高维环境下，多种互不兼容的机制可产生相同的观测结果，且LLM倾向于将复杂的机制解释简化为单一的流利叙述，这给学术研究带来了重大风险。

Conclusion: 作者主张在“机制性机器学习”（Mechanistic ML）领域建立具体标准，以确保LLM驱动的工作流真正支持科学发现，而非仅仅产生看似合理的模拟现象。

Abstract: Modern Machine Learning (ML) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, especially large language models (LLMs), are increasingly used to generate scientific hypotheses and mechanistic explanations from observational data. This position paper argues that in the high-dimensional proxy regimes where modern ML excels, mechanistic learning is generically underdetermined: many incompatible mechanisms induce essentially the same observational relationships on the support of the data, so predictive success and coherent explanations are insufficient evidence of mechanism discovery. This underdetermination becomes uniquely hazardous with large language models (LLMs), which tend to collapse large equivalence classes of explanations into a single fluent narrative. This paper proposes concrete standards for ``mechanistic ML,'' and argues these norms are necessary if LLM-centered workflows are to support science rather than merely simulate it.

</details>


### [279] [Target Updates May Stabilize Linear Q-Learning: Periodic and Soft Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02645)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用切换线性系统与联合谱半径理论，为Q学习中常用的软/硬目标更新机制提供了严格的收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中周期性硬目标更新和软目标更新虽被广泛应用，但缺乏严谨的理论支撑；本文旨在填补这些稳定化机制的理论空白。

Method: 构建了由贝尔曼最大算子诱导的切换线性系统（SLS）动态模型，并利用切换矩阵族的联合谱半径（JSR）进行严格的稳定性分析。

Result: 本文提供了一个严谨的数学框架，证明了在确定性线性Q学习中，目标更新机制能够确保算法的收敛性，并将其扩展至随机强化学习环境。

Conclusion: 通过联合谱半径（JSR）理论，证明了在满足特定谱条件和步长条件下，硬目标更新（周期性）和软目标更新机制对于线性Q学习收敛到精确的投影贝尔曼解是充分的。

Abstract: Periodic target updates in Q-learning and soft target updates in actor-critic methods are empirically well established stabilization mechanisms, but their precise theoretical explanation is still incomplete. This paper gives a rigorous and exact analysis of these mechanisms for Q-learning with linear function approximation (linear Q-learning) using the exact switched linear system (SLS) dynamics induced by the Bellman maximum and the joint spectral radius (JSR) of the resulting switching matrix families. Although linear Q-learning can fail to converge in general, we prove that, under explicit spectral and step-size conditions, periodic hard target updates and soft target updates can guarantee convergence to the exact projected Q-Bellman solution. The main analysis is carried out for deterministic linear Q-learning, where the target-update mechanism is most transparent. Once the corresponding JSR certificate is established for the mean recursion, the stochastic reinforcement-learning setting can be treated by replacing deterministic modes with sampled stochastic modes and adding the corresponding stochastic-noise analysis.

</details>


### [280] [State-Coupled Volatility in Latent Dynamical Systems: Recovery Under Partial Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02664)
*Imani Beckett*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种状态耦合随机波动率框架，通过粒子EM算法解决了在部分观测条件下潜在过程方差随状态位置变化的问题，显著提高了动态系统的建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数潜在状态空间模型假设过程变异性独立于潜在状态位置，无法捕获许多生物、行为和生理系统中存在的结构化随机性（即变异性随动态状态系统性变化）。

Method: 提出了一种状态耦合随机波动率框架，其中潜在过程方差依赖于偏离潜在平衡的位移。为了在部分观测下估计该关系，开发了一种结合自助法粒子滤波（bootstrap particle filtering）和后向轨迹平滑（backward trajectory smoothing）的粒子期望最大化（particle EM）程序。

Result: 通过大规模仿真基准测试评估显示：在强耦合条件下，该框架显著降低了恢复偏差；在大规模噪声下，其检测性能具有明显优势；表现随潜在持久性（persistence）的增加而提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在显式建模潜在状态结构的情况下，可以在部分观测条件下识别和估计状态耦合波动。该框架为研究状态依赖的变异性提供了实用的方法论基础，并有助于评估结构化随机性是否提供了超出平均状态轨迹之外的系统动力学信息。

Abstract: Latent state-space models are widely used to study partially observed dynamical systems, yet most formulations assume that process variability is independent of latent-state position. In many biological, behavioral, and physiological systems, however, variability may depend systematically on the underlying dynamical state, producing structured stochasticity that is not captured by constant-variance models. We introduce a state-coupled stochastic volatility framework in which latent process variance depends on displacement from a latent equilibrium. To estimate this relationship under partial observation, we develop a particle expectation-maximization procedure combining bootstrap particle filtering and backward trajectory smoothing. The model includes a coupling parameter, $γ$, that quantifies the strength of association between latent-state position and process variability. A large-scale simulation benchmark evaluated recovery and detection performance across varying coupling strengths, observation noise levels, trajectory lengths, and persistence regimes. The proposed framework consistently reduced recovery bias relative to an observed-state heteroskedastic proxy, with the largest improvements occurring under strong coupling. Recovery performance improved with increasing latent persistence, while detection performance remained competitive across a broad range of conditions and became increasingly advantageous as observation noise increased. Taken together, the results demonstrate that state-coupled volatility can be identified and estimated under partial observation when latent-state structure is explicitly modeled. The framework provides a practical methodological foundation for studying state-dependent variability and evaluating whether structured stochasticity contributes information about system dynamics beyond that contained in mean-state trajectories alone.

</details>


### [281] [ScoreStop: Gradient-based early stopping using functional score tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02740)
*Oliver J. Hines,Christian L. Hines*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ScoreStop，一种利用函数评分检验而非验证集损失的梯度早停规则，解决了传统早停法中 patience 参数难调及隐式/自定义损失函数支持不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于验证集损失的早停法依赖缺乏可解释性的 patience 超参数，且在面对噪声较大或隐式定义的损失函数（如 LambdaRank）时表现不佳。

Method: 提出 ScoreStop 方法，将早停决策转化为对“当前预测器是否为总体风险最小化器”这一原假设的统计检验；使用基于验证数据的函数评分检验（functional score test），该统计量在更新方向上具有尺度不变性，且在原假设下具有已知的渐近分布。

Result: 在合成实验和真实数据基准测试中，ScoreStop 在早停表现上与传统的基于损失的方法具有同等竞争力，且适用于更广泛的损失函数场景。

Conclusion: ScoreStop 是一种通用的、基于梯度的早停准则，通过函数评分检验避免了传统基于验证集损失早停法中超参数（如 patience）难以调优及损失函数定义不明的问题。其在基准测试中表现出与基于损失的方法相当的竞争力。

Abstract: Gradient boosted decision trees require a stopping rule to avoid overfitting. The standard rule monitors a validation loss and stops if the loss fails to improve for a fixed patience period. However, the patience parameter has no interpretable scale and validation losses can be noisy or implicitly defined by a user-specified gradient. We propose ScoreStop, a gradient-based early-stopping rule that casts the stopping decision at each iteration as a test of the null hypothesis that the current predictor is the population risk minimizer. We use a functional score test, computed on validation data, with a statistic that is scale-invariant in the update direction, with a known asymptotic distribution under the null. Because our test uses gradients rather than loss values, the same construction applies to implicit losses such as LambdaRank, and data-dependent losses such as Cox regression via influence functions. In synthetic experiments and real-data benchmarks, we show that ScoreStop is competitive with loss-based methods.

</details>


### [282] [Scalable Derivative Gaussian Processes via Exact Gradient Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02909)
*Hyunseok Seung,Matthias Katzfuss*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TERA 提出了一种利用梯度约简技术和 Vecchia 近似的高可扩展性导数高斯过程方法，成功解决了标准方法在高维空间中 $\mathcal{O}(n^3 d^3)$ 的计算瓶颈，实现了与维度无关的推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统梯度高斯过程（Derivative GP）在高维空间进行精确推理时受限于 $\mathcal{O}(n^3 d^3)$ 的立方级复杂度，导致在高维、昂贵的函数评估场景下计算不可行。

Method: 提出 TERA 方法：利用平稳核函数的性质，证明了与目标点和条件点连接方向正交的梯度分量与目标函数值条件独立，从而将计算简化为有限的定向导数。结合 Vecchia 近似，将全局高斯过程模型解耦为局部因子，从而大幅降低矩阵求逆的计算复杂度。

Result: TERA 的单目标评估时间复杂度降至 $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^6)$，空间复杂度降至 $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^4)$。实验表明，TERA 在保持先进预测精度的同时，计算速度提升了几个数量级，且计算时间和内存占用几乎不随维度 $d$ 的增加而波动。

Conclusion: TERA 成功通过目标特异性梯度约简消除了高维空间中梯度高斯过程的计算瓶颈，在保持模型数学严谨性的前提下，实现了计算复杂度和内存占用的维度独立性，为大规模、高维数据应用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Gradient observations can substantially improve Gaussian process (GP) surrogates, particularly in high-dimensional settings where function evaluations are expensive. However, exact inference with $n$ function values and $n$ full gradients in $d$ dimensions scales cubically in the joint state size, imposing an intractable $\mathcal{O}(n^3 d^3)$ computational bottleneck. We introduce TERA, a highly scalable derivative GP method based on target-specific exact gradient reduction. We prove that for stationary kernels, the gradient components orthogonal to the directions connecting the target and conditioning points are conditionally independent of the target function value; consequently, the exact conditional density is fully characterized by at most $m^2$ directional derivatives once a conditioning set of size $m$ is specified. By using these reduced, dimension-free conditionals as local factors in a Vecchia approximation, TERA effectively decouples $n$ and $d$ from the dense matrix inversion. This reduces the per-target evaluation cost to $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^6)$ time and $\mathcal{O}(dm^2 + m^4)$ memory, leaving the underlying derivative GP model mathematically unchanged. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that TERA achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while operating orders of magnitude faster than standard derivative GPs. Crucially, both computation time and peak GPU memory remain essentially flat with respect to $d$, enabling highly scalable inference in high-dimensional spaces.

</details>


### [283] [Trajectory-Aware Node Contributions and the Limits of Static Controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03067)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种衡量复杂网络节点动态影响力的度量标准——“涌现贡献度 (EC)”，解决了传统方法在非线性、时变动力系统中失效的问题，并能识别出传统静态中心性无法发现的关键节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络节点影响力评估方法（如静态中心性或线性时不变系统的可控性 Gramian）难以应对实际中普遍存在的非线性与时变系统，导致无法准确衡量节点对系统行为的实际动态贡献。

Method: 提出了基于系统轨迹上冲激响应加权积分的 EC 度量，该方法利用可微模型的雅可比矩阵进行计算，且与估计器无关。通过在非线性、动态变化的受控合成数据集上进行实验，并构建相位图研究其与线性平均可控性的异同，最后将其应用于五个真实估计系统。

Result: EC 在线性时不变限制下与平均可控性一致；在存在持续机制切换（尤其是符号反转）的非线性系统中，EC 显示出优于传统度量的性能；在真实系统实验中，EC 能够识别出那些虽然方差低但扰动传播广泛的关键节点（即“方差-杠杆解离”现象）。

Conclusion: 研究提出了“涌现贡献度 (EC)”作为评估复杂网络中节点动态杠杆作用的度量，通过实证分析表明，在非线性、动态变化的环境下，EC 能够有效捕捉静态中心性和线性控制理论所无法识别的关键节点，证明了其在复杂动力系统诊断中的必要性。

Abstract: A recurring data mining task in complex networks is to determine how individual nodes contribute to system behavior. Existing approaches rely on either static-graph centralities or control-theoretic quantities such as controllability Gramians, which assume linear, time-invariant dynamics. Estimated systems, however, are typically nonlinear and time-varying. We define "emergent contribution (EC)," a finite-horizon measure of a node's dynamical leverage: the metric-weighted energy of its impulse response accumulated along the system trajectory. Computed from the Jacobians of any differentiable model, EC is estimator-agnostic and reduces exactly to average controllability in the linear, time-invariant limit. Our contribution is a characterization of when the two measures agree and diverge. Using a controlled synthetic family with known ground-truth contribution, we construct a phase diagram spanning nonlinearity, regime structure, persistence, and perturbation amplitude. EC and average controllability agree under static or smoothly drifting dynamics and both track ground truth. Divergence emerges under persistent regime switching, is strongest under persistent sign reversal, and disappears when the sign reversal is removed. At extreme perturbation amplitudes, both measures degrade, identifying the limits of local linearization. We place five estimated real systems from several domains within this phase space. Their placement serves as a diagnostic of when EC provides information beyond static controllability and therefore justifies its additional computational cost. On one panel examined in depth, a twenty-seed retraining ensemble reveals a robust variance--leverage dissociation: nodes whose perturbations propagate widely despite low within-system variance, which is not recovered by static centralities nor variance-based summaries.

</details>


### [284] [An Asymptotic Theory of Chain-of-Thought in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03217)
*Kaito Takanami,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过随机矩阵理论分析了思维链（CoT）推理深度与泛化误差的关系，揭示了深度扩展的相变机制及其阈值条件，指出深度推理的效果受预训练与上下文限制，并存在“过度思考”风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大型语言模型中思维链（CoT）推理深度的扩展行为及其对泛化能力的影响尚不明确，缺乏系统的理论解释。

Method: 通过构建线性回归中的上下文权重预测模型（in-context weight prediction），利用高维渐近下的随机矩阵理论（Random Matrix Theory），推导出泛化误差随推理深度、预训练数据量和上下文长度变化的精确公式，并辅以完全学习的线性注意力及Softmax注意力模型实验验证。

Result: 发现在推理深度与泛化误差之间存在明确的相变（phase transition），揭示了从指数级改进到多项式改进、饱和乃至过度思考的过程；同时证明了深度推理的有效性高度依赖于预训练和上下文信息的充足性，否则会导致误差累积。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了CoT推理深度如何影响模型泛化能力的统一理论框架。合理的推理深度能带来显著的性能增益，但过深的推理会导致“过度思考”（overthinking）及误差放大。优化推理深度取决于预训练数据的丰富程度和上下文信息质量。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has become a widely used mechanism for eliciting multi-step reasoning in large language models by generating intermediate reasoning steps at inference time. Yet the scaling behavior of generalization with CoT depth remains poorly understood. To address this question, we study a theoretically solvable model of CoT for in-context weight prediction in linear regression, where test-time reasoning is represented as an iterative refinement of the weight-parameter estimate. Using tools from random matrix theory under high-dimensional asymptotics, we derive an exact formula for the generalization error as a function of reasoning depth, pretraining data amount, and context length. Our analysis reveals a sharp phase transition separating exponential and polynomial improvement, saturation, and overthinking, and characterizes how the optimal reasoning depth scales. We further show that deeper reasoning is most effective with sufficiently rich pretraining and in-context information, whereas limited pretraining or context makes longer reasoning prone to error amplification or saturation. We also validate these predictions through experiments on fully learned linear attention and softmax attention models. Our results provide a unified theoretical account of how test-time CoT depth affects generalization.

</details>


### [285] [Combining Statistical Features and Deep Encodings for Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03292)
*Pablo García-Santaclara,Bruno Fernández-Castro,Rebeca Pilar Díaz-Redondo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对多变量时间序列数据的类增量持续学习，本文提出了一种整合深度时间嵌入与统计特征的双流特征提取方法，有效平衡了新知识获取与旧信息保留，且在多个基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现实应用中分类模型在学习新类别时出现的“灾难性遗忘”问题，特别是针对具有复杂时间结构的多变量时间序列数据的类增量持续学习挑战。

Method: 提出了一种基于双流特征提取管道的方法：第一支路利用预训练并冻结的基础模型提取深度时间嵌入特征，第二支路提取统计特征，两者结合用于类增量持续学习分类。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，该系统在保持极低遗忘率的同时，实现了具有竞争力的平均准确率。

Conclusion: 该双流特征提取框架是处理多变量时间序列类增量持续学习的有效方案，能够在保持低遗忘率的同时，实现多分类任务的高准确度。

Abstract: Many systems used in real-world environments require adding new categories and incorporating new information without forgetting what was previously learnt by the classification model. This is known as class-incremental continual learning, and in the case of multivariate time-series, is further complicated by the temporal structure of the data. In this paper, we present a novel approach for performing class incremental continual learning for the classification of multivariate time series data based upon the construction of a dual-stream feature extraction pipeline (using both deep temporal embedding features generated via a pre-trained frozen foundation model and application of statistical features). Evaluated on five benchmark datasets, the proposed system achieves competitive average accuracy across all datasets while maintaining low forgetting rates across all experimental configurations.

</details>


### [286] [Few-Shot Prediction for Pulsar Noise with Long Short-Term Memory Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03574)
*Qingye Tang,Dechao An,Haoran Peng,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于元学习优化LSTM的轻量化模型，能以极少量数据实现脉冲星计时残差的快速、高精度预测，并兼具低延迟与低内存占用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决脉冲星定时阵列（PTA）数据集中，毫秒脉冲星在不同自转频率子组中普遍存在的数据稀缺问题，以及现有建模方法在资源受限环境下难以进行高效、实时预测的挑战。

Method: 提出了一种结合模型无关元学习（MAML）优化的长短期记忆网络（LSTM），并利用粒子群优化（PSO）算法进行超参数自动调优。模型通过在有限数据下进行微调（Few-shot fine-tuning），实现对特定频率域的快速适应。

Result: 在国际脉冲星定时阵列（IPTA）第二版数据上测试显示，仅使用10%的计时残差数据进行微调即可获得高精度预测；模型具有轻量化特性，CPU内存占用仅16.86 MB，单步预测时间仅需18毫秒。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的基于元学习优化的LSTM模型，通过极少量数据即可实现对脉冲星计时残差的精准快速预测，且具备轻量化优势，极大地解决了脉冲星定时阵列（PTA）中数据稀缺场景下的计算挑战，非常适合资源受限的实时应用环境。

Abstract: This work proposes a novel solution to predict pulsar timing residuals with limited data, addressing the critical challenge of data scarcity across spin-frequency subgroups of millisecond pulsars in PTA datasets. The proposed solution applies a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network optimized using the model-agnostic meta-learning algorithm, enabling rapid adaptation to new frequency domain by fine-tuning the LSTM network with only a few-shot of ground truth timing residuals. Particle swarm optimization algorithm is also used for automatic hyperparameter optimization, leading to improved prediction accuracy. Our solution, evaluated on the second data release of the International Pulsar Timing Array (IPTA), demonstrates robust generalization with accurate predictions in three metrics across high-frequency test frequency domains, while requiring only 10% of the timing residuals from these domains for model fine-tuning. Furthermore, our lightweight structure only costs 16.86 MB CPU memory and 18 milliseconds for single-step residual prediction. All these characteristics make our solution highly suitable for real-world applications, where effective and real-time predictions of pulsar timing residuals are essential-particularly in resource-constrained environments with limited computational power, memory, or energy availability.

</details>


### [287] [Set-Preserving Calibration from Conformal P-Values to E-Values](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03600)
*Nabil Alami,Jad Zakharia,Souhaib Ben Taieb*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新型p-to-e校准器，解决了共形预测中p值转换导致的保守性问题，利用e值框架提升了预测效率和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测（CP）依赖p值存在灵活性局限（如难以合并依赖性证据），而现有的p-to-e转换方法在共形预测环境下效率较低且会导致预测集过于保守。

Method: 1. 识别了经典p-to-e校准器在共形预测中导致预测保守的局限性；2. 开发了一种新型P2E校准器，具备集合保持特性；3. 将该方法应用于交叉共形预测（CCP）和共形聚合（CA），以实现严格的1-α覆盖保证。

Result: 理论和实证研究表明，新方法在保持1-α覆盖率的同时，显著提高了预测效率；在CCP和CA应用中，改进了现有基线方法的表现。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新颖的P2E校准器，将共形p值转换为e值且保持原始预测集不变，从而使共形预测能够利用e值合并和随机化技术的优势，在保持覆盖率的同时提升预测效率。

Abstract: Standard conformal prediction (CP) procedures are typically formulated in terms of p-values, but reliance on p-values alone limits flexibility, for example, when combining dependent evidence across models or data splits. Recent work has explored e-value formulations for conformal inference, yet a direct connection between p- and e-value formulations in CP has been missing, especially regarding their statistical efficiency. We first identify limitations of classical p-to-e calibrators in the CP setting, showing that they are not set-preserving and can lead to overly conservative prediction sets. To address this, we propose a novel P2E calibrator that converts conformal p-values into e-values without altering the prediction set induced by the original conformal p-value. We establish both theoretically and empirically that our calibrator can yield significant efficiency gains over existing p-to-e calibrators. This e-value formulation enables principled use of recent advances in e-value merging and randomization, where we demonstrate its impact in two applications: cross-conformal prediction (CCP), whose variants typically provide only approximate $1-2α$ coverage, and conformal aggregation (CA). In both cases, our e-value-based methods satisfy the desired $1-α$ coverage guarantee while improving efficiency over standard baselines. More broadly, our approach expands the flexibility of CP and opens new directions for efficient, distribution-free uncertainty quantification.

</details>


### [288] [Resource-Constrained Adaptive Inference for Sequential Pricing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03736)
*Ruicheng Ao,Jiashuo Jiang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文解决了资源受限下定价控制器难以进行固定价格推断的问题，提出了基于信息时钟的目标感知定价控制与局部去偏方法，并证明了局部移动对于保证推断收敛的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的环境下，定价控制器的状态可能导致目标价格邻域从可行集中被移除，从而使得基于固定价格的推断失效。现有的探索机制在面对资源约束时缺乏保证，需要新的框架来实现稳健的在线推断。

Method: 1. 形式化定义“支撑排除失效”（support-exclusion failure）及局部不可识别性结果；2. 引入“已实现信息时钟”（realized information clock）来度量推断的可行性；3. 设计目标感知定价控制器（target-aware pricing controller）以认证可行目标区间并记录局部密度；4. 应用局部去偏（localized debiasing）技术生成学生化区间（studentized intervals）。

Result: 1. 证明了在多项式目标质量下可获得多项式收敛速率，而对于单纯的 $1/t$ 目标分支，若无局部移动则无法获得收敛区间；2. 实验验证了算法在认证带内的校准性能，以及在目标支撑集缺失时的诊断性弃权能力。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的情况下，廉价探索不足以保证定价推断，必须引入局部移动机制才能获得收敛的固定目标置信区间。研究提出了目标感知定价控制器和相应的去偏方法，实现了有效的推断与诊断。

Abstract: Resource-constrained pricing controllers can make fixed-price inference impossible: the controller's resource state may remove the target price neighborhood from the feasible set, even when every realized action has a known positive density. We formalize this support-exclusion failure through a local non-identification result and a realized information clock. We then design a target-aware pricing controller that certifies feasible target bands and logs continuous local densities. Localized debiasing gives studentized intervals whose width is governed by this clock. The resulting regret--information accounting, stated up to pilot re-solving error, shows that cheap exploration can be insufficient for inference: polynomial target mass gives polynomial rates, while a pure $1/t$ target branch does not yield shrinking fixed-target intervals without additional local movement. Experiments show calibration in certified bands and diagnostic abstention when the resource state collapses target support.

</details>


### [289] [A Quantitative Approximation Framework for Flow Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03820)
*Weiguo Gao,Ming Li,Lei Shi,Hanfei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩散蒸馏的定量近似框架，量化了低噪声“刚性”区域的误差放大效应，并通过推导出一种基于累积稳定性的非均匀时间网格划分策略，显著提升了少步扩散采样器的性能与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型蒸馏在少步采样（few-step sampling）时，由于低噪声区域动力学刚性（stiffness）导致的局部误差放大，使得传统采样性能受限，亟需建立定量近似框架以理解误差累积机制。

Method: 将扩散蒸馏建模为学习流映射的复合，结合高斯混合 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程进行误差拆解；利用 ReLU-ReQU 网络界定得分场近似误差，推导概率流 ODE 的空间 Lipschitz 常数以量化动力学放大效应。

Result: 在 Gaussian-mixture OU 模型中提供了明确的 Lipschitz 稳定性界，揭示了单步蒸馏的结构性缺陷；实验结果表明，使用稳定性平衡的非均匀时间网格可使端到端相对 MSE 降低高达 51.9%。

Conclusion: 提出了一种稳定性平衡的非均匀时间网格划分方法，证明了通过累积稳定性坐标进行划分能有效降低扩散蒸馏的全局误差，并在实验中显著提升了采样质量。

Abstract: We develop a quantitative approximation framework for diffusion distillation, viewing few-step sampling as error propagation under compositions of learned flow maps. Focusing on trajectory distillation for the probability-flow ODE, we show that local approximation errors can be strongly amplified in low-noise multimodal regimes, where the underlying dynamics become stiff. In an analytically tractable Gaussian-mixture Ornstein--Uhlenbeck setting, we separate two core difficulties: approximating the time-dependent score field and controlling the dynamical amplification governed by the time-integrated Jacobian bound of the probability-flow ODE. On the approximation side, we prove constructive L^p(p_t) guarantees showing that ReLU--ReQU networks approximate the Gaussian-mixture score uniformly over time, with depth and width scaling polylogarithmically in the target accuracy and explicitly with the mixture geometry. On the stability side, we derive an explicit bound L(t) for the spatial Lipschitz constant of the probability-flow velocity and convert it into a flow map stability estimate governed by \int_s^t L(u)\,du, making late-time amplification in stiff regimes computable. Building on these estimates, we prove that deep residual compositions efficiently approximate the long-horizon transport, with global error controlled by the stability amplification factor, and identify a Lipschitz-mismatch regime in which one-step distillation is structurally unfavorable. The resulting theory yields a stability-balanced non-uniform time grid obtained by uniform partitioning in the cumulative stability coordinate. Experiments support the prediction and reduce end-to-end relative MSE by up to 51.9\% with 8 segments compared with uniform grids.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [Too Much of a Good Thing: When sim2real Efforts Impede Policy Learning (And What to Do About It)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02636)
*Kyle Morgenstein,Bharath Masetty,Stephen Welch,Luis Sentis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 当前Sim2Real方法因仿真环境限制过多而束缚了策略探索，论文提出以运动学为核心约束的Sim2Sim2Real范式，旨在解决仿真锁定问题并改善策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出当前的Sim2Real研究存在“过犹不及”的问题，即仿真环境为了逼真而施加了不合理的约束，导致策略学习出现激励错位（misaligned incentives）、陷入仿真锁定（simulator lock-in）以及难以进行有效的策略探索。

Method: 提出“Sim2Sim2Real”范式。该方法主张摆脱对复杂物理环境的过度模拟，仅将机器人的运动学（kinematics）作为设计的唯一硬性约束，从而减轻仿真对策略探索的束缚。

Result: 该论文通过诊断当前领域存在的痛点，明确了现有仿真范式的局限性，并提供了一种在简化约束前提下依然能实现有效策略迁移的路径。

Conclusion: 研究认为过度依赖“仿真到真实”（Sim2Real）导致了策略学习的激励错位，提出“Sim2Sim2Real”范式作为潜在解决方案，将机器人运动学作为设计约束的核心。

Abstract: While sim2real efforts are necessary for effective policy transfer to hardware, there is such a thing as too much of a good thing. We argue that sim2real efforts have led to misaligned incentives with policy learning, resulting in simulator lock in and poor policy exploration due to the unreasonable constraints imposed by the real world. We offer a diagnosis and explanation of the current status of the problem, and propose a potential solution via a sim2sim2real paradigm that leverages the robot's kinematics as the sole design constraint.

</details>


### [291] [CARVE: Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed Maneuvers via Envelopes for Interactive Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02641)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARVE 是一种用于自动驾驶的新型交互修复认证框架，它通过在规则过滤器之上增加一个非预测性的认证层，成功解决了自动驾驶在交互场景中因过度保守而导致的行动受阻问题，确保了交互的安全性和规范性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶规则过滤器（如规则书、盾牌）往往过于死板，导致在某些实际上可以通过其他车辆微小配合即可安全通过的情况下，因“硬规则”限制而拒绝可行操作（即假否定/假 veto）。现有方案缺乏对“交互式修复”证明的支持，即缺失明确的责任归属和 fallback 机制。

Method: 引入了交互式修复认证（Interactive Repair Certification）概念，并开发了 CARVE 架构；利用有限格（finite lattice）的战术算子层，结合“合作包络”（cooperation envelope）技术，在不依赖行为预测的前提下，通过对交互请求的责任划分、修复修复集定义和动态 fallback 机制，实现了对候选动作的实时验证。

Result: 在 589 个 INTERACTION 回放场景中，CARVE-Greedy 成功接受了 98.64% 的初始被否决动作，恢复了 370/378 次人类可解决的假否决，同时保持了 100% 的通行权尊重（right-of-way respect），实现了零优先代理假阳性，并验证了其在理论上的完备性与一致性。

Conclusion: CARVE 提供了一种非预测性的交互式修复认证机制，能够在保证通行权的前提下修复自动驾驶堆栈中的“假否定”问题，通过形式化验证方法实现了对交互请求的责任归属和安全性证明，有效提升了复杂交通场景下的可行性。

Abstract: Interactive driving exposes a failure mode that is easy to miss in rule-aware autonomous-driving stacks: a hard-rule margin can be negative for an ego candidate even though a small lawful accommodation by a non-priority agent would restore feasibility. Existing rulebooks, shields, and reachability filters are strong at vetoing unsafe actions, while prediction-based planners model likely responses. Neither returns a runtime proof object that states which bounded multi-agent edit repairs the maneuver, who owns the edit, whether the request is right-of-way affordable, and what ego fallback remains if the request is not observed. We formulate this missing object as *interactive repair certification* and introduce *CARVE*, a prediction-free certificate layer over a finite lattice of ego-owned and agent-owned tactical operators. Agent-owned requests are admissible only inside \(B_j(s) = β(π_j)α_j^{\max}(s)\), a cooperation envelope that separates kinematic reachability from normative priority. The resulting certificate records the binding rule, repair category, repair set, responsibility-weighted cost split, and fallback. On 589 Lanelet2-geometry-grounded INTERACTION replay episodes, CARVE-Greedy accepts 98.64% of initially vetoed maneuvers and recovers 370/378 human-resolved false vetoes, while preserving 589/589 right-of-way respect, zero priority-agent false positives, and 400/400 negative-stress vetoes. We prove certificate soundness, structural right-of-way respect, exact finite-lattice minimality, fallback contingency, and blame-consistency conditions. CARVE does not predict or require another driver's compliance; it certifies whether a proposed interaction is bounded, attributable, and normatively admissible under declared assumptions.

</details>


### [292] [Fixed-Time Dynamic Landing of Quadrotors using Adaptive Unscented Kalman Filtering and Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02658)
*Mohammadreza Izadi,Zeinab Shayan,Steven Waslander,Reza Faieghi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无人机在移动平台降落的控制框架，结合了非线性模型预测控制和自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波，实现了高精度的受控降落并增强了对感知误差的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升多旋翼无人机在复杂、动态移动平台上降落时的着陆精度、定时同步能力以及对抗感知不确定性的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 采用非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）实现动态运动规划；2. 整合实时最小加加速度（Minimum-Jerk）轨迹规划器以强制执行预定的降落时间；3. 引入自适应无迹卡尔曼滤波（Adaptive UKF）在线实时更新处理与测量噪声统计信息；4. 进行了参考可行性分析以确保指令在推力和力矩约束范围内。

Result: 仿真与实物实验结果表明，该方法实现了高度可重复的降落，并在平台速度感知精度方面优于标准的EKF/UKF方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的控制框架通过整合非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）与自适应UKF，有效解决了移动平台对多旋翼无人机降落的动态约束与环境不确定性问题，在实验中表现出比传统EKF/UKF更优的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper introduces an estimation and control framework for dynamic landing of multi-rotor uncrewed aerial vehicles on moving platforms. The proposed method integrates nonlinear model predictive control with a real-time minimum-jerk trajectory planner that enforces a prescribed touchdown time, enabling consistent timing during the terminal descent. To enhance robustness in the presence of time-varying sensing quality, we utilize an adaptive unscented kalman filter that updates the process and measurement noise statistics online. In addition, we provide a reference feasibility analysis showing that minimum-jerk references induce bounded thrust and torque commands under standard tracking hypotheses. The proposed framework is evaluated in simulation and hardware experiments, and it is shown to achieve repeatable landings and improved platform velocity prediction accuracy relative to EKF/UKF-based methods.

</details>


### [293] [Motion Planning in Dynamic Environments: A Survey from Classical to Modern Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02677)
*Zongyuan Shen,Yaming Ou,Shalabh Gupta,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Gao Wang,Zhongqiang Ren,Junfeng Fan,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015-2025年间138篇关于动态环境运动规划的研究，将其分为采样、搜索、MPC、学习及经典局部规划五类，并分析了相关感知技术、典型挑战及其优缺点，为该领域研究提供了结构化参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管针对静态环境的运动规划综述较多，但缺乏针对动态环境（要求机器人实时感知并适应环境变化）的系统性总结。因此，需要对近十年的动态环境运动规划方法进行综合梳理，以应对导航中的安全性和连续性挑战。

Method: 对2015年至2025年间的138篇相关文献进行综合调研，将运动规划方法分为采样、图搜索、模型预测控制（MPC）、学习方法（监督学习、强化学习）及其他经典局部规划方法（速度障碍法、人工势场法、动态窗口法）五大类别，并探讨了相机、LiDAR和事件相机在动态感知中的作用。

Result: 系统分类并总结了动态环境下的各类运动规划算法，详细对比分析了各类方法的原则、强项与局限性，并讨论了动态感知技术及该领域亟待解决的瓶颈问题。

Conclusion: 本文通过对138篇文献的系统梳理，为研究人员提供了动态环境下运动规划方法的结构化知识框架，明确了现有研究的优劣势及面临的挑战（如预测不确定性、人机交互、冻结机器人问题等），为未来的路径规划研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Motion planning in dynamic environments requires robots to continuously adapt their paths in response to environmental changes for safe and uninterrupted navigation. While many surveys have reviewed planning in static settings, systematic reviews focused on dynamic environments remain limited. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of 138 works, primarily published between 2015 and 2025, spanning both classical and learning-based approaches. The motion planning methods are grouped into five categories based on the concepts of sampling, graph search, model predictive control, learning, and additional classical local planning approaches, including velocity obstacles, potential fields and dynamic windows. The learning techniques include supervised learning and reinforcement learning. We also discuss the role of dynamic perception in motion planning, covering techniques for detecting and modeling moving obstacles using cameras, LiDAR, and event-based sensors. The survey analyzes the principles, strengths, and limitations of each method, with particular attention to challenges unique to dynamic environments, such as prediction uncertainty, human-robot interaction, and the freezing robot problem.
