The survey provides researchers with a structured understanding of motion planning methods in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [294] [See Less, Specify More: Visual Evidence Budgets for Generalizable VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02735)
*Yueh-Hua Wu,Tatsuya Matsushima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S2（See Less, Specify More）框架通过细化语言指令和限制视觉输入范围，提升了VLA模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性，有效解决了模型对无关背景产生依赖的监督混淆问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在面对干扰、外观变化和复杂任务时，往往难以平衡“粗粒度指令”与“局部执行细节”，导致泛化性能较差，模型易受无关视觉上下文干扰。

Method: 提出了S2（See Less, Specify More）框架：1. Specify More：通过重标记轨迹，将原始的高层指令细化为局部的、消歧义的子任务指令；2. See Less：通过引入显式的视觉证据预算，强制执行器仅基于任务充分的视觉特征进行决策，无需额外的区域或掩码标注。

Result: 通过八项真实机器人实验验证，S2相比基准模型（pi0.5）将平均子任务成功率从54.2%大幅提升至79.0%，证明了在训练中解耦任务意图与视觉干扰的有效性。

Conclusion: VLA模型的泛化能力可以通过优化执行器的学习接口得到显著提升。通过引入“精细化指导”和“视觉证据预算”，模型能够减少对背景噪声的依赖，从而在真实机器人任务中实现更高的成功率（从54.2%提升至79.0%）。

Abstract: Generalization remains a central bottleneck for vision-language-action (VLA) models: under distractors, appearance shifts, and semantically similar tasks, the policy must often infer local execution details from coarse instructions while also deciding which parts of the image matter for control. We present S2 (See Less, Specify More), a framework for improving VLA generalization by training the executor under a cleaner interface.
  Specify More preserves the original instruction as a stable high-level goal while relabeling each trajectory into refined trajectory- and subtask-level language that disambiguates the current execution mode. Unlike native attention, See Less imposes an explicit visual evidence budget, training the executor to act from task-sufficient evidence rather than unconstrained visual context, without any region or mask annotation.
  This interface lets the executor follow detailed guidance without relying on distracting visual patches or resolving avoidable ambiguity on its own, and it remains compatible with off-the-shelf VLM planners through in-context learning. Across our main evaluation settings, S2 improves overall generalization metrics by changing the executor's learning problem: coarse instructions induce avoidable supervision aliasing, goal-preserving local guidance outperforms instruction replacement in our main ablations, and explicit evidence budgeting reduces dependence on broad visual context beyond efficiency considerations.
  Across eight real-robot tasks on TX-G2 (an AgiBot G2-compatible variant) and HSR, S2 raises mean subtask success from 54.2% to 79.0% over pi0.5. Together, these results suggest that VLA generalization improves when the executor is trained to act from informative local guidance and task-sufficient visual evidence, rather than recovering both from weak supervision.

</details>


### [295] [SeeTraceAct: Visibility-Aware Latent Planning from Cross-Embodiment Demonstration Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02745)
*Jaehyeon Son,Junhyun Kim,Kyle Kam,Jeremiah Coholich,Seok Joon Kim,Jinhoo Kim,Chris Dongjoo Kim,Jaemin Cho,Dieter Fox,Zsolt Kira*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对单次演示下的机器人任务执行问题，本文提出了 SeeTraceAct，通过引导模型预测末端执行器轨迹来增强空间定位精度，显著提升了跨任务和跨具身的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在处理未见任务时，往往依赖昂贵的特定任务远程操作数据，且在需要对小目标进行精确操作和定位的任务中表现不佳。

Method: 提出 SeeTraceAct 框架，其核心在于通过“可见性感知”（visibility-aware）的未来末端执行器轨迹（end-effector traces）预测，增强 VLA 模型对小目标区域的精准空间定位能力。此外，作者构建了 RoboCasa-DC 数据集以支持跨具身演示的评估。

Result: 在 RoboCasa-DC 和现实世界机器人基准测试中，SeeTraceAct 在所有四项评估配置中均优于现有基准，并将现实世界任务的平均成功率提升了 12.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: SeeTraceAct 通过轨迹辅助的视觉空间定位策略，有效提升了单次演示条件下的机器人任务执行成功率，且在跨具身（cross-embodiment）任务中表现优异。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are promising general-purpose robot policies, but adapting them to new tasks typically requires costly task-specific teleoperation data. As an alternative, we study one-shot demo-conditioned VLAs, where a robot policy is conditioned on a single demonstration video of an unseen task. We find that existing end-to-end approaches often struggle when successful execution requires precisely localizing small target regions. To address this limitation, we propose SeeTraceAct, a demo-conditioned VLA framework that encourages precise spatial grounding through visibility-aware prediction of future end-effector traces. To enable reproducible evaluation with cross-embodiment demonstrations, we introduce and release RoboCasa-DC, a demo-conditioned extension of RoboCasa with episode-paired humanoid videos. Experiments on RoboCasa-DC and a real-world benchmark, where a Franka Panda arm is conditioned on human demonstrations, show that SeeTraceAct outperforms baselines, achieving the best success rate across all four RoboCasa-DC settings and improving real-world average success by 12.5 percentage points.

</details>


### [296] [A Measurement-Driven Digital Twin Architecture for Plant-Level Biomass Estimation and Growth Forecasting in Hydroponic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02796)
*Morgan Mayborne,Abhisesh Silwal,George Kantor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种基于数字孪生技术的水培生菜生长监测与预测系统，通过RGB-D图像深度学习与模型集成，实现了对植物未来产量的精准预估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对城市人口稠密地区的食品供应挑战，探索替代传统土壤种植的水培技术，并希望通过数字化手段实现对植物生长过程的精确追踪与预测。

Method: 开发了一套集成定制硬件与传感器水培温室系统；构建数字孪生模型以动态更新生长轨迹；利用基于RGB-D图像的定制神经网络模型进行产量估算（在1300张图像数据集上训练）。

Result: 产量估算模型与实际值偏差在1.5g以内；数字孪生系统可实现未来1至4天的产量预测，预测误差保持在2g左右。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了“数字孪生”技术在水培系统中监测及预测生菜生长状况的可行性，能够实现对植物未来产量（1-4天内）的精确预测，为城市园艺的精准化和自动化管理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Alternatives to soil-based horticulture, such as hydroponics, have been developed to respond to food distribution concerns for dense urban centers. A new system was developed to track an individual lettuce plant's growth in a hydroponic environment, utilizing streams of measured information and available models to continuously update the growth trajectory estimates for a plant. These "digital twin" models were integrated into an operating hydroponic greenhouse, with custom horticultural and sensor hardware to grow and measure relevant information. To aid in updating model parameters, plant yield was continuously measured with a custom neural network, using RGB-D images of the plants as an input. The network, trained on a collected dataset of 1300 images, was able to estimate mass within 1.5 g of the ground-truth value. After integration into the custom system, digital twin growth projections could approximate future yield between one and four days in the future, maintaining around a 2 g forecasting error.

</details>


### [297] [Direct Informed Sampling on Riemannian Manifolds via Loewner Order Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02879)
*Phone Thiha Kyaw,Jonathan Kelly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对黎曼度量下的运动规划采样问题，本文提出了一种基于矩阵启发式的方法，通过准确捕捉度量张量的方向结构，构建了更紧凑的采样区域，实现了比传统标量界限法更高效的无拒绝采样，从而显著加快了规划器的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动规划采样技术多基于欧氏启发式，在配置相关的黎曼度量下不再适用（不可采纳）。现有的标量特征值界限法虽然恢复了可采纳性，但丢失了度量的方向结构，导致生成的受限采样空间过于保守，效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种基于Loewner序的矩阵值准许启发式算法（Matrix-valued admissible heuristic）。通过计算度量张量的最紧常数下界，利用Cholesky分解将黎曼度量下的空间映射到各向同性的欧氏空间，从而将非欧氏受限集转化为标准的扁长超椭球体（prolate hyperspheroid），实现无拒绝采样。

Result: 在6自由度UR5、7自由度Franka和14自由度PR2等复杂机械臂任务中，该方法生成的受限采样集比欧氏方法和标量特征值边界方法更紧致，且与现有多种最先进的规划器兼容，能显著提升规划效率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于Loewner序的矩阵值启发式算法能有效解决黎曼度量下的非欧氏空间采样问题，通过构建更紧致的受限采样空间，显著加速了渐近最优运动规划算法的收敛速度。

Abstract: Informed sampling techniques accelerate sampling-based motion planners by focusing the search on promising regions of the state space, yet most existing methods rely on Euclidean heuristics that become inadmissible under configuration-dependent Riemannian metrics. While scalar eigenvalue bounds restore admissibility by uniformly scaling the Euclidean distance, they discard the directional structure of the metric, producing overly conservative informed sets. We propose a matrix-valued admissible heuristic that exploits the Loewner order on symmetric positive definite matrices to compute the tightest constant lower bound on the metric tensor while preserving its full directional structure. The Cholesky factorization of this bound defines a linear map to an isotropic Euclidean space in which the Riemannian informed set reduces to a standard prolate hyperspheroid, enabling direct, rejection-free sampling using existing algorithms. Experiments on manipulation tasks with a 6-DoF UR5, 7-DoF Franka, and 14-DoF PR2 under three distinct Riemannian metrics show that our heuristic produces consistently tighter informed sets than both the Euclidean and scalar eigenvalue bounds, accelerating convergence across multiple state-of-the-art asymptotically optimal planners.

</details>


### [298] [Impact of a Soft Wearable Back-Support Device on Postural Stability during Trip-Like Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02888)
*Yuanhao Chen,Rohan Khatavkar,Soubhagya Nayak,Jiefeng Sun,Hyunglae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现柔性可穿戴背部支撑设备可通过调节刚度，有效提升健康人群在站立和行走时应对外部扰动的姿态稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究软性可穿戴背部支撑装置能否增强人体在面对类似绊倒的外部扰动时的姿态稳定性。

Method: 采用实验法，招募健康受试者开展三种条件下（无设备、低刚度、高刚度）的站立和行走扰动实验，通过测量最大不稳定点的最小稳定裕度（MOS）来量化全身稳定性。

Result: 使用设备后MOS增加，表明稳定性提升。站立实验中，MOS随设备刚度增加显著提高；行走实验中，两种刚度条件下的MOS均优于无设备组，但组间无显著差异。

Conclusion: 柔性可穿戴背部支撑设备通过调节刚度能有效提升人体面对外部扰动时的反应性平衡控制能力，在防跌倒领域具有应用潜力。未来应研究个性化刚度优化及针对高跌倒风险人群的有效性。

Abstract: The effectiveness of a soft wearable back-support device in enhancing postural stability was investigated under trip-like perturbations using two experimental paradigms: perturbed standing and perturbed walking. Healthy subjects completed trials under three different back-support conditions: no device, device worn with low stiffness, and device activated with high stiffness. Whole-body stability was quantified using the minimum Margin of Stability (MOS) at the point of maximal instability. Results demonstrated increased MOS during device use, indicating enhanced postural stability. In standing, MOS increased significantly with device stiffness, whereas in walking, both device conditions improved MOS relative to no device but did not differ significantly from each other. These findings highlight the potential of soft wearable back-support devices with adjustable stiffness to improve reactive balance control against external perturbations, with important implications for fall prevention. Future research should explore personalized stiffness optimization and evaluate efficacy in populations at elevated risk of falls.

</details>


### [299] [Improved Postural Stability Using a Lightweight Semi-Active Soft Back Support Device Under Standing Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02928)
*Rohan Khatavkar,Jiefeng Sun,Hyunglae Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究开发了一种轻量化半主动软体背部机器人，通过气动人工肌肉提供快速辅助，有效解决了传统设备因重心位移产生的稳定性问题及被动设备辅助力不足的局限，显著改善了站立干扰后的姿态控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的重型背部支撑设备会因自身质量改变重心，导致稳定性不足；而轻量化被动设备在较小的躯干前屈中辅助力有限。亟需一种既轻便又能提供足够辅助力的方案来降低老年人的跌倒风险。

Method: 设计并评估了一种结合气动人工肌肉（主动组件）与弹性带（被动组件）的轻量化半主动软体背部辅助设备；在5名健康受试者身上进行了站立扰动实验，对比其对姿态稳定性的影响。

Result: 实验表明，该半主动设备显著降低了全身角动量，并增加了稳定性裕度（margin of stability），验证了其在提升平衡恢复能力方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 半主动软体背部辅助设备在预防老年人站立失衡引起的跌倒方面展现出巨大潜力，通过轻量化设计与主动辅助机制，能够在不影响重心的前提下有效提升姿态稳定性。

Abstract: Older adults are particularly susceptible to falls following perturbations during standing, such as forward loss of balance. Back support devices that assist trunk extension may help mitigate fall risk by preventing excessive trunk flexion. Previous studies have investigated heavy back support devices; however, these systems often introduced adverse effects on stability due to their added mass, which shifted the body's natural center of mass unfavorably. In contrast, lightweight passive devices have shown limited benefits, as they can generate only modest assistive forces during the relatively small trunk flexion associated with forward balance loss. In this study, we evaluated the effects of a lightweight semi-active soft back support device on postural stability following standing perturbations. Our device combines an active element (a pneumatic artificial muscle) in parallel with a passive elastic band. The active element rapidly provides assistive force following a perturbation, overcoming the limitations of passive devices. Experiments conducted with five healthy individuals demonstrated that the semi-active device significantly reduced whole-body angular momentum and increased the margin of stability, indicating improved balance recovery performance. These results highlight the promise of semi-active soft wearable robots as an effective and lightweight strategy for fall prevention during standing perturbations.

</details>


### [300] [SCOPE: Real-Time Natural Language Camera Agent at the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02951)
*Nikolaj Hindsbo,Sina Ehsani,Pragyana Mishra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 SCOPE 系统，这是首个用于边缘计算环境下的语言驱动 PTZ 相机控制与场景理解的评估框架，通过 536 项任务基准测试，验证了通过模型量化和 MoE 架构在边缘设备上实现低延迟、高精度任务执行的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言驱动机器人代理缺乏针对边缘部署、实时控制及特定任务评价（如 PTZ 相机操作）的全面评估系统。研究人员旨在解决如何高效连接语言模型与感知/控制工具，并量化延迟、准确率及错误模式的问题。

Method: 提出 SCOPE（模拟与相机感知评估）框架，包含 Blender 模拟环境和物理 PTZ 相机控制，使用语言模型驱动工具调用。构建了包含 536 项任务的基准测试，采用 LM-as-Judge 进行闭环评估。测试了 19 种规划器（Qwen3 SLM）与感知模型（Moondream/Qwen VLM）的组合，并应用混合专家模型（MoE）和量化技术。

Result: 研究发现更强的 SLM 能显著减少幻觉并改善工具调度；当规划器足够强时，感知能力成为性能瓶颈；MoE 模型以与小型模型相当的延迟和内存占用，实现了优于或等同于大型密集模型的效果；量化技术有效提升了资源效率。

Conclusion: SCOPE 证明了在边缘端部署基于语言驱动的 PTZ 相机控制是可行的。通过 SLM 与 VLM 的组合以及量化技术的应用，实现了在低延迟和低存储条件下完成复杂任务的能力，为实时边缘机器人控制提供了一个经过模拟到现实（sim-to-real）验证的设计参考。

Abstract: Deploying language-driven agents in robotics requires evaluations that reflect real-world task demands: natural-language instructions with reproducible outcomes. Such agents must connect language models to callable perception and control tools, and be assessed using deployment-critical metrics including latency, accuracy, and error modes. We present SCOPE (Simulation and Camera Operations for Perception and Evaluation), a modular agent for natural-language, open-vocabulary pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) camera control and visual scene understanding, designed explicitly for edge deployment. SCOPE operates both in a Blender-based simulation environment and on a physical PTZ camera, executing all perception, planning, and control locally at the deployment site using edge-accessible compute.
  We release a 536-task benchmark spanning QA, single- and multi-step commands, counting, spatial reasoning, descriptions, and optical character recognition in a Blender-based simulation environment that exposes realistic PTZ control affordances. Execution traces are combined with an LM-as-Judge to evaluate latency, accuracy, and error modes.
  We evaluate 19 planner-perception model combinations pairing Qwen3 small language models (SLMs) with Moondream and Qwen vision-language models (VLMs). Stronger SLMs substantially reduce hallucinations and improve tool routing, leading to more reliable closed-loop behavior. Once a sufficiently capable SLM is used, perception becomes the dominant performance bottleneck. Mixture-of-Experts models on both the planning and perception side consistently match or exceed dense alternatives at latencies and memory footprints comparable to much smaller networks. Quantization provides additional efficiency gains with minimal accuracy degradation, identifying a practical, sim-to-real validated design point for real-time, edge-feasible language-driven PTZ control.

</details>


### [301] [Hybrid Dynamics Modeling for a Flexible 2-DoF Robotic Arm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02969)
*Maciek Popik,Daniel Yang,Mahdis Bisheban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对比了针对柔性双自由度机械臂的三种建模方法，发现结合物理信息与数据驱动（半参数化/残差学习）的方法比纯物理参数化模型更能有效地处理未建模的动态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刚体动力学模型无法捕捉柔性连杆机器人的未建模动态，需要探索能够有效处理这种动态特性的建模方法。

Method: 对比了三种建模方法：1. 两种基于物理信息的模型（将刚体动力学RBD与高斯混合模型GMM结合以捕获残差和柔性）；2. 基于运动学的回归模型（纯数据驱动基准）。通过岭回归预测扭矩，并将物理模型参数与数据估计参数进行对比。

Result: 基于物理的基准模型准确度最差；而经过正则化的回归模型和普通最小二乘法估计器与测量到的扭矩吻合度更高。

Conclusion: 纯参数化模型在处理柔性连杆系统时存在局限性，正则化与数据驱动的识别方法至关重要，支持开发半参数化残差学习方法。

Abstract: This paper examines three approaches for modeling the dynamics of a flexible-link 2-DoF robotic arm to address unmodeled dynamics not captured by rigid-body models. Two physics informed models combine rigid-body dynamics (RBD) formulations with a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) to capture residual model errors and linkage flexibility. A kinematics-based regression model serves as a purely data-driven baseline. Using an open-source dataset, torque predictions are first estimated using Ridge regression on kinematic features, while the physicsbased baseline is constructed from published specifications, and ordinary least-squares regression is subsequently used to estimate the same parameter set directly from data. Results show that the physics-based parameters yield the poorest accuracy, while regularized and least-squares estimators align more closely with measured torques. Residual analysis and error metrics highlight the limitations of purely parametric models for flexible-link systems and underscore the value of regularization and data-driven identification, supporting developments of semi-parametric residual learning methods.

</details>


### [302] [ModuLoop : Low-Level Code Generation using Modular Synthesizer and Closed-Loop Debugger for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03047)
*Gina Yoon,Sumin Lee,Joo Yong Sim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种闭环模块化代码合成框架，使LLM无需微调即可通过迭代代码生成与动态调试，实现高精度的真实场景机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在代码生成领域表现优异，但在需要精密控制、实时反馈及环境依赖的机器人底层控制任务中，其应用仍受到限制，亟需一种能闭环优化控制程序的框架。

Method: 通过不经特定微调的预训练LLM，执行模块化代码规划与生成；在执行过程中引入调试探针（debugging probes）观察行为，并采用闭环反馈机制进行系统性调试与迭代精炼，最终生成可行的控制程序。

Result: 在RGB-D相机与机械臂标定任务及后续的抓取任务中，该框架均表现出高执行精度与自主性，证实了方案的有效性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的闭环模块化代码合成框架有效缓解了LLM在处理复杂、低级机器人控制任务时的实时性与精确性不足，验证了其在复杂真实环境下的实用性、可扩展性与高自主性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive performance across various domains, including code generation and problem solving. However, their application in robotic control, particularly in low-level tasks that require precise manipulation, real-time feedback, and environment-dependent execution, remains limited. To address this challenge, we propose the Closed-Loop Modular Code Synthesizer framework. This framework leverages a pre-trained LLM without any task-specific fine-tuning to perform modular code planning and generation, and iteratively executes the generated code while inserting debugging probes to observe its behavior. This closed-loop structure facilitates systematic debugging and refinement, ultimately producing executable control programs. We apply the proposed framework to the calibration of an RGB-D camera and a robotic arm, validating its effectiveness in real-world settings. Furthermore, through a subsequent pick-and-place task, we demonstrate not only the accuracy of the calibration but also the potential extensibility of the framework. Across both tasks, the framework achieved high execution accuracy and autonomy, illustrating the practicality and scalability of LLM-based robotic control using our framework.

</details>


### [303] [TTT-VLA: Test-Time Latent Prompt Optimization for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03127)
*Wenbo Zhang,Jianxiong Li,Shuai Yang,Sijin Chen,Jiajun Liu,Lingqiao Liu,Xiao Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了解决 VLA 模型在部署时的分布偏移问题，本研究提出了 TTT-VLA，通过在测试阶段通过自监督信号优化潜在提示（而不是策略本体），实现了高效的策略自适应与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型在部署时容易受到分布偏移的影响，且传统的提示引导依赖外部信息；本研究旨在探索是否可以通过优化提示本身，在测试时实现自主的策略适配（Test-Time Training）。

Method: 提出了 TTT-VLA 框架，该框架基于潜在提示优化（LPO）。在训练阶段，通过额外代理任务学习潜在提示；在测试时，利用环境交互数据，通过代理任务的自监督信号仅对潜在提示进行优化。

Result: 在 SimplerEnv 的单身/多身设定实验中，该方法显著提升了任务成功率，且分析表明改进主要源于对少量关键动作决策的校准。

Conclusion: TTT-VLA 通过潜在提示优化（LPO）提供了一种高效、实用的基座操控策略部署时优化路径，能够通过自我监督信号在不修改策略本身的情况下提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models trained on large-scale data have made remarkable progress, but they remain vulnerable to distribution shifts at deployment time. Recent VLA models suggest that prompts can serve as an efficient interface for steering policy behavior, but existing prompt-based steering typically relies on external guidance. This raises a natural question: can test-time training (TTT) for VLA be achieved by optimizing a prompt, so that the steering interface itself can be learned and adapted from interaction? We address this question with TTT-VLA, a test-time training framework based on Latent Prompt Optimization (LPO). During training, the latent prompt is learned with an additional proxy task, providing an extra learned conditioning signal for policy learning. At test time, TTT is performed by collecting interaction data from the current environment and optimizing only the latent prompt on those data using the proxy task's self-supervised signal, without modifying the policy itself. Experiments on SimplerEnv demonstrate that the proposed method consistently improves task success rates in both single- and multi-embodiment settings. Further analysis shows that the gains arise primarily from correcting a small number of critical decisions rather than globally altering policy behavior. These results suggest that LPO provides an effective and practical pathway for deployment-time improvement of foundation manipulation policies.

</details>


### [304] [How Visible Are Silent Manipulation Failures? An Observability Study of False-Success Detection in Simulated Robot Episodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03134)
*Aarav Bedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究探讨了如何纠正机器人模仿学习中的“虚假成功”标注。通过对比本体感觉与视觉传感器，发现任务类型决定了所需的信息模态，且单纯依赖本体感觉在真实环境中易受噪声挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习的性能受限于训练数据的标签质量，其中“虚假成功”（即任务失败但被机器误判为成功）会导致学习策略发生严重偏差。本研究旨在探讨在已知样本被标记为成功的情况下，究竟需要多大程度的本体感觉或视觉信息才能有效地纠正这些伪标签。

Method: 1. 构建包含两个双臂ALOHA机器人的模拟测试平台；2. 通过环境扰动引发任务失败，并利用只有模拟器可见的特权状态进行正确标记；3. 筛选出所有被机器人误标记为“成功”的样本（即虚假成功）；4. 对比仅使用本体感觉数据与使用视觉数据的检测器对虚假成功的识别准确度。

Result: 结果显示，对于简单的任务（积木搬运），本体感觉数据几乎可以识别出所有虚假成功；但对于需要精确接触的任务（插销插入），本体感觉只能识别一部分，引入视觉信息可以显著提高识别率。研究进一步指出，本体感觉的有效性通常源于远低于现实传感器噪声阈值的速度差异，这意味着在真实硬件上其实践意义可能受限。

Conclusion: 在机器人模仿学习中，识别“虚假成功（false success）”的能力高度依赖于任务类型。仅依靠本体感觉（proprioception）在某些任务中可能足够（如积木搬运），但在复杂交互任务（如插销插入）中，视觉信息是必不可少的补充。此外，本体感觉的判别能力通常依赖于极细微的速度差异，这在实际噪声环境下很难复现，因此该研究结果更倾向于一个乐观的上限。

Abstract: Imitation-learning policies for robot manipulation inherit the quality of the success labels attached to their training episodes, and those labels are usually produced by the robot's own success check. A particularly damaging error is the false success: an episode the robot logs as a success when the task outcome was actually wrong. We ask a narrow but practical question about these episodes. Once an episode has already been flagged as a success, how much of the information needed to overturn that label is present in proprioception, and how much requires vision? We build a simulated testbed on two bimanual ALOHA tasks, induce failures through environment perturbations rather than label edits, label every episode by privileged simulator state that the detector never sees, and keep only episodes the robot flagged as successful. We then compare detectors restricted to proprioception against a vision-based detector. We find that recoverability spans a wide range: in cube transfer the false successes are almost fully recoverable from joint data alone, while in peg insertion proprioception recovers only part of them and a vision detector closes most of the gap. We also show that the proprioceptive separability we measure rests on velocity differences far below any realistic sensor noise floor, so it is best read as an optimistic upper bound that a noiseless simulator inflates. We release the generation and evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [305] [ConTrack: Constrained Hand Motion Tracking with Adaptive Trade-off Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03177)
*Yutong Liang,Quanquan Peng,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ConTrack 是一个针对灵巧操作的学习框架，通过引入约束驱动的控制权分配与自适应状态重置机制，实现了在长程、接触密集任务中对人类演示的高保真跟踪与高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灵巧操作中人类演示向真实机器人迁移时的运动学鸿沟，以及现有方法在处理长程、接触密集型任务时高度依赖手动奖励函数调整的问题。

Method: 1. 将物体追踪视为约束条件，利用双变量更新机制实现在线自适应的任务-风格权衡；2. 引入自适应中途重置库（Adaptive mid-trajectory reset library），实现策略可达状态的复用，从而稳定长程任务的学习。

Result: 在仿真跟踪及真实机器人实验中，ConTrack 在成功率和物体位姿精度方面均显著优于现有方法，同时更好地保持了演示中的关节运动和接触时序特征。

Conclusion: ConTrack 能够有效平衡任务目标与动作保真度，成功解决了复杂灵巧操作任务中动作捕捉与实机执行间的运动学差异及长程任务稳定性问题，显著提升了成功率与物体位姿跟踪精度。

Abstract: Human demonstrations provide strong priors for robot manipulation, yet it is non-trivial to transfer them to execute on real robots due to the kinematic gap. In dexterous manipulation, it remains challenging to track long-horizon, contact-rich sequences even in simulators: a reference-tracking policy must keep objects on their target trajectories while preserving demonstrated joint motion and contact timing. Existing approaches often rely on hand-crafted reward tuning that require per-sequence tuning and break under limited interaction budgets. We introduce ConTrack, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework that scales with tracking data. ConTrack treats object tracking as a constraint and allocates remaining control authority to motion fidelity, which allows it to adapt task--style trade-offs online using a dual-variable update. In addition, ConTrack also stabilizes long-horizon learning with an adaptive mid-trajectory reset library that reuses policy-reachable simulator states. Our qualitative and quantitative results in simulation tracking and real robot demonstrate that ConTrack improves success and object pose accuracy significantly over prior arts while preserving joint and contact fidelity. Website: https://www.lyt0112.com/projects/ConTrack.

</details>


### [306] [GeoSem-WAM: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03188)
*Fulong Ma,Daojie Peng,Wenjun Yue,Jiahang Cao,Bintao Wang,Qiang Zhang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需运行时显式生成预测的结构化世界模型，通过联合学习RGB、几何和语义表示，提升了具身智能在复杂环境中的性能与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有世界模型（WAMs）主要依赖RGB预测、缺乏对复杂环境的结构和空间理解，且关于其有效性来源（是依赖表示学习还是显式想象）存在争议的问题，旨在通过引入表征学习中的结构化监督来增强潜在表示的能力。

Method: 提出了一种结构化世界建模框架，在传统的RGB未来预测基础上，增加了几何（geometry）和语义（semantic）表示的辅助预测分支，使模型在统一的潜在空间中联合捕获场景动态、空间几何与语义上下文。该方法在测试时摒弃了显式的未来观测生成。

Result: 实验表明，结构化世界监督一致地提升了动作预测精度、场景理解能力和在困难具身场景下的鲁棒性，验证了该方法在可扩展且高效的世界模型构建中的潜力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了引入几何和语义监督的结构化世界模型能显著提升具身智能在复杂场景下的动作预测精度、场景理解能力及鲁棒性，同时无需在推理时进行显式的未来滚动预测，兼顾了性能与效率。

Abstract: Recent World Action Models (WAMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in embodied decision-making. However, whether their effectiveness stems from explicit future imagination during inference or representation learning induced by predictive training remains an open question. Emerging evidence suggests the primary advantage lies in learning robust latent representations rather than generating future observations at test time. Nevertheless, existing WAMs mainly rely on RGB-based future prediction, which provides limited structural and spatial understanding of complex environments. To address this, we propose a structured world modeling framework that enhances latent representations through geometric and semantic supervision. Alongside future RGB prediction, our model introduces two auxiliary prediction branches for future geometry and semantic representations, enabling it to jointly capture scene dynamics, spatial geometry, and semantic context within a unified latent space. Crucially, our approach preserves efficient inference by avoiding explicit future rollout or video generation at test time. Extensive experiments show that incorporating structured world supervision consistently improves action prediction accuracy, scene understanding, and robustness under challenging embodied scenarios, highlighting its potential for advancing scalable and efficient WAMs.

</details>


### [307] [Toward Gripper-Integrated Active Electrosense for Pre-Contact Sensing in Underwater Soft Grippers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03204)
*Ahsan Tanveer,Muhammad Hamza,Waqar Hussain Afridi,Chen Wang,Guangming Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出利用主动电感应技术为水下软体抓取器提供接触前的接近感知，通过实验验证了其在视觉受限环境下作为可靠感知模态的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下操作常受浑浊、眩光及抓取器遮挡等因素影响，导致视觉感知可靠性下降。传统的软体抓取器缺乏一种在视觉失效时，能引导接近和抓取动作的预接触感知手段。

Method: 1. 设计并制作了仿章鱼软体抓取器，并在其上集成了离散电极阵列；2. 利用现成的硬件，记录在导电介质中通过施加电场产生的多通道感应电压；3. 通过仿真和水箱实验，在不同激励电压（5-20 V）和频率（1 mHz-1 kHz）下，针对悬挂导电球进行测试，分析电极阵列的电压响应。

Result: 实验结果显示，在不同激励条件下，多电极电压读数随目标物体的存在表现出结构化且与物体相关的变化，证明了该方式能够有效提供接近信号。

Conclusion: 研究表明，集成于软体抓取器的主动电感应技术具备作为水下软体操作中接触前感知手段的潜力，能有效补偿视觉感知在恶劣水下环境中的不足。

Abstract: Underwater manipulation often occurs under degraded visibility due to turbidity, glare, and gripper occlusion, limiting the reliability of vision-based perception during approach and grasping. In such settings, soft grippers are well suited for compliant interaction, but they typically lack an onboard pre-contact cue that can guide approach and closure when vision is unreliable. This extended abstract explores active electrosense as a lightweight sensing modality that can provide a proximity-like signal prior to contact by measuring perturbations of an applied electric field in conductive media. We instrument an octopus-inspired gripper with a discrete electrode layout and record multi-channel sensing voltages using off-the-shelf hardware. Simulation and tank experiments with a suspended conductive sphere show structured, object-dependent changes in the multi-electrode voltage readout relative to empty-water baselines, with detectability varying across excitation of 5 to 20 V and frequencies from 1 mHz to 1 kHz. These findings motivate systematic investigation of gripper-integrated electrosense as a complementary pre-contact cue for underwater soft manipulation.

</details>


### [308] [BotDirector: Robot Storytelling Across the Symmetrical Reality with Multi-modal Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03223)
*Zhe Sun,Meng Wang,Lei Wang,Yuxi Wang,Wanxin Li,Yujia Peng,Zhenliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种交互式机器人叙事系统，通过结合自然语言处理（LLM）和有形交互，让幼儿能利用日常物品作为道具，通过简单的互动控制蜂群机器人编排并表演交互式戏剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人叙事能有效吸引儿童，但现有技术通常过于复杂，限制了儿童的参与度；亟需一种能够降低技术门槛、让幼儿利用日常物品即可参与的交互式创作工具。

Method: 提出了一种结合“有形交互”（Tangible Interaction）与“自然语言交互”的系统；利用LLM（大语言模型）作为故事代理；将故事叙述转化为基于地图和角色的运动序列，并由自导航蜂群机器人执行。

Result: 该系统能够将儿童通过自然语言和实物布置创建的创意转化为可执行的机器人动作，实现了灵活场景下的机器人故事表演。

Conclusion: 该系统成功降低了机器人叙事创作的门槛，使得幼儿能够通过身边物品与大语言模型代理协作，高效、直观地创作并执行机器人戏剧表演。

Abstract: Robot storytelling offers a unique blend of technological innovation and creative expression that engages children in unprecedented ways. However, the technical aspects are often too complicated for children. We propose an interactive system that facilitates robot storytelling with tangible and natural language interactions. Children arrange the playground with their own stuff and create narratives with an LLM agent. The created narratives are transformed into a motion sequence based on the map and characters, and the motions are executed by self-navigating swarm robots. This system enhances robot storytelling with flexible scenarios, enabling young children to create robot dramas with everyday objects.

</details>


### [309] [AirDreamer: Generalist Drone Navigation with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03252)
*Zian Liu,Andong Yang,Chunkai Yang,Ruidong An,Chao Gao,Guyue Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合世界模型与强化学习的无人机导航算法，通过稀疏奖励引导，显著提升了在未知复杂环境中的导航成功率，并实现了无需调参的实机稳定迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统导航方法依赖于人为设计的感知流水线和预设规则，难以适应未见过的混乱环境，导致泛化能力差。

Method: 1. 基于世界模型的环境理解；2. 基于强化学习的导航策略；3. 采用稀疏奖励函数（无人工设计的奖励塑形），避免局部最优陷阱并增强航向控制。

Result: 在模拟和真实无人机测试中表现出显著的导航能力，在挑战性地图中比现有最佳基准方法成功率高出5.3%，并实现了无需额外调参的零样本（zero-shot）仿真实体（sim-to-real）迁移。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于世界模型和强化学习的无人机导航框架，克服了传统方法对特定环境假设和人工设计的感知管道的依赖，实现了优越的泛化能力和零调参的实地部署能力。

Abstract: Navigating a drone in unseen and cluttered environments requires reliable generalization to unseen scene layouts and understanding of environmental structure relative to the robot's capabilities. Previous methods, which assume the same environment configuration, often rely heavily on human-designed perception pipelines and predefined rules to guide the robot toward the target. This process is environment-dependent and generalizes poorly across environments. Inspired by animal navigation behavior, we design a navigation framework that navigates with a reinforcement-learning-based policy on top of a world-model-based environment understanding to overcome these issues. In addition, a sparse reward function without hand-crafted shaping terms is designed to avoid local minima traps and encourage yaw control behaviors. In simulation and on real drones, our method exhibits emergent capabilities for navigating complex, unseen environments and escaping local optima where other methods fail. In challenging maps, it achieves a 5.3% higher navigation success rate than best baseline. Furthermore, the proposed framework achieves effective sim-to-real transfer without any tuning during deployment. The code will be publicly available.

</details>


### [310] [EaDex: A Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation Framework from Low-Cost Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03268)
*Qian Zhao,Xin Tong,Chengdong Wu,Yang Yang,Yingtian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EaDex框架利用单目RGB-D摄像头进行低成本演示数据采集，结合动态演示退火策略，显著降低了灵巧手训练成本并提升了跨具身操作的学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作学习方法依赖高成本数据采集或大规模交互探索，导致训练效率低下且难以推广，亟需一种低成本、高效的演示学习框架。

Method: 1. 数据层：利用单目RGB-D相机配合MANO手部建模、归一化及运动重定向技术构建低成本演示数据；2. 学习层：引入基于接触奖励的动态演示退火（Dynamic Demonstration Annealing）机制，实现从模仿学习向自主优化训练的平滑过渡。

Result: 在涵盖3种灵巧手和3类受限物体操作的9种跨具身实验设置中，相比基准方法，性能提升了55.3%。

Conclusion: EaDex有效证明了通过低成本RGB-D数据采集与动态演示退火机制，可以显著提升复杂灵巧手操作任务的学习效率与跨具身泛化能力。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation learning has long been hindered by the high costs of data and training, as pure reinforcement learning typically requires large-scale interactive exploration and imitation learning depends on high-quality demonstrations that are expensive to collect. To address this problem, we propose EaDex, a multi-embodiment dexterous manipulation learning framework under low-cost demonstration conditions, which enables rapid generation of demonstration data and consequently reduces training time for efficient dexterous manipulation. At the data level, EaDex captures human hand motions using only a single RGB-D camera and constructs structured demonstration data through MANO-based hand modeling, data normalization, and motion retargeting. At the learning level, we introduce a contact-reward-based dynamic demonstration annealing mechanism, which guides early-stage exploration under demonstration and gradually transitions to autonomous optimization with accumulating contact rewards. Using our custom dataset, we evaluate EaDex on three dexterous hands and three articulated object-opening tasks, covering nine cross-embodiment manipulation settings, achieving a 55.3% relative improvement over the baseline without demonstration annealing. These results validate the effectiveness of the proposed low-cost demonstration pipeline and the dynamic demonstration annealing strategy for dexterous manipulation learning.

</details>


### [311] [Bridging Predictive Uncertainty and Safe Action: Sample-Conditioned Differentiable Planning for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03296)
*Chengzhen Meng,Pei Liu,Zhiyu Huang,Chen Lv,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶环境不确定性挑战，提出一种结合条件扩散模型（生成多样轨迹）与可微规划（CVaR风险约束）的框架，有效提升了复杂交通场景下的安全决策能力与规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶系统难以兼顾“强表达的不确定性建模”与“可解释、安全的运动规划”，通常陷入单一确定性预测或黑盒端到端学习的局限。

Method: 1. 采用条件扩散模型生成多样的未来轨迹场景；2. 设计了基于有向图的行为场景表征以提升效率；3. 提出一种样本条件化的可微规划框架，将生成的轨迹样本输入优化器；4. 引入经验性条件风险价值（CVaR）作为尾部风险约束，确保规划在极小概率及关键安全场景下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion和Argoverse 2数据集上的闭环和开环验证表明，该方法在安全性、通行效率和乘坐舒适度方面均达到领先水平。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了不确定性建模与可解释性运动规划之间的鸿沟。通过将扩散模型生成的样本与CVaR尾部风险约束相结合，实现了在复杂多变自动驾驶场景下的鲁棒、安全规划，显著优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: Complex, dynamic, and interactive driving environments pose significant challenges for autonomous driving, primarily due to the pervasive uncertainty of surrounding traffic. A fundamental bottleneck in current systems is the disconnect between highly expressive uncertainty modeling and interpretable, safe motion planning. In this paper, we propose a novel sample-conditioned differentiable planning framework that bridges this gap by explicitly incorporating diffusion-generated future trajectories into the optimization process. Rather than compressing predictions into a single deterministic future or relying on black-box end-to-end architectures, our approach leverages a conditional diffusion model to generate a diverse set of plausible future scenarios. Crucially, these samples are directly fed into a differentiable planner, which explicitly mitigates predictive uncertainty via an empirical Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) tail-risk constraint. This allows the planner to optimize a physically interpretable trajectory that is robust to rare yet safety-critical interactions. Furthermore, we introduce a directed graph representation for scene context that yields substantial improvements in both predictive effectiveness and computational efficiency. Validated through extensive open-loop and closed-loop evaluations on the Waymo Open Motion and Argoverse 2 datasets, our framework significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in safety, efficiency, and ride comfort.

</details>


### [312] [SplitAdapter: Load-Aware Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Factorized Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03297)
*Jeonguk Kang,Hanbyel Cho,Sanghyun Kang,Donghan Koo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 SplitAdapter，通过解耦对象负载与动力学因素，结合分裂世界模型与分层 FiLM 调制，解决了人形机器人在多变负载下的稳健操作与 sim-to-real 迁移难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于历史信息的适配器将负载变化与机器人动力学误差合并为一个隐表示，导致在处理复杂或重负载操纵任务时鲁棒性下降，难以实现有效的仿真到现实 (sim-to-real) 迁移。

Method: 提出 SplitAdapter 方法：1. 冻结预训练的箱体操作策略；2. 引入针对负载和动力学的双分支上下文编码器；3. 采用分裂世界模型目标进行训练；4. 利用基于梯度反转层 (GRL) 的交叉对抗正则化；5. 使用分层 Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) 实现策略调整。

Result: 在仿真和现实部署中，SplitAdapter 在 2kg/4kg/6kg 负载及多种高度下，在成功率方面均优于基准策略和普通世界模型 FiLM 基准，特别是在重负载场景下提升最为显著。

Conclusion: SplitAdapter通过解耦负载和动力学因素，显著增强了人形机器人在变换负载和任务高度下的鲁棒性，特别是在重负载操作中表现出明显优势，优于传统的单隐空间适配器。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation requires stable whole-body control under varying object masses and pickup/placement heights. This becomes particularly challenging in sim-to-real transfer, where object-induced load variation and robot-side dynamics mismatch interact during physical contact. Existing history-based adapters often compress these factors into a single latent representation, which can weaken robustness under heavy-load manipulation. We propose \textbf{SplitAdapter: Load-Aware Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Factorized Adaptation}, which freezes a pretrained box manipulation policy and extends it with object/load and dynamics-aware context encoders trained with split world-model objectives, GRL-based cross-adversarial regularization, and hierarchical Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM). In sim-to-sim experiments and real-world deployment, SplitAdapter improves Full-task success over the base policy and world-model FiLM baselines across object masses of $2$, $4$, and $6$ kg and pickup/placement heights of $0$, $30$, and $60$ cm, with the largest improvements under heavy-load conditions.

</details>


### [313] [RobotValues: Evaluating Household Robots When Human Values Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03312)
*Jongwook Han,Hyeongjin Kim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 RobotValues 基准，用于衡量家用机器人在处理人类价值观冲突场景时的决策能力；研究发现当前 VLM 模型存在僵化的价值偏好，且难以根据指令调整行为，呼吁将价值观对齐纳入机器人评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的家庭机器人评估主要关注任务完成情况，忽略了复杂家庭环境中存在的价值观冲突（如自主性、效率与社交适宜性之间的取舍），且目前缺乏相关基准进行量化评估。

Method: 1. 构建了 RobotValues 基准：包含 10,000 个具有价值冲突的家庭场景；2. 使用 LLM 生成场景、提取人类价值、生成图像并进行自动质量控制；3. 对目前机器人领域的视觉语言模型 (VLM) 进行了基准测试和指令遵循能力评估。

Result: 1. VLM 模型具有固定的价值偏好（倾向于安全和迁就，忽视隐私）；2. 模型在面对与自身偏好冲突的指令时表现较差，在 80% 的情况下未能覆盖其默认行为，无法有效执行特定的价值导向指令。

Conclusion: 家用机器人的评估标准需从单一的任务完成度转向包含价值观权衡的多维度指标。现有的视觉语言模型 (VLM) 在处理价值观冲突时存在明显的固化偏见，且缺乏对特定指令的执行能力，需要进一步研究如何让机器人根据人类价值观动态调整行为。

Abstract: While household robots are often evaluated based on task completion, everyday domestic environments involve value-conflicting situations in which robots are expected to choose actions that prioritize other values than task success, such as human autonomy, efficiency, or social appropriateness. Yet, there are no benchmarks for evaluating robots' value preferences in such scenarios. We introduce RobotValues, a benchmark to evaluate household robot planners in 10K value-conflict scenarios. Each instance consists of a realistic household image with multiple plausible robot actions that prioritize different human values. We construct RobotValues through LLM-assisted scenario generation, stakeholder-grounded value extraction, image generation and automatic quality control. Using RobotValues we evaluate VLMs used in robotics and find that models exhibit default value preferences, including safety and accommodation, while underselecting privacy-prioritizing actions. When the models are instructed to prioritize specific values that conflict with their own preferences, they often fail to override their default actions, choosing incorrect actions for 80% of the time. These findings suggest that household robot evaluation should measure not only task completion or safety compliance, but also whether robots can choose among plausible actions when human values conflict.

</details>


### [314] [GPU-Parallel Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning with Demonstration Guided Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03335)
*Rui Zhang,Qiwei Wu,Zhengyu Zhang,Tao Li,Yunrong Guo,Junjie Lai,Renjing Xu,Weihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 MT-Libero 基准测试和 DGPO 算法，旨在解决机器人多任务并行学习中的效率与小样本训练难题，通过演示引导优化实现了更高效、更稳定的多任务策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人强化学习仍偏重于“一任务一专家”的策略，缺乏大规模高效的多任务通用训练基准，且在稀疏奖励和少量先验数据下训练困难。

Method: 1. 构建了 MT-Libero 基准测试（基于 Isaac Lab 和 LIBERO 资源）；2. 提出了 DGPO（演示引导策略优化）算法，结合了重要性加权的 PPO 与基于匹配演示动作的自适应行为克隆。

Result: DGPO 在多任务学习中优于传统的无演示 RL 和现有的演示驱动方法，既保持了 PPO 的在线改进能力，又实现了对演示分布的灵活偏好控制。

Conclusion: 通过将任务族结构化并引入 DGPO 算法，实现了在 GPU 并行环境下高效解决多任务机器人操作问题的可行性，证明了演示引导与在线强化学习结合的优越性。

Abstract: Large scale GPU-parallel reinforcement learning has changed what can be trained in robot simulation, yet most systems still optimize one specialist policy per task. We propose a construction methodology for turning structured manipulation task families into GPU-parallel multi-task RL benchmarks, and instantiate it as MT-Libero using LIBERO assets and task predicates in Isaac Lab. The resulting benchmark supports simultaneous reinforcement learning over heterogeneous task suites with parallel rendering, physics randomization, and state-input or visual-input policies. To make such training practical under sparse success signals and limited prior data, we further propose DGPO, an on-policy demonstration guided method that combines importance weighted PPO with adaptive behavior cloning on matched demonstration actions. DGPO enables a tunable preference toward demonstrated task distributions, outperforming both prior-free RL and existing demonstration-based methods while preserving the stability and online improvement benefits of on-policy PPO.

</details>


### [315] [Autonomous Navigation System for Library Service Robot Based on Unitree Go2 Edu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03340)
*Aoduo Li,Haoran Lv,Bingquan Ou,Jianfeng Li,Yingdong Li,Zimeng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一套基于Unitree Go2四足机器人的ROS 2导航系统，通过多传感器融合与先进的规划算法，成功解决了图书馆复杂、动态环境下的自主导航与避障问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决图书馆内狭窄通道、地面过渡、零星杂物等现实环境中的导航难题，弥补轮式移动平台在经过障碍物时的不足，实现静音、安全且自主的移动。

Method: 基于ROS 2导航系统；传感器包括4D激光雷达、前置深度相机和IMU；采用RTAB-Map进行视觉-激光雷达SLAM，利用AMCL及基于EKF的传感器融合进行定位，并采用Nav2堆栈结合A*与DWA算法进行路径规划与避障。

Result: 在真实图书馆测试中，静态环境中成功率达100%，低密度动态环境为96%，高密度动态环境为88%；地图测绘平均误差仅为3.7厘米。

Conclusion: 四足机器人在图书馆等复杂室内环境中具有出色的导航能力，能够有效应对各种移动不连续性和动态障碍物，为机器人在受限室内空间的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Libraries require autonomous robots to move quietly through narrow aisles while remaining safe around readers, chairs, bags, and carts. This paper presents a ROS 2 navigation system for a Unitree Go2 Edu quadruped equipped with a 4D LiDAR, a front depth camera, and an IMU. Rather than assuming the library is rough terrain, we target the practical mobility discontinuities of real deployments, including floor transitions, temporary clutter, and partially blocked passages where low-clearance wheeled platforms are less tolerant. RTAB-Map is used for visual-LiDAR SLAM, AMCL and EKF-based sensor fusion provide localization, and a Nav2 stack with A* and DWA supports planning and local avoidance. In a real library, the system achieves 100%, 96%, and 88% success rates in static, low-density dynamic, and high-density dynamic scenes, while map validation against surveyed control distances yields a mean metric error of 3.7 cm.

</details>


### [316] [eMEM: A Hybrid Spatio-Temporal Memory System For Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03374)
*A. Haroon Rasheed,Maria Kabtoul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: eMEM 是一种专为具身智能体设计的混合图记忆系统，它通过多索引集成（时、空、语义）和类脑记忆巩固机制，解决了现有系统在复杂物理环境交互中的遗忘与检索失效问题，并配套了基于认知心理学的评估基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体记忆机制（如串流文本或单一知识图谱）无法满足具身智能体对语义、空间和时间三维统一检索的需求。

Method: 提出一种名为 eMEM 的混合图记忆系统，采用多索引架构（SQLite用于结构化存储，HNSW用于语义搜索，R-tree用于空间查询），并结合基于生物学启发的两级巩固流水线及10个LLM调用工具，同时构建了基于认知心理学范式的基准测试集 eMEM-Bench。

Result: eMEM 在 eMEM-Bench 上取得了 80.8 的加权平均分，在长达一年模拟延时的测试中保持了平稳的留存曲线，且在上下文相关检索和错误记忆排除方面显著优于基准 RAG 模型。

Conclusion: eMEM通过整合多维索引与类生物的巩固机制，显著提升了具身智能体在时空及语义维度上的记忆存储与检索能力，且其设计的 eMEM-Bench 为评估智能体记忆提供了科学的认知心理学范式依据。

Abstract: We present eMEM (Embodied Memory), a hybrid graph-based memory system for embodied agents operating in physical environments. Current agent memory architectures, such as Generative Agents, MemGPT, and A-MEM, treat memory as text streams or knowledge graphs, but embodied agents require memory that is simultaneously searchable by meaning, space, and time. eMEM fills this gap with a multi-index architecture (SQL ITE for structured storage, hnswlib for approximate nearest neighbour semantic search, and an R-tree for spatial queries) unified behind a single graph model. A tiered consolidation pipeline transforms raw perceptual observations into compressed summaries, mirroring hippocampal-neocortical consolidation in biological systems. Ten agent-facing recall tools expose memory retrieval primitives, including concept-to-location resolution and cross layer recall, as first-class operations for LLM tool calling. The system is fully embedded and runs in-process alongside the agent. In addition we introduce eMEM-Bench v1, a benchmark we construct over ProcTHOR-10K scenes for embodied memory evaluation. The benchmark is organised explicitly around eight cognitive-psychology paradigms (DRM lures, pattern separation, pattern completion, source monitoring, context-dependent retrieval, long-horizon interference, serial position, and a foil augmented retention curve), each chosen so that the result is interpretable against the broader memory-systems literature in humans and prior agent-memory systems; a level of diagnostic that surface-task benchmarks like LoCoMo or OpenEQA cannot provide. eMEM scores 80.8 weighted mean over 988 probes, with a flat retention curve at ceiling from 1 h to 1 yr of simulated delay on room-unique items. We show that a pure RAG baseline (the flat_rag ablation) loses 30 pt on context dependent retrieval and 29 pt on DRM lure rejection, isolating the contribution of multi-layer storage and consolidation respectively. We release both the system and the benchmark code.

</details>


### [317] [Grasp-Then-Plan with Failure Attribution: A Closed Two-Stage Framework for Precise and Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03385)
*Jiahao Xu,Peiyuan Wang,Hanzhuo Zhang,Zihao Yu,Tianyu Fu,Hao Chen,Xuanhao Xiang,Jianbo Yu,Chenchen Fu,Wanyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GTP-FA 框架，通过引入“失败归因”模块诊断长程机器人操作的故障源，并反向驱动抓取策略与运动规划器的协同优化，从而解决两阶段耦合带来的效率与成功率瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在长程操作任务中，抓取与运动规划之间紧密耦合，导致难以确定失败的根本原因，进而引发低效的试验与纠错过程。

Method: 提出了 GTP-FA (Grasp-Then-Plan with Failure Attribution) 框架，包含：1. 抓取与运动规划的两阶段解耦策略；2. 基于学习的失败归因模型（Failure Attribution Model），用于诊断失败源；3. 诊断驱动的优化（Diagnosis-driven optimization），通过将反馈注入抓取评分（引入任务先验与风险惩罚）和规划器微调（针对高风险初始状态）来改进系统。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实机器人实验中，GTP-FA 在强化学习、模仿学习、扩散策略和 VLA 架构上均表现出色，相较于基准模型大幅提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: GTP-FA 框架通过引入诊断驱动的优化机制，有效克服了传统长程操作任务中抓取与运动规划纠缠导致的失败问题，显著提升了多种基准模型（RL, IL, Diffusion Policy, VLA）的任务成功率。

Abstract: In robotic manipulation, the tight coupling between grasping and motion planning often obscures the true source of failure, leading to inefficient trial-and-error. To enable efficient long-horizon manipulation, we propose GTP-FA (Grasp-Then-Plan with Failure Attribution), a task-oriented two-stage grasp-then-plan framework that generates grasp candidates and performs downstream motion planning conditioned on the selected grasp. Given a failed manipulation trajectory, we learn a failure attribution model that generalizes to unseen grasps and produces a stable distribution over failure modes for diagnosis-guided optimization. Based on these attribution results, we then optimize both modules in a diagnosis-driven manner: on the grasping side, we inject task-level priors and risk penalties into grasp candidate scoring and optimization to suppress unstable or task-incompatible grasps; on the planning side, we target high-risk initial states through data collection and fine-tuning to address genuine planning bottlenecks. We evaluate the proposed framework in both simulation and real-robot experiments, and show that GTP-FA improves the corresponding base learners across RL, IL, diffusion-policy, and VLA-based settings, achieving substantially higher overall task success rates.

</details>


### [318] [Extreme Motion Generation via Hybrid Null-Space Control for Straight-Line Path Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03390)
*Xinyi Yuan,Weiwei Wan,Kensuke Harada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合控制器，通过结合条件扩散模型采样、强化学习与传统控制，实现了机械臂在空间极限下的最大化路径追踪，提升了工业自动化的作业效率与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业应用（如喷涂、焊接）要求机械臂在有限的运动空间内最大限度地延长路径长度以完成任务，但传统控制方法难以处理靠近运动极限（安全性边界）的长序列决策问题。

Method: 1. 使用强化学习（RL）进行长程决策，利用基于条件扩散模型（Conditional Diffusion）进行初始关节配置采样；2. 采用混合控制策略，根据归一化关节极限距离在RL控制器与传统模型控制器之间进行平滑切换，以应对边界附近的控制挑战。

Result: 在7自由度的Franka FR3机械臂上进行的10,000次直线路径跟踪测试中，平均任务完成长度较基准模型提高了27%，在多个极端运动场景下表现出显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: 提出了一种结合学习策略与模型控制的混合控制器，有效解决了在运动极限区域内进行长程路径规划的难题，显著提升了机械臂的作业范围。

Abstract: This work studies ``extreme motion generation'', which aims to maximize the Cartesian path length along a pre-defined trajectory within the manipulator's workspace. This objective is important in industry as long as path-following is fundamental to a large variety of tasks such as surface coating and welding. More critically, extreme motion enables a fixed-base manipulator to exploit the kinematic capability under limited reachability. However, such exploitation is challenging in practice, as the manipulator must actively avoid the safety boundary through execution, which is inherently a long-horizon problem. Accordingly, we claim that long-horizon decision-making should be delegated to a learning-based policy to maximize exploitation, while a classical model-based controller covers the near-boundary region, where the learning policy degrades sharply due to sparse data coverage. In detail, our proposed method is a step-level hybrid controller that switches between an RL-based and a model-based controller according to the normalized joint-limit distance. The initial joint configuration is sampled through conditional diffusion-based sampling, which improves the achievable path length based on the learned motion prior. We evaluate the proposed framework on 10,000 straight-line path-following tasks with a 7-DoF Franka FR3, extending the average rollout length by 27\% over the model-based baseline. Notably, certain tasks yield a pronounced extension toward the motion extreme, as reflected in the maximum improvement reported in the statistical results. The project website and related videos of this paper can be found at https://yuan-xinyi.github.io/extreme-motion-generation/.

</details>


### [319] [OpenEAI-Platform: An Open-source Embodied Artificial Intelligence Hardware-Software Unified Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03392)
*Jinyuan Zhang,Luoyi Fan,Leiyu Wang,Yeqiang Wang,Yicheng Zhu,Cewu Lu,Nanyang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenEAI-Platform 是一套完整的开源具身智能平台，包含低成本高精度的 6+1 自由度机械臂和基于 Qwen3-VL 的高性能 VLA 模型，旨在赋能可复现的机器人研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前真实场景具身智能研究中，硬件成本高昂、闭源以及缺乏具可复现性的视-语-行（VLA）模型的问题，旨在提供一套完整、开源的软硬件基础设施以促进相关研究。

Method: 1. 硬件：开发了低成本、6+1自由度的开源机械臂（OpenEAI-Arm），并采用柔性控制方法提升精度。
2. 算法：构建了基于 Qwen3-VL-4B 主干网络和 Diffusion Transformer 动作头的 VLA 模型（OpenEAI-VLA），并通过两阶段训练策略利用开源数据集进行训练。

Result: OpenEAI-Arm 在四项真实操作任务中性能优于同类型商业机械臂；OpenEAI-VLA 在有限预训练数据下，表现出与大规模预训练的 pi0 模型相当的成功率。

Conclusion: OpenEAI-Platform 提供了一套低成本、高复现性的具身智能软硬件开源解决方案，有效降低了真实世界机器人研究的门槛，并展示了其在复杂操作任务中的竞争优势。

Abstract: Embodied AI in the real world requires both accurate hardware and robust vision-language-action (VLA) policies. We present OpenEAI-Platform, a fully open-source platform that integrates a low-cost 6+1 degree-of-freedom (dof) robotic arm (OpenEAI-Arm) and a reproducible VLA model (OpenEAI-VLA). OpenEAI-Arm provides open-source mechanical designs for low manufacturing cost and compliant control methods for higher accuracy. OpenEAI-VLA builds on Qwen3-VL-4B and uses a Diffusion Transformer action head, and is trained in two stages with only open-source robot and multimodal datasets. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, OpenEAI-Arm outperforms two commercial 6+1-dof arms under the same policy, and OpenEAI-VLA achieves success rates comparable to the large-scale pretrained pi0 baseline with only limited pretraining data. We will release the full hardware designs, drivers, models, and training/data pipelines to support reproducible research and scalable data collection. Our codes, layouts, and models will be released after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [320] [Reliability-Guided Depth Fusion for Glare-Resilient Navigation Costmaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03421)
*Shang-En Tsai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对 RGB-D 相机反光干扰的鲁棒性代价地图构建方法，通过深度可靠性网络 (DRM-Net) 和置信度加权融合机制，有效消除了由反光导致的幻影障碍物，提升了机器人室内导航的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式立体 RGB-D 相机在面对反光地板、玻璃边界等高光环境时，产生大量的噪声（空洞或尖峰），这些噪声在占用栅格地图中累积形成的“幻影障碍物”严重影响机器人导航安全。

Method: 1. 提出轻量级深度可靠性网络 (DRM-Net)：预测像素级的深度测量置信度。2. 设计可靠性引导的加权与门控融合机制 (RGF)：在占用栅格更新前对测量值进行滤波。3. 采用位姿对齐的多视角基准深度构建法：减少训练过程中的偏差。

Result: 在 RealSense D435 和 Jetson Orin Nano 平台上进行了实验，结果表明该方法有效减少了误报障碍物，保留了自由空间，并保持了实时运行效率。

Conclusion: 将反光造成的深度测量误差视为一种测量可靠性问题，而非密集深度补全问题，是实现安全可靠室内导航的有效途径。该方法在资源受限的嵌入式平台上实现了实时性能，显著提升了机器人对玻璃和高反光地面的感知鲁棒性。

Abstract: Specular glare on reflective floors, glass boundaries, and glossy indoor surfaces frequently corrupts active-stereo RGB-D depth measurements, producing holes and spikes that accumulate as persistent phantom obstacles in occupancy-grid costmaps. This paper presents a glare-resilient costmap construction method based on explicit depth-reliability modeling. A lightweight Depth Reliability Map network (DRM-Net) predicts per-pixel measurement trustworthiness under specular interference, and a reliability-guided weighted-and-gated fusion (RGF) mechanism modulates occupancy updates before corrupted measurements are accumulated into the map. To support robust training and evaluation, the method uses pose-aligned multi-view reference-depth construction to reduce circular-supervision bias and is evaluated through fusion-variant ablations, parameter-sensitivity analysis, cross-condition tests, paired navigation comparisons, reliability-map metrics, and embedded runtime profiling. Experiments on a real mobile robotic platform equipped with an Intel RealSense D435 and a Jetson Orin Nano show that the proposed method reduces false obstacle insertion, improves free-space preservation, and maintains real-time throughput under reflective-floor, glass-wall, and natural-light glare conditions. These results support treating glare as a measurement-reliability problem rather than as a dense depth-completion problem for safety-critical indoor navigation.

</details>


### [321] [PerchRL: Vision-Based Agile Perching on Inclined Platforms under Rapid and Irregular Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03441)
*Zihong Lu,Zongzhuo Liu,Huaxu Li,Jinqiang Cui,Jie Mei,Youmin Gong,U Kei Cheang,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PerchRL 是一种基于强化学习的四旋翼控制框架，通过两阶段训练、轨迹随机化和主动感知机制，解决了飞行器在快速运动且视野受限的倾斜平台上着陆的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在运动倾斜平台上进行基于视觉的自主着陆对于空地协作至关重要，但现有技术受限于相机视野（FOV）不足，难以应对快速且不规则的运动，导致落地困难。

Method: 1. 两阶段学习策略：先进行基于状态的预训练，再进行视觉微调。
2. 泛化优化：引入随机平台轨迹训练防止过拟合，并通过时间增强（temporal augmentation）捕捉历史观测中的潜运动模式。
3. 鲁棒性设计：采用混合学习框架，结合可见性感知状态增强（visibility-aware state augmentation）和主动感知奖励（active perception rewards），以应对视觉丢失。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中均表现出色，具有高度的稳定性、实时性，并且在不同四旋翼平台上表现出极强的适应性。

Conclusion: PerchRL 框架成功实现了四旋翼在运动倾斜平台上的自主视觉着陆。实验证明了该方法在多种平台上的泛化能力、鲁棒性以及实时性能，为解决有限视野下的复杂着陆问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vision-based perching of quadrotors on moving inclined platforms is critical for air-ground collaboration but remains challenging due to the limited field of view (FOV). In this paper, we propose PerchRL, a reinforcement learning (RL) framework for vision-based agile perching on inclined platforms under rapid and irregular motion. Specifically, we employ a two-stage learning strategy consisting of state-based pre-training followed by vision-based fine-tuning. To improve generalization across diverse platform motions, we employ randomized platform trajectories to prevent overfitting and temporal augmentation methods to capture latent motion patterns from historical observations. During vision-based fine-tuning, a hybrid learning framework consisting of visibility-aware state augmentation and active perception rewards is presented to improve robustness under intermittent visual loss. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate the feasibility, stability, and real-time performance of PerchRL, while successful deployment across distinct quadrotor platforms further validates its adaptability. The source code will be released to benefit the community.

</details>


### [322] [Human2Humanoid: Physics-Aware Cross-Morphology Motion Retargeting for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03476)
*Tianchen Huang,Feiyang Yuan,Junchi Gu,Shurui Fang,Xiaohu Zhang,Yu Wang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Human2Humanoid是一个基于CycleGAN的无监督运动重定向框架，通过引入图卷积、末端执行器轨迹语义对齐以及物理约束，解决了人类与人形机器人之间的跨形态动作迁移难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类与机器人之间存在显著的形态差异（骨架拓扑、肢体比例、自由度）以及成对运动数据匮乏，导致动作重定向（Motion Retargeting）任务极具挑战性。

Method: 基于CycleGAN架构，引入骨架感知图卷积网络（Skeleton-aware GCN）提取拓扑相关的动作特征；提出“形态不变末端执行器一致性损失”（morphology-invariant end-effector consistency loss）以对齐轨迹；并增加物理可行性约束以优化接触模式。

Result: 实验证明该框架在无需成对数据的情况下，能有效处理跨域规模失配，减少接触伪影，并提升了动作的物理合理性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了在无需成对数据的情况下，将人类动作高保真地迁移至Unitree G1人形机器人，且在下游控制能力和物理可行性方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Retargeting human motion to humanoid robots is critical for teleoperation, imitation learning and human-robot interaction. However, it remains challenging because of substantial morphological discrepancies between humans and robots, including differences in skeletal topology, limb proportions and degrees of freedom, as well as the scarcity of paired motion data. This paper presents Human2Humanoid, an unsupervised motion retargeting framework that transfers human motions to humanoid robot behaviors with high fidelity. To bridge the domain gap under unpaired data, we adopt a CycleGAN-based architecture equipped with a skeleton-aware graph convolutional network to capture topology-dependent motion features. To address cross-domain scale mismatches, we introduce a morphology-invariant end-effector consistency loss that aligns normalized end-effector trajectories to preserve motion semantics across embodiments. To improve physical plausibility and reduce contact artifacts, we impose explicit physics-aware feasibility constraints to encourage reproduction of the contact patterns in the source motion. Experimental results show that the proposed method successfully retargets human motion to the Unitree G1 humanoid robot without paired data, and outperforms existing methods in both downstream controllability and physical feasibility.

</details>


### [323] [SPADE: Sketch-guided Path Planning Augmented with Diffusion Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03512)
*Charbel Abi Hana,Tatiana Ghantous,Mikael Khalil,Anthony Rizk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合ROS 2增强标注工具和扩散模型增强训练策略的AMR路径规划框架，在大幅压缩模型参数的同时，显著提升了路径的精准度、环境泛化能力及鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有基于模仿学习的AMR路径规划方法存在的“对未见环境泛化能力差”以及“演示数据采集鲁棒性不足”两大问题，旨在寻求平衡环境适应性与计算效率的解决方案。

Method: 1. 开发了一套基于ROS 2的路径规划专家演示数据标注工具；2. 提出了一种将扩散模型增强（Diffusion-based Augmentation）集成到基础行为克隆（Behavioral Cloning）模型中的新型训练策略。

Result: 与现有前沿方法相比，该框架在绝对姿态误差（APE）上降低了39.1%，Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) 降低了33.5%，模型可训练参数量减少了93.8%，且在保持实时边缘计算能力的同时实现了扩散模型级别的泛化效果。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的框架通过引入基于ROS 2的标注工具和扩散模型增强的模仿学习策略，有效提升了AMR路径规划的泛化能力与鲁棒性，同时保持了实时计算性能和极高的轻量化优势。

Abstract: Path planning is essential for Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). Conventional methods for incorporating human preferences into planning typically rely on either complex reward engineering or hardware-intensive solutions. Recent state-of-the-art frameworks leverage imitation learning to train behavior-specific path planning models from expert demonstrations. However, these approaches face two key limitations: limited generalization to unseen environments and low robustness in demonstration collection. To address these challenges, this work introduces an enhanced framework that focuses on two main contributions: an overhauled annotation tool built on ROS 2, and a novel training strategy that integrates diffusion-based augmentation into baseline behavioral cloning models. A dataset of expert demonstrations is provided and evaluated through ablation studies to assess the robustness of the proposed solution. The enhanced approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods with 39.1% lower Absolute Pose Error (APE) and 33.5% lower Fr'echet Inception Distance (FID) while having 93.8% less trainable parameters. Moreover it attains diffusion-level generalization while preserving the real-time, on-edge properties of state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [324] [Bionic Human-Motion Style Transfer for Physically Executable Whole-Body Control of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03536)
*Tianchen Huang,Mingkuan Zhao,Yang Gao,Feiyang Yuan,Junchi Gu,Xiaohu Zhang,Dongdong Zhao,Shi Yan,Yu Wang,Wei Gao,Shiwu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于仿生扩散模型的全身运动风格迁移框架，通过短人类动作样例驱动人形机器人生成具有物理可行性、可调节风格的各种动态任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人表现力运动多依赖固定示范或手动脚本，难以在不同内容间复用。受人类利用姿态和节奏传达情感意图的启发，旨在实现一种能够根据短人类示范自动生成具身、稳定且可调节风格的人形机器人动作方法。

Method: 提出了一个仿生生成到控制（generation-to-control）框架：1. 开发了物理感知的多条件潜在扩散模型（multi-condition latent diffusion model）融合风格、内容和轨迹条件；2. 使用无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）调整风格强度；3. 在训练中施加接触一致性和时间平滑性正则化；4. 采用聚类蒸馏策略（cluster-and-distill）训练预览式全身跟踪策略以执行动作。

Result: 与动画导向的风格迁移基线相比，该方法有效减少了接触和抖动伪影；在Unitree G1机器人上实现了96.0%的成功率（125次真实机器人试验）。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了利用短人类动作范例作为可重用的仿生源，实现具有物理可执行性且具备表现力的人形机器人全身运动的可行性。

Abstract: Expressive whole-body motion is important for humanoid robots operating in human environments, where robots are expected to move stably while presenting readable and adjustable body behaviors. However, most expressive motions are still obtained from fixed demonstrations or manually designed scripts, making it difficult to reuse a demonstrated style across different motion contents. Inspired by the way human motion styles convey affective and intentional cues through gait rhythm, posture, arm swing and body sway, this paper proposes a bionic generation-to-control framework for exemplar-driven style transfer on humanoid robots. Given a short human style exemplar and a target content motion, the proposed framework generates a stylized whole-body reference that preserves the intended motion content while transferring the demonstrated style. A physics-aware multi-condition latent diffusion model is developed to fuse style, content and trajectory conditions, and classifier-free guidance is used to adjust the style intensity without retraining. To improve hardware executability, contact-consistency and temporal-smoothness regularization are imposed on decoded motions during training. The generated references are then converted into G1-compatible robot references and executed by a preview-based whole-body tracking policy trained with a cluster-and-distill strategy. Simulation and Unitree G1 experiments show that the proposed method can transfer short human style exemplars to diverse robot motion contents, reduce contact and jitter artifacts compared with animation-oriented style-transfer baselines, and achieve a 96.0% success rate over 125 reported real-robot trials. The results demonstrate the feasibility of using short human motion exemplars as reusable bionic sources for physically executable expressive humanoid motion.

</details>


### [325] [Static and Dynamic Representations for Tactile Contact-Angle Estimation with Event-Based Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03545)
*Yanhui Lu,Efi Psomopoulou,Benjamin Ward-Cherrier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究通过NeuroTac传感器比较了三种事件触觉表示方法，结果显示静态表示法在接触角估计中表现出最低的延迟、最高的精度及最强的鲁棒性，具备满足机器人高频交互需求的潜能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用事件驱动传感器实现低延迟的触觉信号采集，以应对机器人高频率接触交互的需求，并探究何种事件空间表示方法能最有效地进行接触角度估计。

Method: 利用NeuroTac事件相机触觉传感器，对比了三种事件派生的空间轮廓表示方法：动态表示（近期活动）、静态表示（持续接触状态）以及两者的组合。通过不同运动场景下的接触角估计任务进行评估，测试了系统在不同采样间隔和运动条件下的性能及延迟。

Result: 所有表示方法均实现了低于10ms的P99处理延迟。其中，静态表示法在性能上最优，在连续滚动中实现了0.160°的MAE，在运动中断期间实现了0.251°的MAE，且在不同速度和压深条件下表现出更小的性能波动。

Conclusion: 研究证明了基于事件的触觉传感器在机器人接触角估计任务中的低延迟优势。静态表示法在精度、鲁棒性及对不同运动参数（速度、压深）的适应性上表现最佳，优于动态及组合表示法，达到了亚10毫秒的高频处理要求。

Abstract: Event-based tactile sensing offers low-latency signal acquisition for contact-rich robotic interaction. This paper investigates contact-angle estimation using event streams from an event-based tactile sensor (NeuroTac) and compares three event-derived spatial contour representations: a dynamic representation capturing recent event activity, a static representation recovering a more persistent contact state, and their combined representation. Across the evaluated motion scenarios, all representation pipelines exhibited P99 processing latency below 10 ms at all tested sampling intervals, demonstrating their potential for high-frequency event-based tactile angle estimation in robotic manipulation. The static representation consistently achieved marginally better performance than the dynamic and combined representations under scenario-specific training, yielding a mean overall MAE of 0.160° during continuous sensor rolling and a stop-phase mean MAE of 0.251° during randomly inserted motion interruptions. It also exhibited smaller performance fluctuations across speed and indentation depth variations than the other two representations.

</details>


### [326] [NVIDIA Isaac Sim: Enabling Scalable, GPU-Accelerated Simulation for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03551)
*Sicong Gao,Maurice Pagnucco,Tomasz Bednarz,Yang Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对NVIDIA Isaac Sim仿真器进行了首份系统性综述，分析了其架构、应用范式及局限性，为机器人领域利用大规模并行仿真和合成数据提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献往往将NVIDIA Isaac Sim仅视为一种普通仿真器，缺乏对其特定的架构特点、使用模式及固有局限性的系统性深入分析。

Method: 从系统与应用双重视角出发，对比主流仿真器，分析五大核心领域（包括数据生成、高保真仿真等）的代表性研究，总结通用使用模式。

Result: 明确了Isaac Sim凭借GPU加速和合成数据生成管线，成为机器人大规模并行训练及高保真物理仿真关键基础设施的价值，并识别了当前的应用瓶颈与未来发展挑战。

Conclusion: 本文系统梳理了Isaac Sim的架构与应用，强调了其在数据驱动机器人学习中的核心地位，并指出该领域未来需在物理开放世界学习、训练范式及实用性方面取得突破。

Abstract: Simulation has become a core infrastructure for robotics research. Unlike previous simulators, NVIDIA Isaac Sim leverages GPU acceleration to enable large-scale parallel training and physics-accurate modeling. Its synthetic data generation pipeline alleviates the scarcity of high-quality training data, supporting data-driven robot learning and large-scale simulation-centric experimentation. However, existing surveys often treat it as one simulator among many, without a systematic analysis of its architectural characteristics, usage patterns, and limitations. This survey reviews Isaac Sim from system and application perspectives, outlining its architecture and comparing it with widely used simulators. We analyze representative studies across five major domains and summarize common usage patterns, particularly in data generation and high-fidelity simulation. We also outline key future directions and challenges, including physics open-world learning, simulation-centric training and practical usability constraints.

</details>


### [327] [CANMOT: Class-Aware Noise Modeling for Multi-Object Tracking in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03590)
*Timo Osterburg,Stefan Schütte,Torsten Bertram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶中卡尔曼滤波多目标追踪假设所有对象具有相同噪声特性的局限性，本文提出了 CANMOT 框架，通过类别感知和目标对齐的噪声建模提升了追踪精度与不确定性鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波（KF）多目标追踪通常假设所有物体类别共享全局统一的噪声协方差，忽略了不同交通参与者（如行人、车辆）之间异质的不确定性特征。

Method: 提出 CANMOT 框架，通过引入类别特定（class-specific）的对角过程噪声和测量噪声协方差矩阵，并将其转换至目标坐标系下以保留纵向-横向各向异性（anisotropy）。

Result: 在 nuScenes 基准测试中，CANMOT 显著提升了追踪性能并减少了身份切换（ID switches）。通过 ANEES 和 χ² 测试发现，传统 KF 模型存在严重的过度自信问题，而 CANMOT 在一定程度上改善了不确定性校准。

Conclusion: 虽然 CANMOT 通过引入类别感知和目标对齐的噪声建模改进了追踪性能与不确定性校准，但实验显示仍存在明显的不一致性。这表明仅优化协方差矩阵不足以完全解决卡尔曼滤波在自动驾驶多目标追踪中的不确定性估计问题，未来仍需更深入的研究。

Abstract: Kalman filter (KF)-based multi-object tracking (MOT) remains a strong baseline for autonomous driving due to its strong performance, computational efficiency and interpretability. In most practical systems, the process noise and measurement noise covariances are defined globally and shared across object classes, presuming identical uncertainty characteristics across heterogeneous traffic participants.
  This work revisits this assumption and proposes CANMOT, a class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling framework for KF-based 3D MOT. Class-specific diagonal process and measurement covariance matrices are introduced and optionally expressed in the object coordinate frame to preserve longitudinal-lateral anisotropy.
  Systematic experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that class-aware and object-aligned noise modeling improves tracking performance and substantially reduces identity switches compared to state-of-the-art (SotA). In addition, the consistency of the estimated uncertainty is analyzed using the Average Normalized Estimation Error Squared (ANEES) and $χ^2$-based violation tests. The results reveal severe overconfidence in standard KF-based MOT baselines. While the proposed formulation improves calibration without modifying the underlying filtering framework, it still exhibits substantial inconsistency, highlighting the need for further research in this area.
  Code is available at https://github.com/rst-tu-dortmund/learned-3d-nms.

</details>


### [328] [PHASER: Phase-Aware and Semantic Experience Replay for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03598)
*Ziyang Chen,Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Qianyi Cai,He Zhang,Pengteng Li,Yiren Zhao,Yandong Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHASER 是一种针对 VLA 机器人的持续学习框架，通过自动化的时间分段与智能化的记忆回放策略，解决了因采样不当导致的子技能遗忘问题，显著提升了机器人在开放环境下的长效操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在持续机器人操作任务中面临灾难性遗忘。现有的经验回放（ER）方法使用朴素均匀采样，忽略了操作轨迹的时间特征，导致因果关键的短子技能被“阶段饥饿（phase starvation）”，且无法应对不同任务间差异化的遗忘程度。

Method: PHASER 框架包含三个核心模块：1. 相中心容量分配（Phase-centric capacity allocation）：保证不同子技能在记忆池中获得平等支持；2. 多模态干扰路由（Multi-modal interference routing）：动态优先回放遗忘风险高的历史阶段；3. Auto-PC（自动相位分割）：结合无监督动作变化点检测与VLM语义验证，自动提取任务边界，无需人工标注。

Result: 在LIBERO持续学习基准测试中，PHASER在三种VLA骨干网络上均表现优异；与同预算的ER方法相比，平均成功率（ASR）提升高达31%，在LIBERO-Goal设定下最终ASR达到87.8%。

Conclusion: PHASER 提出了一种解决VLA模型在连续学习中灾难性遗忘问题的有效方案，通过相中心容量分配和多模态干扰路由策略，显著提升了机器人模型在长任务序列中的性能，并实现了全自动的持续学习闭环。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved remarkable success in language-conditioned robotic manipulation. However, deploying these models in open-ended environments requires continuously acquiring novel skills, a process that inevitably triggers severe catastrophic forgetting of previously learned behaviors. While experience replay (ER) serves as a standard mitigating strategy, naive uniform sampling fundamentally misaligns with the temporal characteristics of manipulation trajectories. It systematically under-samples brief but causally critical sub-skills, leading to phase starvation, and completely overlooks the varying degrees of forgetting across historical tasks. To overcome these limitations, we introduce PHASER, an architecture-agnostic continual learning framework. PHASER employs a phase-centric capacity allocation to guarantee equal memory support for all sub-skills, coupled with a multi-modal interference routing strategy that dynamically prioritizes historical phases at high risk of forgetting. Furthermore, to enable fully autonomous lifelong adaptation, we integrate Auto-PC, a lightweight pipeline combining unsupervised action-signal change-point detection with VLM-based semantic verification to extract temporal boundaries without intensive manual supervision. Evaluated across three VLA backbones on LIBERO continual learning suites, PHASER yields substantial empirical improvements, increasing Average Success Rate (ASR) by up to 31% over matched-budget ER and achieving an 87.8% final ASR on the LIBERO-Goal CL setting.

</details>


### [329] [A 3D Isovist World Model -- Revealing a City's Unseen Geometry and Its Emergent Cross-City Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03609)
*Xuhui Lin,Stephen Law,Nanjiang Chen,Kunyao Li,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对具身导航中空间感知缺失的问题，本文提出了一种基于3D等视线（3D isovist）的预测性世界模型，通过预测城市空间的几何动态而非外观，成功捕捉了城市特有的空间结构，为具身智能提供了更有效的导航空间基底。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的城市导航模型要么过度关注视觉外观（photometric entanglement），要么通过鸟瞰图将环境降维（flattening），丢失了复杂的多层级空间几何信息，无法很好地支持具身智能在三维空间中的真实导航。

Method: 1. 采用球面可视深度图（3D Isovist）作为环境表征，而非像素外观；2. 提出一种基于历史帧及动作预测下一时刻isovist的具身世界模型；3. 采用深度残差编码、自滚动计划采样（self-rollout scheduled sampling）及持久化鸟瞰图空间地图（persistent latent BEV map）来提升预测精度与跨路径一致性。

Result: 该模型在预测导航空间方面表现出色，并惊奇地发现，模型在未见过的城市中训练也能习得跨城市的时空特征，即城市身份可以从其时变潜变量（temporal latents）中被线性解码出来，证明了该几何表征成功捕获了空间动态特性而非仅记忆视觉外观。

Conclusion: 该研究通过对“负空间”（isovist）的建模，证明了仅利用几何信息而非视觉外观就能捕捉到城市环境的动态空间特征，为具身智能提供了一种轻量、可解释且具有跨城迁移性的导航几何基底。

Abstract: Embodied agents that navigate cities rely on world models that predict how their surroundings will change as they move. But for navigation, what matters is not what the buildings look like; it is where the agent can go. Most world models nonetheless predict appearance, learning how a scene looks rather than the space an agent can move through. Those that do target geometry, such as bird's-eye-view occupancy grids, flatten the three-dimensional environment onto a ground plane, discarding the above-ground and multi-level structure that shapes real navigation. What is missing is a predictive target that captures the navigable geometry an agent actually traverses, without photometric entanglement and without collapsing the third dimension. Our key idea is to model the open volume between buildings, the negative space, encoded as a 3D isovist: a spherical visibility-depth map recording the distance to the nearest surface in every direction. We introduce an embodied world model that predicts the next isovist from a short history of past isovists and a movement action. The prediction is formulated as a depth residual so the decoder inherits sharp building edges, trained with self-rollout scheduled sampling to keep corrupted context on the geometry manifold, and equipped with a persistent latent bird's-eye-view spatial map for cross-path consistency. Our central finding is emergent and unexpected: a single city-blind model trained on Manhattan and Paris develops a cross-city spatial signature, with city identity linearly decodable from its temporal latents far above single-frame baselines, so the signature lives in the learned dynamics rather than in appearance. The representation is lightweight, interpretable, and reproducible, offering a geometric substrate for spatial reasoning in embodied AI, robotics, and urban analysis, released with an open dataset and pipeline.

</details>


### [330] [GN0: Toward a Unified Paradigm for Generation, Evaluation, and Policy Learning in Visual-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03682)
*Xinhai Li,Xiaotao Zhang,Yuehao Huang,Jiankun Dong,Tianhang Wang,Sunyao Zhou,Yunzi Wu,Chengnuo Sun,Yunfei Ge,Qizhen Weng,Chi Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了GN-Matrix框架，通过大规模3DGS导航数据集、高保真交互式模拟器、BEV基准测试及BAE强化学习模型，显著提升了具身导航系统的泛化能力、长程表现及空间推理水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉语言导航(VLN)系统受到导航数据匮乏及质量受限的影响，导致其泛化能力不足，在长程任务中表现受限，且缺乏能够支持人机交互的高保真模拟环境。

Method: 1. 构建GN-Matrix大规模导航数据集；2. 开发基于3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS)的高保真仿真器；3. 提出基于鸟瞰图(BEV)的评估基准GN-Bench；4. 提出BAE (Break and Establish) 强化学习驱动的导航基础模型，结合DAgger算法优化探索，并利用3DGS渲染的BEV表征提升VLMs的空间推理能力。

Result: 实验表明，GN-BAE模型在GN-Bench基准测试和VLN-CE任务中均优于现有的最先进方法，证明了该框架在复杂导航场景下的有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: GN-Matrix提供了一个涵盖数据构建、高保真仿真和学习框架的统一平台，通过其方法论在具身导航任务中实现了性能飞跃，为具身智能研究和工业应用奠定了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: Embodied navigation connects intelligent agents with the physical world and is fundamental for general robotic intelligence. Limited availability and quality of navigation data have constrained Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) systems' generalization and long-horizon capabilities. To address this, we curate diverse 3D scenes and develop an automated pipeline for large-scale navigation data, resulting in the GN-Matrix dataset. Building on a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) engine, we introduce a high-fidelity simulation platform supporting interactive roaming and collision-aware navigation. We further propose GN-Bench, the first BEV-based benchmark incorporating dynamic 3DGS avatars for human-robot interaction evaluation. To leverage the simulator, we develop an RL-driven navigation foundation model, Break and Establish (BAE). After supervised learning, DAgger exposes the model to rollout-induced states, breaking narrow expert-centric distributions and enabling downstream RL exploration. This unified VLN paradigm integrates map-based and map-free tasks, including instruction following, human following, and goal navigation. GN-BAE formalizes high-fidelity 3DGS-rendered Bird's Eye View representations as compact memory, unlocking latent spatial reasoning in VLMs. Extensive evaluations on GN-Bench and VLN-CE show that GN0 outperforms state-of-the-art VLN methods. Overall, GN-Matrix offers a unified framework spanning data, simulation, and learning, advancing embodied navigation in research and industrial applications.

</details>


### [331] [Face versus Body Tracking for Human-Robot Interaction: An Egocentric Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03694)
*Jessica Wenninger,Gabriel Skantze*

Main category: cs.RO
