TL;DR: 针对现有视觉模型在社交机器人场景下频繁身份切换（IDSW）的问题，本文提出了一种包含复杂社会动态的自中心数据集及优化算法，通过结合空间记忆与ReID技术，将IDSW降低了49%，并指出目前主流基准测试在处理近距离遮挡时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机视觉模型多针对监控或自动驾驶优化，无法应对社交机器人面临的独特挑战（如人类快速运动、相互遮挡、频繁进出画面等），导致频繁的身份切换（IDSW）进而引发交互中断。

Method: 1. 构建了一个包含Furhat机器人采集的复杂社会动态的自标注自中心视角数据集；2. 对检测误差与跟踪逻辑进行系统性隔离评估；3. 对比了人脸与身体跟踪的效果；4. 评估了扩展空间记忆及外观再识别（ReID）的影响。

Result: 增加空间记忆虽能缓解长时间遮挡，但对复杂动态事件效果不佳；引入ReID解决了部分复杂切换问题，但在提升身体跟踪稳定性的同时，因人脸角度敏感性导致了人脸IDSW的增加。综合优化后的流程显著提升了跟踪稳定性。

Conclusion: 开发出的优化计算流程能减少49%的ID切换频率，有效降低交互中断。该研究强调了使用原生采集的社会动态数据对于验证人机交互感知模型至关重要，因为现有基准测试缺乏针对近距离遮挡的密集场景评估。

Abstract: To enable meaningful human-robot interaction (HRI), a robot must continuously assess engagement by consistently tracking users over time. State-of-the-art computer vision models, however, are heavily optimized for surveillance or autonomous driving. A social robot faces distinct egocentric challenges, such as humans bouncing, obstructing each other, or leaving the frame. Frequent identity switches (IDSW) cause the robot to lose its footing mid-conversation. To address this, we introduce a novel, custom-annotated egocentric dataset collected via the Furhat robot to capture complex social dynamics. We present a systematic evaluation isolating detection errors from tracking logic, comparing face versus body tracking, and assessing the impact of extended spatial memory and appearance re-identification (ReID). Results indicate that increasing spatial memory mitigates prolonged occlusions but fails on complex dynamic events. Integrating ReID resolves complex switches but exhibits opposing effects: it substantially improves body tracking stability, yet causes facial IDSW to spike due to profile angle sensitivity. Ultimately, our optimized pipeline reduces IDSW by 49\%, mitigating interaction breakdowns. Because standard benchmarks lack dense, close-quarter occlusions, this work highlights the critical need for natively captured social dynamics to truly validate HRI perception models.

</details>


### [332] [Neural Navigation Functions for Zero-Shot Generalizable Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03756)
*Benjamin D. Shaffer,Pei-An Hsieh,Brooks Kinch,Nathaniel Trask,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Neural-NF 是一种融合了学习特征与结构化偏微分方程（PDE）的导航方法，成功在保证几何安全与全局最优性的前提下，实现了跨未知环境的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端学习导航方案在未见过的新环境几何结构下泛化能力差，且缺乏对路径规划理论（如无碰撞、单调性）的结构性保障。

Method: 提出了一种“神经导航函数（Neural-NF）”框架。它利用拉普拉斯算子提取环境几何特征，映射为偏微分方程（PDE）的局部系数，通过求解边界值问题生成全局价值函数，并将学习过程融入到椭圆规划器的数学结构中。

Result: Neural-NF 在多样化的几何环境中表现出强大的零样本迁移能力，在性能上比直接预测价值函数的传统学习规划器提升了多达 5 倍。

Conclusion: Neural-NF 通过将学习方法与结构化规划器相结合，实现了在零样本场景下具备全局一致性、无碰撞且具有单调收敛性的鲁棒导航策略。

Abstract: We introduce Neural Navigation Functions (Neural-NF), a learned reactive navigation function capable of zero-shot transfer across unseen environment geometries. Neural-NF places data-driven adaptation within a structured elliptic planner, where the navigation objective is learned while planner structure is preserved by construction. Specifically, intrinsic Laplacian-derived features are mapped to local PDE coefficients, and solving the resulting boundary value problem produces a globally consistent value function on each target domain. For every admissible learned model, the resulting policy is collision-free, provides monotonic descent and a global minimum at the goal by construction. This admits a linearly-solvable optimal-control interpretation for any parameter setting. Empirically, Neural-NF achieves strong zero-shot transfer across diverse geometries and outperforms learned planners that directly predict the value function by up to a $5\times$ improvement.

</details>


### [333] [Worth Remembering: Surprise-Gated Robot Episodic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03787)
*Nicolas Gorlo,Derek K. Wise,Alberto Speranzon,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种利用贝叶斯惊奇度作为门控机制的记忆筛选方法，有效解决了通用机器人如何选择性地长期存储有用体验的问题，在多项机器人基准任务中取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人需要具备调用长期记忆以处理与过去事件相关指令的能力，但在记忆容量有限的情况下，如何筛选出对未来潜在任务具有高实用价值的记忆是一个核心挑战。

Method: 利用 V-JEPA-2 提供的语义丰富且与部署无关的潜在空间计算贝叶斯惊奇（Bayesian surprise），作为记忆形成的门控机制，并将其与基于 4D 场景图的空间记忆相结合。

Result: 在机器人问答任务中，相比现有最先进的方法，在时空和二元问题上表现提升至少 12%；在事件分割任务中，超越了现有的监督式和非因果方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于贝叶斯惊奇（Bayesian surprise）的记忆门控机制，能够有效地从海量历史信息中筛选出具有高实用价值的片段，显著提升了机器人处理时空、二元问答的能力及事件分割的准确性，证明了该方法在通用机器人记忆存储中的优越性。

Abstract: Robots solving generalist tasks need to be able to ground instructions in their past experience, since humans may refer to notable past events when giving a task (e.g., ``Take me to where the chemical spill happened yesterday''). Since memory limits make storing all past events infeasible, long-term robot memory must be selective, ideally retaining only those episodes with high utility for future tasks. However, future tasks are not typically given a priori for generalist robots. To select generically useful memories, we propose Bayesian surprise as a gating mechanism for memory formation. We present an approach to compute surprise in a semantically rich deployment-agnostic latent space provided by V-JEPA-2. Using our gated episodic memory to augment 4D scene graph-based spatial memory, we show a consistent improvement over state-of-the-art benchmarks in robot question answering, outperforming prior robot memory methods by $\geq12\%$ for temporal, spatial, and binary questions, and surpassing the performance of supervised and non-causal methods with an unsupervised causal method in event segmentation tasks.

</details>


### [334] [Optimal Design and Analytical Modeling of a Soft Fin-Ray Effect Gripper Finger Using the Finite Rigid Elements Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03798)
*Sara Adeli,Hassan Sayyaadi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究设计并优化了一种基于Fin Ray效应的软体夹爪指，通过引入有限刚体单元法（FREM）解决了建模难题，实现了对番茄等脆弱农产品的高精度力控制，理论与数值模型精度均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对农业领域中精细、不规则物体（如番茄）的抓取需求，旨在解决软体机器人非线性行为、无限自由度及材料属性多变等挑战，以实现精确的力控制和良好的自适应性。

Method: 采用有限刚体单元法（FREM）进行理论建模，利用ANSYS进行有限元分析（FEM），并通过实验测试进行验证；基于末端位移、总挠度、应力分布和接触力四个准则对指状结构进行了多目标优化。

Result: 确定了最优手指结构（长度30mm、肋间距10mm、7根肋且倾角为-15度、肋厚1mm）；FREM理论预测误差为3%，ANSYS数值模型误差为2%。

Conclusion: 利用有限刚体单元法（FREM）对Fin Ray效应软体夹爪指进行建模是高度有效的，该方法在保持分析精度的同时，为后续的力控制算法开发奠定了可靠基础，且经验证与数值模拟及实验结果高度吻合。

Abstract: Fin Ray-inspired soft grippers offer a promising solution for gently handling delicate, irregular objects, especially in agriculture. The objective of this research is to design, fabricate, and model a Fin Ray Effect (FRE) soft gripper finger to enable precise force control in future applications. This design aims to gently grasp delicate agricultural products, such as tomatoes, that require both adaptability and accurate force application. To address the inherent challenges of soft robotics, including nonlinear behavior, infinite degrees of freedom, and variable material properties, the Finite Rigid Elements Method (FREM) was employed for modeling. This method preserves analytical accuracy while providing a reliable foundation for the development of a force controller in later stages. A detailed Finite Element Model (FEM) was created using ANSYS, and the analytical results were validated through simulation and experimental testing. The gripper's fingers were optimized based on four key criteria: tip displacement, total deflection, stress distribution, and contact force. The optimal finger configuration includes a length of 30 mm, rib spacing of 10 mm, seven ribs angled at -15 deg, and a rib thickness of 1 mm. Theoretical modeling using the FREM predicted finger deformation with a 3% error, while the ANSYS numerical model achieved 2% error.

</details>


### [335] [Let the Dynamics Flow: Stable Flow Matching Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03834)
*Rodrigo Pérez-Dattari,Francisco Leiva,Andrea Testa,Leonel Rozo,Javier Ruiz del Solar,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Stable Flow Matching Dynamical Systems (SFMDS)，通过在流匹配框架中融入李雅普诺夫稳定性约束，解决了机器人动力系统表达能力与运动安全性之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于流匹配的模仿学习模型虽在多模态运动策略上表现优异，但缺乏形式化的稳定性保证，而传统的动力系统方法虽具稳定性但表达能力有限，难以捕捉复杂的动作分布。

Method: 提出了“稳定流匹配动力系统”（SFMDS），通过流匹配参数化动力系统，并引入两种稳定性约束机制：一种是基于惩罚项的软约束，另一种是嵌入模型架构的硬结构约束；此外，还将方法扩展到了李群（Lie groups）空间。

Result: 在基准数据集、仿真环境及人形机器人实验中，SFMDS 在低维和高维状态空间下均展示了良好的稳定性、可扩展性和多模态动作生成能力。

Conclusion: SFMDS 成功结合了流匹配的高表达能力与动力系统的李雅普诺夫稳定性保证，为复杂机器人任务提供了既安全又具表现力的动作生成方案。

Abstract: Flow matching has recently emerged as a powerful approach for imitation learning, enabling scalable, expressive, and multimodal motion policies. However, incorporating formal stability guarantees into these generative models, a prerequisite to ensure safe and generalizable robot behaviors, remains a significant challenge. While modeling robot motions as dynamical systems allows for such stability-based inductive biases, existing frameworks struggle to capture the rich action distributions inherent in complex robotic tasks. This paper introduces Stable Flow Matching Dynamical Systems (SFMDS), a novel framework that bridges the gap between high-capacity generative modeling and formal Lyapunov stability guarantees. SFMDS parametrizes dynamical systems via flow matching while simultaneously constraining the model to a family of stable solutions. We propose two variants: a soft constraint based on a penalty term, and a hard structural constraint embedded directly in the model architecture. We further extend both formulations to Lie groups. Experiments on benchmark datasets, in simulation, and on a humanoid robot show that SFMDS learns stable, scalable, and multimodal dynamical systems in low- and high-dimensional state spaces, enabling safe and expressive robot motion generation.

</details>


### [336] [Denoising Tells When to Replan: Denoising-Variance Adaptive Chunking for Flow-Based Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03847)
*Xiangdong Feng,Yuxuan Cheng,Chen Shi,Boyao Han,Yuxuan Yan,Yitong Hong,Zhuotao Tian,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对流式机器人策略固定分块长度不足的问题，提出DVAC方法，通过监测去噪过程的动作估计方差，实现自适应的动作分块执行，有效提升了任务成功率并降低了计算冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流（flow-based）的机器人策略通常使用固定的动作分块长度，这忽略了机器人运动在自由空间与交互阶段对规划频率的不同需求，导致重复规划开销大或精度不足。

Method: 提出了一种名为DVAC（去噪方差自适应分块）的推理时间方法。DVAC通过监控去噪过程中清洁动作估计的方差，动态确定动作块中需要执行的步数：在方差较低（稳态）时执行更多步骤，在方差较高（高精度/交互关键阶段）时提前触发重规划，并引入滚动估计机制以校准阈值。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin、CALVIN及真实世界操作任务中表现优异。以π_0.5策略为例，在LIBERO上成功率从94.75%提升至98.00%，同时重规划频率降低了43.0%。

Conclusion: DVAC通过利用去噪过程中的方差指标，实现了机器人策略的自适应动作块执行。该方法在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了重规划频率，验证了其在模拟环境及真实世界操作任务中的普适性与高效性。

Abstract: Action chunking has become a common inference strategy for flow-based robot policies, improving action coherence by modeling multi-step temporal dependencies in demonstrations. However, the execution horizon is still typically set as an empirical fixed value, overlooking that predictable free-space motions and precision-critical interaction phases often require different replanning frequencies. In this work, we first show that the denoising process of flow-based policies contains an intrinsic signal of task phases: clean-action estimates remain stable during predictable motion phases, but fluctuate more strongly around contact-rich or precision-sensitive operations. Motivated by this observation, we propose DVAC (Denoising-Variance Adaptive Chunking), a test-time method that adaptively determines how many actions to execute from each predicted chunk. DVAC measures the variance of clean-action estimates over the final denoising steps, executes the stable low-variance prefix, and replans before high-variance future actions are committed. To transfer across tasks and rollouts, DVAC further calibrates the threshold with a rolling estimate of the local variance scale. Experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin, CALVIN, and real-world manipulation show that DVAC improves task success while reducing replanning frequency. With a $π_{0.5}$-based policy, DVAC improves LIBERO success from 94.75% to 98.00% and reduces replanning by 43.0%, while also yielding aggregate gains on RoboTwin and CALVIN and improving real-world execution efficiency.

</details>


### [337] [Multi-Robot Bearing-only Pose Estimation via Angle Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03931)
*J. Francisco Presenza,Leonardo J. Colombo,Ignacio Mas,Juan I. Giribet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型分布式方位基位姿估计器，仅需“角刚性”拓扑即可在无需预知方向的前提下实现3D多机器人系统的位姿估计，并证明了其指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的位姿估计方法往往依赖于较强的刚性条件（如方位刚性），本文旨在降低对传感拓扑的要求，并实现在无先验方向知识的情况下对时变多机器人系统进行分布式的位姿估计。

Method: 提出了一种基于方位（bearing）的分布式位姿估计器，利用主体坐标系下的方位角在不依赖自身方向（orientation）的前提下估计三维位置；随后通过位置估计值、方位角及其导数恢复出SO(3)空间中的姿态。

Result: 理论证明了该观测器在特定条件下具有局部一致指数稳定性（LUES），并通过数值仿真验证了算法的性能。

Conclusion: 该分布式估计器在满足持续激励（PE）运动假设的条件下，仅需满足较弱的“角刚性（angle-rigid）”传感拓扑即可实现局部一致指数稳定性，验证了该方案在处理多机器人系统位姿估计时的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: This letter proposes a novel distributed bearing-based pose estimator for time-varying multi-robot systems. The method uses angles computed from body-frame bearings to estimate the robots' positions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ without knowledge of their orientations. The orientations in $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ are recovered from the estimated positions, the bearings, and the bearing derivatives. The proposed observer only requires the (directed) sensing topology to be \textit{angle-rigid}, a weaker condition than the commonly used ones like bearing rigidity. Local uniform exponential stability of the proposed observer is established under the assumption of persistently exciting motions for a subset of robots. Simulations are presented and discussed to evaluate the scheme's effectiveness and practicality.

</details>


### [338] [PointAction: 3D Points as Universal Action Representations for Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03943)
*Mutian Tong,Han Jiang,Qiao Feng,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PointAction通过将视频生成与3D点云预测结合，为机器人动作提供结构化的中间表示（4D点动态），解决了RGB视频动作预测模糊的问题，增强了跨平台的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频扩散模型的动作模型（VAMs）仅处理RGB信息，难以解析精确的3D运动、接触几何及空间约束，导致动作定位模糊，且大规模动作监督数据获取成本高昂。

Method: 提出PointAction框架：微调基础视频生成模型，使其同步输出RGB帧与动态3D点图（Pointmaps）；利用扩散模型驱动的动作解码器将点云动态映射为可执行的机器人动作。

Result: 该方法在机器人场景下实现了SOTA级别的4D生成质量；在仿真实验中超越了现有基线模型；成功在两个未参与预训练的真实机械臂上实现泛化。

Conclusion: PointAction通过引入度量3D点云动态作为视频生成与机器人控制之间的中间接口，有效消除了RGB视频动作预测的模糊性，实现了跨任务、跨具身机器人的高效迁移与零样本泛化。

Abstract: Video-Action Models (VAMs) leverage the broad visual dynamics captured by pre-trained video diffusion models, offering a promising path toward generalizable robot manipulation. However, RGB-only video rollouts are not directly actionable: they leave metric 3D motion, contact geometry, and fine-grained spatial constraints under-specified, making action grounding ambiguous. Meanwhile, scaling action supervision across diverse tasks and embodiments remains costly. We present PointAction, a framework that bridges video predictions to robot actions through explicit point-based 4D modeling. PointAction fine-tunes a foundation video generation model to jointly predict future RGB frames and dynamic 3D pointmaps, producing temporally consistent 3D motion of task-relevant scene geometry. These point dynamics serve as a structured, embodiment-agnostic action interface, which a diffusion-based action decoder maps to executable robot actions. By using metric 3D point dynamics as the interface between video prediction and control, PointAction reduces the ambiguity of RGB-only action grounding and supports transfer across tasks and embodiments with limited action supervision. Experiments show that PointAction achieves state-of-the-art 4D generation quality on robot scenes, outperforms existing baselines in simulation, and generalizes to two real robot arms unseen during pretraining.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [339] [Cost-Aware Query Routing in RAG: Empirical Analysis of Retrieval Depth Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02581)
*Sanjay Mishra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CA-RAG 是一种通过按查询路由机制动态调整检索深度和生成策略的 RAG 框架，能在显著降低成本和延迟的同时，保持大模型生成结果的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 系统面临深度检索（高成本/高延迟/高质量）与浅层/无检索（低成本/低延迟/可能低质量）之间的三难困境，静态检索配置无法适应异构的查询负载。

Method: 引入 Cost-Aware RAG (CA-RAG) 框架，通过 per-query 路由机制，根据查询的复杂程度，从预定义的“策略束”（strategy bundles，结合了检索深度与生成概要）中动态选择最佳配置。决策依据是最大化一个标量效用函数，该函数平衡了预估质量、预测延迟惩罚和 token 成本惩罚。

Result: 在 28 个查询的基准测试中，与总是使用重型检索相比，token 消耗减少了 26%；与总是直接推理相比，平均延迟降低了 34%，同时保持了相当的回答质量。

Conclusion: CA-RAG 提供了一个透明、可审计的框架，能够通过动态化选择检索深度，在成本、延迟和回答质量之间达到最优平衡，证明了针对复杂查询负载进行按查询路由（per-query routing）的有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) faces a fundamental three-way tension: deeper retrieval improves factual grounding but inflates token costs and end-to-end latency. Static retrieval configurations cannot resolve this tension across heterogeneous query workloads -- simple definitional queries waste budget on unnecessary context, while complex analytical prompts are underserved by shallow retrieval. This paper introduces \emph{Cost-Aware RAG} (CA-RAG), a per-query routing framework that selects from a discrete catalog of \emph{strategy bundles} -- each coupling a retrieval depth (from retrieval-free direct inference to top-$k{=}10$ dense retrieval) with a fixed generation profile -- by maximizing a scalar utility that linearly combines an estimated quality prior with normalized penalties for predicted latency and total billed tokens. CA-RAG is implemented with FAISS-backed dense retrieval and OpenAI chat/embedding APIs, and evaluated on a 28-query benchmark spanning four bundles. The router dynamically exercises all bundles, achieving \textbf{26\% fewer billed tokens} than always-heavy retrieval and \textbf{34\% lower mean latency} than always-direct inference while maintaining equivalent answer quality. Per-query delta analysis reveals that savings are non-uniform and concentrated in simpler queries, motivating complexity-aware guardrails. Sensitivity analysis confirms that the same bundle catalog supports multiple cost-latency-quality operating points through weight adjustment alone. All results are generated directly from logged CSV artifacts for full reproducibility. CA-RAG provides a transparent, auditable foundation for cost-conscious LLM deployments.

</details>


### [340] [Attention Calibration for Position-Fair Dense Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02737)
*Andrianos Michail,Elias Schuhmacher,Juri Opitz,Simon Clematide,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在推理阶段通过注意力校准减轻密集检索模型位置偏差的方法，无需重训练即可在提升检索位置公平性的同时，保证整体检索准确率的稳定或优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索模型普遍存在位置偏差（即对段落后部的信息检索效果较差），传统的解决方法通常需要昂贵的重训练，而本文旨在探索如何在推理阶段通过优化注意力机制来缓解这一偏差，且不牺牲检索性能。

Method: 基于Schuhmacher et al. (2026)的推理时注意力校准方法，引入了强度系数（lambda）来平衡原始分布与校准分布；研究了校准的层深度、篮子大小（basket size）和强度对公平性与检索效率的影响，并提出了一套无需模型微调的通用配置（B=128, lambda=0.5, 50%层深度）。

Result: 在SQuAD-PosQ、FineWeb-PosQ及跨语言/跨领域的PosIR数据集上验证，该方法不仅降低了位置敏感度指标（Position Sensitivity Index），还在保持甚至提升nDCG@10整体指标的同时，实现了对多种模型架构（<s>-pooled与last-token-pooled）的通用适用性。

Conclusion: 通过推理时的注意力校准（Inference-time attention calibration）可以有效缓解密集检索模型中的位置偏差，且无需重训练。通过细粒度调整（如部分层校准和强度系数设置），可以在不降低整体检索效果的前提下，显著提高检索的位置公平性，并具有良好的跨模型、跨语言和跨领域普适性。

Abstract: Dense retrieval models exhibit positional bias: retrieval effectiveness degrades when relevant information appears later in a passage (Zeng et al., 2025). We ask whether this bias can be reduced at inference time, without retraining and without sacrificing overall retrieval effectiveness. To this end, we adapt inference-time attention calibration (Schuhmacher et al., 2026) to downstream retrieval and extend it with a strength coefficient lambda that interpolates between the original and fully calibrated attention distributions. Across three embedding models on SQuAD-PosQ and FineWeb-PosQ, we examine how basket size, calibrated layer set, and strength affect the trade-off between positional fairness and retrieval effectiveness, finding that partial calibration frequently outperforms full calibration. A single configuration (B=128, lambda=0.5, 50% layer depth) improves the harmonic mean of nDCG@10 across positional groups on FineWeb-PosQ for all three models without per-model tuning, and applies to both <s>-pooled and last-token-pooled architectures. This default configuration transfers without modification to PosIR, which spans 10 languages and 31 domains, reducing the Position Sensitivity Index in all 16 length-quartile x model x retrieval-setting combinations, while preserving or improving aggregate nDCG@10. We release our extended codebase at https://github.com/impresso/fair-sentence-transformers

</details>


### [341] [Slipstream: Locality-Aware Graph Index Construction for Streaming Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02992)
*Shubing Yang,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Slipstream 通过利用流式数据的时序连续性，优化了图索引的插入搜索路径，显著提升了流式 ANNS 的处理吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图索引（如 HNSW）在处理实时流式向量数据时，频繁的索引更新导致性能瓶颈（计算开销大），无法满足高吞吐实时应用需求。

Method: 提出了一种基于向量流连续性的新方法 Slipstream：1. 将上次查找的候选点作为下次插入的搜索起点（而非每次从 entry point 开始）；2. 引入自适应控制器，根据向量流的稳定性动态调整搜索候选集的范围；3. 建立了理论模型以刻画性能界限。

Result: 在 5 个数据集上的实验显示，Slipstream 在保持至少 0.95 recall@10 的前提下，端到端吞吐量最高提升了 30.8 倍。

Conclusion: Slipstream 是一种高效的流式 ANNS 方案，通过利用向量流的连续性，在不损失召回率的前提下，大幅提升了图索引构建的吞吐量，弥补了高频插入场景下的性能瓶颈。

Abstract: Graph indexes are widely used for high-recall approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS), but many real-time applications require streaming ANNS. In these real-time applications, continuously arriving embeddings must search the existing graph for candidate neighbors before updating graph edges, which makes repeated index construction a bottleneck for streaming ingestion workloads. We propose Slipstream, a new method that significantly reduces the computational cost of frequent insertions in graph indexes for ANNS. The core idea of Slipstream is exploiting the continuity in vector streams: the newly arrived point starts from promising candidates found during the previous insertion rather than searching from the entry point. More technically, Slipstream evaluates distinct subsets of starting candidates followed by an adaptive controller that narrows or widens the range according to the stream's stability. We further show that Slipstream is beyond heuristic: We derive an abstract model to characterize Slipstream's performance and analyze its theoretical bounds. We have implemented Slipstream in two popular open-source libraries (Faiss, HNSWLib) and compared it with four baseline methods on five streaming vector datasets. Experimental results show that Slipstream achieves up to 30.8$\times$ higher end-to-end throughput than baselines while maintaining at least 0.95 recall@10.

</details>


### [342] [Section-Weighted Hybrid Approach for Legal Case Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03138)
*Rajith Arulanandam,Nisansa de Silva*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用LLM结构化分割法律文档，结合BM25/向量检索与段落级精细匹配的法律案例检索框架，显著提升了检索的精准度与逻辑相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的法律案例检索往往只关注表面词汇重叠，难以抓住深度法律推理逻辑，无法实现真正意义上的类案检索。

Method: 1. 离线预处理：利用确定性LLM将法律文书分割为事实、争议点、结论和理由；2. 第一阶段：结合BM25（词法）与Dense ANN（语义）通过RRF进行高召回率的初步检索；3. 第二阶段：执行基于段落的精细化比对，采用Z-score归一化处理不同评分尺度，并通过学习到的段落权重进行加权聚合。

Result: 在司法规模的基准测试中，该系统在候选集覆盖率和最终检索准确性上均超过了强词法和神经检索基准模型。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段、感知段落的框架能有效提升法律案例检索的准确性，通过结构化分析和跨段落的精准匹配，优于现有的词法及语义基准模型。

Abstract: Finding truly analogous precedents requires capturing legal reasoning beyond surface word overlap. We present a two-stage, section-aware framework for legal case retrieval that first segments raw judgments into facts, issues, decision, and reasoning using a deterministic large language model (LLM) offline. In Stage 1, we combine parallel lexical (BM25) and semantic (dense ANN) whole-document searches via Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) to form a high-recall candidate pool. In Stage 2, we perform fine-grained, like-for-like comparisons (e.g., query reasoning vs. candidate reasoning). To address the scale mismatch between unbounded lexical scores and cosine similarities, we apply query-wise Z-score normalization before aggregating signals with learned section weights. For the top results, the system returns the relevant section text with a concise, grounded rationale and party-stance labels. We evaluate on a jurisdiction-scale benchmark, demonstrating consistent gains over strong lexical and neural baselines while maintaining high candidate coverage

</details>


### [343] [VirtualMLE: A Virtual ML Engineer that Optimizes Sequential Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03221)
*Shiteng Cao,Jingwen Liu,Junda She,Zhiheng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VirtualMLE是一个利用大模型智能体实现序列推荐自动调优的框架，通过反射和记忆机制，显著减少了手动调优的试错成本，并提升了跨数据集的调优效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 序列推荐（SR）模型在不同数据集上的调优目前严重依赖机器学习工程师的经验进行重复的试错，缺乏自动化的高效机制。

Method: 提出了VirtualMLE框架，这是一个基于LLM的智能体，通过执行、反射和记忆更新的闭环过程来自动优化推荐模型。该框架利用分层记忆系统存储启发式反馈，以便在后续试验中快速进行优化。

Result: 在三个Amazon SR基准数据集及两个主流骨干网络（SASRec, HSTU）上进行了验证。结果显示，VirtualMLE能在显著减少试验次数的前提下达到竞争性的推荐质量，且能将从先前数据集获得的认知总结迁移到新数据集，显著加速搜索过程。

Conclusion: LLM代理配备反射和记忆机制，可作为实用的虚拟工程师，有效自动化并摊销序列推荐（SR）优化过程中的启发式学习。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in reasoning, reflection, and tool utilization, unlocking new paradigms for automating complex engineering workflows. However, in the domain of sequential recommendation (SR), tuning models on new datasets still relies heavily on the manual trial-and-error of experienced machine learning engineers. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{VirtualMLE}, an LLM-agent framework that leverages the cognitive capabilities of LLMs to organize recommender optimizing into a closed loop of execution, reflection, and memory update. After each trial, the agent explicitly analyzes the observed outcomes and stores concise heuristic feedback in a hierarchical memory system. We evaluate VirtualMLE on three Amazon SR benchmarks with two representative backbones, SASRec and HSTU. VirtualMLE reaches competitive recommendation quality with substantially fewer trials. Furthermore, we observe that cognition summaries distilled from previous datasets can significantly accelerate the search process on unseen datasets, demonstrating the potential of transferring tuning heuristics. Overall, our results provide compelling evidence that LLM agents equipped with reflection and memory can serve as practical virtual engineers to automate and amortize heuristic learning in SR optimization. Our codes are available.

</details>


### [344] [Generalizing Graph Foundation Models via Hyperbolic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03307)
*Yifan Jin,Qirui Ji,Bin Qin,Jiangmeng Li,Lixiang Liu,Fuchun Sun,Changwen Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyRAG框架，利用双曲空间解决图检索增强生成中欧几里得空间与树状知识库不匹配的问题，提升了图基础模型的零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于欧几里得空间的图基础模型（GFM）及检索增强生成（RAG）方法存在几何局限，即欧氏空间的体积增长与树状结构知识库不匹配，导致语义粒度丢失及Hubness问题，限制了模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出了HyRAG（双曲检索增强生成）框架，包含三个核心模块：1. 双曲知识索引（Hyperbolic Knowledge Indexing）：在双曲空间中建模，保留树状层次结构；2. 多粒度检索（Multi-granularity Retrieval）：通过粗粒度和细粒度检索提供全局和局部语义信息；3. 双通道融合（Dual-path Fusion）：在特征和结构层面实现知识的有效融合。

Result: 在多个图基准数据集上的实验结果表明，HyRAG在零样本学习设置下表现优异，显著增强了图基础模型的泛化性能和推理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过在双曲空间中引入HyRAG框架，本文有效解决了图基础模型在处理分布偏移和外部知识检索时的几何不匹配与语义损失问题，显著提升了模型在零样本场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Graph foundation models (GFMs) emerged as a dominant paradigm in graph representation learning by leveraging large-scale pre-training for cross-domain inference. However, the parameterized knowledge encoded within these models is insufficient to cope with distribution shifts, limiting their generalization ability. To mitigate this issue, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has been introduced to incorporate external knowledge at inference time. Nevertheless, existing RAG frameworks operating in Euclidean space suffer from a fundamental geometric limitation: the polynomial volume growth of Euclidean space is inherently mismatched with the tree-structured external knowledge bases. This mismatch leads to the loss of semantic granularity in retrieval and gives rise to the hubness phenomenon.To address this limitation, we propose a Hyperbolic Retrieval-Augmented Generation (HyRAG) framework designed to enhance the generalization capabilities of GFMs. Specifically, the introduced Hyperbolic Knowledge Indexing module retains the tree-like hierarchies of the external knowledge base by modeling them within hyperbolic space. The Multi-granularity Retrieval module then provides GFMs with the global semantic anchors and local semantic nuances through coarse-grained and fine-grained knowledge retrieval, respectively. Finally, the Dual-path Fusion module achieves effective knowledge integration for graph tasks at both the feature and structural levels.Experiments on multiple graph benchmarks demonstrate significant improvements in the zero-shot setting, highlighting the generalization of our method for robust GFMs inference.

</details>


### [345] [Can LLM Rerankers Predict Their Own Ranking Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03535)
*Shiyu Ni,Keping Bi,Jiafeng Guo,Jingtong Wu,Zengxin Han,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了 LLM 重排序器能否进行自我质量评估（QPP）。研究发现，利用“自一致性”方法无需训练即可获得优异结果，而通过监督学习方案（Verb-Num/Verb-List）则可有效校准 LLM 的自我评价，使其成为更可靠的性能预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有查询性能预测（QPP）大多依赖外部预测器。本文旨在探究 LLM 重排序器能否在无需外部模块的情况下，直接准确估计其自身生成的排序质量，以在缺乏人工标注的情况下预判检索效果。

Method: 1. 提出“重排序器内部 QPP”框架；2. 无需训练方法：基于采样排序结果的“自一致性”评估以及直接询问 LLM 的“口头置信度”；3. 监督学习方法：提出了 Verb-Num 和 Verb-List，通过少量额外 tokens 引导模型输出校准后的质量分数。

Result: 在 TREC Deep Learning 2019–2022 数据集上的实验表明，自一致性方法效果与现有 SOTA 持平且校准度更佳；直接询问法往往过度自信；提出的监督学习方法（Verb-Num/Verb-List）显著提升了置信度的校准效果。

Conclusion: LLM 重排序器具备良好的自评估潜力。无需训练的“自一致性”方法表现优异且校准度高，而直接询问模型（verbalized confidence）则存在过度自信。通过监督学习（Verb-Num 和 Verb-List）微调，可以有效解决过自信问题，实现高精度的查询性能预测。

Abstract: Retrieval effectiveness varies substantially across queries, making it important to estimate ranking quality before relevance judgments are available. Query performance prediction (QPP) addresses this need, but most existing methods rely on external predictors after retrieval or reranking. In this paper, we study \textit{reranker-internal QPP}: can an LLM reranker estimate the quality of the ranking it has just produced? We investigate both training-free and training-based approaches. For training-free estimation, we examine metric-specific self-consistency across sampled rankings and verbalized confidence produced directly by the reranker. Experiments on TREC Deep Learning 2019--2022 with four LLMs show that self-consistency is competitive with the state-of-the-art (SOTA) approach and better calibrated in almost all settings, while direct verbalized confidence is severely overconfident. To improve verbalized confidence, we propose two supervised methods, Verb-Num and Verb-List, which enable LLM rerankers to produce calibrated ranking-quality estimates with only a few additional output tokens.

</details>


### [346] [Skill Is Not Document: A Query-Conditional Benchmark and Two-Stage Retriever for LLM Agent Skill Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03565)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Reject-as-Resource Retriever (R3)，旨在解决代理任务中技能组合的兼容性检索问题。研究发现LLM的“拒绝决策”是宝贵的负监督资源，通过将其融入重排序训练，显著提升了技能路由的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能检索方法多基于传统的文档检索逻辑（即关注单项语义相关性），忽略了代理任务中技能间的协作关系（技能兼容性）。LLM在构建代理任务时产生的“拒绝决策”蕴含了关键的负向监督信息，但常被误认为低质量数据而丢弃。

Method: 提出“Reject-as-Resource Retriever (R3)”框架：在使用两阶段检索系统（R3-Embedding + R3-Reranker）时，不仅关注查询与技能的语义匹配，还将LLM在多技能组合任务中的“拒绝”行为建模为显式的偏好信号，并在重排序（Reranker）阶段引入该“推力（push-away）”信号进行训练。同时构建了R3-Skill双语基准数据集以验证该方法。

Result: 在R3-Skill基准测试上，R3-Embedding + R3-Reranker系统实现了Hit@1 = 0.7714、NDCG@10 = 0.8327及Set-Compat = 0.3525的表现，证实了将拒绝决策转化为监督信号对改善技能路由的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的拒绝决策（Rejection Decisions）作为负样本指导训练，能够显著提升代理（Agent）在大规模技能库中的路由准确性与技能协作兼容性。R3框架证明了在两阶段检索流程中，显式利用“技能兼容性”信号可以有效弥补传统语义检索在代理任务中的不足。

Abstract: LLM agents complete complex tasks by composing multiple skills, and skill retrieval is a front-end stage for agents. Skill retrieval differs fundamentally from traditional document retrieval at the supervision level: top-K joint correctness depends not only on the semantic relevance of each individual query-skill pair, but also on whether the skills retrieved together can collaborate to fulfill the task under the given query. Such "skill compatibility" cannot be derived from independent relevance alone. Yet existing LLM-based data synthesis pipelines can produce a direct supervision signal for "which skills should not be jointly retrieved under this query" -- namely the LLM's own rejection decisions -- and this signal is routinely discarded as low-quality data. To address this gap, we propose Reject-as-Resource Retriever (R3) and construct R3-Skill, a bilingual (Chinese-English) skill retrieval benchmark targeting realistic agent skill routing. R3-Skill spans four language directions, features query phrasings close to real user requests, and is verified through multi-expert cross-checking. On R3-Skill, we build a two-stage retrieval system (R3-Embedding + R3-Reranker) with skill compatibility as an explicit training signal. Gradient analysis shows that the "push-away" signal is diluted by bilateral balancing in the bi-encoder but acts as lossless graded ranking supervision in the cross-encoder -- motivating its placement at the cross-encoder stage, as confirmed by ablations on two datasets. The R3-Embedding + R3-Reranker pipeline attains Hit@1 = 0.7714, NDCG@10 = 0.8327 and Set-Compat = 0.3525 on R3-Skill. The dataset, training code and model weights are released as open source for agent skill routing.

</details>


### [347] [MARS: Multi-rate Aggregation of Recency Signals for Sequential Recommendation across Sparse and Dense Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03718)
*Zhenyu Yu,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MARS是一种新型顺序推荐聚合算子，通过显式建模多尺度时间结构并根据数据密度自适应选择Transformer或Mamba备份，在性能和计算效率上均优于现有的Transformer和Mamba基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的顺序推荐模型（如Transformer的自注意力机制或状态空间模型的隐式衰减计划）未能显式建模用户行为中真实的多尺度时间结构。

Method: 提出了一种与编码器无关（encoder-agnostic）的聚合算子MARS（Multi-scale Adaptive Recency Summarization）。利用真实时间戳生成K个反映不同时间跨度的总结，通过上下文感知门控机制融合，并根据训练集序列长度在Transformer（针对稀疏数据）和Mamba（针对密集数据）之间自动选择最佳实例化。

Result: 在五个基准测试上超越了十个主流基线模型，HR@10表现最优。稀疏数据上性能提升显著（最高+36.2%），密集数据上在减少42%计算量（MFLOPs）的情况下优于SIGMA模型。

Conclusion: MARS通过引入多尺度时间结构建模，显著提升了顺序推荐在稀疏和密集数据下的表现，并在保持高效率的同时确立了新的精度-效率帕累托最优边界。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders weight historical interactions either through positional self-attention as in Transformers or through a single implicit decay schedule as in State-Space Models. Neither makes the multi-scale temporal structure of real user behaviour explicit. We propose MARS, an encoder-agnostic aggregation operator that consumes real timestamps and produces K summaries emphasising distinct recency scales, fused by a context-adaptive gate. MARS adds at most 6% parameters and runs in $\mathcal{O}(LdK)$ time. MARS adapts to data density by automatically selecting between two encoder instantiations: MARS-T (Transformer) for sparse data and MARS-M (Mamba) for dense data, based on the average sequence length of the training set. On five public benchmarks against ten Transformer- and Mamba-based baselines under a unified RecBole protocol, MARS attains the best HR@10 on every benchmark, with mean relative gain +19.7% over the strongest content-only Transformer baseline on sparse data (reaching +36.2% on Games) and +3.2% HR@10 / +0.9% NDCG over SIGMA on dense ML-1M at 42% fewer MFLOPs, occupying the accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier across the data-density spectrum. A backbone-only ablation isolates the marginal contribution of MARS at +4% to +19% HR@10 on sparse data and motivates the dual-instantiation design. The code is included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [348] [When Does Latent Reasoning Help? MeRa: Metric-Space Bias for Spatial Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03727)
*Zhenyu Yu,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对空间预测任务中潜在推理效果不佳的问题，本文提出了 MeRa 模型。研究发现空间预测中的推理需锚定于度量空间，通过引入度量空间偏差约束，MeRa 有效提升了模型性能并具备理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对现有的潜在推理方法在应用到空间预测任务时效果不佳的问题，探究潜在推理是否需要基于潜在的度量空间（metric space）进行约束。

Method: 提出一种轻量级、与骨干模型无关的模块——MeRa（Metric-space Reasoning）。MeRa 通过引入从成对距离学习到的度量空间偏差来约束潜在推理过程。

Result: 在 GETNext 骨干模型上，相比未引入度量空间偏差的推理方法，MeRa 将 NDCG@10 提升了 4.5%。在三个空间预测基准数据集上均达到最优水平，超过了 GeoMamba 和 HMST 等先进方法。此外，证明了该推理过程收敛至唯一不动点，且推理步数与其表达能力呈正相关。

Conclusion: 基于度量空间约束的推理能够有效提升空间轨迹预测的性能，且该方法具有良好的通用性和理论收敛性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning has improved sequential recommendation by iteratively refining representations before prediction, but does it help spatial prediction? We find that the answer depends on whether reasoning is grounded in the underlying metric space. Without such grounding, latent reasoning degrades spatial prediction below the unmodified baseline, while a learned metric-space bias derived from pairwise distances produces consistent gains. We formalize this finding through MeRa (Metric-space Reasoning), a lightweight backbone-agnostic module that can be inserted between any sequence encoder and its prediction heads. On the GETNext backbone, the gap between reasoning without and with metric-space bias reaches 4.5% NDCG@10. MeRa achieves the best NDCG@10 on all three spatial prediction benchmarks among the compared methods, surpassing recent approaches such as GeoMamba and HMST. We prove that metric-space-constrained reasoning converges to a unique fixed point and that N-step reasoning is strictly more expressive than (N-1)-step reasoning. A controlled experiment on CLEVR with Euclidean distance confirms that the finding generalizes beyond geographic coordinates. The code is included in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [349] [Taiji: Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization with Semantics-IDs Trade-off for Industrial LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03866)
*Yuecheng Li,Zeyu Song,Jing Yao,Chi Lu,Peng Jiang,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Taiji是一个专为工业级推荐系统设计的LLM增强框架，通过高质量链式思维(CoT)数据优化和帕累托最优策略优化(POPO)平衡语义与推荐偏好，有效解决了LLM与推荐系统的对齐难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM4Rec范式在将LLM语义空间与推荐系统ID空间对齐时存在两大瓶颈：SFT阶段CoT数据质量难以度量与提升，以及RL阶段LLM语义奖励与推荐偏好奖励之间的权衡被忽视。

Method: 1. 逆向推导与开放式拒绝采样：用于生成高质量、领域特定的CoT数据，解决SFT阶段的推理质量问题；2. Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization (POPO)：一种自适应调节跨域奖励权重的方法，平衡LLM语义知识奖励与推荐ID偏好奖励，实现Pareto最优对齐。

Result: Taiji框架在离线评估和线上A/B测试中表现优异；自2026年5月起在快手广告平台部署，日均服务超4亿用户，显著提升了商业收益，证明了其在Web级环境下的强大性能。

Conclusion: Taiji框架通过高质量CoT数据生成和POPO奖励对齐策略，成功解决了LLM与推荐系统ID空间对齐的难题，并在大规模工业场景中验证了其有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Scaling recommender systems via large language models (LLMs) has become a prominent trend in the industry. However, aligning the LLM's semantic space with the recommender's ID space via post-training (e.g., SFT and RL) remains challenging. Existing LLM4Rec paradigms are bottlenecked by two main issues: (1) the difficulty of measuring and improving chain-of-thought (CoT) quality in open-domain recommendation during SFT, and (2) the neglect of the trade-off between LLM semantic rewards and recommendation preference rewards during RL alignment. Inspired by these challenges, we present Taiji, a novel LLM-as-Enhancer framework designed for industrial recommender systems. To overcome the SFT bottleneck, we utilize reverse-engineered reasoning and open-ended rejection sampling to generate high-quality, domain-specific CoT data. To resolve the RL alignment issue, we propose Pareto Optimal Policy Optimization (POPO), which adaptively adjusts cross-domain reward weights. Theoretically, it achieves an optimal trade-off between the semantic world knowledge of LLMs and the collaborative ID features representing online user preferences. Extensive offline evaluations and online A/B tests validate the effectiveness of Taiji. Deployed on Kuaishou's advertising platform since May 2026, Taiji currently serves over 400 million users daily, yielding significant commercial revenue and demonstrating its robust scalability in web-scale environments.

</details>


### [350] [Do Neural Retrievers Prefer Certain Documents? Evidence of Learned Relevance Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02814)
*Francisco Valentini,Edgar Altszyler,Martin Fajcik*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了监督式神经检索器存在隐含的“文档相关性先验”偏见，模型更倾向于检索具有主流特征的文档，导致即便相关的细分或技术性文档被系统性低估，暴露出监督式检索在训练数据偏差下的结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的标注协议往往存在偏差，导致模型可能学习到关于文档的查询无关先验（relevance prior），而非真正的相关性，这可能导致模型对特定类型文档产生不公平的偏好。

Method: 1. 使用冻结的文档编码器训练简单的分类器，以估计模型预测出的“文档相关性先验”；2. 在多个IR基准测试上评估三种最先进的神经检索器；3. 使用LLM生成的解释来分析被标注为相关的文档特征，并将其与未被标注的文档进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：1. 监督式神经检索器普遍存在显著且一致的文档先验；2. 具有较低先验分数的文档更容易被“埋没”，造成可发现性鸿沟；3. 这种偏见在监督式密集检索器中尤为明显；4. 模型偏好的文档通常具备“全面、自包含、主流话题”等特征，而碎片化、细分或高度技术性的内容则易被忽略。

Conclusion: 监督式检索模型不仅学习了查询-文档的相关性，还无意中内化了训练数据中文档的固有偏好（文档相关性先验）。这种偏见导致即使相关文档也因缺乏某些“主流”特征（如全面性、主流话题）而难以被检索，从而产生了检索的“可发现性鸿沟”（findability gap）。

Abstract: Neural retrievers are trained to estimate query-document relevance from annotated query-document pairs. Yet annotation protocols may not purely reflect relevance: they select only a subset of documents for labeling, and this selection can favor certain document types over others. We investigate whether supervised bi-encoder retrievers implicitly learn a document-level relevance prior: a query-independent signal encoded in their representation space as a side effect of training on annotated data. We estimate this prior by training simple classifiers on frozen document embeddings and evaluate three state-of-the-art retrievers across multiple IR benchmarks. We find that supervised neural retrievers encode relevance priors that generalize to unseen documents and are consistent across models. These priors create a findability gap: documents with lower prior are systematically harder to retrieve, even when genuinely relevant. This effect appears in supervised dense retrievers but is weaker and less consistent in BM25, and it persists under controlled matched-document comparisons. Using LLM-based explanations, we find that judged-relevant documents tend to be comprehensive, self-contained summaries of mainstream topics, while niche, fragmentary, or highly technical content is often left unjudged. Retrievers internalize this bias, ranking documents with these favored features higher than documents that lack them, independently of their actual relevance. Our findings expose a structural limitation of supervised retrieval: models trained on annotated data do not just learn relevance, but also the implicit document preferences in their training data.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [351] [Online K-d tree for approximate neighborhood search in data streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02752)
*Eduardo V. L. Barboza,Robert Sabourin,Rafael M. O. Cruz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对大规模数据流中kNN计算开销大的问题，提出了一种支持动态高效更新的在线K-d树，并成功适配堪培拉距离，在维持高精准度的同时显著提升了处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统K-d树在处理动态数据流时，频繁插入与删除操作难以维持树的平衡及不变量，且传统K-d树主要针对闵可夫斯基距离设计，限制了其在特定距离度量下的应用，导致在大规模应用中计算开销过大。

Method: 1. 设计并实现了一种支持数据流快速插入与删除的“在线K-d树”，通过维护树的结构不变性来保障搜索效率；2. 将K-d树的搜索机制适配至堪培拉距离，并实现了相应的子树剪枝（subtree pruning）策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，该在线K-d树在数据流场景下处理速度更优；适配堪培拉距离后，系统在仅损失微小精度的前提下，大幅提升了每秒可处理的实例数量。

Conclusion: 本文提出的在线K-d树（Online K-d tree）成功解决了数据流中动态更新导致的性能下降问题，证明了将其适配至堪培拉距离（Canberra distance）在保持高效检索的同时，能显著提升大规模数据流的处理吞吐量。

Abstract: The k-Nearest Neighbors (kNN) algorithm has long been widely used in Machine Learning (ML) applications. However, the main concern when using it is the computational cost required for neighborhood search, which can make it unfeasible for large-scale applications. Optimization algorithms, such as the K-d tree, become an option in such scenarios. Under data streams, it can be challenging to maintain the properties of the K-d tree, as it requires inserting and deleting nodes on the fly. These operations can make maintaining the tree's balance and invariants difficult. Additionally, traditional K-d trees were initially designed for Minkowski-based distance functions. In this work, we describe an Online K-d tree and its adaptation to the Canberra distance that supports dynamic updates over data streams while preserving the structural invariants required for efficient traversal. Experimental analysis demonstrates that the Online K-d tree algorithm achieves faster processing time under data streams, and that adapting to the Canberra distance enabled effective subtree pruning, as evidenced by a minor loss in average accuracy and a substantial gain in instances processed per second. Our implementation can be found in our GitHub repository

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [352] [DriftSched: Adaptive QoS-Aware Scheduling under Runtime Token Drift for Multi-Tenant GPU Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02982)
*Kathiravan Palaniappan*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对 LLM 推理中因输出长度估计偏差导致的调度失效问题，DriftSched 引入运行时自适应反馈机制进行补偿，显著降低了延迟并提升了资源调度效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理中预估输出长度与实际长度往往存在“运行时 Token 漂移”，导致调度不准确、排队不平衡及尾延迟增加等服务质量（QoS）退化问题。

Method: 提出 DriftSched 框架：包括工作负载分类、Token 预算估计、租户感知队列管理以及基于运行时反馈的漂移补偿机制；并在 NVIDIA L4 GPU 上对比了 FIFO、Priority、Weighted、SJF 和 Aging Priority 等调度策略。

Result: 自适应偏差校正使预估误差（MAE/RMSE）降低约 40%；在 GPU 竞争环境下，采用 SJF 策略使中位数延迟降低约 42%，P99 延迟降低约 16%。

Conclusion: DriftSched 通过引入适应性的运行时 token 漂移补偿机制，有效提升了多租户 LLM 推理任务的调度精度和 QoS，证明了在该场景下 SJF 策略结合漂移校正具有最优的延迟表现。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large language model (LLM) inference services has increased the demand for efficient multi-tenant GPU scheduling. While modern inference runtimes such as vLLM improve throughput through continuous batching and optimized memory management, accurately estimating the runtime cost of heterogeneous inference requests remains a significant challenge. In practice, observed output lengths often deviate from admission-time estimates, creating runtime token drift that can lead to workload misclassification, queue imbalance, increased tail latency, and degraded Quality-of-Service (QoS).
  This paper presents DriftSched, an adaptive QoS-aware scheduling framework for multi-tenant LLM inference serving on NVIDIA L4 GPUs. DriftSched combines workload classification, token-budget estimation, tenant-aware queue management, and runtime feedback-driven drift compensation to improve admission-time scheduling decisions. The framework evaluates FIFO, Priority, Weighted, Shortest-Job-First (SJF), and Aging Priority scheduling policies under heterogeneous multi-tenant workloads.
  Experimental results demonstrate measurable runtime token drift across workload categories. Adaptive bias correction reduces workload estimation error by an average of 38.8% (MAE) and 40.5% (RMSE), improving workload classification stability and scheduling accuracy. Among all evaluated schedulers, SJF achieves the best overall performance, reducing median end-to-end latency by approximately 42% and P99 latency by approximately 16% relative to FIFO under sustained GPU contention.
  The work contributes an adaptive drift-aware scheduling architecture, a runtime token-drift compensation mechanism, and a reproducible benchmarking framework for evaluating QoS-aware LLM inference scheduling on shared GPU infrastructure.

</details>


### [353] [NetKV: Network-Aware Decode Instance Selection for Disaggregated LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03910)
*Mubarak Adetunji Ojewale*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: NetKV 是一种新型调度算法，通过引入网络成本感知，解决了大模型解耦推理中 KV 缓存传输导致的 TTFT 高延迟问题，显著提升了推理性能和系统稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在解耦 LLM 推理架构中，KV 缓存的传输导致了巨大的首字延迟（TTFT），而现有的调度器仅考虑计算负载和缓存局部性，忽略了网络拓扑距离和动态拥塞的影响，导致在大上下文场景下调度性能严重欠佳。

Method: 1. 提出了一个“网络成本预言机”（network cost oracle）作为算子到调度器的接口；2. 开发了 NetKV 调度算法，这是一种复杂度为 O(|D|) 的贪婪算法，专门解决解耦推理中 KV 缓存传输的拓扑距离和动态拥塞问题。

Result: 在基于 Mooncake 追踪的 64 GPU 四层胖树（fat-tree）模拟器中，NetKV 将平均 TTFT 降低了高达 21.2%（对比轮询调度）和 17.6%（对比优化后的缓存/负载感知调度）；SLO 达成率提升了 20.1 个百分点，且将令牌间处理开销保持在 0.5ms 以下。

Conclusion: NetKV 证明了在解耦 LLM 推理架构中，将网络成本显式纳入调度决策对于优化 TTFT 至关重要；即使在过时的遥测数据下，其层次化排位策略也能保持鲁棒性，且不会对现有的传输、推理引擎或硬件产生额外负担。

Abstract: Disaggregated LLM inference forces the KV cache to traverse the datacenter network before decoding begins, so transfer time enters directly into the Time to First Token (TTFT) budget. Current schedulers route on compute load and prefix-cache locality alone, ignoring the topological distance and dynamic congestion between prefill and decode instances. We close this gap with a thin operator-to-scheduler interface, the network cost oracle, and we prove that ignoring the network term renders cache-aware-only scheduling arbitrarily suboptimal as context length grows. NetKV, the O(|D|) per-request greedy that consumes this oracle, has tier rankings that are provably robust to stale telemetry. On a 64-GPU four-tier fat-tree simulator driven by Mooncake traces, NetKV reduces mean TTFT by up to 21.2% over round-robin and 17.6% over a tuned cache+load-aware scheduler, lifts SLO attainment by up to 20.1 percentage points, and keeps the Time Between Tokens overhead below 0.5 ms in every condition tested, with no changes to the transport, inference engine, or hardware.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [GreenGNN: Energy-Aware Windowed Communication Optimization for Distributed GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02916)
*Arefin Niam,Tevfik Kosar,M. S. Q. Zulkar Nine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GreenGNN 是一种能效感知的分布式 GNN 训练系统，通过缓存热点特征及实现请求合并，显著降低了 RPC 开销和 GPU 空转，从而大幅提升了能效与训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 GNN 训练需分布式部署，采样产生的海量分散远程特征获取请求导致了极高的 RPC 开销和 GPU 等待时间，造成严重的能源浪费。

Method: 提出了一种能源感知系统 GreenGNN，通过将训练划分为多个窗口（W），将窗口内的“热点”特征缓存在本地，并将来自同一分区所有者的远程请求合并为批量传输；利用离散事件模拟器离线确定最优窗口大小 W。

Result: 在 4 节点 GPU 集群上，相比基准方法，系统总能耗降低了 27-43%，端到端吞吐量最高提升了 3.9 倍，GPU 能耗降低了 36-71%。

Conclusion: GreenGNN 通过利用邻居采样的局部特性，通过分批次处理和合并远程请求，成功解决了分布式 GNN 训练中通信能耗过高及 GPU 空转的问题，在节能与性能提升之间取得了平衡。

Abstract: Large-scale graph neural network (GNN) training often requires distributed clusters because graph structure and feature tensors no longer fit in a single node's memory. In sampling-based training, each mini-batch expands into a receptive field that spans partitions and triggers thousands of remote feature fetches per epoch. This wastes energy for two main reasons: each small RPC pays a fixed initiation and protocol cost, and GPUs continue drawing substantial baseline power while waiting for remote features. We present GreenGNN, an energy-aware distributed GNN training system that reduces communication energy by exploiting the bursty, short-lived temporal locality of neighbor sampling. GreenGNN groups training into windows of W consecutive mini-batches, stages each window's hot features in a local cache, and merges remote requests from each partition owner into a small number of bulk transfers. This amortizes RPC overhead across many features while preserving an on-demand path for cache misses. Because window size controls the trade-off between communication amortization and hot-set staleness, GreenGNN selects W offline using a discrete-event simulator that replays a deterministic one-epoch access trace with a hybrid energy model. We implement GreenGNN on DGL and evaluate it on a 4-node GPU cluster with benchmark datasets. Across datasets and batch sizes, GreenGNN reduces total system energy by 27--43% relative to baseline while improving end-to-end throughput by up to 3.9x. GPU energy drops by 36--71%, driven by fewer RPC initiations and lower GPU stall time.

</details>


### [355] [Brief Announcement: Generative Markov Model for Distributed Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03061)
*Alfreds Lapkovskis,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式马尔可夫模型的分布式计算系统通用建模框架，通过对系统状态的高维因子分解，简化了复杂系统的模拟与优化，并通过AI协作推理案例证实了其在提升系统调度效率和降低能耗方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算连续体（Computing Continuum）具有高度的异构性、随机性和复杂性，缺乏统一的数学形貌来支持高效的资源调度与管理，使得现有系统在规模化应用中难以进行有效的建模与优化。

Method: 提出了一种基于生成式马尔可夫模型（Generative Markov Model）的通用框架。该框架将系统状态分解为高维变量，并基于分布式系统的稀疏依赖结构对变量元素进行因子分解，从而实现易于处理的模拟、推断及强化学习策略优化。

Result: 通过协作式AI推理案例验证，表明在大规模场景下，相较于传统的集中式调度，采用该框架辅助的分布式计算调度能显著降低延迟，并有效减少对单一服务器资源的消耗。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将分布式系统建模为生成式马尔可夫模型，成功桥接了分布式计算、马尔可夫链理论与强化学习。研究证明，在分布式计算连续体中，通过自适应决策将计算任务分散至用户设备，可有效缓解集中式调度的瓶颈，降低延迟并减少服务器负载。

Abstract: Emerging distributed computing paradigms, such as the computing continuum, are inherently heterogeneous, stochastic, and complex. Efficiently and effectively utilizing all available resources across the continuum demands a unified formal model of the system. To address this gap, we propose a general framework for modeling distributed computing systems as a generative Markov model, factorized over a structured system state. In our model, the state decomposes into high-dimensional variables, each further factorized over its elements, reflecting the sparse dependency structure inherent to distributed systems. This yields a tractable model enabling simulation, inference, and policy learning over otherwise intractable system states, bridging distributed computing with Markov chain theory and reinforcement learning (RL). We demonstrate our framework through a case study of collaborative AI inference, in which a dedicated server combines resources with those volunteered by service users. Our results show that centralized scheduling becomes a bottleneck at scale, while distributing computation across user devices reduces both latency and server resource consumption. These findings highlight the value of adaptive decision-making in distributed computing systems and demonstrate the framework's utility for modeling, simulation, and optimization.

</details>


### [356] [BlobShuffle: Cost-Effective Repartitioning in Stream Processing Systems via Object Storage Exemplified with Kafka Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03364)
*Sören Henning,Otmar Ertl,Adriano Vogel*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BlobShuffle 是一种降低流处理重分区成本的新方法，它通过将数据存入对象存储并仅传输控制消息，在保持低延迟的同时将云端传输成本降低了 40 倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代云原生环境下，分布式流处理框架中的数据重分区（Shuffle）操作会导致跨可用区的大量网络流量，从而产生高昂的成本；此外，维护大规模、强一致性的消息队列链路也带来了沉重的运营负担。

Method: 提出了一种基于云对象存储的中间交换层机制：将数据流批量化存储在云存储中，仅向下游传输紧凑的通知消息（而非全量数据），并结合分布式缓存机制来平衡成本与延迟。以 Kafka Streams 插件形式实现，无需修改底层基础设施。

Result: 在 AWS Kubernetes 环境下的实验显示，BlobShuffle 相比 Kafka Streams 原生重分区方案，成本降低了 40 倍以上，95 分位延迟控制在 2 秒以内，并具备处理超过 2 GiB/s 数据吞吐量的扩展能力。

Conclusion: BlobShuffle 是一种成本效益极高且可扩展的流处理数据重分区方案，证明了利用云对象存储作为中间层可有效解决跨可用区传输的成本与性能瓶颈，适合大规模、密集型重分区工作负载。

Abstract: Shuffling or repartitioning data streams is an essential operation of state-of-the-art stream processing frameworks to support stateful workloads in a large-scale, distributed setting. In today's cloud deployments, however, shuffling can become a major cost driver due to substantial network traffic across multiple availability zones (AZs) as well as an operational burden when operating a high-throughput, strongly consistent messaging backbone at scale. We present BlobShuffle, a novel approach to cost-effective shuffling for stream processing systems that leverages cloud object storage as an intermediate exchange layer. Instead of sending all shuffled records directly, BlobShuffle groups records into batches, stores these batches in cloud object storage, and forwards only compact notifications. Downstream operators use these notifications to retrieve the relevant batches and extract the corresponding records. BlobShuffle balances cost efficiency and latency through configurable batching and a distributed caching mechanism. BlobShuffle is implemented as an add-on for Kafka Streams that requires only minimal code changes to existing applications, leaves Kafka and the underlying infrastructure unmodified, and preserves Kafka Streams' consistency and correctness guarantees. In a large-scale experimental evaluation on a Kubernetes-based AWS deployment, we show that BlobShuffle can reduce shuffling costs by more than 40x compared to native Kafka Streams shuffling while keeping the 95th percentile shuffle latency below 2 seconds. Moreover, it scales to processing more than 2 GiB/s without encountering a scalability limit in our experiments, indicating that BlobShuffle can economically support shuffle-intensive workloads at large scale.

</details>


### [357] [Fast TetraBFT: Optimizing Latency Where It Matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03754)
*Antonio J. Fernández-Pinto,Manuel Bravo,Gregory Chockler,Alexey Gotsman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Fast TetraBFT 提出了一种更简单的协议设计，在无身份验证的 Byzantine 共识中实现了最优的 3 消息延迟，解决了现有替代方案过于复杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实现 3 消息延迟最优值的 ForgetIT 协议设计过于复杂，作者旨在寻找一种更简单、更高效的方法来实现相同的最优好情况延迟。

Method: 在现有的 TetraBFT 协议基础上，集成一个简单的快速路径（fast-path）包装器，从而在不增加复杂性的情况下降低延迟。

Result: 成功将 TetraBFT 协议的好情况延迟（good-case latency）从 5 个消息延迟降低至 3 个，同时保持了该协议原有的有界空间需求和低通信复杂度。

Conclusion: Fast TetraBFT 通过在 TetraBFT 上添加简单的快速路径包装器，证明了在部分同步网络中实现最优的 3 消息延迟无需复杂设计，兼顾了性能、空间效率和通信复杂度。

Abstract: Unauthenticated Byzantine consensus protocols achieve optimal failure resilience while relying only on authenticated point-to-point channels, not authenticated messages. They are an attractive building block for blockchains that do not mandate symmetric trust assumptions as well as for future post-quantum settings. We consider unauthenticated Byzantine consensus in partially synchronous networks and focus on optimizing its good-case latency - the worst-case time for correct processes to reach a decision under favorable conditions. A recently proposed ForgetIT protocol achieves an optimal good-case latency of 3 message delays but employs a highly complex design. We show that this complexity is unnecessary. To this end, we present Fast TetraBFT - an unauthenticated Byzantine consensus protocol that achieves optimal good-case latency by augmenting an existing TetraBFT protocol with a simple fast-path wrapper. Our solution lowers the good-case latency of TetraBFT from 5 to 3 message delays while preserving its bounded space requirements and low communication complexity.

</details>
